Mode 2'b01 is no listed mode (`kmac.hjson:442-452` gives the mode codes 0, 2 and 3 only), so no pair with it is in the list of `theory_of_operation.md:784-785`, and the same blocked outcome with the code 0x06 applies. Spec: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/kmac/regs/gen/adoc/kmac.adoc` (`CFG_SHADOWED`, `CMD`, `INTR_STATE`, `STATUS`, `ERR_CODE`); `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/kmac/data/kmac.hjson` for the mode, strength and command codes; `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/kmac/doc/theory_of_operation.md` (`UnexpectedModeStrength`).

*Test Coverage*: The 20 cells mode {SHAKE, cSHAKE} x strength {128, 224, 256, 384, 512} x `en_unsupported_modestrength` {0, 1}, in EDN entropy mode with `kmac_en` = 0, plus SHA3 with strength 128 and the bit clear, mode 2'b01 with a drawn strength and the bit clear, the same mode 2'b01 cell with the bit set (absorb and error at start graded, outside the owner window), and SHA3-256 with the bit clear as its control. The 12 cells with strength 224, 384 or 512 are unsupported. The 8 supported cells are the control. Each blocked start is followed by `CMD.err_processed`, a write-one clear of `kmac_err` and a legal SHA3-256 hash. Extends `sep_kmac_mode_strength_rand_test`, which drives legal cells only. This test grades only the new cells: the unsupported strengths with the enable bit clear and set, the SHA3-128 cell, the mode 2'b01 cell, the `ERR_CODE` value of each blocked cell, and the recovery. The legal cells of the supported group are controls and are not graded; the owner of the open test keeps the legal digests. Functional coverage: `sep_crypto_invalid_encoding_cg` (`x_kmac_mode_strength`, `cp_kmac_sha3_128`, `x_kmac_err_code`, `cp_kmac_err_recover`), `sep_kmac_cfg_cmd_cg` (`cp_unsup_mode`), `sep_crypto_intr_cg` (`x_line_gate`, `cp_hw_raise` kmac_err). The graded window of the owner opens in each cell after the KMAC reset and closes at the next cell, and steps 5 to 7 run once inside it. Each legal bin is held at its DUT event and samples only after its check passes: the leaf writes the pass label `kmac_unsup_pass` with 1 after CHK-KMAC-UNSUP and CHK-KMAC-ERRCODE of a blocked cell pass, with 2 after CHK-KMAC-ERRPROC of its recovery passes and with 3 after CHK-KMAC-UNSUP-EN of an enabled cell passes. The illegal bins sample at once. The first FAIL closes the graded window for the rest of the leaf.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Select the internal source in `SEP_CPU_CTRL.EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL`. Bring up ESRC, CSRNG and EDN. Set `SW_RESET_N` to the baseline of the common rules with the bits of OTBN, AES, HMAC and ABR clear. Log the baseline.
. Draw the plan with `SepSeededRng`: the three cell orders and the messages. Log `INFO-PLAN seed=<n>` with the orders.
. Supported group, each of the 8 cells in drawn order. Reset KMAC (bit 4 of `SW_RESET_N` low, then high). Read `INTR_STATE` and `ERR_CODE`. For a cSHAKE cell write the PREFIX words of the open helper (empty function name and customization string). Write `CFG_SHADOWED` twice with the mode, the strength and `en_unsupported_modestrength` of the cell, `kmac_en` = 0, `sideload` = 0, `entropy_mode` = EDN and `entropy_ready` = 1. Write `CMD.start`. Wait for `STATUS.sha3_absorb` = 1 (a bounded wait of 20000 `clk_i` cycles, the outer limit of the leaf). Read `INTR_STATE.kmac_err`. Log the clock count from `CMD.start` to absorb as the start time of the cell (`CTRL-KMAC-UNSUP`).
. Take t_clean as the longest start time of step 3 and log it. Step 5 uses t_clean as the length of its hold window, because a healthy KMAC reaches absorb within it.
. Blocked group: first the mode 2'b01 cell with its drawn strength and `en_unsupported_modestrength` = 0, then each of the 6 unsupported cells with `en_unsupported_modestrength` = 0 in drawn order. This is the error injection. Point: `CFG_SHADOWED`. Kind: mode 2'b01, or strength 224, 384 or 512 on SHAKE or cSHAKE. Expected response: `kmac_err` = 1 and no propagation of the start. Reset KMAC and open the graded window. Read `INTR_STATE` and `ERR_CODE` and expect 0. For a cSHAKE cell write the PREFIX words. Write `CFG_SHADOWED` twice with the cell, `kmac_en` = 0, `sideload` = 0, EDN mode and `entropy_ready` = 1. Read `CFG_SHADOWED` back and compare its mode, kstrength and `en_unsupported_modestrength` fields with the written values. Write `CMD.start`. Wait for `INTR_STATE.kmac_err` = 1 (a bounded wait of 8 x t_clean; a timeout fails the check). Read `STATUS`, `INTR_STATE` and `ERR_CODE`. Hold for a window of t_clean. Read `STATUS`, `ERR_CODE` and `INTR_STATE` again and log the window length. Compare bits 31:24 of both `ERR_CODE` values with 0x06 (CHK-KMAC-ERRCODE).
. Recovery for the same cell. Write `CMD.err_processed`. Write 1 to `INTR_STATE.kmac_err`. Read `INTR_STATE` and `STATUS`, and log `INTR_STATE.kmac_done` (`OBS-KMAC-ERRPROC-DONE`). Write `CFG_SHADOWED` twice with SHA3, strength 256, `kmac_en` = 0, `sideload` = 0, EDN mode and `entropy_ready` = 1. Write `CMD.start`. Wait for `STATUS.sha3_absorb` = 1. Write the drawn message to the `MSG_FIFO` window (empty for a message of 0 bytes). Write `CMD.process`. Wait for `STATUS.sha3_squeeze` = 1 and `INTR_STATE.kmac_done` = 1 (a bounded wait). Read the two `STATE` shares at offsets 0x400 and 0x500 and XOR them word by word. Write `CMD.done`. Read `INTR_STATE.kmac_err`.
. SHA3 group, after the blocked group. Control: reset KMAC, write `CFG_SHADOWED` twice with SHA3, strength 256, the bit clear, `kmac_en` = 0, `sideload` = 0, EDN mode and `entropy_ready` = 1, write `CMD.start` and wait for `STATUS.sha3_absorb` = 1 (a bounded wait of 20000 `clk_i` cycles) with `kmac_err` = 0 (`CTRL-KMAC-UNSUP-SHA3`, start time logged as `start_clk`). The hold of the next cell is the larger of t_clean and that start time. Then run steps 5 and 6 for SHA3 with strength 128 and the bit clear, with that hold and a wait bound of 8 times it.
. Enabled group, each of the 6 unsupported cells with `en_unsupported_modestrength` = 1 in drawn order. Reset KMAC. Read `INTR_STATE` and `ERR_CODE` and log them. For a cSHAKE cell write the PREFIX words. Write `CFG_SHADOWED` twice with the cell, `kmac_en` = 0, `sideload` = 0, EDN mode and `entropy_ready` = 1. Write `CMD.start`. Wait for `STATUS.sha3_absorb` = 1 (a bounded wait of 8 x t_clean; a timeout fails the check). Read `INTR_STATE` and `ERR_CODE` and grade the absorb, `kmac_err` = 1 and `ERR_CODE[31:24]` = 0x06 (CHK-KMAC-UNSUP-EN). Reset KMAC. The leaf does not process a message in this group.
. Mode 2'b01 with `en_unsupported_modestrength` = 1, after the enabled group, with the owner window closed. Reset KMAC. Write `CFG_SHADOWED` twice with mode 2'b01, the drawn strength of step 5, the bit set, `kmac_en` = 0, `sideload` = 0, EDN mode and `entropy_ready` = 1. Read `CFG_SHADOWED` back. Write `INTR_ENABLE.kmac_err` = 1 and read it back. Write `CMD.start`. Wait for `STATUS.sha3_absorb` = 1 (a bounded wait of 8 x t_clean; a timeout fails the check). Read `INTR_STATE` and `ERR_CODE` and grade the absorb, `kmac_err` = 1, `ERR_CODE[31:24]` = 0x06 and the `CFG_SHADOWED` readback (CHK-KMAC-UNSUP-EN, mode 2'b01). Wait for a rise of vector bit 22 after the start (a bounded wait of 8 x t_clean; `CTRL-KMAC-UNSUP-LINE`). When absorb reads 1, write the drawn message to the `MSG_FIFO` window. Write `CMD.process`. Wait for `INTR_STATE.kmac_done` or `kmac_err` (a bounded wait of 20000 `clk_i` cycles). Read `INTR_STATE`, `ERR_CODE`, `STATUS` and the first four words of the `STATE` window, and log them (`OBS-KMAC-MODE01`). Reset KMAC. No FCOV bin owns this cell.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-KMAC-UNSUP
|With `en_unsupported_modestrength` = 0, `CMD.start` on SHAKE or cSHAKE with strength 224, 384 or 512, on SHA3 with strength 128, or on mode 2'b01 with a drawn strength, gives `INTR_STATE.kmac_err` = 1, `STATUS.sha3_idle` = 1, `STATUS.sha3_absorb` = 0, and `INTR_STATE.kmac_done` = 0 (`kmac.adoc`, `CFG_SHADOWED.kstrength`: another strength gives an error when hashing starts; `CFG_SHADOWED.en_unsupported_modestrength`: with the bit clear KMAC does not propagate the start; `ERR_CODE`: reset 0; for SHA3-128, `theory_of_operation.md:784-785` and `kmac.adoc:117-118`; for mode 2'b01, `kmac.hjson:442-452`; `theory_of_operation.md:787` says the IP "proceeds the hashing operation" for other combinations, and the TT RDL text of `en_unsupported_modestrength` (`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/kmac/regs/kmac.rdl:212-218`: "If not set, KMAC won't propagate the SW command (CmdStart) to the rest of the blocks") rules for the SEP build). A wrong DUT that propagates the start shows `sha3_absorb` = 1 or no error. A DUT that raises the error and still absorbs fails the first read or the read after the hold window. The check reads `kmac_err` and `ERR_CODE` as 0 before the start (`ERR_CODE` reset value 0), so the later values come from the start. It also reads `CFG_SHADOWED` back after the two writes and requires the mode, kstrength and `en_unsupported_modestrength` fields to equal the written values, so the staged cell reached the DUT. The check grades the `kmac_err` line: `INTR_ENABLE.kmac_err` reads its reset value 0 before the start (`kmac.adoc:67`, reset 0; the leaf writes the enable only in the mode 2'b01 cell with the bit set, which runs after every blocked cell), and vector bit 22 (KMAC error, source 23, `hw/sys/sep/doc/interrupts.adoc:138-139`) is high on no clock from the `CMD.start` write to the end of the hold window (`kmac.adoc:67`: "Enable interrupt when INTR_STATE.kmac_err is set"; the enable is 0). A wrong DUT that drives the line from the state alone fails; the `kmac_err` read of 1 in the same line is the positive probe that the source is set. Control (`CTRL-KMAC-UNSUP-LINE`, in the mode 2'b01 cell with `en_unsupported_modestrength` = 1 and the owner window closed): with `INTR_ENABLE.kmac_err` written and read 1 before `CMD.start`, `kmac_err` reads 1 and the same watcher of vector bit 22 sees a rise within 8 x t_clean of the start, so the watched bit is the live `kmac_err` line; a watcher bound to a dead or wrong bit logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf. Evidence: `CTRL-KMAC-UNSUP-LINE seen: seed=<n> mode=rsvd1 err_en=1 err=1 vec22_rise_clk=<c> start_clk=<s> bound=<b>`. Anchor: `INTR_STATE`, `STATUS` and `ERR_CODE` readback after a bounded wait for `kmac_err`, then a second `STATUS` read after the hold window of t_clean, in which a healthy KMAC reaches absorb on a supported cell. Control: in the same seed each supported cell (128 or 256 on SHAKE or cSHAKE, with either value of the enable bit) raises no `kmac_err` and reaches `STATUS.sha3_absorb` = 1 after `CMD.start`, so the trial flow reaches absorb when the strength is legal; for the SHA3-128 cell the control is SHA3-256 with the bit clear (`CTRL-KMAC-UNSUP-SHA3`). The leaf logs `CTRL-KMAC-UNSUP seed=<n> mode=<m> kstrength=<128 or 256> en_unsup=<e> err=0 absorb=1 start_clk=<c>` for each supported cell, and a missing or failing control logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf. The supported cells are not graded here: `sep_kmac_mode_strength_rand_test` keeps the legal cells, and `sep_kmac_stream_cfg_rand_test` grades the absorb stage after `start`. The `ERR_CODE` value after the start is graded by CHK-KMAC-ERRCODE. Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-UNSUP PASS seed=<n> mode=<m> kstrength=<s> en_unsup=0 cfg=0x<c> cfg_exp=0x<c> pre_err=0 pre_code=0x0 err=1 absorb=0 idle=1 done=0 err_clk=<e> hold_clk=<w> err_en=0 vec22_high_clk=0 expected_vec22_high_clk=0`, with `mode=sha3 kstrength=128` for the SHA3 cell and `mode=rsvd1` for the mode 2'b01 cell, and `CTRL-KMAC-UNSUP-SHA3 seen: seed=<n> mode=sha3 kstrength=256 en_unsup=0 err=0 absorb=1 start_clk=<c> bound=20000`.
|CHK-KMAC-UNSUP-EN
|With `en_unsupported_modestrength` = 1 the unsupported cell reaches `STATUS.sha3_absorb` = 1 after `CMD.start` (`kmac.adoc`, `CFG_SHADOWED.en_unsupported_modestrength`: KMAC moves forward with an unsupported mode and strength). On the reads after absorb `INTR_STATE.kmac_err` = 1 and `ERR_CODE[31:24]` = 0x06 `UnexpectedModeStrength`: the error text names no condition on the bit ("If the combination of the `mode` and `kstrength` does not fall into above, the module reports the `UnexpectedModeStrength` error.", `theory_of_operation.md:785`, code 0x06 at :726), and `kmac.adoc:180-181` says another SHAKE or cSHAKE strength "will result error when hashing starts". A wrong DUT that ignores the bit stays idle, as it does with the bit clear; a wrong DUT that drops the error with the bit set fails the `kmac_err` or code read. Anchor: `STATUS`, `INTR_STATE` and `ERR_CODE` readbacks after a bounded wait. Controls: `kmac_err` and `ERR_CODE` read 0 before the start (`pre_err`, `pre_code`), and the same (mode, strength) cell with the bit clear in the same seed does not absorb (CHK-KMAC-UNSUP). The mode 2'b01 cell with the bit set is graded the same way, after the owner window closes: mode 2'b01 is an unsupported mode (see CHK-KMAC-UNSUP), and the text of the bit ("SW may set this field for KMAC to move forward with unsupported Keccak Mode and Strength configurations", `kmac.adoc:114-115`, `kmac.rdl:212-218`) states that it absorbs; its `CFG_SHADOWED` readback must equal the written mode, kstrength and bit, so the staged cell reached the DUT, and its control is the mode 2'b01 cell with the bit clear and the same drawn strength in CHK-KMAC-UNSUP. Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-UNSUP-EN PASS seed=<n> mode=<m> kstrength=<s> en_unsup=1 absorb=1 absorb_clk=<c> bound=<b> pre_err=0 pre_code=0x0 err=1 code=0x06 expected=0x06 ctrl_absorb=0` and `CHK-KMAC-UNSUP-EN PASS seed=<n> mode=rsvd1 mode_code=1 kstrength=<s> en_unsup=1 cfg=0x<c> cfg_exp=0x<c> absorb=1 absorb_clk=<c> bound=<b> pre_err=0 pre_code=0x0 err=1 code=0x06 expected=0x06`.
|CHK-KMAC-ERRCODE
|For each cell of CHK-KMAC-UNSUP (the bit clear; SHAKE or cSHAKE with strength 224, 384 or 512, SHA3 with strength 128, and mode 2'b01), `ERR_CODE[31:24]` = 0x06 on the read after `kmac_err` sets and on the read after the hold, with `kmac_err` still 1 after the hold. Spec: "0x06 \| UnexpectedModeStrength \| SHA3 mode and Keccak Strength combination is not expected." (`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/kmac/doc/theory_of_operation.md:726`) and :784-785 (the listed combinations); "Bits 31:24 contain the error code ... and bits 23:0 contain additional debug information." (`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/kmac/regs/gen/adoc/kmac.adoc:344`). Bits 23:0 have no stated value and are masked; they are printed as `debug`. A wrong DUT reports another code or none. Anchor: `ERR_CODE` readback. Control: `ERR_CODE` reads 0 after the reset and before the start of the same cell (`pre_code`, `kmac.adoc:343`), so the code comes from that start. Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-ERRCODE PASS seed=<n> mode=<m> kstrength=<s> en_unsup=0 pre_code=0x0 code=0x06/0x06 expected=0x06 err_after_hold=1 debug=0x<d1>/0x<d2>`.
|CHK-KMAC-ERRPROC
|After `CMD.err_processed` and a write-one clear of `INTR_STATE.kmac_err`, `kmac_err` reads 0 and `STATUS.sha3_idle` reads 1 (`kmac.adoc`, `CMD.err_processed`: the state machine returns to its reset state). A legal SHA3-256 hash of the drawn message then returns the golden digest, and `kmac_err` still reads 0 after the hash. A wrong DUT that stays in the error state does not absorb or does not finish (the bounded wait times out), returns a wrong digest, or keeps `kmac_err` set. The check shows that the engine works after the recovery sequence, not that `err_processed` was needed: the blocked start already left `STATUS.sha3_idle` = 1, and no text states that a start before `err_processed` is refused. Anchor: `INTR_STATE` and `STATUS` readback, and the XOR of the two `STATE` shares against the `sep_kmac_golden` SHA3-256 digest of the message. Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-ERRPROC PASS seed=<n> mode=<m> kstrength=<s> len=<L> err_cleared=1 idle=1 digest_eq_golden=1 err_after=0 squeeze_clk=<c>`.
|OBS-KMAC-ERRPROC-DONE
|Not a graded check. `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/kmac/doc/programmers_guide.md:202` says "If the error occurred while the KMAC HW IP was being used from SW (i.e., not via an HW application interface), the `kmac_done` IRQ is raised when the KMAC HW IP is ready again." The TT text of `CMD.err_processed` acts on a state machine that stays in its error state, and the TT text of `en_unsupported_modestrength` = 0 says the start is not propagated; no TT text says whether such a start leaves a state machine in its error state. The DUT reads `kmac_done` = 0 on the `INTR_STATE` read after `err_processed` and the write-one clear of `kmac_err` (bit 2 only), and that gap is open. Evidence: `OBS-KMAC-ERRPROC-DONE seed=<n> mode=<m> kstrength=<s> len=<L> kmac_done=<d> expected_text=1 idle=<i> intr=0x<v>`.
|OBS-KMAC-MODE01
|Not a graded check. CHK-KMAC-UNSUP-EN grades the absorb of the pair of mode 2'b01 and `en_unsupported_modestrength` = 1. No text states the rest: after the message and process the leaf logs the `kmac_done` and `kmac_err` bits, `ERR_CODE`, `STATUS` and the first four words of the `STATE` window. Evidence: `OBS-KMAC-MODE01 seed=<n> mode_code=1 kstrength=<s> en_unsup=1 len=<l> done=<d> kmac_err=<e> end_seen=<s> end_clk=<c> err_code=0x<c> status=0x<s> state=<w0:w1:w2:w3>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: This test grades the unsupported strengths on SHAKE and cSHAKE, SHA3 with strength 128, the `ERR_CODE` code of each blocked cell and a correct hash after `CMD.err_processed`. The following are not claimed. That `err_processed` is needed for the recovery, and `kmac_done` after `err_processed` (logged only, `OBS-KMAC-ERRPROC-DONE`). Mode 2'b01 with `en_unsupported_modestrength` = 1 runs once: only its absorb and its error at start are graded (CHK-KMAC-UNSUP-EN), and its end state, `ERR_CODE` after process and digest are logged only (`OBS-KMAC-MODE01`). SHA3 with strength 128 is driven with the bit clear only. `ERR_CODE[23:0]` is not graded (not stated). The digest of an unsupported cell is not graded. KMAC mode (`kmac_en` = 1) is not driven. The digests of the legal cells belong to `sep_kmac_mode_strength_rand_test`, and the `kmac_err` interrupt line with the enable set belongs to the extension leg of `sep_irq_ip_to_aggregator_test`; CHK-KMAC-UNSUP grades only that the line stays low with the enable at its reset value 0, and `CTRL-KMAC-UNSUP-LINE` uses the enabled line only as its control.

'''''

[[sep_kmac_stream_cfg_rand_test]]
===== 1.36 `sep_kmac_stream_cfg_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy (KMAC)

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense +esrc_noise_force`, 4 seeds; testlist `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`; Verilator and VCS

*Source Module*: `sep_kmac_stream_cfg_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip with the CPU-LSU master readback of the KMAC `INTR_STATE`, `INTR_ENABLE`, `STATUS`, `CFG_REGWEN`, `CFG_SHADOWED` and `STATE` registers (approved); `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` (approved, vector bit 20); `sep_kmac_golden` for the SHA3 and SHAKE digests. No observation tap. Inject: `kmac_fifo_drain_stall_i` (`tb_top.sv`, the crypto message-FIFO drain stall of `SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`), driven to 1 only while the first 16 or more message bytes of a rate + 1 cell are written and `STATUS` is read once, for the control of CHK-KMAC-FIFO-DONE; `STATUS` stays frontdoor.

*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the order of the 18 cells; the message bytes (the bytes of each multi-byte write differ from each other, so that a byte reorder changes the digest); the write width (32, 16 or 8 bit) of each message write; each cell write goes to byte lane 0 of the `MSG_FIFO` window. Cell i in drawn order takes the (`msg_endianness`, `state_endianness`) cell number i mod 4, so all four cells run on every seed. The plan places a one-byte write in at least one cell with `msg_endianness` = 1. After those draws it draws the cell that runs CHK-KMAC-REGWEN-GATED, the `PREFIX` index n and two non-zero masks. `reseed = 4` samples different orders, bytes, widths, cells and masks. The 18 cells are the same on every seed.

*Objective*: `CMD.start` moves `STATUS` to `sha3_absorb`, and `CMD.run` in the squeeze stage gives the next block of a SHAKE output. With no command written `STATUS` stays idle. With `msg_endianness` = 1 each multi-byte `MSG_FIFO` write is added to the message in big-endian byte order, and a one-byte write is not changed. With `state_endianness` = 1 each `STATE` word is converted to big-endian byte order, and the order of the words does not change. With `INTR_ENABLE.kmac_done` set, a completed hash sets `INTR_STATE.kmac_done` and vector bit 20 (source 21) rises. `CFG_REGWEN` reads 0 while the KMAC is in operation, and `CFG_SHADOWED` does not change then. Software writes bytes or words to any address of the `MSG_FIFO` window; the low 12 address bits are ignored (`kmac.hjson`, `MSG_FIFO`, `byte-write: "true"`). Spec: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/kmac/regs/gen/adoc/kmac.adoc` (`CFG_SHADOWED`, `CMD`, `STATUS`, `INTR_STATE`, `INTR_ENABLE`); `hw/sys/sep/doc/kmac.adoc` (OCAH Modifications); `hw/sys/sep/doc/interrupts.adoc` (SEP CPU Interrupt Vector Map); `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/kmac/data/kmac.hjson` for the codes, the Keccak rate of each strength, the `STATE` share layout and `CFG_REGWEN`.

*Test Coverage*: The 18 cells of the six legal (mode, strength) combinations (SHA3 224, 256, 384, 512; SHAKE 128, 256) x message length {rate minus 1, rate, rate plus 1}, with `kmac_en` = 0 in EDN entropy mode. All four (`msg_endianness`, `state_endianness`) cells on every seed, with drawn 32-bit, 16-bit and 8-bit writes. The `run` command after the first squeeze of every SHAKE cell, for both strengths. The idle window before every start. The `CFG_REGWEN` value in the idle window and in the absorb stage, with a locked double write of `CFG_SHADOWED` in the absorb stage of every cell, and on one drawn cell the gated writes of `PREFIX_n` and `ENTROPY_PERIOD`. The `kmac_done` line rise at the event with the enable set. The `STATUS` FIFO fields after the done event. One fixed narrow-lane message per seed (8-bit writes at byte lanes 1 to 3, 16-bit writes at byte lane 2), graded by CHK-KMAC-NARROW-WRITE outside the FCOV window. Extends `sep_kmac_mode_strength_rand_test`, which runs one endianness setting and the first squeeze only, grades the keyed digests, and whose helper polls squeeze after `process` and idle after `done` with failing timeouts. This entry grades only the new cells: the `start` stage, the idle state with no command, the configuration lock, the gated `PREFIX_n` and `ENTROPY_PERIOD`, both endianness bits, the length corners, `run` and the FIFO fields after the done event. It uses the squeeze after `process` and the idle after `done` as controls only; the owner of the open test keeps their grading. The done-line check extends `sep_irq_ip_to_aggregator_test` and `sep_irq_simultaneous_fanin_no_alias_test`, which raise `kmac_done` with `INTR_TEST` and the enable set and grade the line and the write-one clear. This entry grades the rise at the real completion event with the enable set; the enable gate of the line belongs to the extension leg of `sep_irq_ip_to_aggregator_test`, and the owners of the open tests keep the `INTR_TEST` walk. Functional coverage: `sep_kmac_cfg_cmd_cg` (`x_cmd_stage`, `x_endian`, `x_len_corner`, `cp_run_block`, `cp_fifo_status`, `cp_regwen_gated`), `sep_crypto_intr_cg` (`x_line_gate`, `cp_hw_raise` kmac_done). The lock of `CFG_SHADOWED` fills no Phase 2 bin. After every check and control of a cell passed, the leaf writes the pass label `kmac_stream_pass`, and the stage, endianness, length-corner, run and `kmac_done` cells of that cell sample only then. `cp_fifo_status` and `cp_regwen_gated` sample at the graded read and have no label. The first FAIL or missing control closes the graded window for the rest of the leaf.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Select the internal source in `SEP_CPU_CTRL.EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL`. Bring up ESRC, CSRNG and EDN. Set `SW_RESET_N` to the baseline of the common rules with the bits of OTBN, AES, HMAC and ABR clear. Log the baseline.
. Draw the plan with `SepSeededRng`: the cell order, the endianness cell of each cell, the messages and the write widths. Log `INFO-PLAN seed=<n>`.
. Reset KMAC (bit 4 of `SW_RESET_N` low, then high). Run one warm-up hash that no check grades. Write `CFG_SHADOWED` twice with SHA3, strength 256, `kmac_en` = 0, both endianness bits 0, `sideload` = 0, `entropy_mode` = EDN and `entropy_ready` = 1. Write `CMD.start` and log the clock count to `sha3_absorb` as t_start (`INFO-T-START seed=<n> t_start=<c> window_clk=<w>`). Write one message byte, write `CMD.process`, wait for `sha3_squeeze` = 1, write `CMD.done`, wait for `sha3_idle` = 1 (each a bounded wait of 20000 `clk_i` cycles, the outer limit of the leaf; a timeout fails the leaf), write 1 to `INTR_STATE.kmac_done` and read it as 0 (`CTRL-KMAC-SEQ phase=warm_up`; a 1 logs `CTRL-MISSING KMAC-SEQ` and fails the leaf). The idle window and the start wait of the cells are 4 x t_start. The cells run with the graded window open. Before the drawn cell of CHK-KMAC-REGWEN-GATED, with the graded window closed, run the control `CTRL-KMAC-REGWEN-GATED`: at idle with `CFG_REGWEN` = 1 write `PREFIX_n` and `ENTROPY_PERIOD` with pre XOR mask, require both to read back the written value, restore pre and require the restore to read back.
. For each cell in drawn order, write `CFG_SHADOWED` twice with its mode and strength, `kmac_en` = 0, its `msg_endianness` and `state_endianness`, `sideload` = 0, EDN mode and `entropy_ready` = 1. Read `CFG_SHADOWED` back as the cell value C (control of CHK-KMAC-REGWEN). On the drawn cell, read `PREFIX_n` and `ENTROPY_PERIOD` first as pre, at idle inside the graded window. Steps 5 to 15 run once per cell.
. Idle window. Write no command. For a window of 4 x t_start (logged) read `STATUS` and `INTR_STATE` repeatedly. Read `CFG_REGWEN` once in the window (CHK-KMAC-REGWEN).
. Write 1 to `INTR_ENABLE.kmac_done`.
. Write `CMD.start`. Wait for `STATUS` to show `sha3_absorb` = 1 and `sha3_idle` = 0, with a wait as long as the idle window (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check).
. Read `CFG_REGWEN`. Write `CFG_SHADOWED` twice with C and its `state_endianness` bit inverted, then read `CFG_SHADOWED` (CHK-KMAC-REGWEN; the AXI response of each of the two writes is logged as `locked_wr_resp`). On the drawn cell, write `PREFIX_n` with pre XOR mask (pre is the value read before step 4, mask is drawn and non-zero), read `PREFIX_n` (CHK-KMAC-REGWEN-GATED, role=prefix) and write `ENTROPY_PERIOD` with pre XOR mask in the prescaler field.
. Write the message of the cell (length rate minus 1, rate or rate plus 1 bytes) to byte lane 0 of the `MSG_FIFO` window in the drawn widths (the window ignores the low 12 address bits, `kmac.hjson` `MSG_FIFO`). A write that does not complete OKAY fails CHK-KMAC-DIGEST of the cell (`reason=msg_write_resp`). On a rate + 1 cell, drive `kmac_fifo_drain_stall_i` to 1 for the first writes that carry 16 or more message bytes, read `STATUS` once with the inject still at 1 (control of CHK-KMAC-FIFO-DONE), then drive the inject to 0 and write the rest of the message.
. Write `CMD.process`. Read no `STATUS` before the done event of step 11.
. Sample vector bit 20 of `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` at every clock from the `CMD.process` write of step 10 and require it to read 0 there. Poll `INTR_STATE` from the `CMD.process` write and take a clock mark before each read; the lower bound of the rise is the mark of the last `INTR_STATE` read that shows `kmac_done` = 0, or the `CMD.process` mark if the first read already shows 1. Wait for `INTR_STATE.kmac_done` = 1 (a bounded wait). Wait for bit 20 = 1 within 4 x t_start (a bounded wait; a timeout fails CHK-KMAC-DONE-LINE). Then read `STATUS` once, which is the first `STATUS` read after the done event: `sha3_squeeze` = 1 is the control of CHK-KMAC-SEQ, and the read is graded by CHK-KMAC-FIFO-DONE. The read comes after CHK-KMAC-DONE-LINE is graded. Then read `CFG_REGWEN` (CHK-KMAC-REGWEN, `part=squeeze`).
. Write 1 to `INTR_STATE.kmac_done`. Read `INTR_STATE.kmac_done` as 0 (CHK-KMAC-DONE-W1C; also the `CTRL-KMAC-SEQ` field `done_after_w1c`) and log vector bit 20 as `OBS-KMAC-DONE-FALL`.
. Read the two `STATE` shares at offsets 0x400 and 0x500 and XOR them word by word. The compared length is the digest size of a SHA3 cell, or the Keccak rate in bytes of a SHAKE cell.
. For a SHAKE cell, write `CMD.run`. Poll `STATUS` and `STATE` until `sha3_squeeze` = 1 and the rate block equals the second block of the golden output (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check).
. Write `CMD.done`. Poll `CFG_REGWEN` every 4 clocks until it reads 1 (bound 20000 clocks from the write) and log the clocks from the `CMD.done` write to the first read of 1 (`OBS-KMAC-REGWEN-SQUEEZE`). Wait for `STATUS` to show `sha3_idle` = 1 and `sha3_squeeze` = 0 (a bounded wait). Write 1 to `INTR_STATE.kmac_done` and read it as 0, as the warm-up of step 3 does, so the idle window of the next cell starts from a cleared bit (`CTRL-KMAC-SEQ` field `done_cleared`). On the drawn cell, read `ENTROPY_PERIOD` once the poll shows idle (CHK-KMAC-REGWEN-GATED, role=entropy_period). A wait that times out in steps 5 to 15 fails its check, resets KMAC and moves to the next cell.
. `INTR_TEST` leg, once per seed after the last cell, with the graded window closed. Reset KMAC. Read `INTR_STATE.fifo_empty`. Write `INTR_TEST.fifo_empty`. Read `INTR_STATE.fifo_empty` one clock later (CHK-KMAC-FE-INTRTEST grades the read before the write and this read) and after 64 clocks, write 1 to `INTR_STATE.fifo_empty` and read it again. Log the last two reads (`OBS-KMAC-FE-INTRTEST`). Reset KMAC.
. Narrow-lane leg, once per seed after the `INTR_TEST` leg, with the graded window closed. Reset KMAC. Write `CFG_SHADOWED` twice with SHA3, strength 256, both endianness bits 0, `kmac_en` = 0, `sideload` = 0, EDN mode and `entropy_ready` = 1. Write `CMD.start` and wait for `sha3_absorb` (a bounded wait). Write the fixed 10-byte message as eight writes in the fixed order: 8-bit writes at byte lanes 1, 2 and 3 and 16-bit writes at byte lane 2 of the `MSG_FIFO` window. Record the response of each write. Write `CMD.process`, wait for `sha3_squeeze` (a bounded wait), read and XOR the `STATE` shares (CHK-KMAC-NARROW-WRITE). Reset KMAC.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-KMAC-SEQ
|After `CMD.start` `STATUS` shows `sha3_absorb` = 1 and `sha3_idle` = 0, and after `CMD.run` in the squeeze stage a `STATUS` read taken after the `STATE` read that shows block 2 shows `sha3_squeeze` = 1 (`kmac.adoc`, `STATUS`, `CMD`). A wrong DUT stays idle after `start`, shows idle and absorb together, or leaves the squeeze stage after `run`. Anchor: `STATUS` readback, once per cell for `start` and once per SHAKE cell for `run`. Controls in the same cell (`CTRL-KMAC-SEQ`): `sha3_squeeze` = 1 after `process` and `sha3_idle` = 1 after `done`, which show that the polls reach the other stages, and `kmac_done` reads 0 after each write-one clear (steps 12 and 15, and the warm-up of step 3: `INTR_STATE` is RW1C); a missing control logs `CTRL-MISSING KMAC-SEQ` and fails the leaf. Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-SEQ PASS seed=<n> cmd=<start or run> idle=<i> absorb=<a> squeeze=<s> ctrl_process_squeeze=1 ctrl_done_idle=1` and `CTRL-KMAC-SEQ seen seed=<n> ctrl_process_squeeze=1 ctrl_done_idle=1 done_after_w1c=0 done_cleared=1`.
|CHK-KMAC-DIGEST
|The two `STATE` shares XORed equal the Keccak golden of the message. The message is the byte sequence that the writes add: a multi-byte write adds its bytes in big-endian order when `msg_endianness` = 1, and a one-byte write is not changed. Each `STATE` word is byte-swapped when `state_endianness` = 1, and the word order is not changed (`kmac.adoc`, `CFG_SHADOWED.msg_endianness` and `state_endianness`). The compared length is the digest size of a SHA3 cell, or the Keccak rate of a SHAKE cell. A wrong DUT with an endianness bit that does nothing, or that swaps the wrong bytes, returns a different digest. The unique bytes in each write make a reorder visible. Every message write of the cell completes OKAY. Anchor: the B response of each message write and the `STATE` readback against the `sep_kmac_golden` digest. Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-DIGEST PASS seed=<n> mode=<m> strength=<s> msg_end=<x> state_end=<y> len=<L> kmac_en=0 widths=<w> words=<k> digest_eq_golden=1`.
|CHK-KMAC-RUN
|For a SHAKE cell, `CMD.run` in the squeeze stage gives the next block of the extended output, equal to the second rate block of the golden (`kmac.hjson`, `CMD.run`: it triggers the 24 rounds in the squeeze stage). A wrong DUT whose `run` does nothing still shows the first block and times out. Anchor: `STATE` readback against the `sep_kmac_golden` output of twice the rate in bytes, with the same byte swap as the first block. Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-RUN PASS seed=<n> shake=<128 or 256> state_end=<y> block2_eq_golden=1 run_clk=<c> polls=<p>`.
|CHK-KMAC-IDLE
|With no command written, `STATUS` shows `sha3_idle` = 1, `sha3_absorb` = 0 and `sha3_squeeze` = 0, and `INTR_STATE.kmac_done` reads 0, at every read of the window before each start (`kmac.adoc`, `STATUS`: `sha3_idle` resets to 1). A wrong DUT that leaves idle or raises `kmac_done` without a command shows it in the window. The window is 4 x t_start, logged. Anchor: `STATUS` and `INTR_STATE` readback. Control (`CTRL-KMAC-IDLE`): the `CMD.start` that follows changes `STATUS` to absorb within a wait of the same length, so the window is long enough for a healthy KMAC to react. Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-IDLE PASS seed=<n> window_clk=<w> reads=<r> idle=1 absorb=0 squeeze=0 done=0 ctrl_absorb_clk=<c>`.
|CHK-KMAC-DONE-LINE
|At the completion event with `INTR_ENABLE.kmac_done` set, `INTR_STATE.kmac_done` reads 1 and vector bit 20 (source 21) rises. The first rise is at or after the mark of the last `INTR_STATE` read that showed `kmac_done` = 0 (the `CMD.process` write when the first read already shows 1; the state bit is the source that drives the line, `kmac.adoc:57`, `:69`), and within 4 x t_start of the mark of the `CMD.process` write (`interrupts.adoc`, `kmac.adoc`, `INTR_STATE`, `INTR_ENABLE`). A wrong DUT that sets the state bit and never raises the line, or that raises it before the hash ends, fails one of these bounds. Anchor: `INTR_STATE` and `STATUS` readback and the vector probe. Control: vector bit 20 reads 0 at the `CMD.process` write, so the rise comes from the event. Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-DONE-LINE PASS seed=<n> enable=1 state=1 vec20_before=0 vec20=1 rise_clk=<r> lower_clk=<l> lower_src=<process or done0> process_clk=<p> rise_bound=<b>`; `OBS-KMAC-DONE-FALL seed=<n> cell=<i> vec20_after_w1c=<v>` is logged and not graded: no text states the clock at which the line falls.
|CHK-KMAC-DONE-W1C
|After `INTR_STATE.kmac_done` read 1 at the completion event, a write of 1 to `INTR_STATE.kmac_done` makes the next read show 0 (`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/kmac/regs/gen/adoc/kmac.adoc:57`: `kmac_done` is RW1C, reset 0x0). Graded on every cell. A DUT that ignores the write-one clear, or re-sets the bit with no new completion, fails. Anchor: the `INTR_STATE` readback. Control: CHK-KMAC-DONE-LINE read the bit as 1 in the same cell (`state_before=1`). Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-DONE-W1C PASS seed=<n> cell=<i> ... state_before=1 state_after_w1c=0`.
|CHK-KMAC-REGWEN
|In the idle window with no command `CFG_REGWEN` reads 1 (RDL reset value 1). In the absorb stage after `CMD.start` it reads 0, and a double write of `CFG_SHADOWED` with the `state_endianness` bit inverted leaves `CFG_SHADOWED` at the cell value C (`kmac.hjson`, `CFG_REGWEN`: the contents of `CFG_SHADOWED` cannot change while the KMAC is in operation). A wrong DUT that keeps the register writable reads back the inverted bit, and CHK-KMAC-DIGEST of the same cell then fails on the `STATE` byte order. Anchor: the `CFG_REGWEN` and `CFG_SHADOWED` readback. Control (`CTRL-KMAC-REGWEN`): the write of step 4 in the idle state reads back as C, so the write path is live. In the squeeze stage, on the read after `kmac_done` with the `STATUS` read before it showing `sha3_squeeze` = 1, `CFG_REGWEN` reads 0 (`kmac.hjson:354-358`; `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/kmac/doc/programmers_guide.md:5`: "The software can update the KMAC/SHA3 configurations only when the IP is in the idle state."; squeeze is not idle). A wrong DUT that drops the lock once absorb ends reads 1. Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-REGWEN PASS seed=<n> cell=<i> idle_regwen=1 absorb_regwen=0 cfg=0x<C> cfg_after_locked_write=0x<C> locked_wr_resp=[<r>,<r>]` and `CHK-KMAC-REGWEN PASS seed=<n> cell=<i> part=squeeze squeeze=1 squeeze_regwen=0`. The AXI response of each locked write is logged and not graded, because no text states it.
|CHK-KMAC-FIFO-DONE
|After the done event the message has left the FIFO: on the first `STATUS` read after `INTR_STATE.kmac_done` reads 1, `fifo_depth` is 0, `fifo_empty` is 1 and `fifo_full` is 0 (`kmac.adoc:226-239`: `fifo_depth` counts the occupied entries, `fifo_empty` resets to 1). The leaf polls `INTR_STATE` from the `CMD.process` write and takes this read after it graded CHK-KMAC-DONE-LINE. A wrong DUT that misses a FIFO decrement shows a depth above 0, and one with `fifo_empty` stuck at 0 or `fifo_full` stuck at 1 shows that value. The alert bits and the reserved bits are not graded, and the whole word is logged (`INFO-KMAC-FIFO-DONE`). Anchor: the `STATUS` readback. Control (`CTRL-KMAC-FIFO-DONE`, on every rate + 1 cell): the leaf wrote more message bytes than the rate of the strength (`written` above `rate`), and the `STATUS` read of step 9 under `kmac_fifo_drain_stall_i` shows `fifo_depth` >= 1 and `fifo_empty` = 0, so the FIFO held message words that the graded read later shows drained; a `STATUS` stuck at its reset value fails the control. A missing control logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf. Control evidence: `CTRL-KMAC-FIFO-DONE seen: seed=<n> cell=<i> ... rate=<r> written=<w> fill_bytes=16 fill_fifo_depth=<d> fill_fifo_empty=0 fill_status=<s>`. Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-FIFO-DONE PASS seed=<n> cell=<i> mode=<m> strength=<s> len=<L> rate=<r> written=<w> fifo_depth=0 expected_fifo_depth=0 fifo_empty=1 fifo_full=0 squeeze=<s>`.
|CHK-KMAC-REGWEN-GATED
|On one drawn cell per run, with `CFG_REGWEN` reading 0 in the absorb stage, a write of `PREFIX_n` with pre XOR mask reads back the earlier value pre (role=prefix), and a write of `ENTROPY_PERIOD` with pre XOR mask in the prescaler field reads back pre at idle after `CMD.done`, on the `prescaler` and `wait_timer` field bits (role=entropy_period). Spec: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/kmac/doc/registers.md:462` (`ENTROPY_PERIOD`) and `:728` (`PREFIX`) give "Register enable: `CFG_REGWEN`"; `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/kmac/data/kmac.hjson:772`, `:1001`. A wrong DUT that does not gate `PREFIX` or `ENTROPY_PERIOD` by `CFG_REGWEN` reads back the written value. The AXI response of a gated write is logged as `wr_resp` and is not graded, because no text states it. `OBS-KMAC-REGWEN-GATED-ERR` logs `kmac_err` and `ERR_CODE` after the gated writes and grades neither: `registers.md:722-725` (`PREFIX`) says that while the engine hashes it discards attempts to update "the secret keys" and reports an error, and gives no code; whether that error applies to a gated `PREFIX` write is open against the DUT, and `OBS-KMAC-REGWEN-GATED-RSVD` logs the reserved bits of `ENTROPY_PERIOD`. Anchor: the `CFG_REGWEN` read before the write and the register readback. Control (`CTRL-KMAC-REGWEN-GATED`, before the drawn cell with the graded window closed): at idle with `CFG_REGWEN` = 1 the same writes read back pre XOR mask, and the restore reads back pre (`ctrl_twin_landed=1`). Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-REGWEN-GATED PASS seed=<n> cell=<i> role=prefix regwen=0 ctrl_twin_landed=1 prefix_n=<n> pre=0x<p> wrote=0x<w> read=0x<p> expected=0x<p> wr_resp=<r>` and `CHK-KMAC-REGWEN-GATED PASS seed=<n> cell=<i> role=entropy_period regwen=0 ctrl_twin_landed=1 idle_at_read=1 pre=0x<p> wrote=0x<w> read=0x<e> expected=0x<e> wr_resp=<r>`.
|CHK-KMAC-NARROW-WRITE
|A write of 8 or 16 bits at a non-zero byte lane of the `MSG_FIFO` window completes OKAY and adds its bytes to the message (`kmac.hjson`, `MSG_FIFO`, `byte-write: "true"`: software writes bytes or words to any address of the window; the low 12 address bits are ignored). A wrong DUT that refuses the write (SLVERR), or drops or moves its bytes, returns a non-OKAY response or a digest that differs from the golden of the bytes in write order. Anchor: the B response of each write and the `STATE` readback against the `sep_kmac_golden` SHA3-256 digest. Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-NARROW-WRITE PASS seed=<n> mode=sha3 strength=256 len=10 lanes_widths=<l/w,...> writes=8 all_okay=1 refused_lane_width_resp=- digest_eq_golden=1`.
|OBS-KMAC-REGWEN-SQUEEZE
|Not a graded check. No text states the clock at which `CFG_REGWEN` returns to 1 after `CMD.done`, and no line grades that clock. After the `CMD.done` write the leaf reads `CFG_REGWEN` every 4 clocks until it reads 1 (bound 20000 clocks) and logs the clocks from the write to the first read of 1 (`regwen_return_clk`, read-granular), or `not_returned bound=20000`. The squeeze value is graded by CHK-KMAC-REGWEN (`part=squeeze`), and the value at idle by the CHK-KMAC-REGWEN idle read of the next cell, which follows with no reset. Evidence: `OBS-KMAC-REGWEN-SQUEEZE seed=<n> cell=<i> squeeze=<s> squeeze_regwen=<r> regwen_return_clk=<c or not_returned bound=20000> idle_after_done=<d> done_regwen=<r>`.
|CHK-KMAC-FE-INTRTEST
|After a KMAC reset, at idle, `INTR_STATE.fifo_empty` reads 0 (reset value 0x0, `kmac.adoc:50`), and the first read after a write of 1 to `INTR_TEST.fifo_empty` reads 1 (`kmac.adoc:80`: "Write 1 to force !!INTR_STATE.fifo_empty to 1."). A wrong DUT that does not connect the test bit to the state bit reads 0 after the write; one with the bit set before the write fails the first read. Anchor: the `INTR_STATE` readback. The leg runs with the graded FCOV window closed. Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-FE-INTRTEST PASS seed=<n> state_before=0 state_1clk=1`.
|OBS-KMAC-FE-INTRTEST
|Not a graded check. No text states whether `INTR_STATE.fifo_empty` stays set after an `INTR_TEST` write at idle, or whether a write-one clear acts on it. The leaf logs the bit after 64 clocks and after the write-one clear. Evidence: `OBS-KMAC-FE-INTRTEST seed=<n> state_settle=<s> settle_clk=64 state_after_w1c=<c>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: This test grades the stages, the endianness bits, the length corners, `run`, the idle state, the configuration lock in the absorb and squeeze stages, the `kmac_done` line and the narrow-lane message writes for SHA3 and SHAKE with `kmac_en` = 0. The clock at which `CFG_REGWEN` returns to 1 is not stated and is not graded; the leaf logs it after `CMD.done` of each cell (`OBS-KMAC-REGWEN-SQUEEZE`). The following are not claimed. `msg_mask` and `entropy_fast_process` act only in KMAC mode, and the keyed digest belongs to `sep_kmac_mode_strength_rand_test`. The cSHAKE cells are not driven, because their golden needs an encoded prefix that this test does not write. Whether `kmac_done` rises again after `run` or `done` is not stated: the leaf grades no value of the bit after the read that follows the clear of step 12 (CHK-KMAC-DONE-W1C) until the clear of step 15, and the idle window of the next cell follows the clear of step 15. The `INTR_TEST` walk of the `kmac_done` line with the enable set belongs to `sep_irq_ip_to_aggregator_test` and `sep_irq_simultaneous_fanin_no_alias_test`. The enable clear, the enable flip and the write-one clear of the line belong to the extension leg of `sep_irq_ip_to_aggregator_test`. The `fifo_empty` lines belong to `sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test`, and the `kmac_err` line belongs to the extension leg of `sep_irq_ip_to_aggregator_test` and to `sep_kmac_unsupported_cfg_inject_test`. The `STATUS` fields `fifo_depth`, `fifo_empty` and `fifo_full` are graded after the done event only; the depth during a burst, `fifo_full` at depth (the depth is not stated) and the two alert bits are not graded, and the effect of `msg_mask` and `entropy_fast_process` has no stated observation (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>).

'''''

[[sep_kmac_entropy_mode_rand_test]]
===== 1.37 `sep_kmac_entropy_mode_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy (KMAC)

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense +esrc_noise_force`, 4 seeds; testlist `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`; Verilator and VCS

*Source Module*: `sep_kmac_entropy_mode_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip with the CPU-LSU master readback of the KMAC registers (approved); `crypto_edn_req_o[1]` and `crypto_edn_ack_o[1]` (approved; client 1 is KMAC); `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` (approved, vector bit 22 for the `kmac_err` line, used for the clock count of the timer); `sep_kmac_golden` for the SHA3-256 digest. No observation tap. No inject: the stalled EDN is the omitted entropy bring-up after a TRNG reset.

*Randomization*: RANDCFG. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the order of the legs after the six-seed leg, the late-write value of the mode lock, the wait timer T (2 to 4), the non-zero prescaler P (2 to 15), the hash count N of the counter leg (2 to 10), the reseed threshold R (2 to 4), the run length M of the reseed legs (R + 2 to 12) and the key and message rows from the fixed table of test constants. `reseed = 4` samples different orders, values and rows. The legs are the same on every seed.

*Objective*: `entropy_mode` is idle at reset, then edn or sw. After `entropy_ready` is written the PRNG configuration is fixed until a KMAC reset, unless an EDN request timeout occurs in EDN mode, and later writes change only the readback. In EDN mode `ENTROPY_PERIOD.wait_timer` bounds the wait for EDN, expiry moves the entropy module to an error state, and 0 waits for ever. `ENTROPY_REFRESH_HASH_CNT` counts hashes, and `CMD.hash_cnt_clr` and `CMD.entropy_req` clear it. In EDN mode the module reseeds the PRNG from EDN after the hash counter reaches `ENTROPY_REFRESH_THRESHOLD_SHADOWED` and clears the counter, and a threshold of 0 disables the reseed by the counter. In sw mode, after `entropy_ready` and six `ENTROPY_SEED` writes, KMAC processes commands. Whether a `CMD.start` before the sixth seed word is refused or only gives no digest is not stated, so the leaf logs that case and does not grade it. Spec: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/kmac/regs/gen/adoc/kmac.adoc` (`CFG_SHADOWED`, `CMD`, `ENTROPY_PERIOD`, `ENTROPY_REFRESH_HASH_CNT`, `ENTROPY_REFRESH_THRESHOLD_SHADOWED`, `ENTROPY_SEED`); `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/kmac/data/kmac.hjson` (`CFG_SHADOWED.entropy_mode`: "Only after that, the module can start processing commands"; `ENTROPY_SEED`; `ENTROPY_REFRESH_HASH_CNT` and `ENTROPY_REFRESH_THRESHOLD_SHADOWED`: "If the threshold is 0, the refresh by the counter does not work") for the seed count, the command gate and the automatic reseed.

*Test Coverage*: Software mode with six seed words (graded) and with five and zero seed words (logged only, with the graded window closed). The mode lock with sw locked first and with edn locked first, each on every seed, on a KMAC with no EDN timeout since its reset: the readback in both, and the EDN behaviour (an `entropy_req` acknowledge) with edn locked. EDN mode with the entropy chain uninitialized: `wait_timer` T, 2T, 8 and 0, each with prescaler 0 and with a drawn non-zero prescaler, on every seed. N keyed KMAC hashes (N from 2 to 10) with the threshold above N, then `CMD.hash_cnt_clr`, then `CMD.entropy_req` while idle. The automatic reseed in EDN mode with the chain up: on every seed a drawn threshold R and M keyed hashes with M from R + 2 to 12, then the threshold 0 with the same M. Error injection: a stalled EDN, and a missing seed word (logged only). Extends `sep_kmac_mode_strength_rand_test`, which uses one setting in EDN mode, and `sep_hmac_kmac_cpu_crypto_smoke_test`, which runs software entropy mode with six seed words and grades `kmac_done`, `ERR_CODE` = 0 and a non-zero digest. This entry grades only the new cells. The six-seed leg adds only the exact golden digest. The mode lock, the timer, the hash counter and the automatic reseed are new; the five-seed and zero-seed cells are logged only. The owners of the open tests keep the keyed digests and the smoke checks. Functional coverage: `sep_kmac_entropy_cg` (`x_mode_lock`, `cp_wait_timer`, `cp_sw_seed_count`, `cp_hash_cnt`, `cp_entropy_req_ack`, `cp_auto_reseed`), `sep_crypto_intr_cg` (`x_line_gate`, `cp_hw_raise` kmac_err).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Select the internal source in `SEP_CPU_CTRL.EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL`. Bring up ESRC, CSRNG and EDN. Set `SW_RESET_N` to the baseline of the common rules with the bits of OTBN, AES, HMAC and ABR clear. Log the baseline. "Chain up" means the state after this bring-up. "Chain uninitialized" means the state after a TRNG reset (bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N` low, then high) with no bring-up after it. Every leg that needs the chain up runs this bring-up first when the chain is uninitialized.
. Draw the plan with `SepSeededRng`. Log `INFO-PLAN seed=<n>` with the leg order, the late-write values, T, P, N, R, M and the table rows.
. Six-seed leg. Reset KMAC (bit 4 of `SW_RESET_N` low, then high). Write `CFG_SHADOWED` twice with `entropy_mode` = sw, `entropy_ready` = 0, SHA3, strength 256, `kmac_en` = 0 and `sideload` = 0. Write it twice again with `entropy_ready` = 1. Read `CFG_SHADOWED` and expect `entropy_mode` = sw and `entropy_ready` = 1 (a control, `CTRL-KMAC-SWSEED part=program`). Write six `ENTROPY_SEED` words from the table.
. Write `CMD.start`. Wait for `STATUS.sha3_absorb` = 1 (a bounded wait of 60000 `clk_i` cycles, the outer limit of the leaf; a timeout fails the check). Log the clock count from the start to absorb as t_ctrl. A negative window of the leaf is 4 x t_ctrl, at least 64 clocks.
. Write the message row. Write `CMD.process`. Wait for `STATUS.sha3_squeeze` = 1. Read the two `STATE` shares at offsets 0x400 and 0x500 and XOR them word by word. Write `CMD.done`.
. The legs in steps 7 to 20 run in the drawn order. Each leg starts with a KMAC reset and sets its own chain state.
. Seed-count leg, for 5 and for 0 seed words in drawn order, with the graded window closed. Log `OBS-BLOCKED CHK-KMAC-SWSEED-NEG reason=sw_seed_gate_text_vs_dut_open`. Reset KMAC. Write `CFG_SHADOWED` as in step 3, first with `entropy_ready` = 0, then with `entropy_ready` = 1. Write the seed words. Write `CMD.start`. For the negative window read `STATUS` repeatedly. Read `INTR_STATE.kmac_err`. Log the reads, the reads that show `sha3_absorb` = 1 and `kmac_err` as `OBS-KMAC-SWSEED-NEG`. No check grades this leg.
. Mode lock leg, for sw first and for edn first in drawn order. Run the bring-up if the chain is uninitialized. Reset KMAC. Read `CFG_SHADOWED.entropy_mode` and expect idle (0). Write `CFG_SHADOWED` twice with the first mode, `entropy_ready` = 0, SHA3, strength 256, `kmac_en` = 0 and `sideload` = 0. Write it twice again with `entropy_ready` = 1. Read `CFG_SHADOWED` and expect `entropy_mode` = the first mode and `entropy_ready` = 1 (a control, `CTRL-KMAC-MODE-LOCK part=program`).
. Write `CFG_SHADOWED` twice with the late-write value and `entropy_ready` = 1. The late-write value is edn or idle when sw is locked, and sw when edn is locked. Read `CFG_SHADOWED`. Write no seed word. Write `CMD.start`. With sw locked, grade the readback, log `OBS-BLOCKED CHK-KMAC-MODE-LOCK reason=sw_seed_gate_text_vs_dut_open`, read `STATUS` repeatedly for the negative window and log the absorb as `OBS-KMAC-MODE-LOCK-SW`. With edn locked, log the clock count from the rise of `crypto_edn_req_o[1]` to the next rise of `crypto_edn_ack_o[1]` (the initial EDN reseed after `entropy_ready`) as t_reseed, wait for `STATUS.sha3_absorb` = 1 (a bounded wait), write the message row, write `CMD.process`, wait for `sha3_squeeze` = 1, read and XOR the two `STATE` shares, and write `CMD.done`. With the module idle, count the acknowledges on `crypto_edn_ack_o[1]` for a window of 4 x t_reseed (at least 64 clocks) with no command (`CTRL-KMAC-MODE-LOCK`: none). Then write `CMD.entropy_req` and count the acknowledges for a window of the same length.
. Timer leg, for prescaler 0 and for the drawn non-zero prescaler P in drawn order. This is the error injection. Point: the entropy chain state. Kind: starvation (EDN does not answer). Expected response: `kmac_err` after the timer. Make the chain uninitialized. For the wait timers T, 2T and 8 in this order, reset KMAC, write `INTR_ENABLE.kmac_err` = 1, read `INTR_STATE` and `ERR_CODE` and expect 0 (a control, `CTRL-KMAC-TIMER-PRESTATE`; 0 is the reset value of both), and write `ENTROPY_PERIOD` with the wait timer and the prescaler.
. For each of the three runs of step 10, write `CFG_SHADOWED` twice with `entropy_mode` = EDN, `entropy_ready` = 0, SHA3, strength 256, `kmac_en` = 0 and `sideload` = 0. Write it twice again with `entropy_ready` = 1. Count clocks from the rise of `crypto_edn_req_o[1]` to the rise of vector bit 22. Wait for the rise (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). Log the count as the error time. Count the acknowledges on `crypto_edn_ack_o[1]` in that interval. Read `INTR_STATE.kmac_err` and `ERR_CODE`.
. Wait timer 0 of the timer leg, with the same prescaler and chain state. Reset KMAC. Write `INTR_ENABLE.kmac_err` = 1. Write `ENTROPY_PERIOD` with `wait_timer` = 0. Write `CFG_SHADOWED` as in step 11. Hold a window of a multiple of the error time at wait timer 8 (logged): at prescaler 0, (2^16 // 7 + 4) times that time, which is longer than 2^16 timer pulses; at prescaler P, 4 times that time. Watch vector bit 22 and `crypto_edn_ack_o[1]` throughout. Read `INTR_STATE.kmac_err` and `ERR_CODE` at the end. Reset KMAC.
. Counter leg. Run the bring-up if the chain is uninitialized. Reset KMAC. Write `ENTROPY_REFRESH_THRESHOLD_SHADOWED` twice with N + 5. Write `CFG_SHADOWED` twice with `entropy_mode` = EDN, `entropy_ready` = 0 and the keyed program of the open `sep_kmac_mode_strength_rand_test` (cSHAKE, strength 256, `kmac_en` = 1, `sideload` = 0), then twice again with `entropy_ready` = 1. Log the clock count from the rise of `crypto_edn_req_o[1]` to the next rise of `crypto_edn_ack_o[1]` as t_reseed. The leaf uses t_reseed only to set the length of a window and does not assume the number of acknowledges of the initial seeding.
. Run N keyed hashes with the key row, the message row and the PREFIX and `KEY_LEN` writes of that program. The leaf does not grade the digests. After each hash write `CMD.done`, wait for `sha3_idle` = 1 and read `ENTROPY_REFRESH_HASH_CNT`. Expect the number of hashes done.
. Write `CMD.hash_cnt_clr`. Read `ENTROPY_REFRESH_HASH_CNT` and expect 0. Run one more keyed hash and expect the counter to read 1. Wait for `sha3_idle` = 1.
. Count the acknowledges on `crypto_edn_ack_o[1]` for a window of a multiple of t_reseed (fixed by the leaf and logged) with no write. Expect none.
. Write `CMD.entropy_req` while idle. Count the acknowledges on `crypto_edn_ack_o[1]` for a window of the same length. Read `ENTROPY_REFRESH_HASH_CNT` and expect 0.
. Reseed leg with the threshold R. Run the bring-up if the chain is uninitialized. Reset KMAC. Write `ENTROPY_REFRESH_THRESHOLD_SHADOWED` twice with R. Write `CFG_SHADOWED` as in step 13. Run one keyed hash as in step 14, write `CMD.done` and wait for `sha3_idle` = 1 (a bounded wait). The module starts a command only after the initial seeding, so this hash ends after the seeding. The leaf does not count acknowledges for it. Write `CMD.hash_cnt_clr` and read the counter as 0.
. Run M keyed hashes as in step 14 and read `ENTROPY_REFRESH_HASH_CNT` after each hash. Then hold a settle window of a multiple of t_reseed (fixed by the leaf and logged) with no command, and after it wait until `crypto_edn_req_o[1]` is low (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf), so no reseed request of this run is pending. Count the acknowledges on `crypto_edn_ack_o[1]` from the first `CMD.start` of this run (the second hash after the seeding) to the end of that wait. When a counter reseed fires and when EDN acknowledges it are not stated, so a reseed at the last hash (M a multiple of R) is acknowledged inside this run and not in the threshold-0 run.
. Reseed leg with the threshold 0, on the same KMAC. Write `ENTROPY_REFRESH_THRESHOLD_SHADOWED` twice with 0. Write `CMD.hash_cnt_clr` and read the counter as 0. Run M keyed hashes. Count the acknowledges from the first `CMD.start` to the last `CMD.done`. Read `ENTROPY_REFRESH_HASH_CNT` after the last hash. Reset KMAC.
. Msg-mask leg, after the drawn legs, with the graded window closed. Run the bring-up if the chain is uninitialized. For the three variants none, `msg_mask` and `msg_mask` with `entropy_fast_process`, in this order: reset KMAC, write `ENTROPY_REFRESH_THRESHOLD_SHADOWED` = 0 and the key words, write `CFG_SHADOWED` twice with EDN mode, `entropy_ready` = 0, the keyed program of step 13 and the flags of the variant, write it twice again with `entropy_ready` = 1, wait for the first EDN acknowledge, and run one keyed hash of the message row as in step 14. For each flagged variant log whether the digest equals the digest of the variant with no flag, the clocks of the hash and the rises of `crypto_edn_req_o[1]` in it (`OBS-KMAC-MSGMASK`). Reset KMAC.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-KMAC-SWSEED
|In sw mode, after `entropy_ready` and six `ENTROPY_SEED` writes, `CMD.start` reaches `STATUS.sha3_absorb` = 1 and the SHA3-256 digest equals the golden (`kmac.hjson`, `CFG_SHADOWED.entropy_mode`: after six seed writes the module can process commands; `ENTROPY_SEED`). A wrong DUT that does not process commands in sw mode times out, and one that corrupts the hash returns a wrong digest. The check does not show that the six seed words are needed: the absorb before the sixth word is the open question of CHK-KMAC-SWSEED-NEG. Anchor: `STATUS` and `STATE` readback against the `sep_kmac_golden` digest. Control (`CTRL-KMAC-SWSEED part=program`): `CFG_SHADOWED` reads `entropy_mode` = sw and `entropy_ready` = 1 after the program, so the leg runs in sw mode; a missing control logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf. This run gives t_ctrl, the clean start time that the negative windows use. Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-SWSEED PASS seed=<n> seeds=6 absorb=1 t_ctrl_clk=<c> stage=ok digest_eq_golden=1`.
|CHK-KMAC-SWSEED-NEG (blocked, `OBS-BLOCKED`)
|`kmac.hjson:549-551` (`CFG_SHADOWED.entropy_mode`, sw_mode) says the module waits for six `ENTROPY_SEED` writes and "Only after that, the module can start processing commands"; `theory_of_operation.md:705` gives 9 writes. Both counts exceed 5, so under either text a `CMD.start` after 0 or 5 seed words is not processed. The DUT reaches `sha3_absorb` on that start, and this gap between text and DUT is open, so the check is not graded. The leaf runs the five-seed and zero-seed starts with the graded window closed and logs `OBS-BLOCKED CHK-KMAC-SWSEED-NEG reason=sw_seed_gate_text_vs_dut_open` and `OBS-KMAC-SWSEED-NEG seed=<n> seeds_written=<5 or 0> window_clk=<w> reads=<r> absorb_reads=<k> absorb=<a> ctrl_absorb_clk=<c> kmac_err=<e>`. It does not log `CHK-KMAC-SWSEED-NEG PASS` and does not count the check.
|CHK-KMAC-MODE-LOCK
|`CFG_SHADOWED.entropy_mode` reads idle after the KMAC reset. After `entropy_ready` is written, a later write of another value changes the readback to the written value and does not change the behaviour (`kmac.adoc`, `CFG_SHADOWED.entropy_mode`: the PRNG configuration cannot be changed, and later writes change only the value that the register returns). With sw locked the leaf grades the readback; the absorb after the start with no seed word is logged only (`OBS-BLOCKED CHK-KMAC-MODE-LOCK` and `OBS-KMAC-MODE-LOCK-SW seed=<n> seeds_written=0 window_clk=<w> reads=<r> absorb_reads=<k> absorb=<a> kmac_err=<e>`), for the reason of CHK-KMAC-SWSEED-NEG. With edn locked and the chain up, the hash runs and returns the golden digest without any seed word although the late write is sw, and, after the hash with the module idle, `CMD.entropy_req` gives at least one acknowledge on `crypto_edn_ack_o[1]` (`kmac.adoc`, `CMD.entropy_req`: a manual reseed from EDN in EDN mode). A wrong DUT that takes the late write shows no absorb with edn locked, or no acknowledge after `entropy_req`. The KMAC has no EDN timeout since its reset, which is the stated escape of the lock. Anchor: `CFG_SHADOWED` and `STATUS` readback, the digest against the golden, and `crypto_edn_ack_o[1]`. Control (`CTRL-KMAC-MODE-LOCK part=program`, both locks): `CFG_SHADOWED` reads the locked `entropy_mode` and `entropy_ready` = 1 before the late write, so the lock is set and the late write is the write that sets the graded readback. Control (`CTRL-KMAC-MODE-LOCK`, edn locked): the window of the same length with no command, after the hash and with the module idle, gives no acknowledge, so the acknowledge comes from the `entropy_req` write; a missing control logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf. Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-MODE-LOCK PASS seed=<n> locked=sw reset_mode=0 late_write=<edn or idle> readback=<v> part=readback` and `CHK-KMAC-MODE-LOCK PASS seed=<n> locked=edn reset_mode=0 late_write=sw readback=<v> absorb=1 digest_eq_golden=1 entropy_req_acks=<k> ctrl_acks=0 window_clk=<w> t_reseed_clk=<t> behaviour=locked`.
|CHK-KMAC-TIMER
|With the chain uninitialized and `entropy_mode` = EDN, `INTR_STATE.kmac_err` reads 1 and the `kmac_err` line (vector bit 22) rises for each wait timer, and the clock count from the rise of `crypto_edn_req_o[1]` to the rise of the `kmac_err` line is larger for 2T than for T at the same prescaler, and for each wait timer it is larger with the prescaler P than with prescaler 0 (`kmac.adoc`, `ENTROPY_PERIOD.wait_timer`: the entropy module waits up to that many timer pulses after the request, then moves to an error state; `ENTROPY_PERIOD.prescaler`: a timer pulse rises when the clock count hits the prescaler value, so a larger prescaler gives a longer pulse period). A wrong DUT that ignores `wait_timer` shows equal counts for T and 2T, a wrong DUT that ignores the prescaler shows equal counts for P and 0, and a DUT with no timer shows no error. Only the order of the counts is graded, because the exact pulse period is not stated. T is 2 or more, so the phase of the first timer pulse, which the text does not state, cannot make the count with the prescaler P equal the count with prescaler 0. The test reads `kmac_err` and `ERR_CODE` as 0 before the request (`CTRL-KMAC-TIMER-PRESTATE`; a non-zero read logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf). The code after the error is graded by CHK-KMAC-ERRCODE-ENTROPY. No acknowledge occurs in the interval, so the error comes from the starvation. Anchor: `INTR_STATE` and `ERR_CODE` readback, `crypto_edn_req_o[1]`, `crypto_edn_ack_o[1]` and vector bit 22. Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-TIMER PASS seed=<n> t=<T> p=<P> p0_err_clk=<a0,b0,c0> p_err_clk=<a,b,c> acks=0`, logged after both prescaler passes, each list with the counts for T, 2T and 8. The same counts are logged as `OBS-KMAC-TIMER-CLK seed=<n> t=<T> p=<P> p0_err_clk=<a0,b0,c0> p_err_clk=<a,b,c>`, because the period of a timer pulse is not stated. `CTRL-KMAC-TIMER-PRESTATE seen: seed=<n> wt=<w> p=<p> kmac_err=0 err_code=0x0`, one line per run. `OBS-KMAC-TIMER-ERRCODE seed=<n> p0_debug=<a0,b0,c0> p_debug=<a,b,c>` (bits 23:0) is logged and not graded.
|CHK-KMAC-TIMER0
|With `wait_timer` = 0 no `kmac_err` is set over a window of a multiple of the error time at wait timer 8, and `ERR_CODE` stays 0 (`kmac.adoc`, `ENTROPY_PERIOD.wait_timer`: 0 waits for the EDN response for ever). At prescaler 0 the window is (2^16 // 7 + 4) times the error time at wait timer 8; that time spans at least 7 whole timer pulses, so the window is longer than 2^16 pulses (`kmac.adoc`, `ENTROPY_PERIOD.prescaler`: "EDN Wait timer has 16 bit value."). At prescaler P the window is 4 times that time. A wrong DUT that times out with 0, also one that counts 0 as a finite 16-bit count, shows `kmac_err` in the prescaler-0 window. Anchor: `INTR_STATE` and `ERR_CODE` readback at the end, and vector bit 22 and `crypto_edn_ack_o[1]` throughout. Control: wait timer 8 in the same seed, with the same prescaler and chain state, errors within its measured time, and `crypto_edn_req_o[1]` rose, so a request is pending. Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-TIMER0 PASS seed=<n> p=<P> window_clk=<w> err=0 req=1 acks=0 ctrl_err_clk=<a>`.
|CHK-KMAC-ERRCODE-ENTROPY
|For each of the six timer runs (wait timers T, 2T and 8, prescaler 0 and P), `ERR_CODE[31:24]` reads 0x04 on the read after `kmac_err` sets (`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/kmac/doc/theory_of_operation.md:724`: "0x04 \| WaitTimerExpired \| EDN has not responded within the wait timer limit."; `:764`: "If a non-zero timer expires, the module cancels the transaction and reports the `WaitTimerExpired` error."; `kmac.adoc`, `ERR_CODE`: "Bits 31:24 contain the error code ... and bits 23:0 contain additional debug information."). No text gives bits 23:0 a value for this code, so they are masked. A wrong DUT that reports another cause, or no cause, shows a code other than 0x04. A run with no `kmac_err` fails the check. Anchor: `ERR_CODE` readback. Control: `CTRL-KMAC-TIMER-PRESTATE` of the same run reads `ERR_CODE` = 0 (its reset value, `kmac.adoc`, `ERR_CODE`) after the KMAC reset and before the request, so the code comes from the expiry; a non-zero read logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf. Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-ERRCODE-ENTROPY PASS seed=<n> t=<T> p=<P> p0_code=0x04,0x04,0x04 p_code=0x04,0x04,0x04 pre_code=0x0,0x0,0x0,0x0,0x0,0x0 expected=0x04`.
|CHK-KMAC-HASHCNT
|After each of N keyed hashes `ENTROPY_REFRESH_HASH_CNT` reads the number of hashes done, so it reads N at the end, with the threshold above N (`kmac.hjson`, `ENTROPY_REFRESH_HASH_CNT`). `CMD.hash_cnt_clr` makes it 0. After one more hash it reads 1, and `CMD.entropy_req` while idle makes it 0 and adds at least one acknowledge on `crypto_edn_ack_o[1]` (`CMD.entropy_req`: manual reseed in EDN mode that also clears the counter). A wrong DUT that does not count, does not clear, or does not request a reseed shows the wrong counter or no acknowledge. Anchor: `ENTROPY_REFRESH_HASH_CNT` readback and `crypto_edn_ack_o[1]`. Control: a window of the same length without the `entropy_req` write adds no acknowledge, so the acknowledge comes from the write. Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-HASHCNT PASS seed=<n> n=<N> cnt=<N> after_clr=0 after_one=1 after_req=0 acks=<k> ctrl_acks=0 window_clk=<w>`.
|CHK-KMAC-AUTORESEED
|With the threshold R (2 to 4) and M keyed hashes with M of R + 2 or more in EDN mode, at least one acknowledge occurs on `crypto_edn_ack_o[1]` between the first start of the run and the end of its settle window (the request line low after it), and `ENTROPY_REFRESH_HASH_CNT` read after hash i of the run is i mod R, for i from 1 to M (`kmac.hjson:806-810`: "If the KMAC hash counter !!ENTROPY_REFRESH_HASH_CNT hits (GTE) the configured threshold !!ENTROPY_REFRESH_THRESHOLD_SHADOWED, the entropy module in the KMAC IP requests new seed to EDN and reset the KMAC hash counter."; each hash counts one, CHK-KMAC-HASHCNT). A wrong DUT that counts past the threshold without a reseed request shows no acknowledge and a counter that passes R; a DUT that resets the counter late or to another value fails the sequence. The counted run starts after an uncounted first hash, which can start only after the initial seeding (`kmac.hjson`, `CFG_SHADOWED.entropy_mode`: the module starts processing commands only after the seeding), and after a counter clear. No acknowledge of the initial seeding falls into the counted run, so an acknowledge there comes from a reseed on the counter. The number of reseeds is not graded. Anchor: `crypto_edn_ack_o[1]` and `ENTROPY_REFRESH_HASH_CNT` readback. Control: CHK-KMAC-AUTORESEED-OFF of the same seed. Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-AUTORESEED PASS seed=<n> thr=<R> n=<M> clr=0 acks=<k> cnts=<c1,...,cM> exp_cnts=<1 mod R,...,M mod R> settle_clk=<s>`.
|CHK-KMAC-AUTORESEED-OFF
|With the threshold 0 and M keyed hashes no acknowledge occurs on `crypto_edn_ack_o[1]` in the run, and `ENTROPY_REFRESH_HASH_CNT` reads M (`kmac.hjson`, `ENTROPY_REFRESH_THRESHOLD_SHADOWED`: with 0 the refresh by the counter does not work and only `CMD.hash_cnt_clr` resets the counter). A wrong DUT that reseeds with the threshold 0 shows an acknowledge or a counter below M. The run starts after the settle window of the R run, with `crypto_edn_req_o[1]` low, so no acknowledge of the R run falls into it. Anchor: `crypto_edn_ack_o[1]` and `ENTROPY_REFRESH_HASH_CNT` readback. Control: the run with R from 2 to 4 of the same seed acknowledges. If it does not, the leaf logs `CTRL-MISSING` and the leaf fails. Evidence: `CHK-KMAC-AUTORESEED-OFF PASS seed=<n> thr=0 n=<M> clr=0 acks=0 cnt_after=<M> ctrl_acks=<k>`.
|OBS-KMAC-MSGMASK
|Not a graded check. No text states an observable effect of `CFG_SHADOWED.msg_mask` or `entropy_fast_process`. The leaf logs, for each flagged variant, whether the digest equals the digest of the same hash with no flag, the stage that the hash reached, the clocks of the hash and the rises of `crypto_edn_req_o[1]`. Evidence: `OBS-KMAC-MSGMASK seed=<n> flag=<msg_mask or msg_mask+fast_process> stage=<s> digest==unflagged_digest=<0 or 1> start_to_done_clk=<c> edn_req_rises=<k>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes, except the blocked CHK-KMAC-SWSEED-NEG and the sw-locked absorb of CHK-KMAC-MODE-LOCK, which log `OBS-` lines, are not counted, and do not fail the leaf. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: This test grades the entropy mode contract of KMAC listed in the Objective. The following are not claimed. A mode write after an EDN timeout, which is the stated escape of the lock. The number of reseeds per run and the hash at which the first reseed occurs, because the text states a reseed after each threshold of messages and no count at the boundary. The keyed digests of the counter and reseed legs, which belong to `sep_kmac_mode_strength_rand_test`. A `CMD.start` with fewer than six seed words, and with sw locked and no seed word: the absorb and the `kmac_err` state are logged only (`OBS-BLOCKED`), because the DUT absorbs a start that `kmac.hjson:549-551` says is not processed, and that gap is open; no text gives an error code for such a start. The exact period of a timer pulse, which the register text leaves open. The line gate of `kmac_err` belongs to the extension leg of `sep_irq_ip_to_aggregator_test`.

'''''

[[sep_otbn_cmd_status_rand_test]]
===== 1.38 `sep_otbn_cmd_status_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy (OTBN)

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense +esrc_noise_force +sep_otbn_intg_flip`, 4 seeds; testlist `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`; Verilator and VCS

*Source Module*: `sep_otbn_cmd_status_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip with the CPU-LSU master readback and write of the OTBN registers and memories (approved); `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` (approved, vector bit 29); the TRNG reset bit of `SW_RESET_N` (approved, holds the RND read); the compute program and the independent Python operator golden of the open `sep_otbn_compute_rand_test` helper. The OTBN status probe, a read-only probe on the operational state code (`status_q` of the OTBN core) sampled on every `clk_i`, is an observation tap the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. It serves CHK-OTBN-CMD-BUSY and the reset arc of CHK-OTBN-INSN. The crypto alert receiver probe `crypto_alert_pulse_probe_o` (an observation tap on the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list) shows which receiver of the OTBN range `[2:1]` pulses (CHK-OTBN-IMEM-INTG, CHK-OTBN-DMEM-INTG). Added hook, error inject: the OTBN memory integrity flip (`+sep_otbn_intg_flip`, set by the testlist entry). While `otbn_intg_flip_en_i` is 1, it inverts one bit of every read word that the OTBN core takes from the memory that `otbn_intg_flip_mem_i` selects (IMEM or DMEM), after the descrambler and ahead of the integrity check; the stored word and the bus reads do not change. `otbn_intg_flip_count_o` counts the changed responses. The inject is off at every other time. `crypto_edn_ack_o[2]` (approved; the OTBN RND client of EDN) shows the EDN service of CHK-OTBN-RND-CLEAN. A host `AxSIZE` 2 read of an OTBN memory word is the stimulus of CHK-OTBN-ILLEGAL-BUS.

*Entries*: the testlist holds three entries: the full entry `sep_otbn_cmd_status_rand_test`, which runs every leg; `sep_otbn_cmd_status_rand_test_shard2` (`+sep_shard=2`), which runs only the RND-clean and software-error-kind legs; and `sep_otbn_cmd_status_rand_test_shard3` (`+sep_shard=3`), which runs the initial reset, the command leg with EXECUTE only, the `INSN_CNT`, done-line and busy-write legs, the recoverable half of the software-error leg and the ignored-command leg; it issues no SEC_WIPE command at IDLE and never enters LOCKED (its only SEC_WIPE_DMEM writes are the busy-write writes at `STATUS` 0x01, which OTBN ignores), and it passes on VCS. The shard 2 entry issues no SEC_WIPE command and never enters LOCKED, and it passes on VCS. See Known limitation for the full entry.

*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the command sequence (3 to 5 commands from EXECUTE, SEC_WIPE_DMEM and SEC_WIPE_IMEM, with each of the three commands at least once), the gap before each command (0 to 200 clocks), the program operation (ADD, XOR or AND) and its operands, the pair a and b (0 <= a < b <= 40 extra independent instructions), the order of the two enable runs of the done-line leg, the number k of busy `CTRL` writes (1 to 4), the unrecognised `CMD` value (an 8-bit `CMD.cmd` code other than 0xd8, 0xc3 and 0x1e, with bits 31:8 at 0) the number j of busy `CMD` writes (1 to 4) the bit of the IMEM word that the integrity leg inverts (0 to 38), the DMEM word that it inverts (word 0 or 1 of the line, which the two loads of the compute program read) with the bit of that word (0 to 38), the 32-bit value written to `ERR_BITS` in the clear step, and last the software-error kind (`BAD_DATA_ADDR`, `BAD_INSN_ADDR`, `CALL_STACK` or `LOOP`) with the `lw` offset (1 to 3) of the first, and the bus-access target (IMEM or DMEM, the IMEM word 0 to 9, the DMEM word 4 to 15 and a non-zero DMEM value), then, for the shard 3 entry, the number of EXECUTE commands of its command leg (3 to 5) and the gap before each (0 to 200 clocks). `reseed = 4` samples different sequences, gaps, operands, pairs, orders, counts, flipped bits, kinds and targets. The legs are the same on every seed; only the kind of the software-error-kind leg differs, one per seed.

*Objective*: `CMD` accepts EXECUTE (0xd8), SEC_WIPE_DMEM (0xc3) and SEC_WIPE_IMEM (0x1e). Each command moves `STATUS` from IDLE (0x00) to its busy code (0x01, 0x02, 0x03), back to IDLE, and raises `INTR_STATE.done`. After an OTBN reset `STATUS` is BUSY_SEC_WIPE_INT (0x04) during the internal secure wipe and then IDLE (0x00). `ERR_BITS` reads 0 after a run that ends with `ecall`. An illegal instruction (`unimp`, a CSRRW of the CSR 0xC00 that OTBN does not have) is a software error: with `CTRL.software_errs_fatal` = 0 the run ends at IDLE with `done` = 1 and `ERR_BITS.illegal_insn` = 1; with `software_errs_fatal` = 1 it is a fatal error, so the run ends at LOCKED (0xFF) with `done` = 1, `ERR_BITS.fatal_software` and `FATAL_ALERT_CAUSE.fatal_software`. LOCKED ignores `CMD` and ends only with an OTBN reset. A word that fails its integrity check when the core takes it from IMEM (a fetch) or from DMEM (a load) during EXECUTE is a fatal error: the run ends at LOCKED (0xFF) with `done` = 1, `ERR_BITS.imem_intg_violation` (bit 16) or `ERR_BITS.dmem_intg_violation` (bit 17), and the matching `FATAL_ALERT_CAUSE` bit (0 or 1); the fatal alert pulses on a receiver of the OTBN range `[2:1]` of the crypto alert vector until the OTBN reset, and after the OTBN reset `STATUS` leaves LOCKED. Sources: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/otbn/doc/theory_of_operation.md` (List of Errors: IMEM_INTG_VIOLATION, DMEM_INTG_VIOLATION; Reaction to Fatal Errors; Alerts), `.../doc/registers.md` (`ERR_BITS`, `FATAL_ALERT_CAUSE`, `STATUS`), `hw/sys/sep/doc/crypto.adoc` (OTBN alert receivers). A write of any value to `ERR_BITS` at idle clears it. `INSN_CNT` is the number of executed instructions. The done line follows `INTR_ENABLE`. A `CTRL` write while OTBN is not idle is ignored, and the same write while OTBN is idle is stored. A `CMD` write while OTBN is not idle is ignored, and an unrecognised `CMD` value is ignored. An `RND` read stalls OTBN until a number arrives from the EDN (`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/otbn/data/csr.yml`, `rnd`), and native requests get no acknowledgement during the TRNG adapter clear (`hw/sys/sep/doc/trng.adoc`, Reset and Recovery Boundaries); this holds OTBN busy for the busy-write leg. An `RND` program that ends at IDLE leaves `ERR_BITS` at 0 except `rnd_rep_chk_fail` (`theory_of_operation.md:137`). A software error of each kind `BAD_DATA_ADDR`, `BAD_INSN_ADDR`, `CALL_STACK` and `LOOP` is recoverable with `software_errs_fatal` = 0 and sets its own `ERR_BITS` field (`base-insns.yml`; `theory_of_operation.md:153`, `:168-170`, `:211-236`). A host read of an OTBN memory while OTBN is neither idle nor locked returns zero and is a fatal error with the code `ILLEGAL_BUS_ACCESS` (`theory_of_operation.md:30-31`, `:286-288`, `:312-313`). Spec: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/otbn/doc/theory_of_operation.md` (Operational States, Software Execution, Errors, Reaction to Recoverable Errors, Reaction to Fatal Errors, List of Errors, Alerts); `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/otbn/data/base-insns.yml` (`csrrw`, `unimp`); `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/otbn/regs/gen/adoc/otbn.adoc` (`INTR_STATE`, `INTR_ENABLE`, `INTR_TEST`, `CTRL`, `CMD`, `STATUS`, `ERR_BITS`, `FATAL_ALERT_CAUSE`, `INSN_CNT`); `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/otbn/data/otbn.hjson` (`CMD`: writes are ignored if OTBN is not idle, unrecognized commands are ignored; `ERR_BITS`: clear by a write of any value; `FATAL_ALERT_CAUSE`: does not change until OTBN is reset); `hw/sys/sep/doc/otbn.adoc` (OCAH Modifications); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_reset_ctrl.adoc` (`SW_RESET_N`).

*Test Coverage*: Command sequences of 3 to 5 commands that hold each of EXECUTE, SEC_WIPE_DMEM and SEC_WIPE_IMEM at least once on every seed (in the shard 3 entry, 3 to 5 EXECUTE commands), with drawn gaps, each command issued at STATUS IDLE. Two programs of a and b instructions for `INSN_CNT`. The done line with the enable set and clear, and with `INTR_TEST.done`. The busy-write leg: OTBN held busy (STATUS 0x01) by an RND read that gets no acknowledge while the TRNG reset is held; k writes of `software_errs_fatal` = 1 while busy; the same write while idle after the chain returns. The busy-write leg also writes `CMD` = SEC_WIPE_DMEM j times while `STATUS` is 0x01. The integrity leg runs the compute program twice, once with one bit of every IMEM word that the core fetches inverted and once with one bit of a DMEM word that a load of the program reads inverted, each followed by an OTBN reset. The software-error leg runs the `unimp` program with `software_errs_fatal` = 1, writes `CMD` = EXECUTE at LOCKED, resets OTBN, and runs the program again with `software_errs_fatal` = 0.
