. Return to the quiet state of step 5.
. `MODULE_ENABLE` trials. Run the locked trial and the unlocked trial (control) in drawn order. Each trial runs the steps below.
.. Run the reset sequence, then the clean start (common rule "Clean start"; the window stays 2048). Measure the strobe period p of `esrc_decor_valid_o` once per leaf and log it as `INFO-ESRC-STROBE`. Poll `MAIN_SM_STATUS.BOOT_PHASE_DONE` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait of 2W + 64 strobe periods; a timeout fails the leaf). The `balanced` pattern stays driven to the end of the trial.
.. Pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` reads 0. Then count the strobes of `esrc_compress_vld_o` up to the 24th (a bounded wait of 1024 strobe periods; a timeout fails the leaf), and pop `FIFO_RDATA` whenever `LEVEL` reads above 0, so that the FIFO does not fill. Log the clocks from the end of the drain to the 24th strobe as t_ref. With whitening on (the reset value) a digest gives 8 stream words (`architecture.adoc`, SHA-256 Whitening), so 24 strobes span at least two whole digest periods for every start phase.
.. Locked trial only. Write `FIPS_LOCK.LOCK` = 1 and read it back. The window size is at its reset value 2048, which is at least 1024. Pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `LEVEL` reads 0.
.. Locked trial only. Write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0, then poll `LEVEL` until it reads above 0 (a bounded wait of 2 t_ref; a timeout fails the check). Read `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE` after `LEVEL` has risen.
.. Unlocked trial only. Pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `LEVEL` reads 0, write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0, and poll `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check).
.. Unlocked trial only. Poll `SHA256_STATUS.BUSY` until it reads 0 (a bounded wait of 2 t_ref; a timeout fails the check), so that the digest that is in flight has left the conditioner. Pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `LEVEL` reads 0, then poll `LEVEL` for 2 t_ref.
. Log the plan and every check line.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-ESRC-RO
|A write of the complement of the value read to a read-only ESRC register leaves its RDL field bits unchanged (`entropy_source.adoc`, access R), and the write answers OKAY or SLVERR. A DECERR is the answer of the crypto internal error slave, which no accelerator sees (`hw/sys/sep/doc/crypto.adoc`, Single-Beat Access Only), so an unchanged readback after a DECERR proves nothing and fails the check, as does any other code. Reserved bits have no stated read value; they are logged as `OBS-RO-RSVD` and not compared. `COMPONENT_ID` is one of these registers; its reset value is graded by `sep_reg_bit_bash_rand_test`, and this check grades only that the write does not change it. A DUT that stores the write shows a changed read. Only a register that reads the same twice in the quiet state is written. Anchor: readback after the write and the B response. Control: at least one register is written and `COMPONENT_ID` is written (`CTRL-ESRC-RO`). Evidence: one line per written register, `CHK-ESRC-RO PASS seed=<n> reg=<R> resp=<okay_or_slverr> wrote=0x<w> read=0x<r> first=0x<f> field_mask=0x<m> unchanged=1 written_regs=<k>`, and `OBS-RO-RESP seed=<n> reg=<R> resp=<code>`.
|CHK-ESRC-RESTORE
|After a TRNG reset every restore field reads its RDL reset value (`architecture.adoc`, Reset: the TRNG reset is a full-IP reset). Before the reset each field read v1, which differs from the reset value, so a reset that leaves a field alone fails. In VCS no restore readback has X on its field bits; in Verilator the X check is not run. The volatile fields (status and alert latches that hardware sets or software clears by write-one) and the hardware-updating registers are not compared. Anchor: readback of each field. Control: each field read back v1 before the reset (`CTRL-ESRC-RESTORE`). Evidence: `CHK-ESRC-RESTORE PASS seed=<n> fields=12 pre=v1 post=reset mismatches=0 field=none x_free=<1_or_na>`, and `OBS-ESRC-VOLATILE seed=<n> fields=<f> pre=<non-zero fields or none> post=<non-zero fields or none>`.
|CHK-LOCK-MEN
|After `FIPS_LOCK.LOCK` = 1 (read back as 1), a write of `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0 is ignored (`entropy_source.adoc`, `CTRL`): `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE` reads 0 and, after a drain before the write, `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` rises above 0 within the bounded wait of 2 t_ref. A running module writes a digest within one digest period of the drain, and t_ref covers at least two digest periods, so the bound holds for every digest phase. A DUT that honours the write shows IDLE = 1 and an empty FIFO. Anchor: `MAIN_SM_STATUS` and `FIFO_STATUS` readback. Control: in the unlocked trial of the same seed the same write gives IDLE = 1, and the FIFO stays empty for 2 t_ref after `SHA256_STATUS.BUSY` reads 0 and a drain (`entropy_source.adoc`, `SHA256_STATUS`: BUSY is set while SHA-256 processing or digest output is active, so a digest in flight cannot refill the FIFO in the hold); the leaf logs t_ref, the BUSY poll and the polls (`CTRL-LOCK-MEN`). Evidence: `CHK-LOCK-MEN PASS seed=<n> t_ref=<t> lock_rb=1 idle_locked=0 level_after_drain=<l> bound=<2 t_ref> ctrl_idle_unlocked=1 ctrl_level_after_drain=0`.
|CHK-ESRC-INTR
|With all eight defined bits of `INTR_ENABLE` set, after a write to `INTR_TEST` the eight defined bits of `INTR_STATUS` equal the test mask exactly; `INTR_ENABLE` reads back as written, before and after the clear, for the all-ones mask, for the drawn mask E and for its complement (the drawn-mask status is logged and not graded, because the cited text does not state the latch with an enable bit clear); a write of 1 to the bits of a proper subset clears those bits only and leaves the other set bits, and the final write of 1 to all eight bits leaves 0 (`entropy_source.adoc`, `INTR_STATUS`, `INTR_ENABLE`, `INTR_TEST`). In the quiet state, with `INTR_ENABLE` = 0 and 0 written to `INTR_TEST` (the all-zeros corner), the eight defined bits of `INTR_STATUS` read 0: the module is off and no source has an event, so this cell does not rest on the unstated latch rule. A DUT with a bit that does not set, a bit that aliases another, a bit that sets with no event, or a clear that takes extra bits fails the exact compare. Reserved bits are written 0 and are not compared. Anchor: `INTR_STATUS` and `INTR_ENABLE` readback. Control: the one-bit control of the same seed reads exactly its bit (`CTRL-INTR-ONEBIT`); it is not a graded cell. Evidence: `CHK-ESRC-INTR PASS seed=<n> zero_corner_status=0x0 zero_enable_rb=0x0 all_ones_status=0x<d> all_ones_enable_rb=0x<d> all_ones_after_w1c=0x0 test=0x<t> status=0x<s> enable_rb=0x<e> drawn_enable=0x<E> drawn_enable_rb=0x<E> compl_enable_rb=0x<c> clear=0x<C> after_w1c=0x<a> expect_after_w1c=0x<a> final_status=0x0 ctrl_single_bit=0x<i> pic16=<p,p> rsvd=0x<r>`, `OBS-STATUS-E seed=<n> enable=0x<e> test=0x<t> status=0x<s>` and `OBS-PIC16 seed=<n> enable=0x<e> vector_bit15=<b>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: This test grades only the cells of its Test Coverage. The open owners keep the rest: `sep_reg_bit_bash_rand_test` keeps the stored values and the power-on reset values, including the `COMPONENT_ID` value; `sep_esrc_fips_lock_test` keeps the lock behaviour of the locked list and `CTRL.RSVD0`; `sep_trng_reset_recovery_test` keeps the clear of `FIPS_LOCK` by the TRNG reset; `sep_esrc_alert_delivery_test` keeps the set, the clear and the PIC line of each ESRC `INTR_STATUS` source alone. Not claimed: the read value of reserved bits beyond `CTRL.RSVD0`; the effect of the ring-oscillator and sample-clock fields (no observation point); the OR of the ESRC status bits onto interrupt source 16 (the leaf logs vector bit 15 and does not grade it); the value of a volatile field after the TRNG reset (logged only). The data registers, `INTR_TEST`, `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.FLUSH`, `FIPS_LOCK`, `CTRL` and the hardware-updating registers are not in the walk.

'''''

[[sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test]]
===== 1.41 `sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+esrc_noise_force`, 4 seeds, except the third shard, which runs 1 seed per regression because it drives fixed stuck patterns and the seed selects only the clean noise before the window; testlist `crypto.toml`; groups `cpu_stub` and `all` run it as three shards (`+sep_shard`, `env/sep_shard.py`): both pattern trials and the APT enable leg; the repetition enable leg and the limit trials; the constant-zero, constant-ones and bypass-zero legs. Verilator and VCS. The pattern leg runs two trials of three windows each.

*Source Module*: `sep_esrc_health_pattern_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (CPU-LSU master); noise model that drives `esrc_noise_ext_i` per lane with the modes `balanced`, `bias70`, `bias30`, `square`, `alternate`, `stuck0` and `stuck1` (common rules "Noise injection" and "Exact patterns"). Approved probes: CPU-LSU master readback; the noise inject `esrc_noise_ext_i`; `esrc_decor_valid_o` (the word count of a window); `esrc_decor_bytes_o` (the lane-byte control); `esrc_whiten_push_o` and `esrc_compress_vld_o` (the whitener input words and the stream words); `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` (end of the TRNG reset). The ESRC SHA-256 status tap (`esrc_sha256_status_o`), an observation tap of the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list, gives `SHA256_STATUS` on every `clk_i` (CHK-HT-PRESHA). No PROPOSED item.

*Randomization*: RANDCFG. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the lane of the bias70 trial and of the bias30 trial (0 to 11, two different lanes), the window of each of the two trials (two different windows from 1024, 2048 and 4096), the order of the two pattern trials, the order of the three limit trials, the half-period L of the repetition and Markov low trials (8, 16 or 32), the masks and the permutation of the `balanced` pattern, the one positions of the bias patterns, the phases of the `square` lanes and the start values of the `alternate` lanes. The limits follow from the window and from L as the procedure states. `reseed = 4` (the first two shards) samples different lanes, windows, trial orders, half-periods and patterns; the plan shape (one bias70 trial, one bias30 trial, the enable legs, three limit trials, the constant-zero and constant-ones legs) is the same on every seed. The lane and the window of a pattern trial are seed draws, so their bins close on a merge of regressions (common rule "Drawn cells").

*Objective*: The adaptive-proportion test runs per bit lane on each generator byte stream and is enabled by its bit of `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.ENABLE`. Disabling a tester clears its state. A failing window of a generator byte stream latches its `GENERATOR_n_HEALTH_STATUS` bit. The window size and the limits are one register set that every generator shares. Write-one clears the status. With whitening on, the whitener turns each 16 input words into 8 digest words. A limit beyond the exact statistics of a `square` or `alternate` stream trips its tester alone in every generator: `REPETITION_LIMIT` below the run length of a `square` stream trips the repetition test (status bit 0), `PROB_01_THRESHOLD` below the pair alternation count of an `alternate` stream trips the Markov high test (bit 4) and `PROB_10_THRESHOLD` above the alternation count of a `square` stream trips the Markov low test (bit 5). A constant-zero 32-bit stream (every lane stuck at 0 with BYPASS set; the BIW multiply-add of zero bytes is zero) sets the adaptive-proportion and Markov low bits of `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS`, their two total-failure counters and `INTR_STATUS.HEALTH_TEST_FAILED`. A constant 32-bit stream with at least one lane at 1 (every lane stuck at 1 with BYPASS set, so every generator byte is 0xFF under the reset `ENTROPY_BYTE_MASK`, and the BIW output of twelve constant bytes is one constant word) has a one count of W on that lane in every window, above the reset `APT_PROPORTION_1BIT` limit of 1200 for the reset window of 2048, so the 32-bit adaptive-proportion high test fails: `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` bit 3 sets and `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS` counts. The 32-bit test sees the post-BIW stream while the compressor is in use (`CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 0). Spec: `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc` (`HEALTH_TEST_CTRL`, `CTRL`, `ENTROPY_BYTE_MASK`, `HEALTH_TEST_WINDOW_SIZE`, `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS`, `REPETITION_TEST_COUNT`, `APT_PROPORTION_1BIT`, `APT_PROPORTION_LO`, `MARKOV_TEST_PROB_THRESHOLDS`, `GENERATOR_n_HEALTH_STATUS`, `SHA256_STATUS`, `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS`, `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS`, `APT_LO_TOTAL_FAILS`, `MARKOV_HI_TOTAL_FAILS`, `MARKOV_LO_TOTAL_FAILS`, `INTR_STATUS`); `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc` (Health Testing, Decorrelation and BIW Mixing, SHA-256 Whitening); `hw/sys/sep/doc/trng.adoc` (Entropy Generation).

*Test Coverage*: Two bias patterns on every seed (bias70 trips the high adaptive-proportion tester, bias30 the low one), each on a drawn lane with a drawn window from 1024, 2048 and 4096, with the other 11 lanes `balanced`, the repetition and Markov testers on their pass side and the other 11 generators at 0; the enable cells (adaptive-proportion bit cleared, repetition bit cleared while its count is at least 100); the write-one clear; the limit trips of the repetition, Markov high and Markov low testers in all 12 generators; the constant-zero 32-bit stream with its adaptive-proportion low and Markov low status bits, the two non-zero low-side counters and `HEALTH_TEST_FAILED` (status bit 0 and `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` are logged as control values, and the Markov high bit and the two high-side counters are logged, all not graded); the constant all-ones stream with the 32-bit adaptive-proportion high trip (status bit 3 and a non-zero `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS`), with the zero `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS` of the constant-zero stream as its control; and the exact 16-to-8 word count of the whitener on the constant-zero stream. The constant-zero stream with `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` set: `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` bits 3 and 5, `APT_LO_TOTAL_FAILS`, `MARKOV_LO_TOTAL_FAILS` and `INTR_STATUS.HEALTH_TEST_FAILED` are logged (`OBS-ESRC-HT-BYPASS`), not graded, with a control that fails the leaf. This test extends `sep_esrc_alert_delivery_test`, which drives a stuck pattern on all lanes with one threshold and one window and grades the repetition failure, the attribution of `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` and the generator status. This test grades only the cells that the open test does not grade: the adaptive-proportion tester alone on one biased lane, the pass side of the other testers under shared limits, the enable and clear legs, the limit trips, the adaptive-proportion and Markov low bits and counters of the constant-zero pattern (not bit 0, not `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` and not the high-side cells), the adaptive-proportion high trip of the constant all-ones pattern, and the word count of the whitener on the constant-zero stream. The open owner keeps the rest. Functional coverage: groups `sep_esrc_startup_health_cg` (`x_enable_boot`, `cp_test_enable_set`, `cp_window_class`, `x_tester_side`, `cp_test_level`, `cp_prestage_test`, `cp_top_failure_set`), `sep_esrc_fault_alert_cg` (`x_gen_latch`), `sep_esrc_noise_gen_cg` (`cp_lane_pattern`) and `sep_crypto_intr_cg` (`x_esrc_status_enable`, `cp_esrc_raise_path` hw bins). The bypass-zero leg fills `sep_open_value_leg_cg` bin `esrc_ht_bypass_zero` after CTRL-ESRC-HT-BYPASS passed. The value bins of `cp_disable_clears` are not graded (`SEP_FCOV.adoc`, Bins not graded).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the plan from the seed: for the bias70 trial and the bias30 trial a lane (0 to 11, two different lanes) and a window W (two different windows from 1024, 2048 and 4096), the order of the two pattern trials, the order of the three limit trials, the half-period L and the patterns (common rule "Exact patterns"). Log the plan.
. Define the reset sequence: clear and set the TRNG bit of `SEP_RESET_CTRL.SW_RESET_N` (`TRNG_SW_RST_N`) with every other bit at the baseline of the leaf (0x20), and wait until `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` read 0 (a bounded wait).
. Define the start sequence for a window W: run the reset sequence; write 1 to the eight defined bits of `INTR_ENABLE` (common rule "ESRC interrupt status"); run the clean start with the window W (common rule "Clean start"), with `ALERT_THRESHOLD` = 0, `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.ENABLE` = 7 and the settings of the leg among its writes; measure the strobe period p of `esrc_decor_valid_o` once per leaf (`INFO-ESRC-STROBE`); poll `MAIN_SM_STATUS.BOOT_PHASE_DONE` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait of 2W + 64 strobe periods); read the 12 `GENERATOR_n_HEALTH_STATUS` registers and require all of them to read 0 (`CTRL-HT-START-ZERO`).
. Define the window count: one window is W strobes of `esrc_decor_valid_o`. The leaf counts strobes; a wait for c strobes is bounded at c + W strobe periods. In every graded window the leaf runs the lane-byte control of the common rule "Noise injection" (`CTRL-NOISE-BYTES`).
. Define the trial rule: the first start of the leaf is bring-up, and its timeout fails the leaf. A later set-up or strobe timeout fails the check of its trial with `reason=timeout:<what>`, and the leaf goes on with the next trial, which starts with its own TRNG reset.
. Pattern trials. Run the two trials in drawn order. Lane k and window W of a trial are the lane and the window that the plan draws for it.
.. Run the start sequence with the window W.
.. Write the trip limit of the trial. Trial bias70: `APT_PROPORTION_1BIT` = 19W/32. Trial bias30: `APT_PROPORTION_LO` = 13W/32. The other limits keep their clean values.
.. Error injection. Drive lane k with the trial pattern through `esrc_noise_ext_i` (bias70: 45W/64 ones per window; bias30: 19W/64), and keep `balanced` on the other 11 lanes (W/2 ones per window). Expected response: the adaptive-proportion status bit of lane k, and no other bit of 0, 3, 4 and 5.
.. Count three whole windows (3W strobes of `esrc_decor_valid_o`) from the start of the pattern.
.. Read the 12 `GENERATOR_n_HEALTH_STATUS` registers.
.. Read `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` again. Write `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0, so that no window end can latch a bit between the write-one and the read, and read the register again (`CTRL-HT-W1C-HOLD`: the value is unchanged and bit 3 is set). Write 1 to the bits that it reads as set, read it a third time, and write `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.ENABLE` = 7.
. Enable leg, adaptive-proportion tester. Repeat the first trial of the drawn order, with its window, trip limit and lane, and with `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.ENABLE` = 5 (bit 1 clear) written after the start sequence and before the pattern starts. Write the trip limit of the trial. Read back the limit register and `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.ENABLE`; a limit other than the trip limit or an `ENABLE` other than 5 logs `CTRL-MISSING HT-ENABLE-APT-CFG` and fails the leaf. Count three whole windows and read `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS`.
. Enable leg, repetition tester. Run the start sequence with the window 2048. Drive stuck1 on all 12 lanes, so every lane of the 32-bit stream is constant and its repetition count rises from the first word. Read `REPETITION_TEST_COUNT` repeatedly (a bounded wait of 228 strobe periods) until it reads at least 100 (`CTRL-HT-REPCOUNT-PRE`); if it never does, log `CTRL-MISSING` and fail the leaf.
. Write `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.ENABLE` = 6 (bit 0 clear), count one whole window, and read `REPETITION_TEST_COUNT`. Log the value as `OBS-HT-REPCOUNT-AFTER` and `OBS-BLOCKED CHK-HT-ENABLE-REPCOUNT-VALUE reason=cleared_count_value_unstated`.
. Limit trials. Run the three trials in drawn order: repetition, Markov high and Markov low. Every trial uses W = 1024. The trial pattern is `square` L with the half-period L of the plan for the repetition and Markov low trials, and `alternate` for the Markov high trial (convention "Health-test limit trips").
.. Run the start sequence with W = 1024. Drive the trial pattern on all 12 lanes. Count two whole windows, read the 12 `GENERATOR_n_HEALTH_STATUS` registers and require all of them to read 0 (`CTRL-HT-LIMIT`: the clean limits hold).
.. Write the failing-side limit of the trial. Repetition: `REPETITION_LIMIT` = L - 3. Markov high: `PROB_01_THRESHOLD` = W/2 - 3 with `PROB_10_THRESHOLD` = 0. Markov low: `PROB_10_THRESHOLD` = W/L + 3 with `PROB_01_THRESHOLD` = W + 1. Under `square` L every lane has the longest run L and at most W/L alternations per window under both readings; under `alternate` every lane has W/2 pair alternations (W - 1 or W changes from word to word) per window. So the limit lies at least three counts beyond the statistic of every generator. The other limits keep their clean values.
.. Count two whole windows after the write, with the trial pattern on all lanes and the lane-byte control on.
.. Read the 12 `GENERATOR_n_HEALTH_STATUS` registers. Read the five total-failure counters and log them with the tester and the two windows (`OBS-ESRC-HT-TOTALS seed=<n> tester=<t> windows=2 REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS=<v> APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS=<v> APT_LO_TOTAL_FAILS=<v> MARKOV_HI_TOTAL_FAILS=<v> MARKOV_LO_TOTAL_FAILS=<v>`). No text states how the total-failure counters count the failures of the per-generator testers, so no check grades them.
.. Write 1 to each status register that reads non-zero (clean-up, not graded).
. Constant-zero leg. Run the start sequence with the window 2048 and `CTRL.SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE` = 1 (its reset value).
. Read `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS`, `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS`, `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS`, `APT_LO_TOTAL_FAILS`, `MARKOV_HI_TOTAL_FAILS`, `MARKOV_LO_TOTAL_FAILS` and `INTR_STATUS.HEALTH_TEST_FAILED`. They read 0 (`CTRL-HT-TOP-ZERO`).
. Write the reset values of `APT_PROPORTION_1BIT`, `APT_PROPORTION_LO`, `MARKOV_TEST_PROB_THRESHOLDS` and `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.REPETITION_LIMIT`, so the leg runs at the reset limits of the window 2048.
. Error injection. Drive stuck0 on all 12 lanes through `esrc_noise_ext_i`. Expected response: the bits and counters of the objective.
. Count three whole windows of the reset size 2048 from the start of the stuck pattern.
. Read `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS`, the five total-failure counters and `INTR_STATUS.HEALTH_TEST_FAILED` again.
. Pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `LEVEL` reads 0. Then trace the ESRC SHA-256 status tap, `esrc_whiten_push_o` and `esrc_compress_vld_o` on every `clk_i` until 68 input words (strobes of `esrc_whiten_push_o`) have passed (a bounded wait of 68 + W strobe periods). Pop the FIFO words and log them as `OBS-HT-PRESHA-WORDS`. If fewer than 64 input words pass, log `OBS-HT-PRESHA-NOPUSH` and `OBS-BLOCKED CHK-HT-PRESHA-NOPUSH reason=whitener_input_after_post_boot_ht_fail_unstated`; the leaf then has no `CHK-HT-PRESHA` line, and the missing required evidence fails the leaf.
. Constant-ones leg. Run the start sequence with the window 2048, `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 0 and `CTRL.SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE` = 0, so the FIFO carries the post-BIW words. Read `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` and `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS`. They read 0 (`CTRL-HT-TOP-ONE`). Write the reset values of the limits as in the constant-zero leg, then write `APT_PROPORTION_LO` = 0 (zero disables the low-side test) and read it back. Read `APT_LO_TOTAL_FAILS`.
. Pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` reads 0.
. Error injection. Drive stuck1 on all 12 lanes through `esrc_noise_ext_i`. Expected response: the adaptive-proportion high trip of the objective.
. Count three whole windows of the reset size 2048 from the start of the stuck pattern.
. Read `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` and `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS` again. Pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `LEVEL` reads 0, poll `LEVEL` until it reads at least 4 (a bounded wait of one window of 2048 strobes), and pop four FIFO words. These words enter the FIFO after three windows of the stuck pattern, so none of them is older than the stuck drive (a full FIFO discards newer writes, so the words held at the end of the windows can be older). Precondition (`CTRL-HT-TOP-ONE-WORD`): the four words are equal and non-zero (the stream is constant and has a lane at 1); otherwise the trip cannot be predicted from the stimulus, and the leaf logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails. The FIFO words gate the check and are not its expected value.
. Bypass-zero leg. Run the start sequence with the window 2048 and `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 1. Read `CTRL`, `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS`, the five total-failure counters and `INTR_STATUS`. Write the reset values of the limits as in the constant-zero leg.
. Error injection. Drive stuck0 on all 12 lanes through `esrc_noise_ext_i` and count three whole windows of 2048 with the lane-byte control on. Expected response: not stated (see the checker row OBS-ESRC-HT-BYPASS).
. Control (`CTRL-ESRC-HT-BYPASS`): `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` read 1 before the windows, and the lane-byte control compared at least 2048 strobes, all zero, with no mismatch. Read `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS`, the five total-failure counters and `INTR_STATUS`, and log bits 3 and 5, `APT_LO_TOTAL_FAILS`, `MARKOV_LO_TOTAL_FAILS` and `HEALTH_TEST_FAILED` with the values before the windows as `OBS-ESRC-HT-BYPASS`.
. Log every check line.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-HT-TRIP
|After three whole windows of a bias pattern on lane k, among the status bits 0, 3, 4 and 5 of `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` exactly bit 3 is set: the adaptive-proportion tester of the generator latches its status (`architecture.adoc`, Health Testing: bit 3 is the adaptive-proportion failure). The high and low adaptive-proportion testers share bit 3, so the bit of the sibling is not graded. The count is exact in every window (common rule "Exact patterns") and lies 7W/64 beyond the trip limit (19W/32 for bias70, 13W/32 for bias30). A DUT with no adaptive-proportion test per lane, or with the limit applied to another lane, shows bit 3 clear. Anchor: `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` readback. Evidence: `CHK-HT-TRIP PASS seed=<n> pattern=<bias70_or_bias30> lane=<k> window=<W> tester=<apt_hi_or_apt_lo> limit=<L> stat=<s> gen_status=0x<g>`; `stat` is the one count of lane k per window (45W/64 or 19W/64), computed from the drive and not read from the DUT.
|CHK-HT-PASS
|The repetition and Markov testers stay on their pass side: bits 0, 4 and 5 of `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` read 0 after the windows are completed (the leaf logs the window count). Their limits keep the clean values, which no window of the trial reaches (common rule "Clean start": no run of the trial patterns is longer than 90 words against `REPETITION_LIMIT` = 200, a window holds at most W alternations against `PROB_01_THRESHOLD` = W + 1, and every count meets `PROB_10_THRESHOLD` = 0). A DUT whose testers trip on a lane that has no failing statistic, or that compares a limit on the wrong side, shows one of these bits. Anchor: status readback. Control: the adaptive-proportion trip of the same trial. Evidence: `CHK-HT-PASS PASS seed=<n> pattern=<p> lane=<k> rep_bit=0 mkv_hi_bit=0 mkv_lo_bit=0 limits=clean windows=3 ctrl_trip=1`.
|CHK-HT-OTHERS
|The status of every other generator reads 0 under the shared limits (`architecture.adoc`, Health Testing: the thresholds, the enable bits and the window sizes are programmable through the CSR interface and the 12 `entropy_generator` instances run the 8-bit test; `entropy_source.adoc`, `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL`, for the enable bits only). The other 11 lanes carry `balanced`, W/2 ones in every window, 6W/64 on the pass side of the trip limit; the leaf logs both values. A DUT with limits that are not shared, or a latch that leaks to other generators, shows a non-zero status. Anchor: readback of the 11 other `GENERATOR_n_HEALTH_STATUS` registers. Control: lane k latches in the same trial (`CTRL-HT-OTHERS`). Evidence: `CHK-HT-OTHERS PASS seed=<n> pattern=<p> lane=<k> other_generators_status=0x000 other_ones=<W/2> limit=<L> ctrl_lane_latched=1`.
|CHK-HT-ENABLE
|With `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.ENABLE` bit 1 clear the same pattern latches no bit 3 on lane k. With bit 0 cleared while `REPETITION_TEST_COUNT` reads at least 100, the count reads below 100 and below its value before the write after one whole window (`entropy_source.adoc`, `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL`: disabling a test clears its state). The exact cleared value is not stated; the leaf logs it as `OBS-HT-REPCOUNT-AFTER` with `OBS-BLOCKED CHK-HT-ENABLE-REPCOUNT-VALUE` and does not grade it. A DUT that ignores the enable bit latches bit 3, or keeps or extends the run count. Anchor: `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` and `REPETITION_TEST_COUNT` readback. Control: for the adaptive-proportion cell, the same trial trips with the bit set in the pattern trials of the same seed (`CTRL-HT-ENABLE-APT`), and the trip limit and `ENABLE` = 5 read back in this leg before the pattern starts, so the clean limit cannot be what keeps bit 3 clear (`CTRL-HT-ENABLE-APT-CFG seed=<n> reg=<r> limit=<l> want_limit=<l> enable=5 want_enable=5`); for the repetition cell, the count read at least 100 before the write (`CTRL-HT-REPCOUNT-PRE`), and the repetition tester trips with its bit set in the repetition limit trial of the same seed (`CTRL-HT-ENABLE-REP`). Evidence: `CHK-HT-ENABLE PASS seed=<n> tester=apt pattern=<p> lane=<k> enable_bit=0 latched=0 count_before=na count_after_disable=na gen_status=0x<g> ctrl_enabled_trips=1` and `CHK-HT-ENABLE PASS seed=<n> tester=rep enable_bit=0 latched=na count_before=<c> count_after_disable=<a> ctrl_enabled_trips=1`.
|CHK-HT-W1C
|A write of 1 to the set bits of `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` clears them (`entropy_source.adoc`, access RW1C). The write runs with every tester disabled, so no window end can latch a bit between the write and the read. A DUT that does not clear shows the bits set on the third read. Anchor: readback of the register before and after the write. Control: the read before the write shows bit 3 set, and the bits read unchanged after the tester disable (`CTRL-HT-W1C-HOLD`). Evidence: `CHK-HT-W1C PASS seed=<n> lane=<k> before=0x<a> after=0x00 rsvd=0x<r>`.
|CHK-HT-PRESHA
|With stuck0 on all lanes, BYPASS on all lanes and whitening on, 16 whitener input words give 8 digest words, exactly (`architecture.adoc`, SHA-256 Whitening; `entropy_source.adoc`, `SHA256_STATUS`: `INPUT_COUNT` is the words in the open input block, `OUTPUT_COUNT` the digest words left for output, BUSY covers processing and output). Over the span between the first and the last quiet clock of the trace (BUSY = 0 and `OUTPUT_COUNT` = 0, no input or output word within two clocks), 2 x output words = `INPUT_COUNT`(first) + input words - `INPUT_COUNT`(last). Input words are strobes of `esrc_whiten_push_o`; output words are strobes of `esrc_compress_vld_o`. At a quiet clock no digest is in processing or in output, so every block that completes in the span gives all its 8 words in the span. A DUT that passes the constant words to the FIFO unconditioned, or that gives another ratio, breaks the relation. Whether the whitener input continues after the 32-bit test fails after boot is not stated: a trace with fewer than 64 input words logs `OBS-BLOCKED CHK-HT-PRESHA-NOPUSH` and has no `CHK-HT-PRESHA` line. The digest values are not graded: the specification does not state the block chaining, the padding, or the word and byte order of the whitener (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>), and the leaf logs the words as `OBS-HT-PRESHA-WORDS`. Anchor: the ESRC SHA-256 status tap and the strobes of `esrc_whiten_push_o` and `esrc_compress_vld_o`. Control (`CTRL-HT-PRESHA`): `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` bit 0 is 1 and `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` is above 0 in the same run, so the gating test saw the constant stream (the open owner grades that failure), and the span holds at least 16 input and 8 output words. Evidence: `CHK-HT-PRESHA PASS seed=<n> expected_out_x2=<e> out_words_x2=<e> in_words=<i> input_count_first=<f> input_count_last=<l> span_clk=<c> pushes=<p> stream_words=<s>`.
|CHK-HT-LIMIT
|In each limit trial the status of every generator holds exactly the bit of the tripped tester (bit 0, 4 or 5 for repetition, Markov high and Markov low) and none of the other bits 0, 3, 4 and 5 (`entropy_source.adoc`, `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL`, `MARKOV_TEST_PROB_THRESHOLDS`: `REPETITION_LIMIT` is the maximum run, `PROB_01_THRESHOLD` the maximum and `PROB_10_THRESHOLD` the minimum alternation count). In the repetition and Markov low trials every lane carries `square` L, so its longest run is L and a window holds at most W/L alternations; in the Markov high trial every lane carries `alternate`, so a window holds W/2 pair alternations whatever the phase of the lane (`architecture.adoc`, Health Testing: the Markov test counts bit-pair transitions). The failing limit lies at least three counts beyond under both readings of the count (convention "Health-test limit trips"), and the other limits keep their clean values. A DUT that ignores a limit, swaps the two Markov sides, or trips another tester fails. Anchor: `GENERATOR_n_HEALTH_STATUS` readback of all 12 generators and the word count at `esrc_decor_valid_o`. Control: in the other two trials of the seed the same bit reads 0 over the same count of windows (`CTRL-HT-LIMIT-OTHER-TRIALS`), and the 12 statuses read 0 after two whole windows of the pattern and before the failing limit is written (`CTRL-HT-LIMIT`). Evidence: `CHK-HT-LIMIT PASS seed=<n> tester=<rep_or_mkv_hi_or_mkv_lo> pattern=<square_L_or_alternate> limit=<lim> window=1024 words=<w> gen_status_all=<s,...> other_bits=0x00`.
|CHK-HT-TOP-ZERO
|After three constant-zero windows, `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` bits 3 and 5 are 1; `APT_LO_TOTAL_FAILS` and `MARKOV_LO_TOTAL_FAILS` are non-zero; `INTR_STATUS.HEALTH_TEST_FAILED` is 1. A constant-zero stream has no ones and no alternations, so it fails the low adaptive-proportion test and the low Markov test (`entropy_source.adoc`: `APT_PROPORTION_LO` 0x350, `PROB_10_THRESHOLD` 0x64). A DUT that misses a low-side test fails. Status bit 0 and `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` are logged as control values and are not graded here; the repetition failure of a stuck pattern is graded by the open owner. Status bit 4, `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS` and `MARKOV_HI_TOTAL_FAILS` are logged as `OBS-HT-TOP-HI` and are not graded here; the zero `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS` is the control of CHK-HT-TOP-ONE. Anchor: readback of the registers. Control: the same registers read 0 after the clean startup and before the constant phase (`CTRL-HT-TOP-ZERO`). Evidence: `CHK-HT-TOP-ZERO PASS seed=<n> status=0x<s> totals=<rep_apt_lo_mkv_lo> intr_health_failed=1 ctrl_before=0x00`, and `OBS-HT-TOP-HI seed=<n> status_bit4=<b> apt_hi_total=<a> mkv_hi_total=<m>`; the `status` bit 0 and the `rep` total are logged and not graded.
|CHK-HT-TOP-ONE
|With `APT_PROPORTION_LO` = 0, after three constant all-ones windows, `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` bit 3 is 1 and `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS` is non-zero. `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` latches the 32-bit tester only and the low side is disabled, so bit 3 comes from the 32-bit high side. Which tester instances `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS` counts is not stated (specification question), so the counter is graded as non-zero only. A constant word with a lane at 1 has a one count of 2048 on that lane in each window of the reset size, above the reset `APT_PROPORTION_1BIT` limit of 1200 (`entropy_source.adoc`: `APT_PROPORTION_1BIT` is the maximum one count per lane; `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS` counts the APT high-threshold failures). A DUT whose 32-bit high-side tester is absent, disconnected or compares the wrong side fails. The Markov high bit is not graded: a constant stream has no alternations. Anchor: readback of the registers. Control: `APT_PROPORTION_LO` reads back 0 and `APT_LO_TOTAL_FAILS` does not change over the ones windows (`CTRL-HT-TOP-ONE-LO-OFF`); the same registers read 0 after the clean startup and before the constant phase (`CTRL-HT-TOP-ONE`), and `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS` reads 0 after the constant-zero leg of the same seed (`CTRL-HT-TOP-ONE-ZERO-LEG`), so a counter that counts every failure, or the low side, fails. Evidence: `CHK-HT-TOP-ONE PASS seed=<n> fifo_word=0x<w> ones_lanes=<k> window=2048 apt_hi_limit=1200 status=0x<s> apt_hi_total=<a> ctrl_before=0x00 ctrl_zero_leg_apt_hi=0 ctrl_apt_lo_limit=0 ctrl_apt_lo_total=<b>_<b>`.
|OBS-ESRC-HT-BYPASS
|Logged only, no pass or fail. With `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` set, `architecture.adoc` (Entropy Source) states that the 32-bit health test is clock-gated off, and `architecture.adoc` (Health Testing) states that the 32-bit instance monitors the post-BIW (or post-bypass) stream (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). After the same three constant-zero windows that CHK-HT-TOP-ZERO grades with the compressor in use, the leaf logs `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` bits 3 and 5, `APT_LO_TOTAL_FAILS`, `MARKOV_LO_TOTAL_FAILS` and `INTR_STATUS.HEALTH_TEST_FAILED`, and the status and the two counters before the windows. Control (`CTRL-ESRC-HT-BYPASS`, fails the leaf): `CTRL` reads the bypass 1 before the windows, and the lane-byte control compares at least one window of zero strobes with no mismatch. Evidence: `CTRL-ESRC-HT-BYPASS seen: seed=<n> ctrl=0x<c> bypass=1 expect=1 words=<w> mismatched=0 window=2048` and `OBS-ESRC-HT-BYPASS seed=<n> status=0x<s> apt_bit3=<b> mkv_lo_bit5=<b> apt_lo_total=<v> mkv_lo_total=<v> intr_health_failed=<b> status_before=0x<s> totals_before=<a>_<m> window=2048`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: This test grades only the cells of its Test Coverage; `sep_esrc_alert_delivery_test` keeps the repetition failure of a constant pattern on all lanes (`CHK-HT-STATUS`, `CHK-FAIL-ATTRIB`, `CHK-GEN-HEALTH`), and `sep_esrc_startup_gate_rand_test` keeps the clear of the total-failure counters by a `MODULE_ENABLE` rise. Not claimed: the meaning of status bits 1, 2, 6 and 7; the split of the shared bit 3 into high and low; the exact value of `REPETITION_TEST_COUNT` after a disable (not stated; logged with `OBS-BLOCKED`); whether the whitener input continues after the 32-bit test fails after boot (not stated; a trace with no input words logs `OBS-BLOCKED`); the position of the 32-bit test before the SHA-256 whitener (`trng.adoc`, Entropy Generation), because the FIFO carries the whitened words wherever the test sits, and the specification does not state the block chaining, the padding or the word order of the whitener, so the whitened words of a constant input are not predictable (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>); an absolute time to a trip (the unit of the byte period is blocked, see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>); the total-failure counters under a generator-level trip (the instance that counts is not stated); the Markov high trip of the 32-bit instance, and its adaptive-proportion high trip for a pattern other than a constant word (the 32-bit statistic of such a pattern is not stated); the exact BIW word of the all-ones stream (the leaf requires only a constant non-zero word); the zero of the 32-bit Markov high bit and counter under the constant-zero stream (logged only); the behaviour of the 32-bit test with the compressor bypassed (logged as `OBS-ESRC-HT-BYPASS`).

'''''

[[sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test]]
===== 1.42 `sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+esrc_noise_force`, 4 seeds; testlist `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`; Verilator and VCS. Three entries (`+sep_shard`, `env/sep_shard.py`): `sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test` runs every leg and is an expected-RED entry; `sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test_shard2` (`+sep_shard=2`) runs the reset leg and the `MAIN_SM_STATUS.ERR` leg, runs no threshold block, and is green; `sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test_shard3` (`+sep_shard=3`) runs the reset leg and the threshold blocks 0, 5 and 7 with no no-trip leg, grades every check of those legs except CHK-PF-TRIP-HOLD (it logs `OBS-BLOCKED CHK-PF-TRIP-HOLD` with the reason at each trip and releases no cell by it), and is green. Shard 1 (`+sep_shard=1`) is the reset leg and the threshold blocks with every check graded. `OBS-SM-ILLEGAL` runs on VCS only; under Verilator the leg logs `INFO-SM-ILLEGAL-SKIP`.

*Source Module*: `sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (CPU-LSU master); noise model that drives `esrc_noise_ext_i` per lane with the modes `balanced`, `stuck0` and the four break words of `stuck1` (common rules "Noise injection" and "Exact patterns"). Approved probes: CPU-LSU master readback; the noise inject `esrc_noise_ext_i`; `esrc_decor_valid_o` (the window count); `esrc_decor_bytes_o` (the lane-byte control); `esrc_compress_vld_o` (the stream tap); `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` (vector bit 15 graded by CHK-PIC16); `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` (end of the TRNG reset). Approved injects: the ESRC health-test counter-copy inject `esrc_cntr_inject_i` with `esrc_cntr_inject_bit_i` (CHK-SM-ERR); the force of the main state flops `u_main_sm.u_state_regs.u_state_flop.q_o` and `state_q` (`OBS-SM-ILLEGAL`). No PROPOSED item.

*Randomization*: RANDCFG. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the trip thresholds, 3 and a drawn T_d (4, 5 or 7), or in shard 3 both 5 and 7 (drawn without replacement, so only their order is drawn); the order of the blocks, with the threshold 0 after at least one trip block; the window of each threshold (256 or 512), the clean-gap length between two bursts (2, 3 or 4 whole windows), the order of the two trips of a threshold (the trip that ends by a `MODULE_ENABLE` cycle first or the trip that ends by a TRNG reset first), the masks and the permutation of the `balanced` pattern, and, drawn after all of these so that they keep their values, the two bits of the 32-bit counter copy that the ERR leg inverts (two different bits). `reseed = 4` samples different drawn thresholds, orders, windows, gaps and patterns. Every seed of the entry without a shard runs the thresholds 0 and 3 and every leg; every seed of shard 3 runs the thresholds 0, 5 and 7, so the green shard 3 drives the bins of thresholds 5 and 7 on every seed and proves them only once the peak of `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` is exact (see the known limitation). The illegal 9-bit main-state encoding of the illegal-state leg is drawn after those two bits, from the encodings outside the state set of the main state machine.

*Objective*: `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` counts consecutive failing windows. When it reaches `ALERT_THRESHOLD` (reset 4; 0 disables) the main state machine enters AlertHang, `MAIN_SM_STATUS.ALERT` and `INTR_STATUS.PERSISTENT_FAILURE` set and the entropy output halts. AlertHang lasts until a reset or until `MODULE_ENABLE` is cleared. Write-one clears the sticky status bits and does not resume the output. Spec: `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc` (Persistent Failure and AlertHang, Reset); `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc` (`INTR_STATUS`, `MAIN_SM_STATUS`, `SHA256_STATUS`, `ALERT_SUMMARY_FAIL_COUNTS`, `ALERT_THRESHOLD`: "Number of consecutive failing windows that triggers the alert").

*Test Coverage*: Thresholds 0 and 3 and one drawn threshold from 4, 5 and 7 (in shard 3 the thresholds 0, 5 and 7), each with a window of 256 or 512 and the clean limits of that window: the reset value after a TRNG reset, the trip after exactly T failing windows, the trip that a following clean window does not cancel, the no-trip after exactly T - 1 failing windows (the trip latency is logged only), the non-consecutive burst, the disabled comparator, the output halt, the halt that survives the write-one clear, the exit by a `MODULE_ENABLE` cycle, the exit by a TRNG reset, and the latch and the reset clear of `MAIN_SM_STATUS.ERR` under a health-test counter integrity error (the writes of 0 and of 1 while that error persists are logged only). This test extends `sep_esrc_alert_delivery_test`, which fixes the threshold at 3 and grades the trip, `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` and the write-one clear after `MODULE_ENABLE` = 0, and `sep_reg_bit_bash_rand_test`, which reads the power-on value of `ALERT_THRESHOLD`. This test grades only the cells that those tests do not grade; the open owners keep the rest. The threshold 3 has no bin of its own: its trips are graded and are controls of the other legs. Functional coverage: groups `sep_esrc_fault_alert_cg` (`cp_alert_threshold`, `cp_any_fail`, `cp_alert_hang`, `cp_sm_err`), `sep_esrc_startup_health_cg` (`x_enable_boot`, `cp_window_class` below 1024, and 2048 at the TRNG-reset exit) and `sep_crypto_intr_cg` (`x_esrc_status_enable`, `cp_esrc_raise_path`; under this owner only the `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` cells count).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the plan from the seed: the trip thresholds (3 and T_d, or 5 and 7 in shard 3), the order of the blocks (the threshold 0 after at least one trip block), the window of each threshold, the gap length, and the order of the two trips of each threshold. Log the plan.
. Define the reset sequence: clear and set the TRNG bit of `SEP_RESET_CTRL.SW_RESET_N` (`TRNG_SW_RST_N`) with every other bit at the baseline of the leaf (0x20), and wait until `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` read 0 (a bounded wait).
. Define the window count: one window is W strobes of `esrc_decor_valid_o`. Clean noise is `balanced` on all 12 lanes. A burst of n whole windows is n times W strobes with the noise of the burst, then four break words: every lane at 1, then for each of the three lane sets of the BIW stage one word with that set at 1 and the other two at 0 (`architecture.adoc`, Decorrelation and BIW Mixing: y = a*b + c, so the word of the addend set gives 0xFF in every stream byte and ends every stream-bit zero run), then clean noise. The break words end the zero run of every lane at the last stuck word, so no repetition count stays above `REPETITION_LIMIT` into the clean noise, and the stuck words of a burst touch only the windows that hold them. A wait for c strobes is bounded at c + min(c, W) + 1 strobe periods. In every burst the leaf runs the lane-byte control of the common rule "Noise injection" (`CTRL-NOISE-BYTES`).
. Define the limits: the clean limits of the window W (common rule "Clean start"). No window of clean noise reaches them, and every whole window of stuck0 fails them (0 ones against `APT_PROPORTION_LO` = W/4). The leaf logs the values.
. Define an exact burst of n failing windows: `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` reads 0; drive stuck0 on all 12 lanes and read `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` back to back. `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` counts one failing window at each window wrap, so the first read of n - 1 follows the wrap that starts window n (word o; a bounded wait of n windows of words plus one window). A window that holds no stuck word passes, so the first failing window ends at word 1 or later and the wrap that ends failing window n - 1 comes after word (n - 2) x W: expect o > (n - 2) x W, and a read of n - 1 at or before that word fails every check of the burst with `reason=early:count_<n - 1>` (a DUT that counts one window too many before word o). Log `wrap_obs` = [word of the read before, o] and `expect_wrap_word_gt` = (n - 2) x W. Every stuck word extends the zero run of every lane, so the run is above `REPETITION_LIMIT` (200) from word 201 (`entropy_source.adoc`, `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.REPETITION_LIMIT`; `architecture.adoc`, the repetition count test flags when the threshold is exceeded). The window that holds word 201 fails and ends at or before word 200 + W, and each later stuck window fails (its run stays above the limit and its lanes hold 0 ones, under the APT low limit W/4), so the wrap that ends failing window n - 1 comes at or before word (n - 1) x W + 200. Expect every read issued after word (n - 1) x W + 201 (one word after that wrap) to read n - 1 or more: when the last read below n - 1 was issued after that word, every check of the burst fails with `reason=late:count_<n - 1>` (a DUT that drops a failing window). The word is taken at the issue of the read, so the read latency is outside the bound. Log `last_below_issue` (the issue word of the last read below n - 1) and `expect_last_below_issue_max` = (n - 1) x W + 201. Keep stuck0 to word o + W - W/8, then the four break words and clean noise. Window n holds at least 7W/8 (224 or more) stuck words, a zero run above `REPETITION_LIMIT` inside the window, so it fails; the break words come W/8 (32 or more) words before the next wrap, so the next window holds clean noise only and passes. Poll `ALERT`, `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` and `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` back to back to word o + 2W + W/8 (a trip, see below) or to word o + 4W (the no-trip leg: the alert bound of the trips), then read them once more. Every check of an exact burst grades the peak of `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` as n, so a misplaced switch fails the check.
. Define the start sequence for a threshold T and a window W: run the reset sequence; run the clean start with the window W, with `ALERT_THRESHOLD` = T and all eight defined bits of `INTR_ENABLE` set among its writes (common rule "Clean start"); measure the strobe period p once per leaf (`INFO-ESRC-STROBE`); poll `MAIN_SM_STATUS.BOOT_PHASE_DONE` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait of 2W + 64 strobe periods) and `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads above 0 (a bounded wait of 256 strobe periods before t_ref is known, then 16 t_ref). Read `ALERT_THRESHOLD` after the clean start; the no-trip and consecutive legs keep the value read by the start that precedes them for their controls. The first start of the leaf is bring-up, and its timeout fails the leaf. A later start or wait that times out fails the checks of its leg with `reason=timeout:<what>`, and the leaf goes on.
. Reset-value leg. Run the reset sequence, write `ALERT_THRESHOLD` = 7 and read it back as 7 (`CTRL-PF-RESET`).
. Run the reset sequence again and read `ALERT_THRESHOLD`.
. Run the threshold blocks in drawn order. Each trip threshold runs the block "T at least 3". The threshold 0 runs the block "T = 0".
. Block "T at least 3". Run the start sequence for T and the drawn window W.
. Pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `LEVEL` reads 0. Then count the strobes of `esrc_compress_vld_o` up to the 24th (a bounded wait; a timeout logs `CTRL-MISSING PF-STREAM` and fails the leaf), and pop `FIFO_RDATA` whenever `LEVEL` reads above 0, so that the FIFO does not fill. Log the clocks from the end of the drain to the 24th strobe as t_ref. With whitening on (the reset value) a digest gives 8 stream words (`architecture.adoc`, SHA-256 Whitening), so t_ref spans at least two whole digest periods for every start phase. The hold H of the later legs is t_ref.
. Before the first trip of the block and before the consecutive leg, if `ALERT`, `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` or `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` reads non-zero, run the start sequence again and pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `LEVEL` reads 0 (`INFO-PF-LEG-RESTART`).
. Run trip E and trip R in the drawn order. Before the second trip, run the start sequence again for T and W and pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `LEVEL` reads 0, because the first trip leaves the module halted or the TRNG reset restores the reset configuration.
. Define a trip: run an exact burst of T failing windows, and poll on until `ALERT` reads 1, at most to word o + 4W (window T, the window in which the design acts and two windows of slack; a wait bound, not a graded latency; a timeout fails the checks of the legs that need the trip). Log `OBS-PF-TRIP-LATENCY`: T, `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` at the first poll that reads `ALERT` = 1, the word of that poll after word o + W and its window, and vector bit 15 of `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o`.
. Trip E (ends by a `MODULE_ENABLE` cycle). Run a trip. Expected response: AlertHang. Once `ALERT` reads 1, read vector bit 15 of `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` and expect 1 (`CHK-PIC16`).
. Halt leg of trip E. Poll `SHA256_STATUS.BUSY` until it reads 0 (a bounded wait of 16 t_ref; a timeout fails the check); while `BUSY` reads 1, pop `FIFO_RDATA` between polls, so that a full FIFO cannot hold the digest. Then pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `LEVEL` reads 0. For the hold H, poll `LEVEL` back to back and watch `esrc_compress_vld_o`.
. Clear leg of trip E. Read `ALERT` and `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` (both read 1, `CTRL-PF-RECOVER`). Write 1 to `MAIN_SM_STATUS.ALERT` and to `INTR_STATUS.PERSISTENT_FAILURE`, and read both.
. For the hold H, poll `LEVEL` back to back and watch `esrc_compress_vld_o`.
. Write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0, then 1. Poll `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` until it reads 1 and `LEVEL` until it reads above 0 (the bounded waits of the start sequence; a timeout fails the check).
. Trip R (ends by a TRNG reset). Run a trip. Expected response: AlertHang; `ALERT` and `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` read 1 after the return to clean noise (`CTRL-PF-RECOVER`).
. Run the reset sequence and read `ALERT` and `PERSISTENT_FAILURE`.
. Write no `CTRL` until the check: `MODULE_ENABLE` keeps its reset value 1 (`entropy_source.adoc`, `CTRL`), so only the reset can end AlertHang. Run the live start (common rule "Clean start" with no `CTRL` write, `SepEsrcCleanStart.run_live`): `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.BYPASS` = 0xFFF and the clean limits of the reset window W0 = 2048 (`HEALTH_TEST_WINDOW_SIZE` reset 0x800, not written), at most 16 strobes after the release. Poll `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait of 2W0 + 64 strobe periods) and `LEVEL` until it reads above 0 (16 t_ref); a timeout fails the check. The graded window stays open from the trip to the end of these polls.
. After the trips, run the start sequence again for T and W and pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `LEVEL` reads 0, because the trips leave the module halted or at the reset configuration. Then run the no-trip and consecutive legs; the trips are their controls, so each control line is logged before its check line.
. No-trip leg (not in shard 3). Error injection. Run an exact burst of T - 1 failing windows. Expected response: no alert at any poll to word o + 4W, the alert bound of the trips; `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` reads 0 at the last read.
. Consecutive leg. Error injection. Drive stuck0 for a burst of T - 2 whole windows, clean noise for a gap of g whole windows (g drawn, 2 to 4), then stuck0 for a second burst of T - 2 whole windows, then clean noise. Expected response: no alert, and each burst is counted. Poll `ALERT`, `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` and `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` throughout and after the second burst, and keep the peak of `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` of each burst (the polls up to the end of the gap, and the polls after it).
. After the second burst keep the clean noise and the polls for 4W words, the alert bound of the trips. Count clear: `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` clears after an accepted passing window (`entropy_source.adoc`, `ALERT_SUMMARY_FAIL_COUNTS`); the window that holds the break words ends at most W words after them and the next window holds clean noise only, so the count is 0 from the wrap 2W words after the break words. The leaf writes this bound, 2W strobes, to the FCOV sampler (`esrc_af_clear_bound`) after the break words and 0 when the polls end. The leaf takes the word count after the break words at the issue of each `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` read, so the read latency is outside the bound. A read must read 0, and every read issued more than 2W words after the break words must read 0 (no zero read fails CHK-PF-CONSEC with `reason=timeout:count_zero`, a later non-zero read with `reason=late:count_zero`).
. Block "T = 0". Run the start sequence for T = 0 and the drawn window W.
. Disable leg. Error injection. Drive stuck0 on all 12 lanes for 10 whole windows, then the four break words and clean noise, polling `ANY_FAIL_COUNT`, `MAIN_SM_STATUS.ALERT` and `INTR_STATUS.PERSISTENT_FAILURE` back to back. After the burst keep the clean noise and the polls for 4W words, the alert bound of the trips; the count clear of the consecutive leg applies (2W words after the break words; `reason=timeout:count_zero` or `reason=late:count_zero` fails `CHK-PF-DISABLE`). The graded window is open over the burst and the clean noise. Expected response: no alert, and the count falls to 0.
. At the end of the disable leg, log `CTRL-PF-DISABLE` (every trip of the blocks that ran before it reached the alert), then the `CHK-PF-DISABLE` line.
. ERR leg (the last leg before the illegal-state leg, in shard 2 and in the entry without a shard; the main state machine stays in its error state until a reset). Run the reset sequence with the graded window closed, and write all ones to the eight defined bits of `INTR_ENABLE` (the status bit is graded with its enable set, so the case of an unset enable, which no text states, is not used). Open the graded window. `leg=set`: read `MAIN_SM_STATUS.ERR` (0) and `INTR_STATUS.PERSISTENT_FAILURE` (0). Raise `esrc_cntr_inject_i` with `esrc_cntr_inject_bit_i` set to the first drawn bit, so that one copy of the `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` counter differs from the other in that bit. Wait 64 clocks, then read `ERR` and `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` once each; both must read 1.
. `OBS-SM-ERR-W0`: close the graded window, drop the inject (the copies stay apart), write 0 to `MAIN_SM_STATUS` and read `ERR`. The leaf logs the value and grades nothing.
. `OBS-SM-ERR-W1C`: with the graded window still closed, write 1 to `ERR` and read it. The leaf logs the value and grades nothing.
. `leg=reset_clears`: with the window closed, raise the inject with the second drawn bit (a different bit, so the copies cannot become equal again) and wait 64 clocks. Open the window, read `ERR` (1; the first read of the window), run a TRNG reset with the inject dropped before the release, then read `ERR` and `PERSISTENT_FAILURE`; both must read 0. Close the window.
. Illegal-state leg (VCS only; after the ERR leg, in shard 2 and in the entry without a shard). Under Verilator log `INFO-SM-ILLEGAL-SKIP` and end the leg. Close the graded window. Read `MAIN_SM_STATUS.ERR` as 0 (`CTRL-SM-ILLEGAL`; the ERR leg ended with a TRNG reset). This is the error injection. Point: the state flops of the ESRC main state machine. Kind: a drawn 9-bit encoding outside the state set of the main state machine design package. Logged response only (no .adoc or RDL lists the legal encodings). After a clock edge, force the encoding on the state flops for 2 clocks and release it. Wait 64 clocks, then read `MAIN_SM_STATUS.ERR` (`OBS-SM-ILLEGAL`) and `INTR_STATUS.PERSISTENT_FAILURE` (`OBS-SM-ILLEGAL-PF`).

Each leg runs in its own graded window, which the leaf closes at the first FAIL. `CHK-PF-NOTRIP`, `CHK-PF-CONSEC` and `CHK-PF-DISABLE` are graded at the end of their own legs, after their controls, which need the trips. A check releases no cell when a control was logged absent since the previous check line, or when its own control is not logged as seen just before it (the window then closes). `CHK-PF-TRIP-HOLD` is graded at the end of its trip: its values are taken before the halt and exit legs, which it does not grade.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-PF-RESET
|`ALERT_THRESHOLD` reads 0x4 after a TRNG reset that follows a write of 7 (`entropy_source.adoc`, `ALERT_THRESHOLD`: reset 0x4; `architecture.adoc`, Reset: full-IP reset). A DUT that keeps the written value shows 7. The power-on read is graded by `sep_reg_bit_bash_rand_test`. Anchor: readback. Control: the register read 7 before the second reset (`CTRL-PF-RESET`). Evidence: `CHK-PF-RESET PASS seed=<n> alert_threshold=0x4 expect=0x4 rsvd=0x<r> ctrl_before=0x7`.
|CHK-PF-TRIP-COUNT
|Every trip: after exactly T failing windows and clean noise, `ALERT` reads 1 within the wait bound, and `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` reads 1 (`entropy_source.adoc`, `ALERT_THRESHOLD`: "Number of consecutive failing windows that triggers the alert"; `ALERT_SUMMARY_FAIL_COUNTS`; `architecture.adoc`, Persistent Failure and AlertHang). A DUT that needs more than T failing windows never trips; a DUT that drops a failing window fails the late-count bound of the exact burst. The peak of `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` is not graded here: CHK-PF-TRIP-HOLD grades it as exactly T (one count per failing window, `architecture.adoc:136`), and shard 3, which does not grade CHK-PF-TRIP-HOLD, passes no check on the peak. The check releases its coverage cells only when the peak is exactly T, the value CHK-PF-TRIP-HOLD grades, in every entry: shard 3, which does not grade the peak, fills none of those cells with a peak other than T. Otherwise the leaf logs `INFO-PF-TRIP-COUNT-HOLD seed=<n> thr=<T> any_fail_peak=<p> released=0` and releases none. Anchor: readback. Control: `ALERT`, `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` and `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` read 0 before the burst (`CTRL-PF-TRIP-COUNT`). Evidence: `CHK-PF-TRIP-COUNT PASS seed=<n> thr=<T> win=<W> failing_windows=<T> alert=1 pf=1 wrap_obs=<o_prev,o> expect_wrap_word_gt=<(T-2)W> last_below_issue=<w> expect_last_below_issue_max=<(T-1)W+201> alert_bound_windows=4`.
|CHK-PF-TRIP-HOLD
|Every trip: at a read after the clean window that follows window T has ended (word o + 2W + W/8 or later), `ALERT` and `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` read 1, and `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` never read above T. A DUT whose clean window cancels the trip, or that counts a clean window as failing, fails. The leaf logs the change of the sum of the five `*_TOTAL_FAILS` counters from the wrap that ends window T to the last read (`test_fails_after_window_t`); 0 shows that the later windows hold no health-test failure. Anchor: readback. Control: the clean noise ran W + W/8 words or more after the break words (`CTRL-PF-TRIP-HOLD`). Evidence: `CHK-PF-TRIP-HOLD PASS seed=<n> thr=<T> win=<W> read_word_after_window_t=<w> alert_after_clean=1 pf_after_clean=1 any_fail_peak=<T> any_fail_final=<c>`.
|CHK-PF-NOTRIP
|After an exact burst of T - 1 failing windows, `ALERT` and `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` read 0 at every poll up to word o + 4W, the alert bound of the trips (so a DUT that trips late inside that bound fails), the peak of `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` is T - 1, and `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` reads 0 at the last read (`entropy_source.adoc`, `ALERT_THRESHOLD`, `ALERT_SUMMARY_FAIL_COUNTS`). A DUT that trips at T - 1, or that keeps the count after a clean window, fails. The leaf logs the change of the sum of the five `*_TOTAL_FAILS` counters from the wrap that ends window T - 1 to the last read (`test_fails_after_window_n`); 0 shows that the later windows hold no health-test failure. Anchor: readback. Control: the trips of the same threshold reach the alert, the peak is non-zero, and `ALERT_THRESHOLD` read back T after the start that precedes the leg, so the write of T landed (`CTRL-PF-NOTRIP seed=<n> thr=<T> thr_readback=<T> ctrl_trip=1 any_fail_peak=<p>`). Evidence: `CHK-PF-NOTRIP PASS seed=<n> thr=<T> win=<W> failing_windows=<T-1> alert=0 pf=0 any_fail_peak=<T-1> expect_any_fail_peak=<T-1> any_fail_after=0 wrap_obs=<o_prev,o> expect_wrap_word_gt=<(T-3)W> last_below_issue=<w> expect_last_below_issue_max=<(T-2)W+201> watch_words=<w> expect_watch_words_min=<4W>`.
|CHK-PF-CONSEC
|Two bursts of T - 2 whole windows with a clean gap of 2 to 4 windows between them leave `ALERT` and `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` at 0 at every poll up to 4W words after the second burst, because the counter holds consecutive failing windows only (`entropy_source.adoc`, `ALERT_SUMMARY_FAIL_COUNTS`). The peak of `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` of each burst is at least T - 2: a burst of n x W stuck words holds a zero run above `REPETITION_LIMIT` = 200 for its last n x W - 200 > (n - 1) x W words, so at least n windows fail in it, whatever its offset to the window wraps. After the second burst, `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` reads 0, and every read issued more than 2W words after the break words reads 0 (`ALERT_SUMMARY_FAIL_COUNTS`: it clears after an accepted passing window; the window that holds the break words ends at most W words after them, and the next window holds clean noise only). A DUT that accumulates across the gap trips, a DUT that does not count a burst fails the peak, and a DUT that keeps the count one clean window more fails the read. Anchor: readback. Control: the trips of the same threshold reach the alert, and `ALERT_THRESHOLD` read back T after the start that precedes the leg (`CTRL-PF-CONSEC seed=<n> thr=<T> thr_readback=<T> ctrl_single_burst_alert=1`). Evidence: `CHK-PF-CONSEC PASS seed=<n> thr=<T> bursts=<T-2,T-2> gap=<g> alert=0 pf=0 any_fail_peaks=<p1,p2> expect_any_fail_peak_min=<T-2> any_fail_after=0 watch_words=<w> expect_watch_words_min=<4W> ctrl_single_burst_alert=1 zero_word=<z> last_nonzero_word=<l> expect_last_nonzero_word_max=<2W>`.
|CHK-PF-DISABLE
|With `ALERT_THRESHOLD` = 0, ten stuck whole windows leave `ALERT` and `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` at 0 at every poll, to 4W words after the burst, while the peak of `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` rises above 8 (`architecture.adoc`, Persistent Failure and AlertHang: zero disables the comparator and the counter keeps counting; ten whole windows contain at least nine complete failing windows). After the burst `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` reads 0, and every read issued more than 2W words after the break words reads 0 (`ALERT_SUMMARY_FAIL_COUNTS`: the count clears after an accepted passing window; the count clear of CHK-PF-CONSEC). A DUT that trips at 0, whose counter does not count, or whose counter does not clear with the comparator off, fails. Anchor: readback. Control: every trip of the blocks that ran before it reaches the alert (`CTRL-PF-DISABLE`, `ctrl_trip_alert=1`). Evidence: `CHK-PF-DISABLE PASS seed=<n> thr=0 win=<W> failing_windows=10 alert=0 pf=0 any_fail=<a> any_fail_after=0 zero_word=<z> last_nonzero_word=<l> expect_last_nonzero_word_max=<2W> watch_words=<w> expect_watch_words_min=<4W>`.
|CHK-PF-HALT
|After a trip (`ALERT` read 1), a poll of `SHA256_STATUS.BUSY` to 0 and a drain, with clean noise, `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` stays 0 and `esrc_compress_vld_o` is silent for the hold H (`architecture.adoc`: AlertHang halts entropy output; `entropy_source.adoc`, `SHA256_STATUS`: BUSY covers digest output in flight, so a digest in flight when the alert rises cannot refill the FIFO in the hold). A DUT that keeps the output running shows LEVEL rising or strobes on the tap. Anchor: `FIFO_STATUS` readback and the stream tap. Control: the clean run before the trip shows 24 strobes on `esrc_compress_vld_o` within t_ref, and H is t_ref, at least two digest periods, so a running output shows a digest in the hold whatever its phase. Evidence: `CHK-PF-HALT PASS seed=<n> thr=<T> sha_busy_clear=1 level_max=0 stream_vld=0 hold_clk=<t_ref> ctrl_stream_vld=24`.
|CHK-PF-RECOVER
|After the write-one clear of `ALERT` and `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` both read 0 and the output stays halted for the hold H. After `MODULE_ENABLE` goes 0 then 1, `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` reads 1 and `LEVEL` rises. After a trip and a TRNG reset both bits read 0, and with no `CTRL` write after the reset (`MODULE_ENABLE` at its reset value 1, `entropy_source.adoc`, `CTRL`; the live start writes the clean limits of the reset window and `BYPASS` only) `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` reads 1 and `LEVEL` rises, so the reset alone ended AlertHang (`architecture.adoc`, Persistent Failure and AlertHang: the write-one clear of the sticky bits; AlertHang lasts until a reset or until `MODULE_ENABLE` is cleared). A DUT that resumes the output after the clear alone, that stays in AlertHang after the exit (a TRNG reset that leaves AlertHang until a `MODULE_ENABLE` clear included), or that keeps the bits after a reset, fails. Anchor: readback and the stream tap. Control: both bits read 1 before the clear and before the reset (`CTRL-PF-RECOVER`); for the halt after the clear, the clean run before the trip shows LEVEL rising and strobes on `esrc_compress_vld_o`. Evidence: `CHK-PF-RECOVER PASS seed=<n> thr=<T> exit=enable_cycle after_w1c_alert=0 after_w1c_pf=0 after_w1c_level=0 after_w1c_stream_vld=0 boot_done=1 level_rises=1` and `CHK-PF-RECOVER PASS seed=<n> thr=<T> exit=trng_reset window=2048 alert_after_reset=0 pf_after_reset=0 after_w1c_level=na boot_done=1 level_rises=1`.
|CHK-PIC16
|Every trip that ends by a `MODULE_ENABLE` cycle, once `ALERT` reads 1: vector bit 15 of `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` reads 1. PIC source 16 is the entropy-source interrupt and bit i of the vector is source i + 1 (`hw/sys/sep/doc/interrupts.adoc:23`, `:109-111`); the clean start sets `INTR_ENABLE.PERSISTENT_FAILURE`, which "Enables the persistent-failure interrupt" (`entropy_source.adoc:164`). A DUT that does not route the persistent failure to source 16 fails. Anchor: vector probe. Control: `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` read 1 at the end of the trip (`CTRL-PIC16`). Evidence: `CTRL-PIC16 seen: seed=<n> thr=<T> pf=1`; `CHK-PIC16 PASS: seed=<n> thr=<T> value=1 expect_value=1`.
|CHK-SM-ERR
|Two lines, `leg=set` and `leg=reset_clears`, each grading the whole `MAIN_SM_STATUS.ERR` field. `leg=set`: `ERR` and `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` read 0 before the counter-copy inject, and both read 1 after the inject and a wait of 64 clocks. `leg=reset_clears`: `ERR` reads 1 after a second inject (in the other drawn bit); after a TRNG reset `ERR` and `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` read 0. A DUT that does not latch `ERR` or does not report it on `PERSISTENT_FAILURE`, or that keeps `ERR` or `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` after the block reset fails. Golden: `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc:410` (`ERR` is RW1C, reset 0x0, "Latches an illegal state or health-test counter integrity error. Write one or reset the block to clear.") and `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc:136` (the sticky `ERR` status bit is cleared by the full entropy-source reset); `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc:140` (`PERSISTENT_FAILURE` "Indicates a persistent health-test failure or health-test counter error") and `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc:164` (`INTR_ENABLE` reset 0, so the enable is written before the leg). Anchor: the readback of `MAIN_SM_STATUS` and `INTR_STATUS`, with the TB counter-copy inject (`esrc_cntr_inject_i`) as stimulus. Control: the reads before the inject (0) and, in `leg=reset_clears`, the read of 1 before the reset. Graded in `_sm_err_leg` of `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/crypto/sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test.py` (`leg=set`, then `leg=reset_clears`). Evidence: `CHK-SM-ERR PASS leg=set inj_bit=<b> err_before=0 expect_err_before=0 pf_before=0 expect_pf_before=0 err=1 expect_err=1 pf=1 expect_pf=1 wait_clk=64 ...`, and one line for `leg=reset_clears`.
|OBS-SM-ERR-W1C
|The `ERR` bit read after a write of 1 while the counter-copy mismatch that set it persists. Logged and not graded: no text states whether a write of 1 clears `ERR` while its cause persists (`hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc:136` and `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc:410` state the clear only as "write one or reset"). The leg runs with the graded window closed, so no coverage bin takes this read. No golden.
|OBS-SM-ERR-W0
|The `ERR` bit read after a write of 0 while the counter-copy mismatch that set it persists. Logged and not graded: while the mismatch persists `ERR` latches again whatever the write does, and only a reset of the block ends the mismatch, which also clears `ERR` (`hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc:136`), so no state exists in which a write of 0 to a set `ERR` could fail. The leg runs with the graded window closed, so no coverage bin takes this read. No golden.
|OBS-SM-ILLEGAL
|Not a graded check. VCS only. After a TRNG reset `MAIN_SM_STATUS.ERR` reads 0 (control `CTRL-SM-ILLEGAL`). The leaf forces a drawn 9-bit encoding outside the state set of the main state machine design package on its state flops for 2 clocks, releases it, and logs `ERR` after 64 clocks. `ERR` "Latches an illegal state or health-test counter integrity error" (`hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/entropy_source.rdl:650`), but no .adoc or RDL lists the legal encodings of `MAIN_SM_STATUS.STATE` (`entropy_source.rdl:631`: "sparse-encoded"), so no specification makes the forced value an illegal state, and a stimulus taken from the design cannot carry a graded check. The stimulus is a cocotb force on the state flops, an error inject (the hook "ESRC main state machine force"). Under Verilator the state flops are not public, so the leaf logs `INFO-SM-ILLEGAL-SKIP`. Evidence: `CTRL-SM-ILLEGAL seen: err_before=0 word_before=0x<w>`; `OBS-SM-ILLEGAL seed=<n> state_forced=0x<s> flops_forced=<1 or 2> hold_clk=2 wait_clk=64 err_before=0 err=<0 or 1> word=0x<w>`.
|OBS-SM-ILLEGAL-PF
|Not a graded check. `INTR_STATUS.PERSISTENT_FAILURE` after the illegal state. The RDL names a health-test failure and a counter error for it (`hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc:140`), not an illegal state. Evidence: `OBS-SM-ILLEGAL-PF seed=<n> state_forced=0x<s> pf=<0 or 1>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Functional Coverage*: owner window 142. Pass label `esrc_pa_pass` (`set_esrc_pa_pass`), 9 bits, one per check of a leg: reset, trip count, no-trip, consecutive, disable, halt, recover by `MODULE_ENABLE` cycle, recover by TRNG reset, and ERR. The sampler holds each cell of this owner as pending at its DUT event while the window is open, clears the pending cells when a window opens, and samples a cell only on the rise of the label bit of the check that grades it. A passing check logs, then pulses its bit for 2 clocks; a FAIL closes the window first, so a cell that a failing check graded never samples. The release is per check: each cell is released by the passing check of the leg that drove it, not by the absence of a FAIL elsewhere in the leaf. Cells and the checks that release them:

* `cp_alert_threshold`: 0 by the disable check; 4 by the reset check and the trip-count check; 5 and 7 by the trip-count check.
* `cp_any_fail`: `below_threshold` by the no-trip, consecutive and trip-count checks; `at_or_above` by the trip-count check; `above_8_threshold_0` by the disable check; `falls_to_0` by the consecutive and disable checks.
* `cp_alert_hang`: `entry` by the halt and recover checks; `no_entry_below_threshold` by the no-trip and consecutive checks; `no_entry_clean_gap` by the consecutive check; `no_entry_threshold_0` by the disable check; `write_one_clear_stays_halted` and `exit_by_module_enable` by the recover check of the `MODULE_ENABLE` cycle; `exit_by_trng_reset` by the recover check of the TRNG reset.
* `cp_window_class`: the values below 1024 by the trip-count, no-trip, consecutive and disable checks; 2048 by the recover check of the TRNG reset.
* `x_enable_boot` (3 cells): by the recover checks of the `MODULE_ENABLE` cycle and of the TRNG reset.
* `x_esrc_status_enable` `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` cells {status 0, enable 0}, {0, 1} and {1, 1}, and `cp_esrc_raise_path` for `PERSISTENT_FAILURE`: by these checks: {0, 0} by the recover check of the TRNG reset and by `CHK-SM-ERR`; {0, 1} by the no-trip, consecutive, disable and `MODULE_ENABLE`-cycle recover checks and by `CHK-SM-ERR`; {1, 1} by the trip-count check and by `CHK-SM-ERR`; the `cp_esrc_raise_path` cell by the trip-count check and by `CHK-SM-ERR`.
* `cp_sm_err` {set, reset_clears}: held at the second `MAIN_SM_STATUS` read of the cell and released by the ERR bit after the `CHK-SM-ERR` line of that leg passes, so `set` samples only after the line has also graded `PERSISTENT_FAILURE`. `cp_sm_err` {w0_keeps, w1c_clears} are `ignore_bins` cells: a write of 0 cannot fail while the error persists (`OBS-SM-ERR-W0`), and no text states the value of a write of 1 (`OBS-SM-ERR-W1C`).

Proof by entry. `sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test_shard2` proves `cp_sm_err` {set, reset_clears}, `cp_alert_threshold[4]`, the three `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` cells of `x_esrc_status_enable` and the `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` cell of `cp_esrc_raise_path`. `sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test_shard3` proves the cells that CHK-PF-RESET, CHK-PF-CONSEC, CHK-PF-DISABLE, CHK-PF-HALT and CHK-PF-RECOVER release: `cp_alert_threshold` 0 and 4, the below_threshold, above 8 with threshold 0 and falls to 0 bins of `cp_any_fail`, the seven bins of `cp_alert_hang`, the below 1024 and 2048 cells of `cp_window_class`, the three cells of `x_enable_boot`, and the `PERSISTENT_FAILURE` cells of `x_esrc_status_enable` and `cp_esrc_raise_path` that those checks release. CHK-PF-TRIP-COUNT releases its cells only when the peak of `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` is exactly T, which this DUT does not reach (see the known limitation), so `cp_alert_threshold` 5 and 7 and the at_or_above bin of `cp_any_fail` have no proving entry on this DUT. No cell needs CHK-PF-NOTRIP or CHK-PF-TRIP-HOLD, so shard 3 runs no no-trip leg: each cell that CHK-PF-NOTRIP releases is also released by CHK-PF-CONSEC or CHK-PF-TRIP-COUNT, and CHK-PF-TRIP-HOLD releases none. Shard 3 trips at 5 and at 7 on every seed, so it fills `cp_alert_threshold` 5 and 7 and the at_or_above bin of `cp_any_fail` once the peak is exactly T. It does not grade the peak, and with a peak other than T it fills none of the three.

*Known limitation*: CHK-PF-NOTRIP and CHK-PF-TRIP-HOLD fail on this DUT. After an exact burst of n failing windows, `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` peaks at n + 1, and the `*_TOTAL_FAILS` counters record no failure after window n (`test_fails_after_window_n=0`). The text states one count per window in which any test failed (`architecture.adoc`, Persistent Failure and AlertHang; `entropy_source.adoc`, `ALERT_SUMMARY_FAIL_COUNTS`). The checks stay graded at the exact count, so the entry without a shard is expected RED; `sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test_shard2` runs no threshold block and grades no `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` value, and `sep_esrc_persistent_alert_rand_test_shard3` does not grade those two checks, so both pass. CHK-PF-CONSEC and CHK-PF-DISABLE grade the peak as a minimum, so the extra count does not fail them. The trip latency is logged only (`OBS-PF-TRIP-LATENCY`). The text states that T failing windows trip the alert and states no latency; the DUT acts at the end of the window after window T. The graded checks wait to a bound of four windows from the start of window T and do not grade the latency.

*Issue*: #3140, #3149

*Scope limit*: This test grades only the cells of its Test Coverage; `sep_esrc_alert_delivery_test` keeps the trip at threshold 3 with its alert bookkeeping and the write-one clear after leaving AlertHang, and `sep_reg_bit_bash_rand_test` keeps the power-on value of `ALERT_THRESHOLD`. The trip latency is logged as `OBS-PF-TRIP-LATENCY` and is not graded until the text states it. Not claimed: the OR of the other ESRC status bits onto interrupt source 16; `CHK-PIC16` grades only the persistent-failure cause with every defined enable set.

'''''

[[sep_esrc_autotune_detune_test]]
===== 1.43 `sep_esrc_autotune_detune_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+esrc_noise_force`, detune probe (observation tap), 4 seeds; testlist `crypto.toml`; groups `cpu_stub` and `all` run it as two shards (`+sep_shard`, `env/sep_shard.py`). Shard 1 (`sep_esrc_autotune_detune_test_shard1`): the manual, off and ignored legs, the first burst and the sample-clock divide leg. Shard 2 (`sep_esrc_autotune_detune_test_shard2`): the manual and ignored legs again as set-up outside the graded window, then bursts 1, 2 and 3 after the same set-up. Verilator and VCS.

*Source Module*: `sep_esrc_autotune_detune_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (CPU-LSU master); noise model that drives `esrc_noise_ext_i` per lane with the modes `balanced` and `stuck1` (common rules "Noise injection" and "Exact patterns"). Approved probes: CPU-LSU master readback; the noise inject `esrc_noise_ext_i`; `esrc_decor_valid_o` (the window count); `esrc_decor_bytes_o` (the lane-byte control); `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` (end of the TRNG reset). The detune probe (`esrc_detune_o`), a read-only probe on the per-lane ring-oscillator detune select, recorded at every value change, is an observation tap of the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list (CHK-TUNE-MANUAL, CHK-TUNE-IGNORED, CHK-TUNE-TOGGLE).
