*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the window (256, 512 or 2048), the non-zero `DETUNE` masks, the clean-gap length after a burst (2, 3 or 4 whole windows) and the masks and the permutation of the `balanced` pattern; the lane k of the manual, off, ignored and three-burst legs (0 to 11); the order of the sample-clock divide values 0 to 5 (a shuffle) and one more divide value from 6 to the field maximum (31). `reseed = 4` samples different windows, masks, gaps, lanes and patterns; the plan shape (the manual, off, ignored and three-burst legs) is the same on every seed. The lane is a seed draw, so the lane bins close on a merge of regressions (common rule "Drawn cells"). The seven divide values run on every seed of shard 1, so `cp_div_ratio` does not depend on the seed. The enable of the one-enable step (module or ring) and its new divide value (0 to 5, at a ratio other than that of the last divide value) are drawn after every other value.

*Objective*: With `CTRL.AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` = 1, a lane that fails its health test is detuned automatically (the tune state machine toggles the lane between its two lengths on a rising edge of a health-test failure) and `INTR_STATUS.AUTOTUNE_FAIL` sets. With the bit clear, the lane detune follows `RING_OSC_TUNE.DETUNE` bit i. Other lanes do not change. Spec: `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc` (`CTRL`, `INTR_STATUS`, `RING_OSC_ENABLE`, `RING_OSC_TUNE`); `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc` (Noise Generation, Health Testing).

*Test Coverage*: Manual detune masks 0 and two drawn non-zero masks with autotune off; a lane failure with autotune off; a drawn non-zero mask and its complement ignored with autotune on and clean noise; three failure bursts on the drawn lane k with autotune on (probe bit k changes, other probe bits stay, `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` sets, the lane status latches). This test extends `sep_esrc_fips_lock_test`, which grades the stored readback of `AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` and `DETUNE` before and after the lock. This test grades their effect, and the division of the lane 0 sample clock. Only the new cells are graded here; the open owner keeps the stored readback. Functional coverage: groups `sep_esrc_fault_alert_cg` (`cp_autotune`), `sep_esrc_noise_gen_cg` (`cp_detune_mode`, `cp_detune_lane`, `cp_div_ratio`) and `sep_crypto_intr_cg` (`x_esrc_status_enable`, `cp_esrc_raise_path`, the `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` cells).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the plan from the seed: lane k (0 to 11), the window W, the three clean-gap lengths, the non-zero masks m1 and m2 (m1 differs from m2), and the non-zero mask m3 that is not all ones (its 12-bit complement m3c is then non-zero). Log the plan.
. Define the reset sequence: clear and set the TRNG bit of `SEP_RESET_CTRL.SW_RESET_N` (`TRNG_SW_RST_N`) with every other bit at the baseline of the leaf (0x20), and wait until `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` read 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). Then write 1 to the eight defined bits of `INTR_ENABLE` (common rule "ESRC interrupt status").
. Define the window count: one window is W strobes of `esrc_decor_valid_o`. Clean noise is `balanced` on all 12 lanes. A burst is one whole window (W strobes) of stuck1 on lane k, with `balanced` on the other lanes, then clean noise for the clean gap of the burst (gap x W + 1 strobes). A wait for c strobes is bounded at c + min(c, W) strobe periods plus 64 clocks; a timeout fails the check of its leg with `reason=timeout:<what>`. In every burst and its gap the leaf runs the lane-byte control of the common rule "Noise injection" (`CTRL-NOISE-BYTES`).
. Run the reset sequence.
. Run the clean start with the window W, with `ALERT_THRESHOLD` = 0 and `CTRL.AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` = 0 among its writes (common rule "Clean start"). The leaf logs the clean limits. No window of clean noise reaches them, and a burst fails them on lane k (W ones against `APT_PROPORTION_1BIT` = 3W/4, and a run of W words, at least 256, against `REPETITION_LIMIT` = 200). Measure the strobe period p (`INFO-ESRC-STROBE`).
. Poll `MAIN_SM_STATUS.BOOT_PHASE_DONE` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait of 2W + 64 strobe periods; a timeout fails the leaf). Start recording every value change of the detune probe.
. Manual leg. Write `RING_OSC_TUNE.DETUNE` = 0, read it back, and sample the detune probe four `clk_i` cycles after the readback.
. Write `DETUNE` = m1, read it back, and sample the detune probe in the same way.
. Write `DETUNE` = m2, read it back, and sample the detune probe in the same way.
. Off leg. Read `INTR_STATUS.AUTOTUNE_FAIL` and `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS`. Both read 0 (`CTRL-TUNE-OFF`).
. Error injection. Drive stuck1 on lane k for one burst, with `balanced` on the other lanes. Expected response: the status of lane k latches and `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` stays 0. Read `ALERT_SUMMARY_FAIL_COUNTS.ANY_FAIL_COUNT` before the burst and after it, and log both (`OBS-ESRC-ANY-FAIL-GEN`); the same two reads surround each burst of the failure leg.
. Return to clean noise for the first clean gap. Count the probe changes from the start of the burst to the end of the gap, sample the probe, and read `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` and `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS`.
. Write 1 to the set bits of `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS`.
. Ignored leg. Write `CTRL` with `MODULE_ENABLE` = 1 and `AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` = 1. Sample the detune probe as p0.
. Write `DETUNE` = m3 and wait one window with clean noise. Sample the detune probe.
. Write `DETUNE` = m3c and wait one window with clean noise. Sample the detune probe. At least one of the two masks differs from p0.
. Failure leg. Run three bursts; burst i (1 to 3) uses clean-gap length i. Before each burst, read `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` and `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` (both read 0, `CTRL-TUNE-AUTO`).
. Error injection, each burst. Drive stuck1 on lane k for one burst, with `balanced` on the other lanes. Expected response: the status of lane k latches, `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` sets and probe bit k changes.
. After each burst and its clean gap, count the changes of probe bit k from the start of the burst to the end of the gap as t_i, and the other probe bits that changed. Read `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` and `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS`.
. Write 1 to the set bits of `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` and to `INTR_STATUS.AUTOTUNE_FAIL`.
. Release the pass label of the tuning bins (see Functional Coverage) when no check or control of the tuning legs failed. The leaf logs `INFO-TUNE-BIN-RELEASE released=<True/False> failures=<n>`.
. Shard 1 and the whole leaf: sample-clock divide leg. Four periods of `entropy_rosc_sample_clk_i` equal the bench period (`CTRL-SCLK-SRC`). For each divide value v of the drawn order, the host clears `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` and bit 0 of `RING_OSC_ENABLE.ENABLE` (the generator of lane 0 is disabled; the leaf logs both enables as `INFO-SCLK-DISABLE`), writes `SAMPLE_CLK_DIVIDE` = v (for the first value also `SAMPLE_CLK_SELECT` = its reset with bit 0 clear, so lane 0 takes the external sample clock), restores both enables, and reads back DIVIDE, SAMPLE_CLK_SELECT, RING_OSC_ENABLE and CTRL. It discards 2 periods of `esrc_sample_clk_lane0_o` and grades the next 4 (`CHK-SCLK-DIV`, phase `closed`). After that pass, when no earlier check or control of the run failed, the host opens the graded window, takes 3 more periods, closes the window and grades them (`CHK-SCLK-DIV`, phase `window`).
. One-enable step, in shard 1 and the whole leaf, after the divide leg, with the graded window closed. Clear only the drawn enable: `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` (with `AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` = 0) or bit 0 of `RING_OSC_ENABLE.ENABLE`. Count the rises of `esrc_sample_clk_lane0_o` for four periods of the old divide. Write the drawn new `SAMPLE_CLK_DIVIDE`, restore the enable, read the divide back and measure the lane period against the periods of the new and the old divide. Log the counts and periods (`OBS-SCLK-ONE-ENABLE`).
. Log every check line.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-TUNE-MANUAL
|With autotune off the `DETUNE` readback and the detune probe vector equal the written mask for the masks 0, m1 and m2, and no probe bit changes from the start of the off-leg burst to the end of its clean gap, so the probe still reads m2 after it (`architecture.adoc`, Noise Generation: the lane detune follows `RING_OSC_TUNE.DETUNE` bit i when `CTRL.AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` is clear). A DUT that ignores the mask, or that lets a failure move the detune with autotune off, fails. Anchor: the detune probe (an observation tap). Control for the no-change leg: in the failure leg of the same seed, with `AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` = 1, the same failure of lane k changes probe bit k (CHK-TUNE-TOGGLE), and in the off leg `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` bit 0 latches (CHK-TUNE-OFF), so the failure is present and the probe can show a change. If either event is missing, the leaf logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails. Evidence: `CHK-TUNE-MANUAL PASS seed=<n> detune_reg=0x000,0x<m1>,0x<m2> probe=0x000,0x<m1>,0x<m2> written=0x000,0x<m1>,0x<m2> after_fail=0x<m2> off_changes=0 ctrl_auto_toggle=1 ctrl_off_gen_bit0=1`.
|CHK-TUNE-IGNORED
|With autotune on and clean noise the probe keeps its value p0 and does not take either written mask, m3 or its complement m3c, and no probe bit changes from the p0 sample to the end of the leg (`architecture.adoc`, Noise Generation: with autotune set the lane detune follows the auto-tune state machine). The two masks differ, so at least one of them differs from p0 and would show in the probe. A DUT that ignores `AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` follows that mask. A DUT whose probe toggles and restores p0 at both samples fails the zero-change rule. Anchor: the detune probe (an observation tap). The `DETUNE` readback after each write must equal the written mask (`entropy_source.rdl`, `RING_OSC_TUNE.DETUNE`: `sw = rw`, `hw = r`), so a write that did not land fails rather than passing as an ignored write. Control: the manual leg of the same seed follows the mask, and the leg lasts at least as long as the latest first toggle of bit k after an autotune burst start. Evidence: `CHK-TUNE-IGNORED PASS seed=<n> detune_written=0x<m3>,0x<m3c> detune_regs=0x<m3>,0x<m3c> p0=0x<p0> probe=0x<p0>,0x<p0> ctrl_manual_probe=0x<m2>`.
|CHK-TUNE-AUTO
|On the failing lane k with autotune on, bit 0 of `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` latches and `INTR_STATUS.AUTOTUNE_FAIL` is 1 in each of the three bursts (`entropy_source.adoc`, `INTR_STATUS.AUTOTUNE_FAIL`; the stuck lane fails the repetition test with a run of W words against `REPETITION_LIMIT` = 200). A DUT that does not raise `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` on a lane failure fails. Anchor: `INTR_STATUS` and status readback. Control: both read 0 before each burst (`CTRL-TUNE-AUTO`), and CHK-TUNE-OFF of the same seed leaves `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` at 0 for the same failure. Evidence: `CHK-TUNE-AUTO PASS seed=<n> lane=<k> burst=<1_or_2_or_3> autotune_fail=1 gen_bit0=1 gen_status=0x<g>`, one line for each of the three bursts.
|CHK-TUNE-TOGGLE
|In each autotune burst and its clean gap, the probe bit of the failing lane k changes at least once and every other probe bit is unchanged (`architecture.adoc`, Noise Generation: the tune state machine toggles the lane on a rising edge of a health-test failure; other lanes do not change). A DUT that does not detune the failing lane, or that detunes another lane, fails. The number of toggles per burst is logged and not graded. Anchor: the detune probe (an observation tap), recorded at every value change. Evidence: `CHK-TUNE-TOGGLE PASS seed=<n> lane=<k> burst=<1_or_2_or_3> changes_ge1=1 others_unchanged=1 toggles=<t> moved_lanes=none`, and `INFO-TUNE-TOGGLES seed=<n> lane=<k> toggles=<t1,t2,t3>`.
|CHK-TUNE-OFF
|With autotune off the same failure latches bit 0 of `GENERATOR_k_HEALTH_STATUS` and leaves `INTR_STATUS.AUTOTUNE_FAIL` at 0 (`entropy_source.adoc`, `INTR_STATUS.AUTOTUNE_FAIL`: set while automatic detuning is enabled). A DUT that raises `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` with autotune off fails. Anchor: `INTR_STATUS` and status readback. Control: CHK-TUNE-AUTO of the same seed sets `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` for the same failure, and both registers read 0 before the burst (`CTRL-TUNE-OFF`). Evidence: `CHK-TUNE-OFF PASS seed=<n> lane=<k> gen_status=0x<g> autotune_fail=0 ctrl_auto_fail=1`.
|CHK-SCLK-DIV
|For each divide value v of lane 0 (the six values 0 to 5 and one value from 6 to 31, in a drawn order), with the generator disabled while the value is written, the lane 0 sample clock `esrc_sample_clk_lane0_o` shows 4 consecutive periods, after 2 discarded, that each equal the stated ratio times the period of `entropy_rosc_sample_clk_i`, exact in ps: ratios 1, 2, 4, 8, 16 and 32 for the values 0 to 5, and 4 for any other value. A second pass in the graded window grades 3 more periods of the same value. A wrong ratio for a value, a value outside 0 to 5 that does not divide by 4, a select that is ignored, a divide that is not stored, or a missing re-enable (no edges, so `period_ps=none` after a bounded wait) fails. Golden: `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc:75` (division ratios 1, 2, 4, 8, 16 and 32; "A division value outside 0-5 selects divide-by-4"), `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/entropy_source.rdl:674` ("Selects division by 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, or 32") and `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/entropy_source.rdl:595` (a clear `SAMPLE_CLK_SELECT` bit selects the external sample clock); the base period is the bench entropy clock period of 3 ns (`hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/env/sep_env_cfg.py:22`). Positive probes in the same line, all whole values: the DIVIDE readback is v, SAMPLE_CLK_SELECT reads 0xFFE (reset 0xFFF with bit 0 clear, `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/entropy_source.rdl:597`), RING_OSC_ENABLE reads its reset word 0xFFFFFF (`hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/entropy_source.rdl:565`, `:571`) and CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE reads 1. Graded at `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/crypto/sep_esrc_autotune_detune_test.py:876` (phase `closed`) and `:887-895` (phase `window`). Evidence: `CHK-SCLK-DIV PASS ... phase=closed v=<v> ratio=<r> period_ps=<p,p,p,p> want_ps=<w> ...`.
|CTRL-SCLK-SRC
|Four consecutive periods of `entropy_rosc_sample_clk_i` equal the bench period of 3 ns. Positive probe that the source clock runs at the golden base of `CHK-SCLK-DIV`, so a wrong ratio is not an artifact of the base.
|INFO-SCLK-DISABLE
|`CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` and `RING_OSC_ENABLE` as read while the divide value is written. Logged and not graded: the text says software changes a lane's source or division only while that generator is disabled, and does not name which enable disables it. The leg clears both, and no check depends on which one is meant. The one-enable step clears one enable at a time (`OBS-SCLK-ONE-ENABLE`).
|INFO-TUNE-BIN-RELEASE
|Whether the pass label of the tuning bins was released, and the failure count at that point. Logged and not graded.
|OBS-ESRC-ANY-FAIL-GEN
|Not a graded check. No text states whether the failure of one generator counts into `ALERT_SUMMARY_FAIL_COUNTS.ANY_FAIL_COUNT`. The leaf reads the counter before and after each burst on lane k and logs both. Evidence: `OBS-ESRC-ANY-FAIL-GEN seed=<n> leg=<l> lane=<k> any_fail_before=<b> any_fail_after=<a>`.
|OBS-SCLK-ONE-ENABLE
|Not a graded check. No text names the enable that disables a generator for a divide change. The leaf clears `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` or `RING_OSC_ENABLE.ENABLE` bit 0, not both, counts the lane 0 edges while it is clear, writes a new divide, restores the enable and logs the lane periods against the periods of the new and the old divide. Evidence: `OBS-SCLK-ONE-ENABLE seed=<n> which=<module or ring> v_before=<v> v_after=<v> lane_edges_while_disabled=<k> period_ps_after=<p,...> want_ps_new=<n> want_ps_old=<o>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Functional Coverage*: owner window 143. Pass label `esrc_tune_pass` (`set_esrc_tune_pass`): the sampler holds the hits of `cp_detune_mode`, `cp_detune_lane`, `cp_autotune` (not the illegal bin, which samples at once) and the `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` cells of `x_esrc_status_enable` and `cp_esrc_raise_path` while the window is open, and samples them only on the rise of the label. The leaf clears the label before the tuning legs, closes the window after the bursts, grades, and writes the label to 1 for 2 clocks only when no check or control failed. Under this owner, only interrupt source 5 (`AUTOTUNE_FAIL`) counts in `sep_crypto_intr_cg`; no other source samples. Window per shard: shard 1 opens it before the manual leg; shard 2 opens it after the set-up legs, at the first burst, so its first toggle is burst 1. Shard 1 fills `cp_detune_mode` (all three bins), `cp_detune_lane`, `cp_autotune[autotune_off]`, `[autotune_on]` and `[burst_1]`; shard 2 fills `cp_detune_lane`, `[autotune_on]` and `[burst_1]` to `[burst_3]`. Check that grades each bin: `CHK-TUNE-MANUAL` and `CHK-TUNE-IGNORED` for `cp_detune_mode`; `CHK-TUNE-TOGGLE` for `cp_detune_lane` and the burst bins; `CHK-TUNE-OFF` with `CHK-TUNE-MANUAL` for `autotune_off`; `CHK-TUNE-AUTO` and `CHK-TUNE-OFF` for the `AUTOTUNE_FAIL` cells {status 0, enable 1} and {status 1, enable 1}; the leaf sets every `INTR_ENABLE` bit, so it fills no {status 0, enable 0} cell. `cp_div_ratio` (7 bins) is graded by `CHK-SCLK-DIV`: the leaf grades the closed pass first, then opens the window for 3 more graded periods. The sampler holds each lane-period hit of that window and samples it only on the rise of bit 1 of `esrc_tune_pass` (`ESRC_TUNE_PASS_SCLK`), which the leaf writes after the exact in-window `CHK-SCLK-DIV` (`phase=window`) of that value passes; the tuning label is bit 0 and releases no `cp_div_ratio` hit. All seven bins fill on every shard-1 run. `cp_detune_lane` is the only bin that depends on the seed (the drawn lane k); it closes on a merge of regressions.

*Scope limit*: This test grades only the cells of its Test Coverage; `sep_esrc_fips_lock_test` keeps the stored readback of `AUTOTUNE_ENABLE` and `DETUNE` and their lock. Not claimed: the ring-oscillator lane lengths (no observation point); the number of toggles per failing window and whether a failure of one generator counts into `ANY_FAIL_COUNT` (blocked, see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>; the leaf logs the toggle count and grades the change of probe bit k only).

'''''

[[sep_esrc_startup_gate_rand_test]]
===== 1.44 `sep_esrc_startup_gate_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+esrc_noise_force`, 4 seeds; testlist `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`; Verilator and VCS

*Source Module*: `sep_esrc_startup_gate_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (CPU-LSU master); noise model that drives `esrc_noise_ext_i` per lane with the modes `balanced` and `stuck0` (common rules "Noise injection" and "Exact patterns"). Approved probes: CPU-LSU master readback; the noise inject `esrc_noise_ext_i`; `esrc_decor_valid_o` (the strobe that paces the noise) and `esrc_decor_bytes_o` (the lane-byte control); `esrc_compress_vld_o` (the stream tap); `esrc_whiten_push_o` (the whitener input); `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` (end of the TRNG reset). No PROPOSED item.

*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the masks and the permutation of the `balanced` pattern, the order of the three toggle moments, the fall instant of each moment (a fraction of the clean boot time t_boot of the same seed, inside the interval that the procedure states) and whether the BIW observe capture is on in each toggle leg. `reseed = 4` samples different patterns, orders, instants and observe settings; the plan shape (clean start, idle, bad start, restart and the three toggles) is the same on every seed. The BIW observe capture is on in the clean start and in the bad start on every seed, because their checks grade its level.

*Objective*: `MODULE_ENABLE` starts startup health testing. `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` reports a passing startup window and permits output. Before it, the whitener input and the BIW observe FIFO receive nothing. Clearing `MODULE_ENABLE` holds the main state machine idle. A `MODULE_ENABLE` rising edge restarts startup testing (`BOOT_PHASE_DONE` reads 0 after the rise) and the total-failure counters. Disabling a health test clears its run state. Spec: `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc` (`CTRL`, `MAIN_SM_STATUS`, `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL`, `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS`, `SHA256_STATUS`, `BIW_OBS_CTRL`, `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS`, `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS`, `APT_LO_TOTAL_FAILS`, `MARKOV_HI_TOTAL_FAILS`, `MARKOV_LO_TOTAL_FAILS`); `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc` (Entropy Source, Reset).

*Test Coverage*: A clean start; a start with stuck0 on all lanes from the first sample; the idle state after `MODULE_ENABLE` = 0; the restart of the total-failure counters by the rising edge; `MODULE_ENABLE` falls at three moments (inside the boot window, after boot, while the bad pattern runs), each followed by a rise with clean noise. This test extends `sep_esrc_ht_watermark_arming_test`, which arms the watermark on a `MODULE_ENABLE` edge. This test grades the boot gate with a failing startup window, the idle state and the counter reset. Only the new cells are graded here; the open owner keeps the watermark arming. Functional coverage: groups `sep_esrc_startup_health_cg` (`x_enable_boot`, `cp_restart_edge`, `cp_startup_fail_hold`, `cp_toggle_moment`) and `sep_esrc_fault_alert_cg` (`cp_total_fails`).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the plan from the seed: the pattern, the order of the three toggle moments, the fall instants and the BIW observe setting of each toggle leg. Log the plan.
. Define the reset sequence: clear and set the TRNG bit of `SEP_RESET_CTRL.SW_RESET_N` (`TRNG_SW_RST_N`) with every other bit at the baseline of the leaf (0x20), and wait until `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` read 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf).
. Define the start: run the reset sequence; write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0; write `ALERT_THRESHOLD` = 0, `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.BYPASS` = 0xFFF, the clean limits of the reset window 2048 (common rule "Clean start") and `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` (1 in the clean and bad starts); apply the noise of the leg; write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 1. The time of this write is t0. With `balanced` on all lanes this is a clean start, and the startup window passes by construction; with stuck0 it fails (0 ones against `APT_PROPORTION_LO` = 512, and a run above `REPETITION_LIMIT` = 200). In the startup window the leaf runs the lane-byte control of the common rule "Noise injection" (`CTRL-NOISE-BYTES`).
. Define a poll: read `BIW_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL`, then `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL`, then `MAIN_SM_STATUS`, back to back, in this order. When a poll reads a `MAIN_SM_STATUS.STATE` that differs from the last one read, log it with `IDLE`, `BOOT_PHASE_DONE`, `ALERT` and the clock (`OBS-ESRC-SM-STATE seed=<n> state=0x<s> idle=<i> boot_phase_done=<b> alert=<a> clk=<c>`). No text states the values of `STATE`.
. Define the leg rule: a set-up or strobe timeout inside a leg fails the check of that leg with `reason=timeout:<what>`, and the leaf goes on with the next leg. Every later bound is a multiple of t_boot or t_fill and is logged.
. Clean start. Run the start with `balanced` on all 12 lanes.
. Poll until `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` reads 1 (a bounded wait of 2W + 64 strobes; on a timeout t_boot and t_fill take the strobe bounds and CHK-BOOT-CLEAN fails). At every poll, apply the rule: if either level reads above 0, `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` of the same poll reads 1. Log t_boot, the time from t0 to the first poll that reads 1.
. Keep polling until both levels read above 0 (a bounded wait of one window of strobes; a timeout logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf). Log t_fill, the time from the first poll that reads `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` = 1 to the first poll that reads the main FIFO level above 0, and the maximum of each level. Count the strobes of `esrc_whiten_push_o` and `esrc_compress_vld_o` from the start to the first poll that reads both levels above 0 (`CTRL-BOOT-TAPS`); a tap with no strobe logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf.
. Idle leg. Read `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` as the level before the write, then pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `LEVEL` reads 0.
. Read `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE`. It reads 0 and the level before the write is above 0 (`CTRL-BOOT-IDLE`; otherwise the leaf logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails). Write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0 and poll `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). Log the clocks as `idle_clk`.
. Poll `SHA256_STATUS.BUSY` until it reads 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check), then pop `FIFO_RDATA` until `LEVEL` reads 0. For a hold of 3 t_fill, poll `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` back to back and watch `esrc_compress_vld_o`.
. Bad start. Run the start with stuck0 on all 12 lanes from the first sample. Error injection: stuck-at noise through `esrc_noise_ext_i`. Expected response: no `BOOT_PHASE_DONE`, and no word through the gated paths.
. Poll for a hold of 3 t_boot after t0. Watch `esrc_whiten_push_o` and `esrc_compress_vld_o` from t0.
. At the end of the hold, read `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS`.
. Restart leg. Read the five total-failure counters (`REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS`, `APT_HI_TOTAL_FAILS`, `APT_LO_TOTAL_FAILS`, `MARKOV_HI_TOTAL_FAILS`, `MARKOV_LO_TOTAL_FAILS`) and log them. `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` reads above 0 (`CTRL-BOOT-RESTART`); otherwise log `CTRL-MISSING` and fail the leaf.
. Return to `balanced` on all lanes and wait two strobes. Write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0 and poll `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check).
. Read the five counters and log them as `OBS-BOOT-COUNTERS` only.
. Write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 1 and read the five counters at once, as the first reads after the write response.
. Poll `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait of 3 t_boot; a timeout fails the check).
. Toggle legs. Run the three moments in drawn order. Every leg uses the start with `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` as drawn, and with `balanced` on all 12 lanes.
. Moment `boot`. The fall instant is a drawn fraction of t_boot from 0 to t_boot/2 after t0, so that the boot window is still open at every draw. Poll `MAIN_SM_STATUS` until a read shows `IDLE` = 0 and the instant has passed (a bounded wait of 3 t_boot), and require that last read to show `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` = 0 and `IDLE` = 0 (`CTRL-BOOT-TOGGLE`). Read the five counters, then write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0.
. Moment `after_boot`. Poll until `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` reads 1 (a bounded wait of 3 t_boot), then wait a drawn delay from 0 to t_boot. Read the five counters and `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE`, then write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0.
. Moment `bad_pattern`. Poll until `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` reads 1 (a bounded wait of 3 t_boot). Error injection: drive stuck0 on all 12 lanes through `esrc_noise_ext_i`. Expected response: `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` rises above 0.
. Poll `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` until it reads above 0 (a bounded wait of 3 t_boot; a timeout logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf), wait a drawn delay from 0 to t_boot while the pattern runs, read the five counters and `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE`, then write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0.
. At the `after_boot` and `bad_pattern` moments, the `IDLE` read before the fall is 0 (`CTRL-BOOT-TOGGLE`; a 1 logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf). After each fall, poll `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check), and return to `balanced` on all 12 lanes at once (with `MODULE_ENABLE` = 0 the change applies at the next sample).
. At the `bad_pattern` moment only, write `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0, then 7, with `REPETITION_LIMIT` at its clean value, so that the run state of every tester is cleared (`entropy_source.adoc`, `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL`: disabling a test clears its state). A stuck0 sample still in the pipeline then starts a short run far below the limit. Read the five counters again; they are the counters before the fall for the check. `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` still reads above 0 (`CTRL-BOOT-TOGGLE leg=tester_clear`), so the tester clear does not clear the total counter that the rise grades.
. Write `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 1 and read the five counters at once, as the first reads after the write response (`REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` first, before the first strobe after the rise; the window counters fail only at a window end). Each counter that read non-zero before the fall reads 0. Then read `MAIN_SM_STATUS` and require `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` = 0.
. Poll `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` until it reads 1 (a bounded wait of 3 t_boot; a timeout fails the check). Read the five counters again and log them as `OBS-BOOT-COUNTERS`; failures after the rise count again by contract.
. Log every check line.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-BOOT-CLEAN
|Whenever the BIW observe level or the main FIFO level reads above 0, the `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` read of the same poll (read after both levels) is 1: entropy reaches the BIW observe FIFO and the main FIFO only after the boot gate opens (`architecture.adoc`, Entropy Source: the boot gate qualifies the whitener input, the uncompressed-byte path and the BIW observe FIFO). A DUT that releases entropy before `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` shows a level above 0 with the bit at 0. Anchor: readback in poll order. Control: both levels rise above 0 after the gate opens, or `CTRL-MISSING` (`CTRL-BOOT-CLEAN`). Evidence: `CHK-BOOT-CLEAN PASS seed=<n> boot_done_clk=<t> fifo_gt0_implies_done=1 biw_obs_gt0_implies_done=1 fifo_level_max=<f> biw_obs_level_max=<b> violations=none`.
|CHK-BOOT-BAD
|With a failing startup window for a hold of 3 t_boot, `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` is 0, both levels are 0, `esrc_whiten_push_o` and `esrc_compress_vld_o` are silent, and `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` bit 0 is 1 (the 32-bit health test sees the ungated stream, `architecture.adoc`, Entropy Source). A DUT that passes words into the whitener, the BIW observe FIFO or the main FIFO before the boot gate opens, or that opens the gate on a failing window, fails. Anchor: readback and the two stream taps. Control: the clean start of the same seed raises both levels above 0 after the gate opens and shows strobes on `esrc_whiten_push_o` and `esrc_compress_vld_o`, and the hold is 3 t_boot. Evidence: `CHK-BOOT-BAD PASS seed=<n> window_clk=<w> polls=<p> boot_done=0 fifo_level=0 biw_obs_level=0 whiten_push=0 stream_vld=0 ht_status_bit0=1 rsvd=0x<r> ctrl_fifo_level_max=<f> ctrl_biw_obs_level_max=<b> ctrl_whiten_push=<p> ctrl_stream_vld=<v>`.
|CHK-BOOT-IDLE
|After `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 0, `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE` is 1 and the FIFO takes no new word for the hold of 3 t_fill, which starts after `SHA256_STATUS.BUSY` reads 0 and a drain (`entropy_source.adoc`, `CTRL`: clearing the field holds the main state machine idle; `SHA256_STATUS`: BUSY covers digest output in flight). A DUT that keeps filling the FIFO after the write, or that does not reach IDLE, fails. Anchor: `MAIN_SM_STATUS` and `FIFO_STATUS` readback and the stream tap. Control: `IDLE` read 0 and the FIFO level read above 0 before the write (`CTRL-BOOT-IDLE`), the clean start of the same seed shows strobes on `esrc_compress_vld_o`, and the hold is a multiple of the clean fill time of the same seed. Evidence: `CHK-BOOT-IDLE PASS seed=<n> idle_before=0 idle=1 idle_clk=<c> sha_busy_clear=1 level_before=<l> level_stable=1 stream_vld=0 hold_clk=<h> polls=<p> ctrl_stream_vld=<v>`.
|CHK-BOOT-RESTART
|After `CTRL.MODULE_ENABLE` = 1, every total-failure counter that read non-zero before the fall reads 0 at once, and `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` returns to 1 (`architecture.adoc`, Reset; `entropy_source.adoc`, `CTRL`: the rising edge restarts startup health testing and the total-failure counters). A DUT that keeps the counters or does not restart startup fails. Anchor: counter readback. Control: `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` read non-zero before (`CTRL-BOOT-RESTART`), or `CTRL-MISSING`. Evidence: `CHK-BOOT-RESTART PASS seed=<n> boot_done_again=1 done_clk=<c> total_fails_before=<rep_apt_hi_apt_lo_mkv_hi_mkv_lo> total_fails_after_edge=<...> rep_before_nonzero=1 nonzero_after_edge=0`.
|CHK-BOOT-TOGGLE
|At each drawn fall moment, `MAIN_SM_STATUS.IDLE` becomes 1. After the rise with clean noise, every counter that read non-zero before the fall reads 0 at once, the first `MAIN_SM_STATUS` read shows `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` = 0, and `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` returns to 1 (`architecture.adoc`, Reset: the rise restarts startup health testing; `entropy_source.adoc`, `MAIN_SM_STATUS.BOOT_PHASE_DONE`; the total-failure counters count since the last `MODULE_ENABLE` rise). The counters at `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` are logged as `OBS-BOOT-COUNTERS` and not graded, because failures after the rise count again by contract. A DUT that stays out of IDLE inside the boot window or while the bad pattern runs, that keeps `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` through the toggle, that keeps a counter, or that does not restart after the rise, fails. Anchor: readback. Control (`CTRL-BOOT-TOGGLE`): at every moment `IDLE` read 0 before the fall; at the boot moment the last `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` read before the fall is 0; at the bad-pattern moment `REPCNT_TOTAL_FAILS` read non-zero before the fall and again after the tester clear (`leg=tester_clear`). Evidence: `CHK-BOOT-TOGGLE PASS seed=<n> moment=<boot_or_after_boot_or_bad_pattern> raw_enable=<r> idle_before=0 idle_clk=<c> done_at_rise=0 boot_done_again=1 rep_before=<r> total_fails_before=<...> total_fails_after_rise=<...> nonzero_after_rise=0`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: This test grades only the cells of its Test Coverage; `sep_esrc_ht_watermark_arming_test` keeps the watermark arming on a `MODULE_ENABLE` edge. Not claimed: the high-side adaptive-proportion and Markov counters, which read 0 under stuck0; whether the tester run state clears on a `MODULE_ENABLE` rise (not stated; the leaf clears it with `HEALTH_TEST_CTRL.ENABLE` at the bad-pattern moment); the counters after `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` returns (logged only); an absolute boot time (every bound is a multiple of the clean time of the same seed).

'''''
[[sep_esrc_conditioning_matrix_rand_test]]
===== 1.45 `sep_esrc_conditioning_matrix_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy (ESRC)

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense +esrc_noise_force`, 4 seeds; testlist `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`; Verilator and VCS

*Source Module*: `sep_esrc_conditioning_matrix_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip with the CPU-LSU master readback and writes of the ESRC registers (approved); the noise inject `esrc_noise_ext_i` (approved); the probes `esrc_decor_bytes_o`, `esrc_decor_valid_o`, `esrc_whiten_push_o`, `esrc_compress_vld_o` and `esrc_compress_data_o` (approved; the FIFO word data is logged only, in `OBS-COND-BYPASS-MIDGROUP`); `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` (approved, the release of the TRNG reset). The ESRC SHA-256 status tap (`esrc_sha256_status_o`), a read-only tap on the next value of the `SHA256_STATUS` fields sampled on every `clk_i`, is an observation tap of the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list (CHK-COND-SHA, CHK-COND-SHA-COUNT). The ESRC downsample and divider taps (`esrc_downsample_count_o`, `esrc_stream_valid_gated_o`, `esrc_decor_div_lane0_o`) and the generator enable tap (`esrc_ro_enable_o`), read-only taps of the FIFO downsample count, the decorrelator stream strobe after the boot gate, the divider count of lane 0 and the stored `RING_OSC_ENABLE.ENABLE` at the generators, are observation taps of the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list (CHK-COND-FIRST-PUSH counts the gated strobe; CHK-COND-FIRST-STROBE anchors on the enable; the counts are logged only). No inject beyond the noise modes. Expected values come from the rules of the specification only: a lane byte from the constant noise bit and the mask, the specified generator-to-byte bypass packing, an exact strobe count from `DOWNSAMPLE_RATE`, the gated strobe of the first FIFO word from `DOWNSAMPLE_RATE`, the ratio of two byte periods and the first-strobe distance from `SAMPLE_CLK_DIV`, and the 16-to-8 word count of the whitener.

*Randomization*: RANDCFG. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the order of the legs, the partial mask M (some bits 0 and some bits 1), the drawn rate N_r (2 to 15), the lane noise mask Z (12 bits, at least one 1 and one 0) plus its polarity for the mask leg, the drawn divider value D_r (64 to 1023) and the order of 0 and D_r after the reset value 63 in the period walk. `reseed = 4` samples different masks, rates, mask-leg noise and leg orders. The bypass packing leg walks four deterministic lane-signature patterns: pattern p gives lane j the bit `(j >> p) & 1`, for p = 0 to 3, so every lane has a unique four-pass signature; an all-ones control follows. The plan shape is the same on every seed: every seed walks the four (`BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR`, `SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE`) cells, every generator-to-byte position, the rates 0, 1, N_r and 1023, and the divider values 0, 63 and D_r. The leaf logs the plan first (`INFO-PLAN seed=<n> ...`). The number of gated strobes (1 or 2) after which the compressor bypass is set and cleared in the mid-group step is drawn after every other value.

*Objective*: `DECORRELATOR_MASK` ANDs every decorrelated byte. With `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.BYPASS` set for a lane, a constant noise bit gives a constant byte. With `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` set, the twelve bytes reach the FIFO as three 32-bit words in word order 0, 1, 2; generator `4n+k` occupies bits `[8k+7:8k]` of word `n`. Every (N+1)th decorrelator output is selected when `DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` = N, counted from the first output that the boot gate passes after the TRNG reset. `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.SAMPLE_CLK_DIV` = D sets the byte period to D + 1 cycles; while a lane is disabled its divider holds at zero, so a D written before the enable (`RING_OSC_ENABLE.ENABLE`) sets the distance to the first sample. With `SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE` = 1, 16 whitener input words give 8 digest words, and `SHA256_STATUS.OUTPUT_COUNT` reports 8 and counts down to 0. With it clear, the BIW words reach the FIFO unchanged. Spec: `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc` (`CTRL`, `SHA256_STATUS`, `DECORRELATOR_CTRL`, `DECORRELATOR_MASK`); `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc` (Entropy Source, Decorrelation and BIW Mixing bypass-packing paragraph, SHA-256 Whitening, Output FIFO); `doc/programmer/src/sep-programming.adoc:409-411,427` (disable `RING_OSC_ENABLE.ENABLE`, program, enable).

*Test Coverage*: The four (`BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR`, `SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE`) cells on every seed. The reset mask 0xFF and a drawn partial mask, with constant lane noise and BYPASS on all lanes. The three-word bypass order and all twelve generator-to-byte positions, distinguished by four deterministic lane-signature patterns under both whitening-control values; an all-ones control. `DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` 0, 1, a drawn rate 2 to 15 and 1023, each on every seed, with the compressor bypassed, and the rate N_r with the compressor in use and whitening off as the control. The first FIFO word after the TRNG reset with the compressor bypassed and N_r written before the boot gate passes a strobe. The byte period at `SAMPLE_CLK_DIV` 0 and D_r against the reset value 63, as a ratio, and the first strobe after a lane enable at all three values. `SAMPLE_CLK_DIV` at its maximum 1048575 is not run: one period is about one million `clk_i` cycles, beyond the leaf length; the ratio form at D_r covers the counter. Logged only: the absolute period in `clk_i` cycles (its time base is blocked, see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>), and a `DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` or `SAMPLE_CLK_DIV` write while the stream runs (increase, decrease to at or above the count, decrease below the count), a rate written while the stream is stopped, the compressor bypass turned on while the gated stream runs, and the compressor bypass set and cleared again between two outputs of the compressor (no text states their effect). Stuck0 on all lanes with BYPASS on all lanes, with the compressor bypassed and not bypassed, with `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` logged only (the 32-bit health test in bypass is blocked, see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The exact 16-to-8 count of the whitener and its `OUTPUT_COUNT` on the status tap, and the pass-through; the `OUTPUT_COUNT` bus reads of the pass-through window are logged only. In the pass-through window the first four FIFO words are logged with the lane bytes of the two strobes before each word (`OBS-ESRC-BIW-MAP`); the map from the twelve bytes to the BIW word is not graded. Extends `sep_esrc_e2e_smoke_test`, which grades the default path (decorrelator, BIW, SHA-256, seed, DRBG) with one configuration, and `sep_esrc_fips_lock_test`, which grades the stored value of the byte mask, the compressor bypass, `DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` and `SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE` and their lock. This test grades only their effect on the data path; the owners of the open tests keep the default path, the stored values and the lock. Functional coverage: `sep_esrc_conditioning_cg` (`cp_byte_mask`, `x_path_matrix`, `cp_downsample_rate`, `cp_sha_counts`, `cp_sample_clk_div`, `cp_first_strobe`, `cp_ds_first_push`, `cp_ds_midrun`, `cp_div_midrun`, `cp_ds_resume`).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Set-up, run at the start of each leg and at the start of each trial named in a leg. Pulse the TRNG reset: write 0 then 1 to bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N` (`TRNG_SW_RST_N`) with every other bit at the baseline of the leaf (0x20), and wait until `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` read 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). No ESRC, CSRNG or EDN access comes before this release, because the write of 0 isolates their host paths until the write of 1 (`reset_controller.adoc`, Isolation and Reset Sequencing). Write 1 to the eight defined bits of `INTR_ENABLE` (common rule "ESRC interrupt status"). Write `ALERT_THRESHOLD.THRESHOLD` = 0, so that no persistent alert halts the stream. Run the clean start (common rule "Clean start"), which leaves `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.BYPASS` = 0xFFF and `balanced` on all twelve lanes. Measure the strobe period p once per leaf (`INFO-ESRC-STROBE`). Wait for `MAIN_SM_STATUS.BOOT_PHASE_DONE` = 1 (a bounded wait of 2W + 64 strobe periods; a timeout fails the leaf). Steps 2 to 29 form five legs (mask, bypass words, downsample, health test, whitener and pass-through). The legs run in the drawn order.
. Mask leg. Run the set-up. Write `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.BYPASS` = 0xFFF and `DECORRELATOR_MASK` = 0xFF (the reset value).
. Drive lane j with the constant noise bit Z[j] (stuck1 or stuck0), with Z in the drawn polarity. Discard every strobe of `esrc_decor_valid_o` until at least 29 `clk_i` cycles (the 29-stage chain shifts on every `clk_i` cycle) and at least 4 strobes have passed since the noise change. Log both counts.
. Compare the 12 lane bytes of `esrc_decor_bytes_o` at each of the next 16 strobes with (0xFF if Z[j] is 1, else 0x00) AND 0xFF.
. Write the drawn mask M to `DECORRELATOR_MASK`. Discard the next 2 strobes. Compare the 12 lane bytes of the next 16 strobes with (0xFF if Z[j] is 1, else 0x00) AND M.
. Bypass-words leg. Run the set-up. Write `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.BYPASS` = 0xFFF, `DECORRELATOR_MASK` = M, `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 1, `CTRL.DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` = 0 and `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0. Steps 7 to 11 run for `SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE` w = 1 and w = 0, each with lane-signature patterns p = 0, 1, 2 and 3, then with all lanes stuck1.
. Write `CTRL` with `BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 1 and `SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE` = w, then read `CTRL` back. The control (`CTRL-COND-BYPASS`) needs the readback to equal the written word, so a dropped write cannot pass as the other whitening value; the check logs w from the readback. For signature pass p, drive lane j with `(j >> p) & 1`; for the control drive every lane with 1. Wait for the settle period of step 3.
. Disable all ring generators, wait four `clk_i` cycles for any active three-word output to finish, and read `FIFO_RDATA` until `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` reads 0. No new word enters while the generators and FIFO write are disabled.
. Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 1, then re-enable the generators. The first new decorrelator output therefore starts at bypass word 0. Poll `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads 64 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0.
. Read 63 words from `FIFO_RDATA`, 21 complete decorrelator outputs.
. For each output, build byte `b[j]` as (0xFF if the pass's deterministic lane bit is 1, else 0x00) AND M. Compare FIFO word `n` with `{b[4n+3], b[4n+2], b[4n+1], b[4n]}` for `n` = 0, 1, 2, in that order (`architecture.adoc`, Decorrelation and BIW Mixing bypass-packing paragraph). This checks all twelve generator-to-byte positions and both whitening-control values. With all lanes stuck1 every expected word is M in all four byte lanes.
. Downsample leg. Run the set-up with `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 1 and `CTRL.DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` = N_r in both `CTRL` writes of the clean start. From the release of the TRNG reset to the first FIFO word, count on every `clk_i` the strobes of `esrc_stream_valid_gated_o` and the words of `esrc_compress_vld_o` (a bounded trace; a timeout fails the check). The control (`CTRL-COND-FIRST-PUSH`) needs no gated strobe when the clean start returns, no FIFO word before the first gated strobe, and a `CTRL` readback with `BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 1 and `DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` = N_r. The trace also counts every `esrc_decor_valid_o` strobe: u0 before the first gated strobe and u_e before the clean start returns (its last write sets `MODULE_ENABLE`). A count of every strobe from the TRNG release or from that write would push on gated strobe N_r + 1 only when u0 or u0 - u_e is a multiple of N_r + 1, so the control also needs neither to be. When either is, the leg repeats the TRNG reset and clean start with the first rate after the last one (wrapping from 15 to 2) for which neither u0 nor u0 - u_e of the last try is a multiple of that rate + 1, at most three times, and grades the last try. Require the first FIFO word on the (N_r + 1)th gated strobe (CHK-COND-FIRST-PUSH). Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0, so that the FIFO never fills (the stream is not gated by it). Write `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 1 and `CTRL.DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` = 0.
. Define an output: the words on `esrc_compress_vld_o` with no strobe of `esrc_decor_valid_o` between them (a strobe and a word in the same `clk_i` cycle: the strobe first). Define the span of a rate N: from the clock of the last word of the second output after the response of the rate write to the clock of the last word of the sixth output; its strobe count is the strobes after its first clock up to and including its last clock. The span is aligned when every output in it has the expected word count, every strobe gap from the last strobe before the span to its end is one value P, and both ends lie the same number of clocks after their last strobe. An aligned span is a whole number of strobe periods, so its strobe count does not depend on the latency from a strobe to its words. The wait is bounded by a clock count from the rate and the measured strobe period; a timeout fails the check.
. For N = 0, 1, N_r and 1023, in ascending order: write `CTRL.DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` = N (with the compressor bypassed) and measure the span of four three-word outputs.
. Require each span to be aligned, to hold 12 words, and to hold exactly 4(N + 1) strobes.
. Control. Write `CTRL` with `BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 0, `SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE` = 0 and `DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` = N_r. Measure an aligned span of 16 one-word outputs and require exactly 16 strobes (`CTRL-COND-DOWNSAMPLE`): with the compressor in use every decorrelator output is one BIW word that reaches the FIFO unchanged, whatever the rate.
. Period walk. Write `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 1 and `DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` = 0. For D = 63 (the reset value), then 0 and D_r in the drawn order: write `RING_OSC_ENABLE.ENABLE` = 0 and wait until `esrc_ro_enable_o` reads 0 (a bounded wait); write `DECORRELATOR_CTRL` with `BYPASS` = 0xFFF and `SAMPLE_CLK_DIV` = D; write `RING_OSC_ENABLE` with its reset value. On every `clk_i` take the first clock where `esrc_ro_enable_o` reads 0xFFF, the strobes of `esrc_decor_valid_o` before it, and the clocks of the next six strobes (a bounded trace). The control (`CTRL-COND-PERIOD`) needs the enable seen, six strobes, five equal gaps g_D and no strobe before the enable. Take the offset o_D = (distance from the enable clock to the first strobe) - g_D, and, from `esrc_decor_div_lane0_o` on every `clk_i` from the disable write to the end of the `DECORRELATOR_CTRL` write, the set k_D of counts a divider that keeps its count while disabled would hold: the count on the clock before the last run of zeros, and also 0 when that count is the stored `SAMPLE_CLK_DIV` (the run can then start at a wrap); {0} when every count is 0; no set when the trace does not end in zeros. While k_63 contains 0, repeat the walk of 63, 32 clocks after the last one, up to three walks in all, and keep the last; log the walks as `tries` of `INFO-COND-FIRST-OFFSET`. For D = 0 and D_r the control (`CTRL-COND-FIRST-STROBE`) needs min(k, D) different from min(k', 63) for every k in k_D and k' in k_63, and require o_D = o_63 (CHK-COND-FIRST-STROBE); log o_D and k_D as `INFO-COND-FIRST-OFFSET`. For D = 0 and D_r require g_D x 64 = g_63 x (D + 1) (CHK-COND-PERIOD-RATIO). Log g_D as `OBS-COND-PERIOD-ABS` and the lane 0 divider count at the enable clock as `INFO-COND-DIV-TAP`. Restore `SAMPLE_CLK_DIV` = 63 in the same order.
. Mid-run writes, logged only. On every `clk_i` record `esrc_decor_valid_o`, `esrc_stream_valid_gated_o`, `esrc_compress_vld_o`, `esrc_downsample_count_o`, `esrc_decor_div_lane0_o` and `esrc_compress_data_o`. With the lanes running, on the clock after a strobe write `SAMPLE_CLK_DIV` 63 -> D_r, D_r -> 63, 63 -> D_r and D_r -> 0, each logged with the lane 0 divider count when the first strobe after the write fires, whether it is above the new value, the first gap and the steady gap (`OBS-COND-DIV-MIDRUN-UP`, `OBS-COND-DIV-MIDRUN-DOWN`); restore 63 with the lanes disabled. With the compressor in use, whitening off and rate N_r, after N_r + 3 gated strobes write `BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 1 and log the downsample count and the gated strobes to the next FIFO word (`OBS-COND-DS-BYPASS-ON`). With the compressor in use and whitening off, after one FIFO word and the drawn 1 or 2 gated strobes write `BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 1 and then 0, log the FIFO words pushed between the two write responses and the next three FIFO words with their gaps (`OBS-COND-BYPASS-MIDGROUP strobe=<s> pushes_between=<n> words_between=<w,...> next_words=<w,w,w> next_gaps_clk=<g,g>`), and write `BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 1 with the rate N_r again. With the compressor bypassed write `DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` N_r -> 16 after a word, 16 -> 0 once the count reads 2 or more, 0 -> 16 and 16 -> 1 after a word, and 1 -> 16 after a word, each logged with the count at the write response, the gated strobes to the next word, the count at that word and whether it is above the new rate (`OBS-COND-DS-MIDRUN-UP`, `OBS-COND-DS-MIDRUN-DOWN`). Once the count reads 1 to 14, disable the lanes, write `DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` = N_r, enable the lanes, and log the held count and the gated strobes to the next word (`OBS-COND-DS-RESUME`). Write `CTRL` with its reset values.
. Health-test leg, control trial. Run the set-up. Write `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.BYPASS` = 0xFFF. Write 0xFF to `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS`, read it and log it. Drive stuck0 on all lanes. Wait for `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` bit 0 = 1 (a bounded wait of W + 256 strobe periods). Log the wait as t_ctrl, or the bound if the bit does not set (`INFO-COND-HT-CTRL`).
. Health-test leg, bypass trial. Run the set-up. Write `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.BYPASS` = 0xFFF and `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 1. Write 0xFF to `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS`, read it and log it. Drive stuck0 on all lanes. Wait for 3 times t_ctrl (the leaf logs t_ctrl and the multiple). Read `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` and log it with its value before the noise change (`OBS-BLOCKED CHK-COND-HT-OFF`); nothing is graded on it.
. Whitener leg. Run the set-up. Keep `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.BYPASS` = 0xFFF and the `balanced` pattern of the set-up. Write `CTRL` with its reset values (`BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 0 and `SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE` = 1). Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0. Read `FIFO_RDATA` until `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` reads 0.
. Start the trace of the ESRC SHA-256 status tap, `esrc_whiten_push_o` and `esrc_compress_vld_o` on every `clk_i`. Write `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1, then `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 1. Poll `BIW_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads at least 32 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). After each poll read `SHA256_STATUS` and log the values as `OBS-COND-SHA-STATUS-READS`. Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0, then `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 0, and stop the trace.
. Read `BIW_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL`, `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` and `INTR_STATUS.BIW_OBS_OVERFLOW`. The control (`CTRL-COND-SHA`) needs the BIW level reached, no observe overflow and both levels above 0.
. Over the span between the first and the last quiet clock of the trace (BUSY = 0 and `OUTPUT_COUNT` = 0, no input or output word within two clocks), compare 2 x output words with `INPUT_COUNT`(first) + input words - `INPUT_COUNT`(last). Input words are strobes of `esrc_whiten_push_o`; output words are strobes of `esrc_compress_vld_o`. The control (`CTRL-COND-SHA`) needs a span with at least 16 input words and 8 output words. Log the BIW, push and FIFO counts as `OBS-COND-SHA-INTAKE` with `OBS-BLOCKED CHK-COND-SHA-INTAKE reason=whitener_intake_while_hashing_unstated`.
. During step 22 the trace of the SHA-256 status tap requires every `OUTPUT_COUNT` step to be -1 on the clock after an `esrc_compress_vld_o` word, and a step without a word to be 0 -> 8; each run from 8 holds exactly 8 steps and ends at 0, and at least one run completes. During step 27 a second trace requires `OUTPUT_COUNT` to stay 0.
. Pass-through leg, in the same trial. Read `FIFO_RDATA` until `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` reads 0. Read `BIW_OBS_RDATA` until `BIW_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` reads 0. Write `CTRL.SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE` = 0. Poll `SHA256_STATUS.BUSY` until it reads 0 (a bounded wait), so that no digest word is in flight when the FIFO write opens. `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` and `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` stay 0 during the wait, so both FIFOs stay empty.
. Start the second trace of the SHA-256 status tap. Write `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1, then `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 1. From the start of the trace, on every `clk_i`, keep the twelve lane bytes of `esrc_decor_bytes_o` at each strobe of `esrc_decor_valid_o`. For each of the first four words on `esrc_compress_vld_o` after the second strobe, keep the word of `esrc_compress_data_o`, the bytes of the last strobe and of the strobe before it, and the clocks from the last strobe to the word. Poll `BIW_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads at least 16 (a bounded wait). After each poll read `SHA256_STATUS` and keep `OUTPUT_COUNT`. Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0, then `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 0, and stop the trace.
. Read `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` and require it to be at least 8, with the BUSY poll and the BIW level reached (`CTRL-COND-PASSTHRU`). Read all BIW observe words in order, then all FIFO words in order.
. Take the smallest offset d (0 to 8) at which every FIFO word i equals the BIW observe word i + d. No such offset fails the check. Log the kept `OUTPUT_COUNT` values as `OBS-COND-PASSTHRU-OUTCOUNT` (`reads=<r> nonzero=<z> values=<v,...> busy_zero=<b>`). Log the kept words as `OBS-ESRC-BIW-MAP`, each with its bytes and whether it is one of the BIW observe words read in the previous step.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-COND-MASK
|With constant lane noise and BYPASS set on all lanes, each lane byte equals (0xFF if the lane noise bit is 1, else 0x00) AND `DECORRELATOR_MASK`, once the 29-stage chain is full (`architecture.adoc`, Decorrelation and BIW Mixing: a cleared mask bit reads as zero; BYPASS forces the feedback to zero). The check runs with the reset mask 0xFF and with the drawn partial mask M. Z has at least one 1 and one 0, so a lane swap, an ignored mask or a mask that covers only some lanes changes at least one byte. Anchor: `esrc_decor_bytes_o` at each `esrc_decor_valid_o`, 12 lane bytes per strobe, 16 strobes per mask. The 0xFF run is the control of the M run: the same lanes give the full byte (`CTRL-COND-MASK`). Evidence: `CHK-COND-MASK PASS seed=<n> mask=0x<m> noise=0x<z> lanes=12 strobes=16 mismatches=0`, logged once per mask.
|CHK-COND-BYPASS
|With `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 1, each decorrelator output produces FIFO words 0, 1 and 2 in that order, and generator `4n+k` occupies bits `[8k+7:8k]` of word `n` (`architecture.adoc`, Decorrelation and BIW Mixing bypass-packing paragraph; `entropy_source.adoc`, `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR`). The expected lane byte is (0xFF for stuck1, 0x00 for stuck0) AND M. Four deterministic passes give every lane a unique signature, so any lane swap changes at least one expected byte; the check compares 21 complete three-word outputs bit-exactly with `SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE` = 1 and = 0, then runs an all-ones control. A word reorder, whitening, dropped byte or wrong output count also fails. Generator disable, FIFO drain and generator re-enable establish word 0 as the first captured word. Anchor: `FIFO_RDATA`; the deterministic pattern and M are the independent oracle. Evidence: `CHK-COND-BYPASS PASS seed=<n> whiten=<w> mask=0x<m> pattern=<p or all1> outputs=21 words=63 mismatches=0 first=0x<w0>,0x<w1>,0x<w2> expect=0x<e0>,0x<e1>,0x<e2>`.
|CHK-COND-DOWNSAMPLE
|With the compressor bypassed, every (N+1)th decorrelator output reaches the stream as three words (`entropy_source.adoc`, `CTRL.DOWNSAMPLE_RATE`: every (N+1)th decorrelator output while BIW extraction is bypassed; `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR`: the twelve generator bytes as three 32-bit words). One strobe of `esrc_decor_valid_o` is one decorrelator output. For N = 0, 1, N_r and 1023, the aligned span of four outputs holds exactly 12 words and exactly 4(N + 1) strobes. The span is a whole number of strobe periods, so the count is exact and does not depend on the strobe-to-word latency. A DUT that selects every Nth or every (N+2)th output gives 4N or 4(N + 2) strobes, a DUT that ignores the rate gives 4 at every rate, and a DUT that writes other than three words per output fails the word count or the alignment. The check uses no absolute time. Anchor: the words of `esrc_compress_vld_o` and the strobes of `esrc_decor_valid_o`, with `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0 (the stream is not gated by it). Control, exact (`CTRL-COND-DOWNSAMPLE`): with the compressor in use, whitening off and rate N_r, an aligned span of 16 one-word outputs holds 16 strobes (`architecture.adoc`, Decorrelation and BIW Mixing; `entropy_source.adoc`, `CTRL.SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE`), so the effect needs the bypass. Evidence: `CHK-COND-DOWNSAMPLE PASS seed=<n> n=<N> bypass=1 outputs=4 expect_strobes=<4(N+1)> strobes=<4(N+1)> words=12 sizes=<3,3,3,3> phase=<a_b> gaps=<P> span_clk=<c>`.
|CHK-COND-FIRST-PUSH
|With the compressor bypassed and `DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` = N_r written after the TRNG reset and before the boot gate passes any strobe, the first FIFO word comes on the (N_r + 1)th gated decorrelator output (`entropy_source.adoc:106`: every (N+1)th decorrelator output while BIW extraction is bypassed; `architecture.adoc:40-47`: the boot gate passes the uncompressed bytes only after a passing boot window and the output select downsamples them; `doc/programmer/src/sep-programming.adoc:400-411,427-429`: reset, program, enable, then poll `BOOT_PHASE_DONE`). Every (N+1)th output selects outputs N+1, 2(N+1), and so on, counted from the first output after the reset; for N at least 1 the first output is not one of them, so a DUT that selects the first gated output, or every Nth, fails. One strobe of `esrc_stream_valid_gated_o` is one gated output, and a bypassed output writes its first word on the clock of its strobe, so the count does not depend on a latency. Anchor: `esrc_stream_valid_gated_o` and `esrc_compress_vld_o`. Control (`CTRL-COND-FIRST-PUSH`): no gated strobe when the clean start returns and no FIFO word before the first gated strobe, N_r at least 1. Evidence: `CHK-COND-FIRST-PUSH PASS: seed=<n> n=<N_r> first_push_gated=<N_r+1> expect=<N_r+1> gated_at_ctrl=0 pre_push=0 ds_count=<c>`.
|CHK-COND-PERIOD-RATIO
|The byte period is `SAMPLE_CLK_DIV` + 1 cycles (`entropy_source.adoc:387`; `architecture.adoc:107`), so the steady strobe gaps at D and at the reset value 63 meet g_D x 64 = g_63 x (D + 1), for D = 0 (one strobe per enabled cycle) and D = D_r. The unit of a cycle cancels, so the check uses no absolute time. Each value is written with every lane disabled (`doc/programmer/src/sep-programming.adoc:409-411,427`), so no write while the divider runs is graded. A DUT whose period is D + 2, D or ignores the value fails. Anchor: `esrc_decor_valid_o`. Control: `CTRL-COND-PERIOD` of each value. Evidence: `CHK-COND-PERIOD-RATIO PASS: seed=<n> div=<D> ref_div=63 gap=<g_D> ref_gap=<g_63> lhs=<g_D x 64> rhs=<g_63 x (D+1)>`, for D = 0 and D_r.
|CHK-COND-FIRST-STROBE
|While a lane is disabled its divider holds at zero, so a `SAMPLE_CLK_DIV` written before the enable sets the first distance (`architecture.adoc:113`). The enable of a lane is `RING_OSC_ENABLE.ENABLE` (`entropy_source.adoc:357`). The specification gives no delay from the stored enable to the lane, so the check assumes only that this delay L is the same for every value: the first distance is L + g_D, and the offset o_D = first distance - g_D is the same for D = 63, 0 and D_r. The check requires o_0 = o_63 and o_(D_r) = o_63, measured from the first clock where `esrc_ro_enable_o` reads 0xFFF. All values are DUT clock counts, so the check uses no absolute time and no assumed L. A DUT that fires at the enable, or that loads the value while disabled, gives o_D = L + 1 - g_D, which changes with D, and fails. A DUT that keeps its count k while disabled fires min(k, D) clocks early and gives o_D = L - min(k, D). The held count k is read from the divider trace, not from the enable tap, so it needs no assumed L: a correct divider goes to zero on the clock where the lane stops, and the count on the clock before is the one such a DUT keeps. The control needs min(k, D) different from min(k', 63) for every held count k at D and k' at 63, so that DUT fails. A k_63 that contains 0 leaves no D = 0 that meets the control, so the walk of 63 repeats with its disable half a period later until k_63 has no 0. Anchor: `esrc_ro_enable_o` (the enable tap, not the bus response), `esrc_decor_valid_o` and `esrc_decor_div_lane0_o` (k). Control: `CTRL-COND-PERIOD` (the enable seen, six strobes, five equal gaps and no strobe before the enable) and `CTRL-COND-FIRST-STROBE` (min(k, D) different from min(k', 63) for every held count k at D and k' at 63). Evidence: `CHK-COND-FIRST-STROBE PASS: seed=<n> div=<D> ref_div=63 first_clk=<c> gap_clk=<g_D> offset=<o> ref_offset=<o>`.
|CHK-COND-HT-OFF
|Blocked: the specification does not state one behaviour of the 32-bit health test with the compressor bypassed. `architecture.adoc` (Entropy Source) states that the test is clock-gated off in that mode, and `architecture.adoc` (Health Testing) states that the 32-bit instance monitors the post-BIW or post-bypass stream (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The leaf runs the control trial and the bypass trial, logs `HEALTH_TEST_STATUS` of the bypass trial after 3 t_ctrl with t_ctrl, and grades nothing on them. The check is not counted in `checks=<k>`. Evidence: `OBS-BLOCKED CHK-COND-HT-OFF reason=ht32_in_bypass_unstated seed=<n>` and `OBS-COND-HT-OFF seed=<n> bypass=1 ht_status_before=0x<b> ht_status=0x<s> t_ctrl_clk=<c> wait_clk=<w>`.
|CHK-COND-SHA
|With whitening on, 16 whitener input words give 8 output words, exactly (`architecture.adoc`, SHA-256 Whitening: 16 input words give 8 digest words; Output FIFO: `esrc_compress_vld_o` carries each word as it is written into the FIFO; `entropy_source.adoc`, `SHA256_STATUS`: `INPUT_COUNT` is the words of the open input block). Over the span between the first and the last quiet clock of the trace, 2 x output words = `INPUT_COUNT`(first) + input words - `INPUT_COUNT`(last), with input words the strobes of `esrc_whiten_push_o` and output words the strobes of `esrc_compress_vld_o`. At a quiet clock no digest is in processing or in output, so every block that completes in the span gives all its 8 words in the span. A DUT that passes the words through, or that gives another ratio, breaks the relation. Anchor: the ESRC SHA-256 status tap and the two strobes. Control (`CTRL-COND-SHA`): the BIW level is reached with no observe overflow and both levels above 0, and the span holds at least 16 input words and one digest (8 output words). Evidence: `CHK-COND-SHA PASS seed=<n> expected_out_x2=<e> out_words_x2=<e> in_words=<i> input_count_first=<f> input_count_last=<l> span_clk=<c> fifo_words=<b> biw_words=<a>`.
|CHK-COND-SHA-INTAKE
|Blocked: the specification does not state whether the whitener takes every BIW word while it hashes a block, so the BIW observe count and the whitener input count can differ. The leaf logs the BIW, push and FIFO counts and grades nothing on them. The check is not counted in `checks=<k>`. Evidence: `OBS-BLOCKED CHK-COND-SHA-INTAKE reason=whitener_intake_while_hashing_unstated seed=<n>` and `OBS-COND-SHA-INTAKE seed=<n> biw_words=<a> pushes=<p> fifo_words=<b>`.
|CHK-COND-SHA-COUNT
|With whitening on, `SHA256_STATUS.OUTPUT_COUNT` reports 8 when a digest is available and decrements by one for each word accepted on `esrc_compress_vld_o` (no backpressure, `architecture.adoc`, Output FIFO) through 0: every step is -1 on the clock after a word or 0 -> 8 without one, and each run from 8 holds exactly 8 steps (`entropy_source.adoc`, `SHA256_STATUS.OUTPUT_COUNT`). With whitening off it stays 0 at every clock of the pass-through window (`SHA256_STATUS.OUTPUT_COUNT`: remains 0 while bypassed). A DUT that never reports 8, steps by other than one per accepted word, changes the count without a word, or reports a digest count while bypassed, fails. Anchor: the ESRC SHA-256 status tap (an observation tap). Control: the BIW observe count is non-zero in the same run (`CTRL-COND-SHA-COUNT`), and the reach of 8 in the whitened window of the same seed is the control of the zero in the pass-through window. Evidence: `CHK-COND-SHA-COUNT PASS seed=<n> steps=<s> runs_complete=<r> min_runs=1 run_steps=[8, ...] expected_run_steps=8 open_run_steps=<k> step_errors=0 first_errors=[] out_count_max=8 out_count_end=<e> biw_words=<a> bypass_out_count_max=0 bypass_clocks=<c>`.
|CHK-COND-PASSTHRU
|With whitening off, every FIFO word equals the BIW observe word at one fixed offset d (`architecture.adoc`, SHA-256 Whitening: the BIW output passes to the FIFO without modification). A compliant DUT matches at its true offset, so the outcome does not depend on the word values; a whitened, dropped or reordered word breaks the match at every offset and fails. The offset d (0 to 8) absorbs the few BIW words captured before the FIFO write enable; the leaf logs d. The FIFO write opens only after `SHA256_STATUS.BUSY` reads 0 with whitening off, so no digest word of the whitened run enters the compare. Anchor: `FIFO_RDATA`, `BIW_OBS_RDATA` and `SHA256_STATUS` readback. Control: the FIFO LEVEL is at least 8 in the same window, so the compare has words (`CTRL-COND-PASSTHRU`). Evidence: `CHK-COND-PASSTHRU PASS seed=<n> offset=<d> compared=<k> biw_words=<a> mismatches=0`.
|OBS-COND-PASSTHRU-OUTCOUNT
|Logged only, no pass or fail. Every `SHA256_STATUS.OUTPUT_COUNT` read polled in the pass-through window (`hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc:206`, from `entropy_source.rdl:88`: OUTPUT_COUNT "remains 0 while idle or bypassed"). No bus read of the leaf shows the field non-zero, also in the whitened window, so a read of 0 does not tell a correct field from one that is not connected or tied to 0. The count itself is graded on the status tap (CHK-COND-SHA-COUNT). Evidence: `OBS-COND-PASSTHRU-OUTCOUNT seed=<n> reads=<r> nonzero=<z> values=<v,...> busy_zero=<b>`.
|OBS-ESRC-BIW-MAP
|Logged only, no pass or fail. `architecture.adoc` (Decorrelation and BIW Mixing) states that four `gf_muladd` units with the polynomial 0x1B combine the twelve bytes, grouped in three sets of four, into one 32-bit word. It does not state which byte enters which input of which unit, or the coefficients. With the compressor in use and whitening off, each FIFO word is a BIW word (CHK-COND-PASSTHRU grades that the FIFO words equal the BIW observe words). The leaf logs the first four words of the pass-through window with the bytes of the last two strobes before each word, the clocks from the last strobe, and whether the word is among the BIW observe words read. The line does not tell which of the two strobes made the word; the clock distance is the reader's evidence. Evidence: `OBS-ESRC-BIW-MAP seed=<n> pairs=<k> word=[0x<w>,...] bytes=[0x<b>,...] bytes_prev=[0x<b>,...] lag_clk=[<c>,...] in_biw_obs=[<0\|1>,...]`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: Only the new cells are graded; the owners of the open tests keep the rest. The leaf grades the effect of the four options and of `SAMPLE_CLK_DIV` on exact counts, exact bypass packing, period ratios and equality. It does not grade an absolute byte period (the unit is blocked, see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>; logged as `OBS-COND-PERIOD-ABS`), a `DOWNSAMPLE_RATE` or `SAMPLE_CLK_DIV` write while the stream runs, a rate written while the stream is stopped, the compressor bypass turned on while the gated stream runs, or the compressor bypass set and cleared between two compressor outputs (no text states their effect; logged as `OBS-COND-DS-MIDRUN-UP`, `OBS-COND-DS-MIDRUN-DOWN`, `OBS-COND-DIV-MIDRUN-UP`, `OBS-COND-DIV-MIDRUN-DOWN`, `OBS-COND-DS-RESUME`, `OBS-COND-DS-BYPASS-ON` and `OBS-COND-BYPASS-MIDGROUP`), `SAMPLE_CLK_DIV` at its maximum (not run), the 32-bit health test with the compressor bypassed (blocked, logged only), the map from the twelve lane bytes to the BIW word (logged as `OBS-ESRC-BIW-MAP`), whether the whitener takes every BIW word while it hashes (blocked, logged only), or the whitened word values (the specification states no BIW coefficient). The default path belongs to `sep_esrc_e2e_smoke_test`. The stored values of the options and the lock belong to `sep_esrc_fips_lock_test`.

'''''

[[sep_esrc_fifo_flow_rand_test]]
===== 1.46 `sep_esrc_fifo_flow_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy (ESRC)

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense +esrc_noise_force`, 4 seeds; testlist `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`; Verilator and VCS

*Source Module*: `sep_esrc_fifo_flow_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip with the CPU-LSU master readback and writes of the ESRC registers (32-bit accesses, approved); the probes `esrc_compress_data_o` and `esrc_compress_vld_o` (approved); the noise inject `esrc_noise_ext_i` (approved); `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` (approved, the release of the TRNG reset). Added hook: the ESRC FIFO integrity inject, an error inject that forces one stored parity bit of the main FIFO entry at the read pointer, or one bit of one pointer copy of the main FIFO (CHK-FIFO-INTEG-PARITY, CHK-FIFO-INTEG-PTR, CHK-FIFO-INTEG-W1C); the test adds it to the inject exceptions of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`. Expected values come from the specification only: the FIFO depth, the pointer arithmetic, the churn rule and the stream rule.

*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the pop count k (1 to 64), the non-zero 8-bit mask M of the churn leg (0x01 to 0xFF), the number of idle register reads between two `FIFO_STATUS` samples of the fill (0 to 3), the held word count H of the integrity trials (2 to 40), the parity bit (0 to 3) and the pointer pair, pointer copy and bit of the pointer trial. `reseed = 4` samples different counts, flags, words and injection points. The plan shape is the same on every seed. The leaf logs the plan first (`INFO-PLAN seed=<n> ...`).

*Objective*: The main FIFO has 64 entries. `LEVEL` counts the valid words, and `RPTR` and `WPTR` move modulo 64. A write while full is discarded and sets `FIFO_OVERFLOW`. With `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0 writes stop and reads continue. The status bits clear by write-one (the subset clear is graded in `sep_esrc_csr_rw_walk_rand_test`). Churn XORs each incoming word with the entry 32 positions ahead of the write pointer. The stream port carries each word as written into the FIFO. A one-bit error in the stored parity of a word that is read, or a mismatch between a pointer and its inverted copy, sets `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR`, which clears by write-one. Spec: `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc` (`INTR_STATUS`, `FIFO_CTRL`, `FIFO_STATUS`, `FIFO_RDATA`); `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc` (Output FIFO: four odd-parity bits per entry, "pointer integrity checking", "parity verification on reads", "parity error, and pointer error conditions are all reported to the CSR block").

*Test Coverage*: A fill without pops. Pops with the producer stopped, down to empty and one more. The stream order after a drain and a restart. Churn on and off with a constant incoming word. The integrity trials, each from a TRNG reset: the FIFO holds a drawn number of words (2 to 40); a one-bit error in the stored parity of the word at the read pointer, then a pop of that word; and, in a separate trial, a one-bit error in one copy of a pointer pair, then one write and one pop. The error is released after the pop. Error injection: a write while full and a read while empty (point: the producer and `FIFO_RDATA`; kind: overflow and underflow; expected response: the two status bits); a one-bit parity error and a one-bit pointer-copy error (point: the ESRC FIFO integrity inject; expected response: `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` set). Extends `sep_esrc_e2e_fifo_closed_test`, which grades the set and clear of `FIFO_UNDERFLOW` on a closed FIFO; here an underflow is only the precondition of the mask clear. This test grades only the new cells; the owner of the open test keeps the underflow set and clear. The write-one clear of a proper subset of the two flags is graded in `sep_esrc_csr_rw_walk_rand_test`. Functional coverage: `sep_esrc_conditioning_cg` (`cp_fifo_occupancy`, `cp_fifo_ctrl`, `cp_churn_age`), `sep_esrc_fault_alert_cg` (`cp_fifo_flags`, `cp_fifo_integrity`), `sep_crypto_intr_cg` (`x_esrc_status_enable`, `cp_esrc_raise_path` hw bins).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Set-up. Pulse the TRNG reset: write 0 then 1 to bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N` (`TRNG_SW_RST_N`) with every other bit at the baseline of the leaf, and wait until `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` read 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). No ESRC, CSRNG or EDN access comes before this release, because the write of 0 isolates their host paths until the write of 1 (`reset_controller.adoc`, Isolation and Reset Sequencing). Write 1 to the eight defined bits of `INTR_ENABLE` (common rule "ESRC interrupt status"). Write `ALERT_THRESHOLD.THRESHOLD` = 0. Run the clean start (common rule "Clean start"), which leaves `balanced` on all twelve lanes. Wait for `MAIN_SM_STATUS.BOOT_PHASE_DONE` = 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). The reset values hold: `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 1, churn off, compressor not bypassed, whitening on. Steps 2 to 20 run in this order without a reset.
. Fill leg. From the first fill sample to the sample that reads `LEVEL` = 64, count the words on `esrc_compress_vld_o` and keep the longest gap g between two of them.
. Read `FIFO_STATUS` repeatedly, with the drawn number of idle register reads between two samples, and no pop. At each sample compare (`WPTR` - `RPTR`) mod 64 with `LEVEL` mod 64. Continue until `LEVEL` reads 64 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). Log the time of the fill.
. Read `INTR_STATUS` until `FIFO_OVERFLOW` reads 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). This is the error injection: the producer writes while the FIFO is full.
. Keep sampling `FIFO_STATUS` for as long as the fill took. Require `LEVEL` = 64 at every sample.
. Require at least two stream words in the fill (`CTRL-FIFO-STREAM-FULL`), so every window of 2 x g clocks inside the fill held a word. Count the words on `esrc_compress_vld_o` over 2 x g clocks while the FIFO is full, then read `FIFO_STATUS`. Require at least one word and `LEVEL` = 64 (CHK-FIFO-STREAM-FULL).
. Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` and require 0.
. Pop leg. Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0. Read `FIFO_STATUS`, and start counting the words on `esrc_compress_vld_o`. Pop k words (a 32-bit read of `FIFO_RDATA` each) and read `FIFO_STATUS` after each pop. If no word has passed `esrc_compress_vld_o` since the first read, read `FIFO_STATUS` until one passes (a bounded wait of 16 times the fill time per word). Read `FIFO_STATUS` once more and require `LEVEL` and `WPTR` unchanged. The stream port offers words whatever `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` is (`architecture.adoc`, Output FIFO: "the DRBG seed stream continues whatever its value"); zero offered words logs `CTRL-MISSING FIFO-POP` and fails the leaf.
. Pop until `LEVEL` reads 0. This is the error injection: then pop once more at `LEVEL` = 0. The data is undefined and is not graded.
. Read `INTR_STATUS`. If `FIFO_OVERFLOW` or `FIFO_UNDERFLOW` reads 0, log `CTRL-MISSING FIFO-FLAGS` and fail the leaf. Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` and require 0.
. Clear leg. Write 1 to both flags in `INTR_STATUS`, read them and require both to read 0 (`CHK-FIFO-FLAGS-CLEAR`; control `CTRL-FIFO-FLAGS` of step 10).
. Resume leg. Read `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` as 0. Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 1. Poll `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads above 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` and require 0.
. Order leg (churn off, compressor not bypassed, `balanced` noise). Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0. Pop until `LEVEL` reads 0.
. Arm the recording of the words on `esrc_compress_data_o` at `esrc_compress_vld_o`, then write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 1 at once. The recording starts before the write, because the write enable takes effect at the register write and the stream words that pass before the write response returns enter the FIFO.
. Poll `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads 64 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0 and keep recording until the response of that write returns. Pop 64 words. Take the smallest offset d (0 to 8) at which every popped word i equals the recorded word i + d; no such offset fails the check. Log d. Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` and require 0.
. Churn leg. Draw the non-zero 8-bit mask M (0x01 to 0xFF; `DECORRELATOR_MASK.ENTROPY_BYTE_MASK` is bits 7:0). Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0. Pop until `LEVEL` reads 0. Write `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.BYPASS` = 0xFFF, `DECORRELATOR_MASK` = M and `CTRL.BYPASS_ENTROPY_COMPRESSOR` = 1. Drive all lanes stuck1. The incoming word C is M in each of its four byte lanes.
. Wait until the last 6 words on `esrc_compress_data_o` all equal C (a bounded wait; a timeout logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf).
. Write `FIFO_CTRL` with `ENABLE` = 1 and `ENTROPY_CHURN_ENABLE` = 1. Poll `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads 64 (a bounded wait). Write `FIFO_CTRL` with `ENABLE` = 0 and `ENTROPY_CHURN_ENABLE` = 1.
