Pop 64 words w_0 to w_63 in write order. For every k from 32 to 63 compare w_k XOR w_(k-32) with C. The words w_0 to w_31 are not graded.
. Control. Pop until `LEVEL` reads 0. Write `FIFO_CTRL` with `ENABLE` = 1 and `ENTROPY_CHURN_ENABLE` = 0. Poll `LEVEL` until it reads 64. Write `FIFO_CTRL` with `ENABLE` = 0 and churn off. Pop 64 words and compare each with C.
. Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` and require 0. Write 1 to `INTR_TEST.FIFO_ERROR`. Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` and require 1. Write 1 to `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` and read it as 0.
. Parity trial. Run the set-up (it pulses the TRNG reset). Poll `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads at least H. Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0. Read `LEVEL` as the held count.
. Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` and require 0. Pop one word and read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` again and require 0.
. Force one drawn bit of the stored parity of the entry at the read pointer. Pop that word. Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` and require 1. Release the force.
. Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` as 1. Write 1 to it. Read it as 0.
. Pointer trial. Run the set-up (it pulses the TRNG reset). Poll `FIFO_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads at least H. Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0. Read `LEVEL` as the held count.
. Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` and require 0. Pop one word and log its clock count as the clean pop time. Read `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` again and require 0.
. Force one drawn bit of one drawn copy of the drawn pointer pair. Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 1 until at least one word passes `esrc_compress_vld_o` (a bounded wait), then write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0. Pop one word.
. Poll `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` for a bound of 16 times the clean pop time (the leaf logs the bound and the clock count of the first read of 1). Release the force. Write 1 to `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR`, read it, and log the value as `OBS-FIFO-PTR-W1C`. It is not graded: the released force can leave the forced value in the pointer copy, and the specification does not state whether the mismatch then persists.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-FIFO-FILL
|Without a pop `LEVEL` rises to 64 and stays, `FIFO_OVERFLOW` is 1, and (`WPTR` - `RPTR`) mod 64 equals `LEVEL` mod 64 at every sample (`entropy_source.adoc`, `FIFO_STATUS`, `INTR_STATUS`; `architecture.adoc`, Output FIFO: 64 entries). A DUT with a shorter FIFO, a pointer that does not wrap at 64, or a missing overflow flag fails. The overflow comes from a write while full, so the check reads the flag only after `LEVEL` is 64. Anchor: `FIFO_STATUS` and `INTR_STATUS` readback. Control: the first sample of the fill reads `LEVEL` below 64, so `LEVEL` rises before it plateaus (`CTRL-FIFO-FILL seed=<n> level_first=<l>`). Evidence: `CHK-FIFO-FILL PASS seed=<n> level_max=64 overflow=1 wptr=<w> rptr=<r> ptr_diff_ok=1 plateau_bad=0 samples=<k> fill_clk=<t>`.
|CHK-FIFO-STREAM-FULL
|With `LEVEL` held at 64, the stream port carries at least one word in a window of 2 x g clocks, g the longest gap between stream words of the fill, and `LEVEL` reads 64 after the window (`hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/entropy_source.rdl:269`: "Words offered to the FIFO are also the DRBG seed stream, which this field does not gate"; `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc:177-178`: "The same words leave on `entropy_stream_data_o` whatever the FIFO does."). A DUT that gates `entropy_stream_vld_o` on a full FIFO shows no word and fails. Anchor: `esrc_compress_vld_o` and the `FIFO_STATUS` readback. Control: the fill offered at least two stream words, so g is measured and every window of 2 x g clocks in the fill held a word (`CTRL-FIFO-STREAM-FULL seed=<n> fill_words=<w> fill_max_gap_clk=<g>`). Evidence: `CHK-FIFO-STREAM-FULL PASS seed=<n> full_window_words=<a> window_clk=<c> fill_max_gap_clk=<g> level_after=64`.
|CHK-FIFO-POP
|With the producer stopped each pop lowers `LEVEL` by 1, raises `RPTR` by 1 modulo 64 and leaves `WPTR` unchanged (`entropy_source.adoc`, `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE`: reads remain available while writes are disabled; `FIFO_RDATA`; `FIFO_STATUS`). A DUT that keeps writing, pops two words or moves `WPTR` fails. The stopped producer makes the level change the effect of the pop alone. Anchor: `FIFO_STATUS` readback after each pop and once more after at least one word is offered on the stream port. Control: at least one stream word is offered between the first `FIFO_STATUS` read and the last one, so a DUT that ignores `ENABLE` = 0 has a word to write (`CTRL-FIFO-POP seed=<n> offered=<o> wait_bound=<b>`). Evidence: `CHK-FIFO-POP PASS seed=<n> pops=<k> level_start=<l0> level_end=<l1> rptr_delta=<k> wptr_delta=0 bad_pops=0`.
|CHK-FIFO-RESUME
|After `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 1, `LEVEL` rises again from 0 (`entropy_source.adoc`, `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE`: enables FIFO writes). A DUT that stays closed fails the bounded wait. Anchor: `FIFO_STATUS` readback. Control: `LEVEL` reads 0 before the write. Evidence: `CHK-FIFO-RESUME PASS seed=<n> enable=1 level_rises=1 level=<l> clk=<t>`.
|CHK-FIFO-ORDER
|After a drain and a restart, the 64 popped words equal 64 consecutive words of the stream port recording, in order, at one offset d from 0 to 8 (`architecture.adoc`, Output FIFO: the stream port carries each word as written into the FIFO). A DUT that reorders words, drops a word or pops a different word than it wrote fails, because all 64 words must match at the same d. The recording starts before the enabling write and the offset d absorbs the stream words that enter the FIFO before the write response returns, as in `CHK-COND-PASSTHRU`. A compliant DUT matches at its true offset, so the outcome does not depend on the word values. A popped sequence that matches at no offset from 0 to 8 fails. Anchor: `esrc_compress_data_o` at `esrc_compress_vld_o` and `FIFO_RDATA`. Evidence: `CHK-FIFO-ORDER PASS seed=<n> words=64 offset=<d> equal_order=1 recorded=<r>`.
|CHK-FIFO-CHURN
|With churn on and a constant incoming word C, for each k from 32 to 63 w_k XOR w_(k-32) equals C (`entropy_source.adoc`, `FIFO_CTRL.ENTROPY_CHURN_ENABLE`: XORs each incoming word with the entry 32 positions ahead of the write pointer). The entry 32 ahead of write k is the stored word of write k - 32, so a DUT without churn gives 0 and a DUT with another distance gives a value other than C. The words w_0 to w_31 depend on stale entries and are not graded; their values XOR C are logged (`OBS-FIFO-CHURN-FIRST32`). C is non-zero, because M is a non-zero 8-bit mask. Anchor: `FIFO_RDATA`. Control: with churn off every popped word equals C. Evidence: `CHK-FIFO-CHURN PASS seed=<n> churn=1 pairs=<m> c=0x<c> mismatches=0 ctrl_churn_off_words_equal_c=1`.
|CHK-FIFO-NOERR
|After every fill, pop, restart and churn leg `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` reads 0 (`entropy_source.adoc`, `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR`: set only by an integrity error). A DUT that raises a spurious integrity error fails. This is a "never" check. Anchor: `INTR_STATUS` readback at the end of each leg. Control: a write of 1 to `INTR_TEST.FIFO_ERROR` in the same seed sets the bit and a write of 1 to `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` clears it, so the bit is observable (`CTRL-FIFO-NOERR seed=<n> intr_test_sets=1 w1c=0 legs=<k>`). Evidence: one line per leg, `CHK-FIFO-NOERR PASS seed=<n> leg=<fill\|pop\|resume\|order\|churn> fifo_error=0`.
|CHK-FIFO-INTEG-PARITY
|A pop of a word with a one-bit parity error sets `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR`; a pop of an intact word of the same trial does not (`architecture.adoc`, Output FIFO: "parity verification on reads"). A DUT that does not check parity on reads leaves the bit at 0, and a DUT that flags an intact word sets it at the first pop. Anchor: `INTR_STATUS` readback. Control: the pop of the corrupted word in the same trial sets the bit, so the 0 after the intact pop is a read of an observable bit. Evidence: `CHK-FIFO-INTEG-PARITY PASS seed=<n> held=<n> parity_bit=<b> pre_error=0 intact_pop_error=0 parity_pop_error=1`.
|CHK-FIFO-INTEG-PTR
|A one-bit error in one copy of a pointer pair sets `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` within a bound that is a multiple of the clean pop time (`architecture.adoc`, Output FIFO: "pointer integrity checking", normal and inverted pointer pairs with mismatch detection). The text does not state the time of the detection, so the bound is 16 times the clean pop time of the same seed and the leaf logs both. A DUT without pointer checking leaves the bit at 0. Anchor: `INTR_STATUS` readback. Control: the pops before the error leave the bit 0, so the rise comes from the pointer error. Evidence: `CHK-FIFO-INTEG-PTR PASS seed=<n> pointer=<rptr or wptr> copy=<c> bit=<b> held=<h> detect_clk=<d> clean_pop_clk=<c> bound=<16c> fifo_error=1 ctrl_pre_error=0 word_passed=1`.
|CHK-FIFO-INTEG-W1C
|In the parity trial, a write of 1 to `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR` after the error is released clears it (`entropy_source.adoc`, `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_ERROR`: write one to clear). The corrupted entry is popped and the FIFO is closed, so no read of that entry follows and no fault remains. The clear of the pointer trial is logged as `OBS-FIFO-PTR-W1C` and not graded. A DUT that holds the bit, or that sets it again with no fault, fails. Anchor: `INTR_STATUS` readback. Control: the bit reads 1 before the write. Evidence: `CHK-FIFO-INTEG-W1C PASS seed=<n> before=1 after=0`.
|CHK-FIFO-FLAGS-CLEAR
|After a write of 1 to `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_OVERFLOW` and `INTR_STATUS.FIFO_UNDERFLOW`, both read 0. The RDL states "Write one to clear." for each (`hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/entropy_source.rdl:129` for `FIFO_OVERFLOW`, `:136` for `FIFO_UNDERFLOW`). A DUT that ignores the write keeps a flag at 1 and fails. Anchor: `INTR_STATUS` readback. Control (`CTRL-FIFO-FLAGS`): both flags read 1 before the clear; a read of 0 logs `CTRL-MISSING FIFO-FLAGS` and fails the leaf. Evidence: `CHK-FIFO-FLAGS-CLEAR PASS seed=<n> overflow=0 underflow=0`.
|OBS-FIFO-CHURN-FIRST32
|Not a graded check. The churn rule names no partner for the first 32 words written with churn on. The leaf logs the constant C and each of those words XOR C. Evidence: `OBS-FIFO-CHURN-FIRST32 seed=<n> c=0x<c> words_xor_c=0x<w0>,...,0x<w31>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: Only the new cells are graded; the owner of the open test keeps the rest. Not graded: the integrity error of the observe FIFOs (not stated), the effect of `FIFO_ERROR` on the data path, and the churn words w_0 to w_31 (they depend on stale entries). The set and clear of `FIFO_UNDERFLOW` on a closed FIFO belongs to `sep_esrc_e2e_fifo_closed_test`. The conditioning options that make the constant word C belong to `sep_esrc_conditioning_matrix_rand_test`.

'''''

[[sep_esrc_observe_fifo_rand_test]]
===== 1.47 `sep_esrc_observe_fifo_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy (ESRC)

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense +esrc_noise_force`, 4 seeds; testlist `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`; Verilator and VCS

*Source Module*: `sep_esrc_observe_fifo_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip with the CPU-LSU master readback and writes of the ESRC registers (32-bit accesses, approved); the noise inject `esrc_noise_ext_i` (approved); the probe `esrc_compress_vld_o` (approved); `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` (approved, the release of the TRNG reset). Added hook: the ESRC noise-source stuck-at inject, an error inject that forces the output of the noise ring oscillator of each lane to a constant 0 or 1 (a stuck noise source). The constant reaches the pre-decorrelator samples that `NOISE_OBS_RDATA` returns (`entropy_source.adoc`, `NOISE_OBS_RDATA`); `esrc_noise_ext_i` forces only the decorrelator input, which the noise observe FIFO does not sample. The test adds the inject to the inject exceptions of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`. The sample-clock sequence leg drives the stuck-at inject with one bit per sample-clock edge and the external sample clock `entropy_rosc_sample_clk_i` (a restartable bench clock), and reads the synchronizer output of the lane (`esrc_noise_bit_o[lane]`, the net `u_generator.noise_bit`, an approved observation tap). No PROPOSED item. Expected values come from the specification only: the selected lane and its constant noise value, the packing rule of an observe word, the sample sequence and the synchronizer depth, and the rules of flush, overflow and lock.

*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the order of the 16 `LANE_SEL` values (constraint filter: the first value and the value before each of 0 and 12 to 15 come from 1 to 11, so every write changes the effective lane), the value z (0 or 1) that the effective lane carries at each lane change (both values among the lane changes of a seed), the lane a of the flush leg (0 to 11) and its value z, the number f of words buffered before the `FLUSH` (4 to 8), the number k of words buffered at the lock moment (1 to 4), the value z of each of the four lock passes, the lane of the sample-clock sequence leg (0 to 11) and its 160 sequence bits (5 words; bit 8 of each word is the complement of bit 7 and the last bit is the complement of the one before, so a lost or repeated sample changes a graded bit or the word count), and the 64 bits of the fast-clock sequence. `reseed = 4` samples different orders, values, moments, lanes and sequences. The plan shape is the same on every seed. The leaf logs the plan first (`INFO-PLAN seed=<n> ...`). The lane-0 `LANE_SEL` value of the same-lane write (12 to 15) and the 66-word sample sequence of the overflow step are drawn after every other value.

*Objective*: With `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` the samples of the selected lane pack LSB-first into 32-bit observe words, and each word holds 32 pre-decorrelator samples of the selected lane. `LANE_SEL` 0 to 11 selects the lane, and other values select lane 0. Changing lanes restarts packing and discards the buffered words, and a write of `FLUSH` discards the buffered words. The noise observe FIFO sees the stream before the boot gate. A word offered while the FIFO is full is discarded and sets `NOISE_OBS_OVERFLOW`. The BIW observe FIFO captures after startup completes and sets `BIW_OBS_OVERFLOW` the same way. Both `RAW_ENABLE` bits are held by `FIPS_LOCK`. The noise source of a lane is sampled by a flop on its sampling clock and then synchronized to `clk_i` by a two-stage synchronizer, so with a sample-clock divide of 1 each sample-clock edge gives one sample, in order, and the value that the sampling flop captures appears at the synchronizer output on the 2nd `clk_i` rising edge after the capture. Spec: `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/gen/adoc/entropy_source.adoc` (`NOISE_OBS_CTRL`, `NOISE_OBS_STATUS`, `NOISE_OBS_RDATA`, `BIW_OBS_CTRL`, `BIW_OBS_STATUS`, `BIW_OBS_RDATA`, `INTR_STATUS`, `FIPS_LOCK`); `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc` (Entropy Source, Debug Observation Pin).

*Test Coverage*: Constant lane noise in which the effective lane carries z and every other lane carries the opposite value. A failing startup (stuck0 with BYPASS set on all lanes, `ALERT_THRESHOLD.THRESHOLD` = 0) with both observe paths on. On every seed all 16 `LANE_SEL` values in drawn order. The drawn order makes every write change the effective lane, so each value, 12 to 15 included, is graded on every seed. The first word after each lane change is graded for the packing restart, and the next seven words for the lane value. A write that keeps the effective lane (0 and 12 to 15 all select lane 0) is driven once and logged only (`OBS-LANESEL-SAME`), because whether it flushes is blocked (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). A `FLUSH` write after the lane noise changes. Overflow of the noise FIFO and of the BIW FIFO after startup; which word a full noise FIFO keeps is logged only (`OBS-OVF-KEEP`). A main-chain leg that grades the stream word count over 256 decorrelator strobes with the observe paths on against the count with them off, with SHA-256 conditioning off. A sample-clock sequence leg on one lane: the lane divides its sample clock by 1, the leaf restarts the sample clock at a period that is not a multiple of the `clk_i` period (8 x `clk_i` + 2 ps), drives one seeded sequence bit per sample edge, and grades the words that `NOISE_OBS_RDATA` returns and the cycle on which each captured value reaches the synchronizer output. A second window at 2 x `clk_i` + 2 ps is logged only. Lock legs for each `RAW_ENABLE` field on every seed: locked at 1 and locked at 0. The BIW observe FIFO has no `FLUSH`, so the flush after the lock runs on the noise FIFO only. Error injection: a full observe FIFO (point: no read of the observe FIFO; kind: overflow; expected response: the overflow bit); a stuck noise source on every lane (point: the ESRC noise-source stuck-at inject; kind: stuck-at 0 or 1 per lane; expected response: noise observe words of the stuck value of the selected lane). Extends `sep_esrc_fips_lock_test`, which grades that both `RAW_ENABLE` fields are held by the lock and that `LANE_SEL` stays writable under the lock (`CHK-OBS-LANE-SEL`). This test adds capture under the lock, `LEVEL` staying 0 when locked at 0, and `FLUSH` after the lock on the noise FIFO, and grades only those cells; the owner of the open test keeps the held fields and the writable `LANE_SEL`, which this test reads only as controls. Functional coverage: `sep_esrc_conditioning_cg` (`x_obs_capture`, `cp_obs_lane_sel`, `cp_obs_flush`, `cp_obs_overflow`, `cp_obs_lock`), `sep_crypto_reg_lock_cg` (`x_fips_field`), `sep_crypto_intr_cg` (`x_esrc_status_enable`, `cp_esrc_raise_path` hw bins).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Set-up, run at the start of each leg and of each trial named in a leg. Pulse the TRNG reset: write 0 then 1 to bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N` (`TRNG_SW_RST_N`) with every other bit at the baseline of the leaf, and wait until `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` read 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). No ESRC, CSRNG or EDN access comes before this release, because the write of 0 isolates their host paths until the write of 1 (`reset_controller.adoc`, Isolation and Reset Sequencing). The reset returns both `RAW_ENABLE` fields to 0 and `RING_OSC_CTRL.SAMPLE_CLK_SELECT` to 0xFFF (the shared internal sample clock). Select the external sample clock on every lane: write `RING_OSC_ENABLE.ENABLE` = 0, then `RING_OSC_CTRL.SAMPLE_CLK_SELECT` = 0, then `RING_OSC_ENABLE.ENABLE` = 0xFFF (`entropy_source.adoc`, `RING_OSC_CTRL`: a clear bit selects the external sample clock; `architecture.adoc`, Noise Generation: software changes the source of a lane only while its generator is disabled). The bench drives the external sample clock `entropy_rosc_sample_clk_i` free-running in both simulators, and the internal ring oscillators do not toggle under Verilator (`hw/sys/sep/dv/tb/tb_top.sv`), so this selection lets the stuck-at values reach the pre-decorrelator samples of the noise observe FIFO in both simulators. Write 1 to the eight defined bits of `INTR_ENABLE` (common rule "ESRC interrupt status"). Write `ALERT_THRESHOLD.THRESHOLD` = 0. Run the clean start (common rule "Clean start"), which leaves `balanced` on all twelve lanes. Wait for `MAIN_SM_STATUS.BOOT_PHASE_DONE` = 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). The legs run in the order below. Every lane value z or its opposite that a later step drives is applied through the ESRC noise-source stuck-at inject and, with the same value, through `esrc_noise_ext_i`; the inject is released at each set-up and after the leaf. The clean start and the stuck0 drive of the failing trial use `esrc_noise_ext_i` only. The first observe wait of a kind is bounded by an outer cap of 20 times the words it waits for times 32 samples at a sample clock period of at most 128 `clk_i` (a cap only, not a contract); a later wait of the same kind is bounded by 16 times the clean time of that first wait in the same seed (`INFO-CLEAN-TIME`). The leaf logs each bound.
. Ungated leg, clean trial. Run the set-up with `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1 and `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1 among the writes of the clean start. Poll `BIW_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads above 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout logs `CTRL-MISSING OBS-UNGATED-CLEAN` and fails the leaf). Log the clock count from `BOOT_PHASE_DONE` = 1 to that read as t_clean; the leaf uses the larger of t_clean and one strobe period. Write both `RAW_ENABLE` fields to 0.
. Ungated leg, failing trial. Run the set-up up to the `ALERT_THRESHOLD` write, with no clean start. Write `DECORRELATOR_CTRL.BYPASS` = 0xFFF. Drive stuck0 on all lanes and wait for two strobe words; the noise driver compares the decorrelator bytes with the driven value (`CTRL-NOISE-BYTES`). Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1 and `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1.
. Poll `NOISE_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads above 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). Wait for 4 times t_clean (the leaf logs both). Read `MAIN_SM_STATUS.BOOT_PHASE_DONE`, `NOISE_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` and `BIW_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL`. Write both `RAW_ENABLE` fields to 0.
. Lane leg. Run the set-up. Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1 with `LANE_SEL` = 0. For each of the 16 `LANE_SEL` values v in drawn order, run steps 6 to 10. The effective lane e(v) is v for v from 0 to 11 and lane 0 for v from 12 to 15.
. Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 0 with the current `LANE_SEL`, so that no new word arrives, and pop `NOISE_OBS_RDATA` until `NOISE_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` reads 0 (a bounded number of pops: at most the FIFO depth; more fails the leaf). e(v) differs from the effective lane of the current `LANE_SEL` (the drawn order makes it so). Drive lane e(v) with the next value z and every other lane with the opposite value. Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1 with the current `LANE_SEL`.
. Poll `NOISE_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads at least 4 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). Read `LEVEL` as the level before the write.
. Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.LANE_SEL` = v (with `RAW_ENABLE` = 1). Read `LEVEL` at once as the level after the write.
. Poll `LEVEL` until it reads at least 8 (a bounded wait; the leaf logs the wait). Pop 8 words.
. Compare the first popped word with 0xFFFFFFFF if z is 1 and with 0x00000000 if z is 0 (CHK-OBS-PACK). Compare words 2 to 8 with the same value (CHK-OBS-LANE). Compare the level after the write with the level before. Count the popped words that equal the value of the old lane (CHK-OBS-FLUSH). Last in the lane leg, with no check on it: pop the noise FIFO empty and drive lane 0 with 0. Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1 with `LANE_SEL` = 0, poll `LEVEL` until it reads at least 4 (a bounded wait) and read it. Write `LANE_SEL` = the drawn value 12 to 15, which keeps lane 0, and read `LEVEL` at once. Write `RAW_ENABLE` = 0 and pop the FIFO empty. Log the two levels (`OBS-LANESEL-SAME`).
. Flush leg. Run the set-up. Drive lane a with z and every other lane with the opposite value. Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1 with `LANE_SEL` = a. Poll `NOISE_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` until it reads at least f.
. Drive lane a with the opposite value of z and every other lane with z. Poll `LEVEL` until it has risen by at least 2 words since the change. Stop `entropy_rosc_sample_clk_i` and wait 16 `clk_i` cycles, so the last sample reaches the FIFO. Read `LEVEL` as the level before the flush. Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.FLUSH` = 1 (with `RAW_ENABLE` = 1 and `LANE_SEL` = a).
. Read `LEVEL` at once as the level after the flush, then restart the sample clock at its bench period. Poll `LEVEL` until it reads at least 2, read it as m, pop m words and compare each with the new value of lane a. Write `RAW_ENABLE` = 0.
. Overflow leg. Run the set-up. Drive lane 0 with 0 and every other lane with 1 (the word values are not graded). Read `INTR_STATUS` and require both overflow bits 0 (a control). Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1 and `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1. Pop nothing. Poll `INTR_STATUS` until `NOISE_OBS_OVERFLOW` reads 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the check). Read `NOISE_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` as the depth D of the noise FIFO. Log the fill time. Do the same for `BIW_OBS_OVERFLOW` and `BIW_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` as the depth of the BIW FIFO.
. Wait for twice the longer of the two fill times. Read both levels and compare each with its depth.
. Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 0 and `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 0, so that no new word arrives and neither FIFO overflows again. For the noise FIFO and then the BIW FIFO: pop until its `LEVEL` reads 0 (the values are not graded); read `INTR_STATUS` and require its overflow bit 1 (a control: the drain does not clear the bit); write 1 to the overflow bit and read it as 0.
. Main-chain leg. Run the set-up with `CTRL.SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE` = 0 in the clean start, so each BIW word passes straight to the FIFO stream (`entropy_source.rdl`, `CTRL.SHA256_WHITENING_ENABLE`). Write `FIFO_CTRL.ENABLE` = 0 (the stream is not gated by it). Keep both `RAW_ENABLE` fields at 0.
. A window starts on the clock after a strobe of `esrc_decor_valid_o` and ends on the 256th strobe after it, inclusive; the leaf counts the `esrc_compress_vld_o` words in it (a bounded wait of 2 x 257 strobe periods; a timeout fails the check). Count two windows with the observe paths off.
. Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1 and `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1. Pop nothing. Count one window. Read both observe `LEVEL` fields. Write both `RAW_ENABLE` fields to 0 (CHK-OBS-MAIN).
. Capture-off leg. Run the set-up. Read both `RAW_ENABLE` fields and both `LEVEL` fields as 0 (`CTRL-OBS-OFF`). Open the graded window. Hold for 256 strobes (eight observe words of 32 samples; a bounded wait, a timeout fails the check) and at least 4 times the longer of the two first-word times of the same seed: t_noise, the clocks from the `RAW_ENABLE` write to the first noise word in the failing ungated trial, and t_biw, the clocks to the first BIW word in the clean ungated trial (the leaf logs both and the hold). Read both `LEVEL` fields as 0 (CHK-OBS-OFF). Close the graded window.
. Sample-clock sequence leg. Run the set-up. Write `GENERATOR_<lane>_SAMPLE_CLK_CONFIG` = 0 (divide by 1) and read it back, and read `RING_OSC_CTRL.SAMPLE_CLK_SELECT` for the lane (a clear bit selects the external clock; `CTRL-OBS-SEQ`). Stop `entropy_rosc_sample_clk_i`, write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` = 1 with the lane, and restart the clock at 8 x `clk_i` + 2 ps with its first edge at least 150 ps from every `clk_i` edge, so that the sample edges drift across the `clk_i` period by 2 ps per edge and each lies 150 ps or more from every `clk_i` edge (measured, `CTRL-SYNC-DEPTH`). Sample edge k takes sequence bit k: the leaf writes bit k to the stuck-at inject after edge k - 1, and the inject takes it at the next `clk_i` falling edge, before edge k. Count `clk_i` rising edges in the leaf. At each sample edge k record the value of `esrc_noise_bit_o[lane]` at the 1st and at the 2nd `clk_i` rising edge after the edge. Stop the sample clock after the last edge. Read `NOISE_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` and pop the words (CHK-OBS-SEQ, CHK-SYNC-DEPTH). Then repeat at 2 x `clk_i` + 2 ps with 64 seeded bits, align the captured bits with the driven bits, and log the lost and repeated bits (`OBS-FAST`). Restart the sample clock at its bench period and release the inject. After the fast window, write 1 to the noise overflow bit of `INTR_STATUS`, run one more window at 8 x `clk_i` + 2 ps with the drawn 66-word sequence (the FIFO depth bound plus 2 words), and read the overflow bit, `LEVEL` and the popped words. Clear the overflow bit and log the bit index in the sequence of the first and of the last popped word (`OBS-OVF-KEEP`).
. Lock leg, for each of the two FIFOs (noise and BIW), first locked at 1, then locked at 0, each pass with its own drawn z. Locked at 1: run the set-up. Drive lane 0 with z and every other lane with the opposite value. Write the `RAW_ENABLE` of the FIFO = 1 and read it back as 1. Poll `LEVEL` until it reads at least 2 and log the clock count from the `RAW_ENABLE` write as t_cap. The second word needs one whole word period after the first, so t_cap covers at least one word period whatever the phase of the first capture.
. Poll `LEVEL` until it reads at least k. Write `FIPS_LOCK.LOCK` = 1 (`HEALTH_TEST_WINDOW_SIZE.SIZE` stays at its reset value, which is above the minimum of 1024 that the lock requires) and read the lock as 1. Read `LEVEL` as L (the words held at the lock). The lock holds `RAW_ENABLE` at 1, so capture cannot be stopped for a drain.
. Pop L + 2 words. Before each pop, poll `LEVEL` until it reads above 0 (a bounded wait of 16 times t_cap; a timeout fails the check). The FIFO holds only L words at the lock, so the last 2 pops need words captured after the lock. For the noise FIFO compare the last 2 popped words with 0xFFFFFFFF if z is 1 and with 0x00000000 if z is 0. For the BIW FIFO compare no value: the L + 2 pops are the check.
. For the noise FIFO only: poll `LEVEL` until it reads at least 4 (a bounded wait of 16 times t_cap), so the FIFO holds words of z. Drive lane 0 with 1 - z and every other lane with z. Poll `LEVEL` until it has risen by at least 2 words since the change. Stop `entropy_rosc_sample_clk_i` and wait 16 `clk_i` cycles. Read `LEVEL` as the level before the flush. Write `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.FLUSH` = 1 (with `RAW_ENABLE` = 1 and `LANE_SEL` = 0). Read `LEVEL` at once as the level after the flush, then restart the sample clock at its bench period. Poll `LEVEL` until it reads at least 2, read it as m, pop m words and compare each with the word of 1 - z (0xFFFFFFFF or 0x00000000). Each popped word completed after the flush and after the second word of the change, so it holds samples of 1 - z only; a word of z or a mixed word is a word that the flush kept.
. Locked at 0: run the set-up. Drive lane 0 with z and every other lane with the opposite value. Read the `RAW_ENABLE` of the FIFO as 0. Write `FIPS_LOCK.LOCK` = 1 and read it as 1. Write the `RAW_ENABLE` of the FIFO = 1 and read it back as 0 (a control).
. Wait for 4 times t_cap of the locked-at-1 pass of the same FIFO (the leaf logs both). Read `LEVEL` as 0.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-OBS-UNGATED
|With a failing startup (`BOOT_PHASE_DONE` = 0) the noise observe `LEVEL` is above 0 (`architecture.adoc`, Entropy Source: the noise observe FIFO sees the stream ungated). A DUT that gates the noise path shows noise `LEVEL` 0. Anchor: `NOISE_OBS_STATUS`, `BIW_OBS_STATUS` and `MAIN_SM_STATUS` readback. Control: the BIW observe `LEVEL` of the same trial reads 0, so the trial runs before the boot gate opens, or the leaf logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails; the clean trial fills the BIW FIFO (`CTRL-OBS-UNGATED-CLEAN`), and the stuck0 bytes reach the decorrelator (`CTRL-NOISE-BYTES`). The BIW observe gate itself is graded by `sep_esrc_startup_gate_rand_test` (CHK-BOOT-BAD) and is a control here. Evidence: `CHK-OBS-UNGATED PASS seed=<n> boot_done=0 noise_obs_level=<n> ctrl_biw_obs_level=0 t_clean_clk=<t> wait_clk=<w>`.
|CHK-OBS-PACK
|The first word popped after each write of `LANE_SEL` that changes the effective lane equals the value of the new lane in all 32 bits: 0xFFFFFFFF when the lane carries 1 and 0x00000000 when it carries 0 (`entropy_source.adoc`, `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.LANE_SEL`: changing lanes restarts packing and flushes buffered words; `NOISE_OBS_RDATA`: 32 LSB-first pre-decorrelator samples from the selected lane). A packer that keeps a sample of the old lane in the first word after the restart fails. The old lane carries the opposite value, so a stale sample shows as a wrong bit. Anchor: `NOISE_OBS_RDATA` readback. Evidence: `CHK-OBS-PACK PASS seed=<n> lane_sel=<s> lane=<l> z=<b> first=0x<f> expect=0x<e>`, one line per lane change.
|CHK-OBS-LANE
|After a write of `LANE_SEL` that changes the effective lane, `LEVEL` falls below its value before the write, and popped words 2 to 8 are all ones or all zeros as the selected lane noise is 1 or 0 (`entropy_source.adoc`, `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.LANE_SEL`: values 0 to 11 select the lane, other values select lane 0, changing lanes flushes; `NOISE_OBS_RDATA`: LSB-first samples of the selected lane). The effective lane carries z and every other lane carries the opposite value, both applied at the ring-oscillator output by the ESRC noise-source stuck-at inject, upstream of the pre-decorrelator samples, so a word from a wrong lane has the other value and fails. A DUT that maps a value from 12 to 15 to a lane other than lane 0 fails the same way. All 16 values run on every seed. The first word after the change is graded by CHK-OBS-PACK. Control: `LEVEL` reads at least 4 before each change and the effective lane changes, so the fall is visible (`CTRL-OBS-LANE`). Anchor: `NOISE_OBS_STATUS` and `NOISE_OBS_RDATA` readback. Evidence: `CHK-OBS-LANE PASS seed=<n> lane_sel=<s> lane=<l> z=<b> words=7 expect=0x<e> mismatches=0 level_before=<b> level_after_change=<c> popped=[...]`.
|CHK-OBS-FLUSH
|After a `FLUSH` write or an effective lane change, no word from before it is popped (`entropy_source.adoc`, `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.FLUSH`: write one to discard buffered words; `LANE_SEL`: changing lanes flushes). In the flush leg the buffered old words carry the old value and the new words carry the opposite value. The sample clock is stopped across the write, so no word completes between the write and the next read: `LEVEL` reads 0 after the write, and every popped word equals the new value. A DUT that keeps any buffered word, old or new, fails. In the lane leg the capture runs, so `LEVEL` falls below its value before the write. In the lane leg no popped word of the 8 equals the value of the old lane. Control: the noise value changes before the flush, so old and new words differ (`CTRL-OBS-FLUSH`). Anchor: `NOISE_OBS_RDATA`, and `LEVEL` 0 after the flush (flush leg) or below its value before the change (lane leg). Evidence: `CHK-OBS-FLUSH PASS seed=<n> via=flush lane=<a> level_before=<b> level_after=0 old_words_after=0 not_new_words=0 words=<k>`, and one `CHK-OBS-FLUSH PASS seed=<n> via=lane lane_sel=<s> old_words_after=0 words=8 old=0x<o>` line per lane change.
|CHK-OBS-OVERFLOW
|Without pops `LEVEL` plateaus at the depth D = 64, the overflow bit is 1, `LEVEL` stays D after twice the fill time, and a write of 1 clears the bit once capture is off and the FIFO is drained; the BIW FIFO shows the same plateau and bit after startup (`entropy_source.adoc`, `INTR_STATUS.NOISE_OBS_OVERFLOW` and `BIW_OBS_OVERFLOW`: a word is discarded while full; write one to clear). D is the `LEVEL` at the first read of the overflow bit, and it must be 64: the observe FIFOs use the same FIFO as the output FIFO (`hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc:189`), a 64-entry FIFO (`:146`). A DUT with no overflow bit, one that overwrites and keeps rising, or one with a shallower or deeper observe FIFO fails. The leaf grades the plateau and the bit and not which word is discarded. Anchor: `NOISE_OBS_STATUS`, `BIW_OBS_STATUS` and `INTR_STATUS` readback. Control: both overflow bits read 0 before the fill (`CTRL-OBS-OVERFLOW overflow_before=0`), and each bit reads 1 after its drain and before its write-one (`CTRL-OBS-OVERFLOW overflow_before_w1c=1`), so the clear comes from the write. Evidence: `CHK-OBS-OVERFLOW PASS seed=<n> fifo=<noise or biw> depth=64 expect_depth=64 overflow=1 level_after_two_fills=<D> w1c=0 fill_clk=<t> wait_clk=<w>`.
|CHK-OBS-SEQ
|With a seeded bit sequence on one lane's noise source, one bit per sample-clock edge, `NOISE_OBS_STATUS.LEVEL` is 5 after the last edge and word w of `NOISE_OBS_RDATA` equals sequence bits 32w to 32w + 31, LSB first, in order, with no sample lost or repeated (`entropy_source.adoc`, `NOISE_OBS_RDATA`: 32 LSB-first pre-decorrelator samples from the selected lane; `architecture.adoc`, Noise Generation: one sample per edge of the sampling clock). A DUT that drops or repeats a sample, packs MSB first, or takes the samples from another lane fails a word. Anchor: `NOISE_OBS_STATUS` and `NOISE_OBS_RDATA` readback against the driven sequence. Control (`CTRL-OBS-SEQ`): the divide is 0 and the external clock is selected on the lane, `LEVEL` is 0 and `RAW_ENABLE` is 1 before the first edge, and the synchronized bit of the lane holds the value of the preamble. Evidence: `CHK-OBS-SEQ PASS lane=<l> period_ps=<p> edges=160 level=5 expect_level=5 bad_words=[] words=[...] expect=[...]`.
|CHK-SYNC-DEPTH
|A value that the sampling flop captures at sample edge k reaches the synchronizer output `esrc_noise_bit_o[lane]` at the 2nd `clk_i` rising edge after the edge, and where bit k differs from bit k - 1 the output still holds bit k - 1 at the 1st (`architecture.adoc`, Noise Generation: "a two-stage synchronizer"). The sequence has transitions, and the check requires every sample edge to be seen. A wire in place of the synchronizer shows the new value at the 1st edge, and a third stage shows the old value at the 2nd. Anchor: the synchronizer output tap, counted on `clk_i` rising edges in the leaf. Control (`CTRL-SYNC-DEPTH`): every sample edge lies at least 150 ps from every `clk_i` rising and falling edge (measured), so no sample edge is a coincident or marginal case. The check makes no claim about metastability. Evidence: `CHK-SYNC-DEPTH PASS lane=<l> sample_edges=160 expect_edges=160 transitions=<t> expect_transitions=<t> changed_at_edge1=0 wrong_at_edge2=0`.
|CHK-OBS-MAIN
|With SHA-256 conditioning off, the `esrc_compress_vld_o` word count over a window of 256 decorrelator strobes with both observe paths on equals the count over the same window length with both off (`hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/entropy_source.rdl:1027`, `NOISE_OBS_CTRL`: "Copy-only: the main datapath is unaffected"; `:975-977`, `BIW_OBS_CTRL`: "without disturbing the main datapath"). A DUT whose observe capture stalls, drops or adds main-chain words fails. Every window spans the same number of whole strobe periods. Anchor: the stream tap `esrc_compress_vld_o` counted on `clk_i` rising edges in the leaf. Controls (`CTRL-OBS-MAIN`): the two windows with capture off give the same count above 0, so the window is exact; both observe `LEVEL` fields read above 0 after the on window, so both paths captured during it. Evidence: `CHK-OBS-MAIN PASS seed=<n> strobes=256 words_obs_on=<w> words_obs_off=<w>`, `CTRL-OBS-MAIN seen: seed=<n> words_off_a=<w> words_off_b=<w> strobes=256 bound=<b>` and `CTRL-OBS-MAIN seen: seed=<n> noise_obs_level=<n> biw_obs_level=<n>`.
|OBS-FAST
|Logged only, no pass or fail. The lost and repeated bits of a 64-bit sequence at a sample period of 2 x `clk_i` + 2 ps. No sentence sets a maximum sample rate for the lane.
|CHK-OBS-LOCK
|With `RAW_ENABLE` set before the lock, capture continues after the lock: the FIFO delivers L + 2 words, where L is the `LEVEL` read at the lock, and for the noise FIFO the last two popped words equal the lane value; with it clear at the lock, a write of 1, which must get a B response of any code, leaves `LEVEL` at 0; for the noise FIFO a `FLUSH` after the lock, with old words of z and words of 1 - z buffered and the sample clock stopped across the write, leaves `LEVEL` at 0, and every word popped after it equals the word of 1 - z (`entropy_source.adoc`, `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE`, `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE`: `FIPS_LOCK.LOCK` prevents changes; `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.FLUSH` discards the buffered words). A DUT that stops capture at the lock, that lets a post-lock write enable capture, or whose flush under the lock keeps any buffered word, fails. The locked-at-0 check is a "never" check: its control is the locked-at-1 pass of the same FIFO and seed with the same noise, which captures after t_cap, and the pass waits for 4 times t_cap. Control: the `RAW_ENABLE` readback, the lock readback and, locked at 0, the arrival of a B response for the refused write (`CTRL-OBS-LOCK`, `b_seen=1`). No text states the code of a write the lock refuses, so that code is logged only (`OBS-LOCK0-RESP: fifo=<noise or biw> B=<code>`). Anchor: `NOISE_OBS_STATUS`, `BIW_OBS_STATUS` and `NOISE_OBS_RDATA` readback. The held field and the writable `LANE_SEL` are read as controls only. Evidence: `CHK-OBS-LOCK PASS seed=<n> fifo=<noise or biw> locked_at=1 level_at_lock=<L> post_lock_pops=<L+2> expect_pops=<L+2> noise_flush_level_after=<0 or n/a> [noise_flush_level_before=<b> flush_old_words_after=0 flush_not_new_words=0 flush_words=<m> flush_expect=<word>] capture_clk=<t> wait_clk=<w>` (the bracketed fields for the noise FIFO only) and `CHK-OBS-LOCK PASS seed=<n> fifo=<noise or biw> locked_at=0 level=0 capture_clk=<t> wait_clk=<w>`.
|CHK-OBS-OFF
|With both `RAW_ENABLE` fields 0 and the module running after the boot phase, neither observe FIFO takes a word: after a hold of 256 strobes and at least 4 times the longer first-word time of the two FIFOs in the same seed, both `LEVEL` fields read 0 (`entropy_source.adoc`, `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` and `BIW_OBS_CTRL.RAW_ENABLE` enable the capture). A DUT that captures with the field clear fails. The check is a "never" check: the strobes run over the hold (the wait counts them), the hold spans at least four first-word times of each FIFO, and the ungated trials of the same seed capture on both FIFOs with the fields set (they measure the first-word times). Control: the fields and the levels read 0 before the hold (`CTRL-OBS-OFF`). Anchor: `NOISE_OBS_STATUS` and `BIW_OBS_STATUS` readback. Evidence: `CHK-OBS-OFF PASS seed=<n> noise_level=0 biw_level=0 strobes=256 hold_clk=<h> expect_hold_clk_min=<4 x max(t_noise, t_biw)> t_noise_word_clk=<t_noise> t_biw_word_clk=<t_biw>`.
|OBS-LANESEL-SAME
|Not a graded check. The `LANE_SEL` text states a flush on a lane change; the `FLUSH` text states a flush on a `LANE_SEL` change (`entropy_source.adoc`, `NOISE_OBS_CTRL`). The two disagree for a write that changes the field and keeps lane 0, so no golden is stated. The leaf writes `LANE_SEL` with a value that keeps lane 0 and logs `LEVEL` before and after the write. Evidence: `OBS-LANESEL-SAME seed=<n> sel=<12 to 15> level_before=<n> level_after=<n>`; a wait for `LEVEL` of 4 that times out adds `reason=timeout:level_before bound=<b>`.
|OBS-OVF-KEEP
|Not a graded check. No text states which word a full observe FIFO discards. The leaf drives a known sequence of 66 words at 8 x `clk_i` + 2 ps and logs the overflow bit, `LEVEL`, the number of popped words and the bit index in the sequence at which the first and the last popped word start. Evidence: `OBS-OVF-KEEP seed=<n> lane=<l> seq_words=66 edges=<n> overflow=<0 or 1> level=<n> popped=<n> first_at_bit=<i or none> last_at_bit=<i or none>`.
|OBS-LOCK0-RESP
|A logged line, not a graded check: the B response code of the `RAW_ENABLE` = 1 write to `NOISE_OBS_CTRL` or `BIW_OBS_CTRL` that the lock refuses in the locked-at-0 leg. No text states the code of a write the lock refuses (`FIPS_LOCK.LOCK` prevents changes). The leg grades only the arrival of the B response (`CTRL-OBS-LOCK`, `b_seen=1`) and `LEVEL` staying 0. Evidence: `OBS-LOCK0-RESP: fifo=<noise or biw> B=<code>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Known limitation*: CHK-OBS-SEQ fails on this DUT on every seed: the FIFO writes each word before its 32nd sample shifts in, so after a restart every word is offset by one sample and the first word has a 0 in bit 0. CHK-OBS-PACK fails on this DUT. The first noise observe word after an effective lane change carries a stale bit 0 and 31 samples of the new lane, not 32 samples of the new lane (`entropy_source.adoc`, `NOISE_OBS_CTRL.LANE_SEL` and `NOISE_OBS_RDATA`). The check stays graded, and every other check of the leaf passes on its own.

*Issue*: #3141

*Scope limit*: Only the new cells are graded; the owner of the open test keeps the rest. Not graded: which word an observe FIFO discards when it is full (blocked, see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>; `OBS-OVF-KEEP` logs it), whether a `LANE_SEL` write that keeps the effective lane flushes (blocked; driven once and logged as `OBS-LANESEL-SAME`), observe capture with SHA-256 conditioning on (the digest bursts make the count of a fixed window depend on its phase), the loss or repeat of samples at a sample period near two `clk_i` periods (`OBS-FAST`: no stated maximum sample rate), and the word values of the BIW observe FIFO (the specification states no BIW coefficient). The sequence legs use sample-clock divide 1 and the external sample clock; other divide values and the shared internal clock are not driven. The held value of the `RAW_ENABLE` fields under the lock and the writable `LANE_SEL` belong to `sep_esrc_fips_lock_test`. The startup gate of the main FIFO and of the BIW observe FIFO belongs to `sep_esrc_startup_gate_rand_test`.

'''''

[[sep_drbg_fatal_err_inject_rand_test]]
===== 1.48 `sep_drbg_fatal_err_inject_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy (CSRNG and EDN)

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense +esrc_noise_force`, 1 seed per regression, because every run walks all 12 cells and both FIFO trials; testlist `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`; Verilator and VCS

*Source Module*: `sep_drbg_fatal_err_inject_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip with the CPU-LSU master readback and writes of the CSRNG and EDN registers (approved); the interrupt vector probe `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` (approved; CSRNG fatal error on vector bit 26, EDN fatal error on vector bit 28, Crypto alert on vector bit 32); `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` (approved, the release of the TRNG reset). No PROPOSED item. No inject beyond the register writes of the scenario. Expected values come from the register text and the interrupt map only. The entropy bring-up helper grades CHK-CHK1-RO-ENABLE twice, with the generators off and after they are on: the `RING_OSC_ENABLE.ENABLE` readback is the written value (0, then 0xFFF; `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/entropy_source.rdl:563`, "Enables each generator noise ring oscillator", software read-write) with an OKAY response, and the generator enable probe `esrc_ro_enable_o` (approved, SEP_TB_ARCH observation probe) equals it on the clock after the read. A FAIL fails the leaf. Evidence: `CHK-CHK1-RO-ENABLE PASS: seed=<n> expect=<0x000 or 0xfff> esrc_ro_enable_o=<v> ring_osc_enable=<v> resp_ok=1`.

*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the order of the 12 cells, the order of the two EDN FIFO trials, the extra word count e (2 to 12) of each EDN FIFO trial and the 14 unique test data words of each trial. Drawn after all of these, so that the earlier values keep their mapping: the number k of words held in the FIFO before the clear (1 to 12) and those k words, for each trial. Each regression draws a new seed, so orders, counts and words vary between regressions. The plan shape is the same on every seed: every seed walks the 12 source-bit cells and the two EDN FIFO trials. The leaf logs the plan first (`INFO-PLAN seed=<n> ...`).

*Objective*: Writing a defined source bit number to `ERR_CODE_TEST` forces one error. The `ERR_CODE` source bit sets and, except for EDN bit 0, stays set until reset (the register text of EDN bit 0 does not state it). CSRNG sets `INTR_STATE.CS_FATAL_ERR` for a FIFO error or a fatal alert, and EDN sets `edn_fatal_err` for a FIFO error. The sources that the RDL says "signal a fatal alert" pulse the crypto alert (PIC source 33). A write beyond 13 words to the EDN `RESEED_CMD` or `GENERATE_CMD` FIFO raises `edn_fatal_err` and a fatal alert. Spec: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/csrng/regs/gen/adoc/csrng.adoc` (`INTR_STATE`, `ERR_CODE`, `ERR_CODE_TEST`); `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/edn/regs/gen/adoc/edn.adoc` (`RESEED_CMD`, `GENERATE_CMD`, `ERR_CODE`, `ERR_CODE_TEST`); `hw/sys/sep/doc/interrupts.adoc` (SEP CPU Interrupt Vector Map); `hw/sys/sep/doc/crypto.adoc` (OpenTitan Alert Termination).

*Test Coverage*: On every seed the 12 source-bit cells, one per trial: CSRNG bits 0, 1, 20, 21, 22, 25 and 26, and EDN bits 0, 1, 20, 21 and 22, each with the fatal-error enable set. EDN is enabled in software-port mode after CSRNG, so the FIFO-error cells run on an enabled unit and no command is sent. The write-one clear of the interrupt state, and a TRNG reset. For each of `RESEED_CMD` and `GENERATE_CMD` on every seed, with k drawn words (1 to 12) held in the FIFO and a `CTRL.CMD_FIFO_RST` pulse after them: 1, a drawn 2 to 12, 13 and 14 words. Error injection: `ERR_CODE_TEST` writes and a 14th command word (point: the register of each unit; kind: a forced error and a FIFO overrun; expected response: as the objective states). Extends the open entropy tests (`sep_esrc_e2e_smoke_test` and the others), which read the CSRNG and EDN `ERR_CODE` and `RECOV_ALERT_STS` as zero (`CHK-ALERTS-ZERO`, a passive read). This test grades only the forced errors; the owners of the open tests keep the zero reads. Functional coverage: `sep_drbg_err_inject_cg` (`x_err_source`, `cp_fatal_consequence`, `cp_sticky`, `cp_edn_cmd_fifo`), `sep_edn_mode_cg` (`cp_auto_fifo_words`), `sep_crypto_intr_cg` (`x_line_gate`, `cp_hw_raise` cs_fatal_err and edn_fatal_err), `sep_crypto_reset_cg` (`cp_reset_evidence`). `cp_cmd_fifo_rst` of `sep_drbg_err_inject_cg` (`reseed_cleared`, `generate_cleared`) is filled by the FIFO trials.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the plan. Log `INFO-PLAN seed=<n>` with the cell order, the FIFO trial order, the enables, the extra word counts and the test data words.
. Per trial, set-up. Pulse the TRNG reset: write 0 then 1 to bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N` (`TRNG_SW_RST_N`) with every other bit at the baseline of the leaf, and wait until `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` read 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). No ESRC, CSRNG or EDN access comes before this release, because the write of 0 isolates their host paths until the write of 1 (`reset_controller.adoc`, Isolation and Reset Sequencing). Bring up ESRC and enable CSRNG as the open end-to-end test does. Then enable EDN in software-port mode, after CSRNG, in the order of the Programmer's Guide: write `CTRL` with `BOOT_REQ_MODE` False, `AUTO_REQ_MODE` False, `EDN_ENABLE` True and `CMD_FIFO_RST` False. Software-port mode sends no command, so the `RESEED_CMD` and `GENERATE_CMD` FIFOs are not consumed. Read the CSRNG `ERR_CODE` once and log the clock count of that read as t_ref. Every `ERR_CODE` compare of this entry uses the field bits of the register text (CSRNG bits 0, 1, 20 to 22, 25, 26 and 28 to 30; EDN bits 0, 1, 20 to 22 and 28 to 30) and logs the Reserved bits as `rsvd` (common rule "Field bits"). The leaf takes every bit position from the field names of the generated register map (for example `SFIFO_CMD_ERR`, `CMD_STAGE_SM_ERR`, `EDN_CNTR_ERR`), not from literals. Every wait below is a bounded wait of 16 times t_ref (the leaf logs t_ref and the bound); a timeout fails the check.
. Source-bit cells, one cell per trial, in drawn order. Run the set-up. Read `ERR_CODE` and the fatal-error bit of `INTR_STATE` of the unit (`CS_FATAL_ERR` of CSRNG, `edn_fatal_err` of EDN) and require 0. Write 1 to the fatal-error bit of `INTR_ENABLE`. Watch vector bits 26, 28 and 32 of `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` on every `clk_i` from this step to step 7. After the enable write, record the level of the vector bit of the unit (26 for CSRNG, 28 for EDN) as `vec_pre`.
. This is the error injection. Write the source bit number to `ERR_CODE_TEST` of the unit.
. Wait for `ERR_CODE` to change (a bounded wait). Read `ERR_CODE` and require the forced source bit set. Log the other source bits (bits 0 to 27 apart from the forced bit) and the type bits 28 to 30 as `OBS-DRBG-ERRCODE-FIELDS`. Nothing is graded on them.
. Read `INTR_STATE` and `INTR_ENABLE` of the unit. For CSRNG (any of the 7 bits) and for EDN bits 0 and 1 compare `CS_FATAL_ERR` or `edn_fatal_err` with 1. For EDN bits 20 to 22 log the state and grade no value. For the cells with a graded state require `vec_pre` = 0, at least one rise of the vector bit of the unit (26 for CSRNG, 28 for EDN) after the `ERR_CODE_TEST` write, and the bit at 1 at the read; for EDN bits 20 to 22 log them.
. For the fatal-alert sources (CSRNG bits 20, 21, 22, 25 and 26; EDN bits 20, 21 and 22) require at least one cycle with vector bit 32 high during the watch and 0 before the write of step 4. For the FIFO-error bits (0 and 1) log the vector bit 32 cycles as `OBS-DRBG-ALERT` and grade no value.
. Sticky leg. Read the fatal-error bit of `INTR_STATE` of the unit. For the FIFO-error bits (CSRNG bits 0 and 1, EDN bits 0 and 1) require 1 (a control); for the other bits log it. Write 1 to that bit. Read it again. For the FIFO-error bits, which force a single pulse, require 0. For the other bits log the state as `OBS-DRBG-STATE-AFTER-W1C` and grade no value. Read `ERR_CODE` and require its source bit still set for every cell except EDN bit 0; for EDN bit 0 log it as `OBS-EDN-STICKY0`.
. Reset leg. Read `ERR_CODE` as non-zero (a control, `CTRL-DRBG-RESET`). Pulse the TRNG reset as in the set-up. Read `ERR_CODE` as 0.
. EDN FIFO trials, one trial each for `RESEED_CMD` and `GENERATE_CMD` in the drawn order, with the drawn extra count e and the drawn held count k. Run the set-up. With the graded window closed, run the no-clear control: write the k drawn words (1 to 12) and then 13 - k test data words, read `ERR_CODE` and require 0, write test data word 14 - k (word 14 in the FIFO), wait for the FIFO source bit in `ERR_CODE` and require it (`CTRL-EDN-FIFO-HELD`: the k words land and count toward the 13-word limit). Run the set-up again, which clears the error by a TRNG reset. With the graded window closed, write the same k words and read `ERR_CODE`: it must read 0 (`CTRL-EDN-FIFO-PRECLEAR`, the k words are held without an error). Open the graded window, then write the EDN `CTRL` with `CMD_FIFO_RST` True and then with `CMD_FIFO_RST` False (the other fields as in the set-up), so that the count starts from an empty FIFO (`edn.adoc`, `CTRL.CMD_FIFO_RST`: True clears both command FIFOs, and software sets it back to False before the next command). A clear that leaves the k words shows as an error at word 14 - k, which is 13 or fewer. Write 1 to `INTR_ENABLE.edn_fatal_err`. Watch vector bits 28 and 32 on every `clk_i` from this write to step 13. Write one test data word to the FIFO. Read `ERR_CODE` and `INTR_STATE` and require 0. Require vector bits 28 and 32 low on every clock since the enable write.
. Write test data words until e words are in the FIFO. Read `ERR_CODE` and `INTR_STATE` and require 0. Require vector bits 28 and 32 low. Write test data words until 13 words are in the FIFO and repeat the reads.
. This is the error injection. Write the 14th word.
. Wait for `ERR_CODE` to change. Read `ERR_CODE` and require `SFIFO_RESCMD_ERR` (bit 0) for `RESEED_CMD` or `SFIFO_GENCMD_ERR` (bit 1) for `GENERATE_CMD`. Read `INTR_STATE.edn_fatal_err` as 1. Require vector bit 28 high and at least one cycle of vector bit 32 high since the write of the 14th word. Run the reset leg of step 9.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-DRBG-ERRCODE
|After the `ERR_CODE_TEST` write, `ERR_CODE` holds the forced source bit; `CS_FATAL_ERR` (CSRNG) or `edn_fatal_err` (EDN bits 0 and 1) is 1; and with the enable set the vector bit (26 for CSRNG, 28 for EDN) reads 1 (`csrng.adoc` and `edn.adoc`, `ERR_CODE`, `ERR_CODE_TEST`, `INTR_STATE`: the error is forced at the bit number of `ERR_CODE`; `interrupts.adoc`: PIC source 27 and 29 on vector bits 26 and 28). The text does not say that no other bit sets, and a forced fatal source can set other source bits through the escalation of the unit, so the other source bits and the type bits 28 to 30 are logged only. For the cells with a graded state the vector bit reads 0 after the enable write and before the `ERR_CODE_TEST` write, rises after that write and reads 1, so a vector bit stuck at 1 or driven by another source fails. The `INTR_ENABLE` readback of the fatal-error bit must read 1, so the enable write is shown to have landed. A DUT that sets no bit at the forced position, or a state that does not reach the vector, fails. Anchor: `ERR_CODE`, `INTR_STATE` and `INTR_ENABLE` readback and the vector probe. Control: `ERR_CODE` and the fatal-error state bit read 0 before the write (`CTRL-DRBG-ERRCODE`). Evidence: `CHK-DRBG-ERRCODE PASS seed=<n> unit=<csrng or edn> bit=<b> err_code=0x<c> rsvd=0x<r> source_bit_set=1 fatal_state=<s> enable=1 vec=<v> vec_pre=<0> vec_rose=<1> state_graded=<1 or 0>`; `vec`, `vec_pre` and `vec_rose` are graded for the cells with a graded state and logged for EDN bits 20 to 22. `OBS-DRBG-ERRCODE-FIELDS seed=<n> unit=<csrng or edn> bit=<b> other_src=0x<o> type=0x<t>` logs the other source bits and the type bits.
|CHK-DRBG-ALERT
|For the sources whose register text says a fatal alert (CSRNG bits 20, 21, 22, 25, 26; EDN bits 20, 21, 22) vector bit 32 pulses (`csrng.adoc` and `edn.adoc`, `ERR_CODE`: "This error will signal a fatal alert"; `interrupts.adoc`: source 33 is the Crypto alert). A DUT that forces the error and raises no alert fails. This is a pulse that must come from the write: the control is that vector bit 32 reads 0 before the `ERR_CODE_TEST` write. For the FIFO-error bits the pulse is logged and not graded. Anchor: the vector probe, watched on every `clk_i`. Evidence: `CHK-DRBG-ALERT PASS seed=<n> unit=<u> bit=<b> pic33_high_cycles=<c> pre=0 t_ref=<t>`, and `OBS-DRBG-ALERT seed=<n> unit=<u> bit=<b> pic33_high_cycles=<c>` for the FIFO-error bits.
|CHK-DRBG-STICKY
|After a write-one clear of the fatal-error state bit the `ERR_CODE` source bit stays set, for every source except EDN bit 0 (`csrng.adoc` and `edn.adoc`, `ERR_CODE`: "This bit will stay set until the next reset"; the register text of EDN bit 0 does not carry this sentence, so that bit is logged). For the FIFO-error bits (CSRNG bits 0 and 1, EDN bits 0 and 1) the state also reads 0 after the clear (`INTR_STATE`: write one to clear; `ERR_CODE_TEST`: the write forces an error pulse, so no source holds the state). A DUT that clears `ERR_CODE` with the state fails on every graded cell; a DUT that does not clear the state fails on the FIFO-error cells. For the fatal-alert sources the register text does not say that the state stays clear while the forced source is held, so the state after the clear is logged and not graded; the state can read 1 before and after the clear there, so on those cells the check grades only that the source bit stays set until the next reset, not that the clear landed. Anchor: readback. Control: on the FIFO-error cells the state reads 1 before the clear (`CTRL-DRBG-STICKY`). Evidence: `CHK-DRBG-STICKY PASS seed=<n> unit=<u> bit=<b> after_w1c=1 err_code=0x<c> rsvd=0x<r> source_bit_kept=<1 or na> state_graded=<1 or 0> fatal_state=<s>`; `OBS-EDN-STICKY0 seed=<n> err_code=0x<c> rsvd=0x<r>` for EDN bit 0; `OBS-DRBG-STATE-AFTER-W1C seed=<n> unit=<u> bit=<b> before=<s0> after=<s1>` for the fatal-alert sources.
|CHK-DRBG-RESET
|After a TRNG reset `ERR_CODE` is 0 (`csrng.adoc` and `edn.adoc`, `ERR_CODE`: stays set until the next reset; `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc`, Reset: the TRNG reset is a full-IP reset of the entropy source and DRBG). A DUT that keeps the error through the reset fails. Anchor: `ERR_CODE` readback. The reset leg runs after every source-bit cell and after each EDN FIFO trial. Control: the bit is set before the reset (`CTRL-DRBG-RESET`). Evidence: `CHK-DRBG-RESET PASS seed=<n> unit=<u> bit=<b> after_trng_reset=1 err_code=0x0 rsvd=0x<r>` (`trial=fifo_<f>` in place of `bit=<b>` after an EDN FIFO trial).
|CHK-EDN-FIFO13
|After k words (1 to 12) are held and a `CTRL.CMD_FIFO_RST` pulse, up to 13 words written to `RESEED_CMD` or `GENERATE_CMD` leave `ERR_CODE` at 0 and raise no interrupt (`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/edn/regs/gen/adoc/edn.adoc:98-101`: True clears both command FIFOs; `:225` and `:241`: up to 13 command words). The leaf checks the counts 1, e and 13. A DUT with a shorter FIFO fails at its limit, and a DUT whose clear leaves the k held words fails at word 14 - k, which is 13 or fewer. EDN is enabled in software-port mode, so the FIFOs take the words and nothing consumes them, and the FIFO reset removes the k words, so the count starts from an empty FIFO. The line logs `held_before_clear=<k>`. The enable of `edn_fatal_err` is 1, so a spurious state shows on vector bit 28. Anchor: `ERR_CODE` and `INTR_STATE` readback and the vector probe. Control: the 14th word of the same FIFO sets the error (CHK-EDN-FIFO14). Evidence: `CHK-EDN-FIFO13 PASS seed=<n> fifo=<reseed or generate> words=<n> err_code=0x0 rsvd=0x<r> intr_state=0x0 vec28_high_cycles=0 vec32_high_cycles=0`, logged for each count.
|CHK-EDN-FIFO14
|The 14th word sets `ERR_CODE` to the source bit of the FIFO written, `SFIFO_RESCMD_ERR` (bit 0) or `SFIFO_GENCMD_ERR` (bit 1), together with `FIFO_WRITE_ERR` (bit 28), and sets `INTR_STATE` to exactly `edn_fatal_err`, raises vector bit 28 and pulses vector bit 32. The whole `ERR_CODE` field value is compared, taken at the first read that shows the source bit. Golden: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/edn/regs/gen/adoc/edn.adoc:232` and `:249` (more than 13 entries are ignored and EDN signals an `edn_fatal_err` interrupt as well as a fatal alert), `:318-326` (the source bits 0 and 1, a fatal error condition), `:305` (`FIFO_WRITE_ERR` is set when a source bit is asserted by an error pulse from a write to a full FIFO) and `:41` (`edn_fatal_err`). A DUT that accepts a 14th word silently, that sets the source bit without `FIFO_WRITE_ERR` or sets another type or source bit, whose `INTR_STATE` is not exactly `edn_fatal_err`, whose vector bit 28 is low, or that raises no alert fails. The `ERR_CODE` source bit of `RESEED_CMD` has no "stays set" text, so no later re-read is graded. The unit is enabled, so a healthy EDN reports the FIFO error. Anchor: `ERR_CODE` and `INTR_STATE` readback and the vector probe. Control: the same FIFO reads 0 after 13 words (`CTRL-EDN-FIFO14 ctrl_fifo13_ok=1`). Evidence: `CHK-EDN-FIFO14 PASS seed=<n> fifo=<f> words=14 err_code=0x<c> rsvd=0x<r> fifo_err_bit=<0 or 1> fatal_intr=1 vec28=1 alert_cycles=<a>`.
|CTRL-EDN-FIFO-HELD
|The k drawn words land in the FIFO: with no `CMD_FIFO_RST`, the k words and 13 - k test data words leave `ERR_CODE` at 0, and the next word (word 14 in the FIFO) sets the FIFO source bit (`edn.adoc:225`, `:232`, `:241`, `:249`). A held word that did not land moves the error past word 14 and fails the control, so CHK-EDN-FIFO13 cannot pass on a clear of an empty FIFO. Evidence: `CTRL-EDN-FIFO-HELD seen: seed=<n> fifo=<f> held_before_clear=<k> err_code_at_13=0x00000000 src_bit_at_14=1 err_code=0x<c> rsvd=0x<r>`. A missing control fails the leaf.
|CTRL-EDN-FIFO-PRECLEAR
|After the TRNG reset of the second set-up, the same k words are held again without an error: `ERR_CODE` reads 0 with the graded window closed, before the clear. Logged as `held_before_clear=<k>` with the whole `ERR_CODE` value. A missing control fails the leaf.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Functional Coverage*: owner window 148. `_setup` closes the window; it opens only while no check or control has failed, and the first FAIL or missing control closes it for the rest of the run. For a source-bit cell the window is open from the enable write to the end of the reset leg. For an FIFO trial the no-clear control and the k words of the graded trial are written and `ERR_CODE` is read with the window closed, and the window opens before the `CMD_FIFO_RST` write, so the sampler sees the clear. Pass label `drbg_fatal_pass` (`set_drbg_fatal_pass`): the sampler holds each cell of the owner as pending at its DUT event and samples it only on the rise of the label, which the leaf writes after the end of each cell and each FIFO trial (label 1 for 2 clocks, then 0, then the window closes), and only when no check has failed; a pending cell is dropped when the next window opens, so a failed step never samples into a later one. Cells: `sep_drbg_err_inject_cg` `x_err_source` (graded by `CHK-DRBG-ERRCODE`), `cp_fatal_consequence` (`CHK-DRBG-ERRCODE` and `CHK-DRBG-ALERT`), `cp_sticky` survives (`CHK-DRBG-STICKY`) and cleared-by-reset (`CHK-DRBG-RESET`), `cp_edn_cmd_fifo` 13 words without an error (`CHK-EDN-FIFO13`) and the 14th-word error (`CHK-EDN-FIFO14`), `cp_cmd_fifo_rst` `reseed_cleared` and `generate_cleared` (the 14th-word event of a trial armed by a clear with 1 to 12 words held; `CHK-EDN-FIFO13` for all 13 clean words and `CHK-EDN-FIFO14`); `sep_edn_mode_cg` `cp_auto_fifo_words` (word 1 and word 13 after the clear; `CHK-EDN-FIFO13`); `sep_crypto_reset_cg` `cp_reset_evidence` `csrng_err_code` and `edn_err_code` (the first `ERR_CODE` read after the TRNG reset equals 0; `CHK-DRBG-RESET`). Every seed runs both FIFOs and every cell, so no bin depends on the seed.

*Scope limit*: Only the forced-error cells are graded; the owners of the open tests keep the zero reads. Not graded: undefined bit numbers (never driven), the other source bits and the type bits 28 to 30 (logged), the EDN interrupt for bits 20 to 22 (logged), the state after the clear for the fatal-alert sources (logged), a pulse on the FIFO-error bits (logged), the `ERR_CODE` source bit of EDN bit 0 after a write-one clear (logged), the receiver range and the receiver index of an alert (owned by `sep_pic_pending_map_walk_rand_test`; see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>), the enable gate of the fatal-error lines (owned by the extension leg of `sep_irq_ip_to_aggregator_test`), and the drop of the 14th command word (`edn.adoc`, `RESEED_CMD`, `GENERATE_CMD`: "they are ignored"; no register reads the command FIFO level or contents, the FIFO is consumed only in auto request mode after which the fatal error condition has no stated send behavior, and the test data words do not match `clen`). The zero reads of `ERR_CODE` and `RECOV_ALERT_STS` belong to the open entropy tests. The EDN command and the CSRNG instantiate paths belong to `sep_drbg_cmd_sequence_rand_test`.

'''''

[[sep_drbg_regwen_lock_rand_test]]
===== 1.49 `sep_drbg_regwen_lock_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy (CSRNG and EDN)

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense`, 1 seed per regression, because every run walks all three targets; testlist `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`; VCS only (`tools = ["vcs"]`), because the reset readback must show no X

*Source Module*: `sep_drbg_regwen_lock_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip with the CPU-LSU master readback and writes of the CSRNG and EDN registers (32-bit accesses, approved); the VCS X check on the readback (approved: a tap of the read-data channel flags an X or Z bit in the accessed 32-bit lane of the graded reads); `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` (approved, the release of the TRNG reset). No inject. No PROPOSED item. Expected values come from the register text, the RDL reset values and the upstream register description (`regwen` binding) only.

*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed: the order of the three targets, the values A and B of each target, the data on the inactive 32-bit lane of the 64-bit bus in each write (the strobe covers the register lane only), the value written to `INT_STATE_READ_ENABLE` for the observation, and the number r of re-set attempts (1 to 3). Each regression draws a new seed, so orders, values, fills and attempts vary between regressions. The plan shape is the same on every seed: every seed walks the three targets. The leaf logs the plan first (`INFO-PLAN seed=<n> ...`).

*Objective*: With CSRNG `REGWEN` (RW0C, reset 1) cleared, the CSRNG registers that the register description binds to it (`CTRL`, `FIPS_FORCE` and `ERR_CODE_TEST`) are read-only; this test grades `CTRL`, `FIPS_FORCE` and `ERR_CODE_TEST`. With EDN `REGWEN` cleared, `CTRL` is read-only. A write of 1 does not set a cleared `REGWEN`. A reset restores it. Spec: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/csrng/regs/gen/adoc/csrng.adoc` (`REGWEN`); `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/edn/regs/gen/adoc/edn.adoc` (`REGWEN`); `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/csrng/data/csrng.hjson` and `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/edn/data/edn.hjson` (the `regwen` binding of each register). The `REGWEN` text of `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/csrng/regs/csrng.rdl` ("all writeable registers ... become read-only") names no register and binds more registers than the `regwen` binding. The two texts differ for the registers outside the binding, so those are logged only (`OBS-REGWEN-OTHER`).

*Test Coverage*: On every seed the three targets CSRNG `CTRL`, CSRNG `FIPS_FORCE` and EDN `CTRL`, each with values A and B that differ from the reset value and from each other. Multi-bit-boolean fields take only True (6) or False (9). `ENABLE` and `EDN_ENABLE` stay False and `CMD_FIFO_RST` stays False, so no module starts and no FIFO clears. 1 to 3 re-set attempts per target. For the two CSRNG targets, a write of the bit number of `SFIFO_GENBITS_ERR` to `ERR_CODE_TEST` under the cleared `REGWEN`, with a control after the reset. Error injection: the write of `ERR_CODE_TEST` under the lock, which the lock must refuse; the control write with `REGWEN` set forces `SFIFO_GENBITS_ERR`, and the next TRNG reset clears it. Extends `sep_reg_bit_bash_rand_test`, which writes and reads back the unlocked storage registers and does not clear `REGWEN`. This test grades only the lock; the owner of the open test keeps the unlocked read and write. Functional coverage: `sep_crypto_reg_lock_cg` (`x_csrng_regwen`, `x_edn_regwen`), `sep_crypto_reset_cg` (`cp_reset_evidence`).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Draw the plan with `SepSeededRng`. For each target draw A and B so that A differs from the RDL reset value in at least one field, B differs from the RDL reset value in at least one field and B differs from A in at least one field. For CSRNG `CTRL` and EDN `CTRL` draw each multi-bit-boolean field of A and B from True and False, with `ENABLE`, `EDN_ENABLE` and `CMD_FIFO_RST` False. For CSRNG `FIPS_FORCE` draw A and B as non-zero 3-bit values that differ. For each write draw the data of the inactive 32-bit lane. Log `INFO-PLAN seed=<n>`.
. For each target in drawn order, run steps 3 to 11.
. Pulse the TRNG reset: write 0 then 1 to bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N` (`TRNG_SW_RST_N`) with every other bit at the baseline of the leaf, and wait until `trng_reset_active_probe_o` and `trng_axi_isolated_probe_o` read 0 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). No ESRC, CSRNG or EDN access comes before this release, because the write of 0 isolates their host paths until the write of 1 (`reset_controller.adoc`, Isolation and Reset Sequencing). Read `REGWEN` of the unit as 1. Read the target and compare it with its RDL reset value.
. Write A to the target. Read the target and require A. The reads of step 3 and this read are one control, logged as `CTRL-REGWEN-PRE`.
. Write 0 to `REGWEN` of the unit. Read `REGWEN` and require 0.
. Write B to the target. Read the target and compare it with A.
. For the CSRNG targets only: write a drawn value that differs from the reset value to `INT_STATE_READ_ENABLE`. Read it and log both values as `OBS-REGWEN-OWN`. Nothing is graded. Then write the complement of the readback to `RESEED_INTERVAL` and to `INT_STATE_NUM`, one after the other, read each back and log the values (`OBS-REGWEN-OTHER`). Then write the bit number of `SFIFO_GENBITS_ERR` to `ERR_CODE_TEST` and read `ERR_CODE_TEST` (`CHK-REGWEN-ERRTEST`).
. Write 1 to `REGWEN` of the unit, r times. Read `REGWEN` after each write and compare it with 0.
. Pulse the TRNG reset as in step 3. Read `REGWEN` of the unit and require 1. The coverage window stays open from the write of step 5 to this read.
. Read the target and compare it with its RDL reset value, with no X or Z bit in the accessed lane of the read data of both reads.
. Write B to the target. Read the target and require B. For the CSRNG targets, with `REGWEN` read as 1, write the bit number of `SFIFO_GENBITS_ERR` to `ERR_CODE_TEST` and read `ERR_CODE_TEST` (`CTRL-REGWEN-ERRTEST`).

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-REGWEN-LOCK
|After 0 is written to `REGWEN` it reads 0, and a write of B to the target leaves A (`csrng.adoc` and `edn.adoc`, `REGWEN`: when false the registers become read-only; the `regwen` binding in `csrng.hjson` and `edn.hjson` names `CTRL`, `FIPS_FORCE` and `ERR_CODE_TEST` for CSRNG and `CTRL` for EDN). A DUT that takes the write, or that locks another register in place of the target, fails. Values A and B differ from the reset value and from each other, so a write that lands, a write that is lost and a reset value are all distinguishable. Anchor: readback of the target. Control: `REGWEN` reads 1 and the target reads its reset value after the TRNG reset, and the write of A before the lock reads back A (`CTRL-REGWEN-PRE`). The B response of the write of B is on the check line (`wr_resp`, 0 = OKAY; a response other than OKAY fails the leaf at the write). Evidence: `CHK-REGWEN-LOCK PASS seed=<n> unit=<csrng or edn> reg=<r> regwen=0 kept=0x<a> tried=0x<b> wr_resp=0 read=0x<a> rsvd=0x<x>`, and `CTRL-REGWEN-PRE seed=<n> unit=<u> reg=<r> wrote=0x<a> read=0x<a> rsvd=0x<x> regwen_start=1 start=0x<reset> reset=0x<reset>`.
|CHK-REGWEN-NOREARM
|A write of 1 to a cleared `REGWEN` leaves it at 0 (`csrng.adoc` and `edn.adoc`, `REGWEN`: RW0C, so only a write of 0 changes it and only a reset sets it). A DUT that sets the bit again, which unlocks the registers, fails. The leaf repeats the write r times (1 to 3). Anchor: `REGWEN` readback after each write. Control: `REGWEN` reads 0 before the first write of 1 (`CTRL-REGWEN-NOREARM`). Evidence: `CHK-REGWEN-NOREARM PASS seed=<n> unit=<u> writes=<r> after_write1=0 reads=[0,...] wr_resp=[0,...]` (the B response of each write of 1).
|CHK-REGWEN-RESET
|After a TRNG reset `REGWEN` is 1, the target is at its reset value with no X, and a new write lands (`csrng.adoc` and `edn.adoc`, `REGWEN`: reset 0x1; `hw/ip/entropy_source/doc/architecture.adoc`, Reset: the TRNG reset is a full-IP reset of the entropy source and DRBG). A DUT that keeps the lock, keeps the old value A, leaves an X in the target or ignores the new write fails. Anchor: readback of `REGWEN` and of the target, with the VCS X check. Control: `REGWEN` reads 0 before the reset, and the X tap sees both graded read beats (`CTRL-REGWEN-RESET regwen_before_reset=0 graded_read_beats=<k>`, k at least 2). Evidence: `CHK-REGWEN-RESET PASS seed=<n> unit=<u> regwen=1 reg=0x<reset> reset=0x<reset> rsvd=0x<x> post_write_lands=1 wrote=0x<b> read=0x<b> x_free=1 x_lanes=none`.
|CHK-REGWEN-ERRTEST
|With CSRNG `REGWEN` cleared, a write of the bit number of `SFIFO_GENBITS_ERR` (bit 1) to `ERR_CODE_TEST` leaves `ERR_CODE_TEST` at its RDL reset value. `ERR_CODE_TEST` is bound to `REGWEN` (`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/csrng/data/csrng.hjson:793`), and "When false, they become read-only." (`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/csrng/regs/gen/adoc/csrng.adoc:97-98`). Bit 0 is not used, because it writes the reset value of the register and a readback could not tell a dropped write from a landed one. A DUT that takes the write stores the value, and fails. Anchor: `ERR_CODE_TEST` readback, and `REGWEN` reading 0. Control (`CTRL-REGWEN-ERRTEST`): after the TRNG reset (`REGWEN` reads 1) the same write reads back the written value (`sw = rw`); a missing control logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf. Whether the forced error pulse sets `ERR_CODE` while CSRNG is disabled is not stated, so `ERR_CODE` is not read. CSRNG targets only. Evidence: `CHK-REGWEN-ERRTEST PASS seed=<n> unit=csrng reg=<CTRL or FIPS_FORCE> regwen=0 wrote=0x1 wr_resp=0 errtest_read=0x0`; `CTRL-REGWEN-ERRTEST seen: seed=<n> unit=csrng reg=<r> regwen=1 wrote=0x1 errtest_read=0x1`.
|OBS-REGWEN-OTHER
|Not a graded check. The `REGWEN` text of `csrng.rdl` states that when it is false "all writeable registers" become read-only, so the golden is the value before the write. The upstream `regwen` binding names only `CTRL`, `FIPS_FORCE` and `ERR_CODE_TEST`, and the `csrng.rdl` text names no register, so the two texts differ for these registers and the line stays an observation until a text states the scope. Under the cleared `REGWEN` the leaf writes the complement of the readback to `RESEED_INTERVAL` and to `INT_STATE_NUM` and logs the value written and the readback. Evidence: `OBS-REGWEN-OTHER seed=<n> reg=<RESEED_INTERVAL or INT_STATE_NUM> regwen=0 before=0x<b> wrote=0x<w> read=0x<r>`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Scope limit*: Only the lock is graded; the owner of the open test keeps the unlocked read and write. Not graded: `INT_STATE_READ_ENABLE` under the CSRNG `REGWEN` (the text is ambiguous; the leaf logs `OBS-REGWEN-OWN seed=<n> reg=INT_STATE_READ_ENABLE wrote=0x<b> read=0x<r>`), and `RESEED_INTERVAL` and `INT_STATE_NUM` (the `csrng.rdl` text and the upstream `regwen` binding differ for them, see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>; the leaf logs a write and the readback of each, `OBS-REGWEN-OTHER`). The CTRL values are graded by readback only and not as an alert contract, and the leaf writes only True and False values to them. The unlocked read and write of the storage registers belong to `sep_reg_bit_bash_rand_test`.

'''''
[[sep_drbg_cmd_sequence_rand_test]]
===== 1.50 `sep_drbg_cmd_sequence_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy (CSRNG and EDN)

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+esrc_noise_force`, `reseed = 4`; Verilator and VCS; `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`

*Source Module*: `sep_drbg_cmd_sequence_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (CPU-LSU master); the open entropy bring-up helper with its entropy golden (bring-up only: this test grades register state, not data). Probes: approved: CPU-LSU master readback of the EDN, CSRNG and `SEP_RESET_CTRL` registers; `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o` (vector bit 27 graded in the EDN software port leg and vector bit 23 graded in the CSRNG software instance leg; the other bits logged and not graded); `trng_reset_active_probe_o`. Injects: the raw ESRC noise on `esrc_noise_ext_i` (approved, `+esrc_noise_force`), driven by the DRBG scoreboard that the entropy bring-up helper starts in every clean start, and in the instantiate-seed leg from the open entropy noise model so that ESRC fills a seed. This noise is the source of every instantiate seed; no check grades a noise value, only the DUT handshakes, acknowledges and register state. No other inject beyond the command writes of the Test Procedure. No PROPOSED item. Observation tap added by the test: the DRBG seed ingress tap (`esrc_compress_vld_o` and `drbg_seed_word_ready_o`), read by `OBS-DRBG-SEED-INGRESS`. The entropy bring-up helper grades CHK-CHK1-RO-ENABLE twice, with the generators off and after they are on: the `RING_OSC_ENABLE.ENABLE` readback is the written value (0, then 0xFFF; `hw/ip/entropy_source/regs/entropy_source.rdl:563`, "Enables each generator noise ring oscillator", software read-write) with an OKAY response, and the generator enable probe `esrc_ro_enable_o` (approved, SEP_TB_ARCH observation probe) equals it on the clock after the read. A FAIL fails the leaf. Evidence: `CHK-CHK1-RO-ENABLE PASS: seed=<n> expect=<0x000 or 0xfff> esrc_ro_enable_o=<v> ring_osc_enable=<v> resp_ok=1`.

*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed.
