Dimensions: the order of the boot group and the two software legs (the reference boot stays first inside the boot group, and the graded boots run with `AUTO_REQ_MODE` true, then false); the idle gap before each poll read (0 to 16 clocks, from a drawn table of 256 gaps); after these, the invalid value of each of the eight `CTRL` fields (from the 14 codes other than 6 and 9), the invalid `flag0` (same set), the reserved `acmd` (0x0, 0x6, 0x7, 0x8, 0xe or 0xf: the reserved values whose bits [2:0] are also a reserved value; the reserved values 0x9 to 0xd carry a defined command code in bits [2:0], and the leaf sends 0x9 on every seed in its last leg, with no draw), the `RESEED_INTERVAL` value K (3 or 4) the `SW_APP_ENABLE` value of the `GENBITS` read (the 15 codes other than 6), and then the 384-bit seed of the known-answer Generate, the reseed count N of the second auto-sequence trial (2 to 4) and the hold H of the instantiate-seed leg (256 to 2048 clocks). The last three are drawn after all the others, so the earlier values keep their mapping. The counter-wrap leg draws its 384-bit seed from its own stream (the run seed XOR a leg salt), so the plan draws keep their mapping; its seed word 0 is then set from the golden so that V[31:0] = 0xfffffffe after the Instantiate. N = 1 runs on every seed. The `INTR_ENABLE` bit of `edn_cmd_req_done` and of `cs_cmd_req_done` is 1 on every seed, because this leaf fills the (state 1, enable 1) cell of these two sources from a real event. The checked values do not depend on the seed; the known-answer control compares with the golden of the drawn seed. `reseed = 4` samples four leg orders, poll gaps and injection values; the invalid values and the reserved acmd close over merged regressions. The leaf logs its plan first (`INFO-PLAN seed=<n> ...`).

*Objective*: In boot request mode EDN reports `HW_CMD_STS.BOOT_MODE` = 1 and `AUTO_MODE` = 0, also when both `CTRL.BOOT_REQ_MODE` and `CTRL.AUTO_REQ_MODE` are set (boot mode takes precedence). With neither mode set EDN enters software port mode, where `SW_CMD_STS.CMD_REG_RDY`, `CMD_RDY` and `CMD_ACK` follow the stated handshake and a word written to `SW_CMD_REQ` reaches CSRNG. The instantiate word is the reset value of `BOOT_INS_CMD` (0x901). An instantiate sent to an instantiated state sets `RECOV_ALERT_STS.CMD_STAGE_INVALID_CMD_SEQ_ALERT` (bit 14), which clears by a write of 0. Spec: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/edn/regs/gen/adoc/edn.adoc` (`CTRL`, `BOOT_INS_CMD`, `SW_CMD_REQ`, `SW_CMD_STS`, `HW_CMD_STS`); `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/csrng/regs/gen/adoc/csrng.adoc` (`CMD_REQ`, `SW_CMD_STS`, `RECOV_ALERT_STS`); `hw/ip/drbg/doc/architecture.adoc` (OpenTitan CSRNG and EDN); `hw/sys/sep/doc/interrupts.adoc` (source numbers).

*Test Coverage*: EDN boot mode with `BOOT_REQ_MODE` true and `AUTO_REQ_MODE` false (this boot also logs the boot time as an observation), and with both true, each on every seed. EDN software port (both modes false, the cell (false, false)): a valid instantiate, a second instantiate as error injection, then a reserved acmd. The CSRNG software instance: the two instantiates. The reset values of the EDN boot words. The `CTRL` field alerts of CSRNG and EDN, each field once. The CSRNG command header: a reserved acmd, an instantiate with flag0 0x9, 0x6 and an invalid value, uninstantiate. The reseed limit with Generate, Reseed and the `GENBITS` gate, and a known-answer Generate on the CSRNG software port. The EDN auto-mode command sequence after the software instantiate: N generates, one reseed and a generate, for N = 1 and for a drawn N from 2 to 4. The EDN instantiate with the ESRC generators off: no acknowledge, no seed handshake and no `edn_cmd_req_done` for a drawn hold, then the acknowledge after the seed. This entry extends `sep_esrc_e2e_smoke_test` and the other open entropy tests. Those tests bring EDN up in boot mode, grade the data chain, and read the CSRNG and EDN `ERR_CODE` and `RECOV_ALERT_STS` as zero after a boot instantiate (`CHK-ALERTS-ZERO`). This entry grades only the cells that those tests do not grade. The open owners keep the rest. Auto request mode, with `BOOT_REQ_MODE` false: after the software instantiate, the first hardware command reads `HW_CMD_STS.AUTO_MODE` = 1, `BOOT_MODE` = 0 and `CMD_TYPE` Generate when `MAX_NUM_REQS_BETWEEN_RESEEDS` = 1. The first command with that register at 0 is logged only (`OBS-EDN-AUTO-ZERO`), outside the graded coverage window. Out of scope: `CMD_RDY` after a software acknowledge (EDN and CSRNG), the `CMD_STS` code of a CSRNG instantiate, a repeated instantiate or an uninstantiate, the acknowledge of an ignored command, EDN `CSRNG_ACK_ERR` after a repeated instantiate, `HW_EXC_STS`, `CS_HW_INST_EXC` and the `GENBITS` data (logged only). Functional coverage: `sep_edn_mode_cg` (`x_mode_fields`, `cp_reported_mode`, `cp_auto_hw_cmd` including the reseed-after-N cells, `cp_sw_port_hs`, `cp_boot_cmd_reset`, `cp_ctrl_field_alert`, `cp_sw_cmd_sts`), `sep_csrng_cmd_cg` (`x_inst_outcome`, `cp_sw_handshake`, `cp_recov_alert_set`, `cp_sw_cmd_sts`, `cp_reseed_limit`, `cp_genbits_gate`, `cp_kat_generate`, `cp_inst_ack_vs_seed`), `sep_crypto_alert_cg` (`cp_csrng_recov_seq`), `sep_crypto_intr_cg` (`x_line_gate`, `cp_hw_raise` for `cs_cmd_req_done` and `edn_cmd_req_done`). The sampler holds every sample of the leaf windows and samples it only on the rise of the pass label `drbg_seq_pass`, which the leaf writes after every check and control since its last write passed, at the end of each graded instance and before the next window opens. When one of them failed, the leaf writes the drop value 2 instead, which discards every held sample, so a later pass cannot release a sample of the failed instance (docs/SEP_FCOV.adoc, pass labels).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Set the baseline. Write `SEP_RESET_CTRL.SW_RESET_N` = 0x20 (TRNG released; Key Manager and every engine held in reset) and log it. Write `SEP_CPU_CTRL.EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL` = 0x0 (internal source on all streams). Draw the plan and log it. From here to the end of the last leg, a monitor counts each clock with `esrc_compress_vld_o` high as accepted when `drbg_seed_word_ready_o` is high and as dropped otherwise, and notes the number of accepted words before the first dropped one. The leaf logs the counts once at the end of the run (`OBS-DRBG-SEED-INGRESS`).
. Define the clean start used by every leg. Write 0 then 1 to bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N` and wait for the fall of `trng_reset_active_probe_o` (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). Bring up ESRC as the open entropy tests do. Write the CSRNG `CTRL` with `ENABLE` and `SW_APP_ENABLE` true (the multi-bit true code 6) and the other fields at their reset value. Read the CSRNG `RECOV_ALERT_STS`: bit 14 reads 0 (`CTRL-CLEAN-START`; a 1 logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf). The entropy scoreboard runs non-strict for the bring-up only. Every wait below is a poll with the drawn gaps. The first wait of a kind is bounded by an outer cap of 20000 clocks (a cap only, not a contract); the boot waits after the reference boot and the waits of the second command are bounded by 16 times the clean time of the same seed (t0 or the first-command time t_cmd); the vector-bit wait is bounded by an outer cap of 2000 clocks. The leaf logs each bound. At the end of the clean start read `MAIN_SM_STATE` of CSRNG and EDN and log both with the step name `idle` (`OBS-DRBG-MAIN-SM`). The same two reads are logged with the step names `edn_invseq` (after the invalid-sequence command of the EDN software port leg), `csrng_inst_ack` (after the first acknowledge of the CSRNG software instance leg), `csrng_invseq` (after its invalid-sequence command) and `edn_auto` (after the auto request leg).
. Read the EDN `BOOT_INS_CMD` and `BOOT_GEN_CMD` before any write to EDN. Expect 0x901 and 0xFFF003 (edn.adoc:128, :142). Decode each with the command header (acmd [3:0], clen [7:4], flag0 [11:8], glen [23:12]; `theory_of_operation.md:156-188`): expect acmd 0x1 (Instantiate) and 0x3 (Generate), clen 0 in both (edn.adoc:131, :145) and glen 0xFFF (`CHK-EDN-BOOT-CMD`). Then run three groups in the drawn order: the boot group (steps 4 and 5), the EDN software port leg (steps 6 to 12) and the CSRNG software instance leg (steps 13 to 17). Each group starts with the clean start of step 2. After the groups run steps 18 to 28 in that order.
. Reference boot. Run the clean start. Write the EDN `CTRL` with `BOOT_REQ_MODE` true (6), `AUTO_REQ_MODE` false (9) and `EDN_ENABLE` false (9), then write it again with `EDN_ENABLE` true (0x9969, then 0x9966). Count the clocks from the end of the `EDN_ENABLE` write until `HW_CMD_STS.BOOT_MODE` reads 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-EDN-BOOT` with `run=reference`). Log the count as `t0` in `INFO-EDN-TSEED` (a bench record, not graded).
. Graded boot, twice. The boot word `BOOT_INS_CMD` is 0x601 (flag0 True, clen 0, a zero seed) in every boot. For each of the two graded boots, run the boot first with the graded window closed and grade it (`instance=first`). Only after that boot passed, run a second clean start and boot with the graded window open and grade it again (`instance=cov`). Run the clean start. Write the EDN `CTRL` with `BOOT_REQ_MODE` true and `AUTO_REQ_MODE` true (6) in the first run (0x9669, then 0x9666), and with `AUTO_REQ_MODE` false in the second run (0x9969, then 0x9966), `EDN_ENABLE` true last in both. Poll `HW_CMD_STS` (with the drawn gaps) until `BOOT_MODE` reads 1 (a bounded wait of 16 times t0; a timeout fails `CHK-EDN-BOOT`). In that read expect `AUTO_MODE` = 0. Then read the EDN `CTRL` back; the control (`CTRL-EDN-BOOT`) needs the readback equal to the written word, so that both mode fields are stored during the boot. Log the count of clocks.
. EDN software port leg. Run the clean start. Write the EDN `CTRL` with both modes false (9) and `EDN_ENABLE` true last (0x9999, then 0x9996). Write the EDN `INTR_ENABLE` with the `edn_cmd_req_done` bit 1 and read it back. Write 1 to `INTR_STATE.edn_cmd_req_done`, read `INTR_STATE` and expect the bit 0, and expect vector bit 27 (source 28) at 0, so that a rise after the command is caused by the command. Poll `SW_CMD_STS` until `CMD_REG_RDY` = 1 and `CMD_RDY` = 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-EDN-SW-HS`) and log both values.
. Write 0x901 (the instantiate word, the RDL reset value of `BOOT_INS_CMD`) to `SW_CMD_REQ`. Log `SW_CMD_STS.CMD_ACK` of the first read after the write in `OBS-EDN-ACK0` (not graded: a fast acknowledge can make it 1). Poll `SW_CMD_STS` until `CMD_ACK` = 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-EDN-SW-HS`). Log the clocks from the write to that read as t_cmd. Log `CMD_RDY` of that read in `OBS-EDN-RDY-AFTER-ACK` (not graded: no sentence states it). Keep `CMD_STS` of that read for `OBS-EDN-STS` (step 12). Read `INTR_STATE`: expect `edn_cmd_req_done` = 1. Wait for a rise of vector bit 27 after the command write (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-EDN-SW-HS`). Log vector bit 23 (source 24) in `OBS-EDN-VEC` (not graded). Write 1 to `INTR_STATE.edn_cmd_req_done`.
. Control for the injection. Read the CSRNG `RECOV_ALERT_STS`: expect bit 14 = 0 after the first command (`CTRL-EDN-INVSEQ`).
. Error injection: an instantiate to an instantiated instance. Point: `SW_CMD_REQ` of EDN. Kind: an out-of-order command (a second instantiate, word 0x901). Poll `SW_CMD_STS` until `CMD_REG_RDY` = 1 and `CMD_RDY` = 1 first. Write 0x901. Expected response: CSRNG `RECOV_ALERT_STS` bit 14 sets. Poll the CSRNG `RECOV_ALERT_STS` until bit 14 = 1 (a bounded wait of 16 times t_cmd; a timeout fails `CHK-EDN-INVSEQ`).
. Log the effects that the specification does not state: EDN `SW_CMD_STS.CMD_ACK` and `CMD_STS`, EDN `RECOV_ALERT_STS.CSRNG_ACK_ERR`, CSRNG `HW_EXC_STS`, CSRNG `INTR_STATE.cs_hw_inst_exc`, the vector bit 25 (source 26), and `CMD_RDY` afterwards, all in `OBS-EDN-INVSEQ` (not graded).
. Write 0 to the CSRNG `RECOV_ALERT_STS`. Read it: expect bit 14 = 0.
. Control and check for the EDN status. Write 0 to the EDN `RECOV_ALERT_STS`. Read it: expect `CSRNG_ACK_ERR` = 0 (`CTRL-EDN-ACK-ERR`). Write 0 to the CSRNG `RECOV_ALERT_STS` and 1 to EDN `INTR_STATE.edn_cmd_req_done`; read both: expect bit 13 = 0, `edn_cmd_req_done` = 0 and vector bit 27 at 0 (`CTRL-EDN-ACMD-INV`). Read EDN `INTR_ENABLE`: expect `edn_cmd_req_done` = 1 (written in the software port leg). Poll `SW_CMD_STS` until `CMD_REG_RDY` = 1 and `CMD_RDY` = 1 (16 times t_cmd). Write the drawn reserved acmd word (acmd from 0x0, 0x6 to 0xf; clen 0; flag0 0x9) to `SW_CMD_REQ`. Poll until `CMD_ACK` = 1 (16 times t_cmd). Expect `CMD_STS` = 1 (INVALID_ACMD, `csrng.hjson:413-417`) (`CTRL-EDN-STS`). Read the EDN `RECOV_ALERT_STS`: expect `CSRNG_ACK_ERR` = 1 (`CHK-EDN-ACK-ERR`). Read the CSRNG `RECOV_ALERT_STS` and EDN `INTR_STATE`: expect bit 13 = 1 and `edn_cmd_req_done` = 1, and wait for a rise of vector bit 27 after the command write (a bounded wait; no rise fails `CHK-EDN-ACMD-INV`) (`CHK-EDN-ACMD-INV`). Log the `CMD_STS` kept in step 7 in `OBS-EDN-STS` (not graded: the success code is stated only for the sequence with `AUTO_REQ_MODE` = true, `doc/programmer/src/sep-programming.adoc:457-462`, and this leg enables EDN with `AUTO_REQ_MODE` = false and the endpoints in reset).
. CSRNG software instance leg. Run the clean start (EDN stays disabled). Write the CSRNG `INTR_ENABLE` with the `cs_cmd_req_done` bit 1 and read it back. Write 1 to `INTR_STATE.cs_cmd_req_done`, read `INTR_STATE` and expect the bit 0, and expect vector bit 23 at 0. Poll the CSRNG `SW_CMD_STS` until `CMD_RDY` = 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-CSRNG-SW`).
. Write 0x901 to the CSRNG `CMD_REQ`. Log `SW_CMD_STS.CMD_ACK` of the first read after the write in `OBS-CSRNG-ACK0` (not graded). Poll the CSRNG `SW_CMD_STS` until `CMD_ACK` = 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-CSRNG-SW`). Log the clocks from the write to that read as t_cmd. Log `CMD_RDY` of that read in `OBS-CSRNG-RDY-AFTER-ACK` (not graded: no sentence states it). Read the CSRNG `INTR_STATE`: expect `cs_cmd_req_done` = 1. Wait for a rise of vector bit 23 (source 24) after the command write (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-CSRNG-SW`). Log `CMD_STS` and t_cmd in `OBS-CSRNG-STS` (not graded). Write 1 to `cs_cmd_req_done`.
. Control for the injection. Read the CSRNG `RECOV_ALERT_STS`: expect bit 14 = 0 after the first command (`CTRL-CSRNG-SW`).
. Error injection: an instantiate to an instantiated instance. Point: `CMD_REQ` of CSRNG. Kind: an out-of-order command (a second instantiate, word 0x901). Read `INTR_STATE`: expect `cs_cmd_req_done` = 0, and expect vector bit 23 at 0. Poll `SW_CMD_STS` until `CMD_RDY` = 1 first (a bounded wait of 16 times t_cmd). Write 0x901. Expected response: `RECOV_ALERT_STS` bit 14 sets, and the command completes. Poll until bit 14 = 1, then poll `INTR_STATE` until `cs_cmd_req_done` = 1 (each a bounded wait of 16 times t_cmd; a timeout fails `CHK-CSRNG-SW`). Read `INTR_STATE`: expect `cs_cmd_req_done` = 1. Wait for a rise of vector bit 23 after the second command write (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-CSRNG-SW`). Log `CMD_STS` and `HW_EXC_STS` in `OBS-CSRNG-INVSEQ` (not graded).
. Write 0 to the CSRNG `RECOV_ALERT_STS`. Read it: expect bit 14 = 0.
. Field alert leg. Write 0 then 1 to bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N` (the TRNG reset of step 2, no bring-up). For CSRNG (`ENABLE`, `SW_APP_ENABLE`, `READ_INT_STATE`, `FIPS_FORCE_ENABLE`, `RECOV_ALERT_STS` bits 0 to 3), then EDN (`EDN_ENABLE`, `BOOT_REQ_MODE`, `AUTO_REQ_MODE`, `CMD_FIFO_RST`, bits 0 to 3), for each field: write `CTRL` with every field False (9); read `RECOV_ALERT_STS` and expect the field bit 0 (`CTRL-CSRNG-CTRL-MUBI`, `CTRL-EDN-CTRL-MUBI`). Write `CTRL` with that field at its drawn invalid value and the others False. Poll `RECOV_ALERT_STS` until the field bit = 1 (an outer cap of 2000 clocks; a timeout fails the check). Write `CTRL` with every field False, write 0 to `RECOV_ALERT_STS`, read it and expect the field bit 0 (`CHK-CSRNG-CTRL-MUBI`, `CHK-EDN-CTRL-MUBI`).
. Command header leg. Run the clean start. Write the instantiate word 0x901 (flag0 0x9) to `CMD_REQ` and poll until `CMD_ACK` = 1 (outer cap 20000 clocks); log the clocks as t_cmd and `CMD_STS` in `OBS-CSRNG-HDR-INS`. Read `RECOV_ALERT_STS`: expect bit 13 = 0 (`CTRL-CSRNG-ACMD-INV`) and bit 4 = 0 (`CTRL-CSRNG-FLAG0`). Every later wait of this leg is bounded by 16 times t_cmd.
. Error injection: a reserved acmd. Point: `CMD_REQ`. Kind: the drawn reserved acmd (0x0, 0x6, 0x7, 0x8, 0xe or 0xf; clen 0, flag0 0x9). Wait for `CMD_ACK` = 1; expect `CMD_STS` = 1 (INVALID_ACMD). Poll `RECOV_ALERT_STS` until bit 13 = 1. Write 0 to `RECOV_ALERT_STS`; read it and expect bit 13 = 0 (`CHK-CSRNG-ACMD-INV`).
. Write the uninstantiate word 0x905 and wait for `CMD_ACK` (`CMD_STS` logged in `OBS-CSRNG-UNINST`). Write the instantiate word with flag0 0x6 (0x601) and wait for `CMD_ACK`. Read `RECOV_ALERT_STS`: expect bit 4 = 0 (`CTRL-CSRNG-FLAG0`). Write 0x905 again and wait for `CMD_ACK`.
. Error injection: an invalid flag0. Point: `CMD_REQ`. Kind: an instantiate word with the drawn invalid value in [11:8]. Poll `RECOV_ALERT_STS` until bit 4 = 1 (the acknowledge is not awaited; `CMD_ACK` and `CMD_STS` are logged in `OBS-CSRNG-FLAG0-CMD`). Write 0 to `RECOV_ALERT_STS`; read it and expect bit 4 = 0 (`CHK-CSRNG-FLAG0`).
. Reseed leg. Run the clean start. Write `RESEED_INTERVAL` = K. Write 0x901 and wait for `CMD_ACK` (outer cap 20000 clocks, as for every wait of this leg). Then issue up to K + 1 Generates of one block (0x1903: acmd 0x3, flag0 0x9, glen 1). For each Generate, after `CMD_RDY` = 1 write the word, then poll `SW_CMD_STS`; while `CMD_ACK` = 0, read `GENBITS_VLD` and, if it is 1, read `GENBITS` once (data not graded; the acknowledge comes "after all of the requested entropy is consumed", csrng.adoc `SW_CMD_STS`). At `CMD_ACK` = 1 read `CMD_STS` and `RECOV_ALERT_STS`. The first Generate must ack with `CMD_STS` other than 4 and bit 15 = 0 (`CTRL-CSRNG-RESEED-LIMIT`; with K >= 3 it is legal under either reading of "reaches"). Stop at the first Generate with `CMD_STS` = 4.
. Expect the first Generate with `CMD_STS` = 4 to be Generate K + 1, and bit 15 = 1 at its ack. Write 0 to `RECOV_ALERT_STS`; read it and expect bit 15 = 0 (`CHK-CSRNG-RESEED-LIMIT`).
. Write the Reseed word 0x902 (acmd 0x2, clen 0, flag0 0x9) and wait for `CMD_ACK`. Issue one Generate as in step 23, then read `GENBITS` while `GENBITS_VLD` = 1 until four words are read in all: expect `CMD_STS` = 0 and bit 15 = 0 at its ack, and four words (`CHK-CSRNG-RESEED-HDR`; control `CTRL-CSRNG-RESEED-HDR`: the Generate of step 24 was refused).
. `GENBITS` gate. The earlier Generates of this leg leave words in `GENBITS`, so first drain it: read `GENBITS_VLD`; while it reads 1, read `GENBITS` once and read `GENBITS_VLD` again, at most four reads per earlier Generate of this leg (`GENBITS_VLD` "is set when genbits are available on this application interface after a generate command has been issued"; `GENBITS` "must be read four times for each request made", csrng.hjson). Then write 0x1903 after `CMD_RDY` = 1 and poll `GENBITS_VLD` until it reads 1. `CTRL-CSRNG-GENBITS-GATE` requires `GENBITS_VLD` = 0 after the drain and `GENBITS_VLD` = 1 after this Generate. Write `CTRL` with `ENABLE` True and `SW_APP_ENABLE` at the drawn value other than 6. Read `GENBITS_VLD`: expect 1, the block is still pending (`CTRL-CSRNG-GENBITS-GATE`, `pending_after_ctrl`). Read `GENBITS` four times: expect 0 each time (`CHK-CSRNG-GENBITS-GATE`). Log `GENBITS_VLD` in `OBS-CSRNG-GENBITS-GATE`. Read `RECOV_ALERT_STS`: expect bit 1 = 1 when the drawn value is not 9 and 0 when it is 9 (it read 0 before the `CTRL` write) (`CHK-CSRNG-GENBITS-GATE`). Known-answer control: write `CTRL` with `ENABLE` and `SW_APP_ENABLE` True, drain `GENBITS` as above, write the uninstantiate word 0x905 and wait for `CMD_ACK`, write the instantiate word with clen 12 and flag0 True (0x6c1) followed by the twelve words of the drawn 384-bit seed, least significant word first (`programmers_guide.md:37-61`), and wait for `CMD_ACK`. Write 0x1903 and read `GENBITS` four times, each after `GENBITS_VLD` reads 1. Expect the four words of the first 128-bit block of the SP 800-90A CTR_DRBG golden (`env/sep_ctr_drbg_golden.py`) instantiated with that seed, least significant word first (`programmers_guide.md:108-114`) (`CHK-CSRNG-KAT`). The graded window is open over this step and closes before the checks of the leg are graded.
. Auto request mode, before the reserved-acmd leg. Run the clean start. Write `CTRL` with `CMD_FIFO_RST` true and the other fields false, then with `AUTO_REQ_MODE` true and `EDN_ENABLE` false. Write `GENERATE_CMD` = 0x1903 (Generate, glen 1), `RESEED_CMD` = 0x902 (Reseed, clen 0, flag0 0x9) and `MAX_NUM_REQS_BETWEEN_RESEEDS` = 1. Write `CTRL` with `BOOT_REQ_MODE` false, `AUTO_REQ_MODE` true and `EDN_ENABLE` true. Write 1 to `INTR_STATE.edn_cmd_req_done` and read it: expect 0. Read `HW_CMD_STS`: expect `AUTO_MODE` = 0 (`CTRL-EDN-AUTO`, with the `edn_cmd_req_done` read; the instantiate is not the hardware-controlled part). Poll `SW_CMD_STS` until `CMD_REG_RDY` = 1 and `CMD_RDY` = 1, write 0x901 and poll until `CMD_ACK` = 1 (each wait is bounded by 20000 clocks). Read `INTR_STATE`: expect `edn_cmd_req_done` = 1 (`CHK-EDN-AUTO`). Poll `HW_CMD_STS` with no idle gap until the first read with `AUTO_MODE` = 1 and a `CMD_TYPE` other than 0 and Instantiate (a bounded wait of 60000 clocks; a timeout fails `CHK-EDN-AUTO`); expect `CMD_TYPE` = 3 (Generate) and `BOOT_MODE` = 0. Write `CTRL` with every field false. Run the auto-sequence trial with N = 1 and then with the drawn N (2 to 4): the clean start, the FIFO clear, `GENERATE_CMD` = 0x1903, `RESEED_CMD` = 0x902 and `MAX_NUM_REQS_BETWEEN_RESEEDS` = N, `CTRL` with `AUTO_REQ_MODE` true and `EDN_ENABLE` true, and a wait for `CMD_REG_RDY` and `CMD_RDY`. The host starts a watch on the EDN to CSRNG command port, writes the instantiate 0x901 to `SW_CMD_REQ`, and collects the command headers on the port (a bounded wait of 60000 clocks). The first header must be the instantiate (`CTRL-EDN-AUTO-TAP`, the positive probe that the watch sees the port) and the next N + 2 headers must be N generates (0x1903), the reseed (0x902) and a generate (`CHK-EDN-AUTO-SEQ`). After a PASS the host opens the graded window for the one clock edge that takes the last handshake and closes it. Write `CTRL` with every field false. Repeat the clean start, the FIFO clear and the command writes with `MAX_NUM_REQS_BETWEEN_RESEEDS` = 0. After the same instantiate, poll the same way and log the first hardware command in `OBS-EDN-AUTO-ZERO` (not graded: edn.adoc:107 says the first command is a generate, and `edn.hjson:480-484` describes a down-counter that requests a reseed when it reaches zero; no sentence states the first command for the value 0). Write `CTRL` with every field false.
. Instantiate-seed leg. Close the graded window. Run a TRNG reset (no bring-up) and configure ESRC with its generators off, so that no seed exists. Write the CSRNG `CTRL` with `ENABLE` and `SW_APP_ENABLE` true, write the EDN `CTRL` with every field false and then with `AUTO_REQ_MODE` true and `EDN_ENABLE` true (`BOOT_REQ_MODE` false, as `sep-programming.adoc:457-462` states). Write 1 to `INTR_ENABLE.edn_cmd_req_done`, clear `INTR_STATE`, and read both back. Wait for `CMD_REG_RDY` and `CMD_RDY`, start the port watch, open the graded window one clock before the write, and write the instantiate 0x901 to `SW_CMD_REQ`. Hold for the drawn H clocks (256 to 2048). In the hold the instantiate must be on the EDN to CSRNG port, and there must be no seed handshake (`drbg_seed_valid_o` with `drbg_es_ack_o`, both read with no unknown bit allowed), no `CMD_ACK` and no rise of vector bit 27. A seed handshake, an unknown bit on either seed handshake signal or a rise of bit 27 in the hold closes the graded window at once. Then enable the generators. The first seed handshake must come before the acknowledge; after it `CMD_ACK` reads 1 with `CMD_STS` = 0, `INTR_STATE.edn_cmd_req_done` reads 1, the next command on the port is a generate, and `HW_CMD_STS` reads `AUTO_MODE` = 1 and `BOOT_MODE` = 0 (`CHK-EDN-INST-SEED`). Close the graded window and write `CTRL` with every field false.
. Reserved acmd 0x9 leg, after every other graded check. Run the clean start. Write 0x901 and wait for `CMD_ACK` (outer cap 20000 clocks); log the clocks as t_cmd. Write the reserved acmd 0x9 (word 0x909, fixed on every seed) and wait for `CMD_ACK` (16 times t_cmd). Read `RECOV_ALERT_STS`. Log `CMD_STS` and `RECOV_ALERT_STS` in `OBS-CSRNG-ACMD-HI` (not graded; see its row).
. Enable-order leg, after the reserved acmd 0x9 leg, with the graded window closed. Run a TRNG reset (no bring-up). Drive the raw noise from the leaf, as the instantiate-seed leg does. Write the EDN `CTRL` with `EDN_ENABLE` true and both modes false, then the CSRNG `CTRL` with `ENABLE` and `SW_APP_ENABLE` true, then configure ESRC and enable its generators. Wait for `SW_CMD_STS.CMD_REG_RDY` = 1 and `CMD_RDY` = 1 of EDN (a bounded wait of 20000 clocks), write the instantiate word 0x901 to the EDN `SW_CMD_REQ` and wait for `CMD_ACK` = 1 (the same bound). Read the EDN and CSRNG `SW_CMD_STS`, `ERR_CODE` and `RECOV_ALERT_STS` and log them (`OBS-DRBG-ENABLE-ORDER`). Write the EDN `CTRL` with every field false.
. Bus compare replay leg, after the enable-order leg, with the graded window closed. Run the clean start. Write 0x901 and wait for `CMD_ACK`. Run the known-answer Generate of step 26 (Uninstantiate, Instantiate with flag0 True, clen 12 and the drawn seed, Generate glen 1, four `GENBITS` reads, the acknowledge). Run it again with the same seed; between the Instantiate acknowledge and the Generate, write 0 to `RECOV_ALERT_STS` and read it. After the Generate acknowledge read `RECOV_ALERT_STS`. Control `CTRL-CSRNG-BUS-CMP`; bit 12 is logged (`OBS-CSRNG-BUS-CMP`). On a passed control write the open-value leg `csrng_bus_cmp_replay` (held two clocks).
. Counter-wrap leg, on the instance of the replay leg. Compute C[31:0] from the golden (an Instantiate with seed word 0 = 0) and set seed word 0 = C[31:0] XOR 0xfffffffe, so V[31:0] = 0xfffffffe after the Instantiate. Write `CTRL` with `ENABLE` and `SW_APP_ENABLE` true. Uninstantiate; Instantiate with flag0 True, clen 12 and this seed; Generate with glen 2; read eight `GENBITS` words (each after `GENBITS_VLD` = 1); wait for the acknowledge; Uninstantiate. Control `CTRL-CSRNG-VCTR-WRAP` on block 1; on a passed control, block 2 is graded against block 1 (`CHK-CSRNG-VCTR-DISTINCT`) and logged with its candidates (`OBS-CSRNG-VCTR-WRAP`). On a passed control write the open-value leg `csrng_vctr_wrap` (held two clocks).
. Log the `RESULT` line.

*Checkers*:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|CHK-EDN-BOOT
|Each graded boot runs twice, first with the graded window closed (`instance=first`) and, only after that passed, again with the window open (`instance=cov`); both instances have the same golden. With `CTRL.BOOT_REQ_MODE` true and `AUTO_REQ_MODE` true or false, the read of `HW_CMD_STS` that first shows `BOOT_MODE` = 1 shows `AUTO_MODE` = 0, and `BOOT_MODE` = 1 shows within a bounded wait (`edn.adoc`, `CTRL`: boot mode takes precedence over auto mode; `HW_CMD_STS`). A DUT that gives auto mode precedence, reports `AUTO_MODE` = 1 in boot mode, or never enters boot mode (the wait times out), fails. Control: `CTRL-EDN-BOOT` (the `CTRL` readback after the `BOOT_MODE` read equals the written word, so a DUT that drops the `AUTO_REQ_MODE` write cannot pass the both-modes boot as a boot-only one). The wait of each graded boot is bounded by 16 times t0 of the reference boot. The clock counts are logged and not graded. Anchor: `HW_CMD_STS` readback. Evidence: `INFO-EDN-TSEED seed=<n> tseed_clk=<t0> auto_mode=<a>` and, for each graded boot, `CHK-EDN-BOOT PASS seed=<n> ctrl=0x<c> boot_mode=1 auto_mode=0 boot_clk=<t> clean=<t0> bound=<16 t0> rsvd=0x<r>`.
|CHK-EDN-AUTO
|With `AUTO_REQ_MODE` true and `BOOT_REQ_MODE` false, "the first `generate` command is sent out automatically" once CSRNG acknowledges the software instantiate (`edn.adoc:107`, Auto Request Mode; `HW_CMD_STS`: `AUTO_MODE` is 1 in the hardware-controlled part and `CMD_TYPE` is the application command issued last). With `MAX_NUM_REQS_BETWEEN_RESEEDS` = 1 the first `HW_CMD_STS` read with `AUTO_MODE` = 1 and a hardware command type (not 0, not Instantiate) reads `CMD_TYPE` = 3 (Generate) and `BOOT_MODE` = 0 ("0b0: The EDN is not in boot mode.", edn.adoc:214-215; `BOOT_REQ_MODE` is false). After the `CMD_ACK` of the software instantiate, `INTR_STATE.edn_cmd_req_done` reads 1 ("Asserted when a software CSRNG request has completed.", `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/edn/data/edn.hjson:30-31`). A DUT that stays in software port mode, reports boot mode, or whose first hardware command is not the generate (for example a reseed first), fails. Anchor: `HW_CMD_STS` readback, back-to-back reads. Control: `AUTO_MODE` reads 0 and `edn_cmd_req_done` reads 0 after a write of 1, before the instantiate (`CTRL-EDN-AUTO`). The first hardware command with the register at 0 is logged only (`OBS-EDN-AUTO-ZERO`). Evidence: `CHK-EDN-AUTO PASS seed=<n> sw_done=1 first_type=3 first_auto=1 first_boot=0 first_ack=<a> expected_type=3`.
|CHK-EDN-SW-HS
|In software port mode `SW_CMD_STS.CMD_REG_RDY` and `CMD_RDY` read 1 before the write of 0x901 (`edn.adoc`: both reset to 0 and are polled before a write). After the write, within a bounded wait, `CMD_ACK` reads 1, and `INTR_STATE.edn_cmd_req_done` reads 1 (`edn.adoc`: `CMD_ACK` goes high once CSRNG acknowledges the command). `CMD_RDY` in the read that shows `CMD_ACK` is logged only (`OBS-EDN-RDY-AFTER-ACK`): the text states only that firmware polls `CMD_RDY` before a command, not its value after the acknowledge. With `INTR_ENABLE.edn_cmd_req_done` = 1, vector bit 27 (source 28) rises to 1 within a bounded wait after the command write (`interrupts.adoc`, source N on bit N - 1). Before the command the leaf clears `edn_cmd_req_done` by write-one and requires the state bit and vector bit 27 at 0, so the rise is caused by the command. A DUT that never raises `CMD_REG_RDY` or `CMD_RDY`, never acknowledges, does not raise the interrupt state, holds the state or the line at 1, or does not raise the line with the state and the enable at 1, fails. The `CMD_ACK` of the first read after the write is logged only: `CMD_ACK` resets to 0, so that read cannot separate a DUT that holds it high, and its value depends on the acknowledge timing that the specification does not state. Anchor: `SW_CMD_STS` and `INTR_STATE` readback. Evidence: `CHK-EDN-SW-HS PASS seed=<n> cmd_reg_rdy=1 cmd_rdy=1 ack_done=1 edn_done=1 enable=1 vec27=1 edn_done_pre0=1 vec27_pre0=1 vec_clk=<v>`; `OBS-EDN-ACK0 seed=<n> ack_first_read=<a>`; `OBS-EDN-RDY-AFTER-ACK seed=<n> cmd_rdy=<r>`.
|CHK-EDN-INVSEQ
|The second 0x901 through EDN sets CSRNG `RECOV_ALERT_STS` bit 14, and a write of 0 clears it (`csrng.adoc`, `RECOV_ALERT_STS`: an instantiate to an instantiated state sets the bit; writing zero resets it). A DUT that accepts the second instantiate silently, or does not clear the bit, fails. Anchor: CSRNG register readback. Control: bit 14 reads 0 after the first command (`CTRL-EDN-INVSEQ`, and in the CHK line), so the 1 follows the second command. Bit 14 also reads 1 immediately before the write of 0 (`set_before_clear=1`), so the clear is a clear of a set bit. Evidence: `CHK-EDN-INVSEQ PASS seed=<n> ctrl_first_invseq=0 csrng_invseq=1 set_before_clear=1 cleared_by_write0=1 bound=<16 t_cmd>`; the unstated effects go to `OBS-EDN-INVSEQ seed=<n> cmd_ack=<a> cmd_sts=<s> ack_err=<e> hw_exc=0x<h> hw_inst_exc=<i> vec25=<v> cmd_rdy_after=<r>`.
|CHK-CSRNG-SW
|For the CSRNG software instance `SW_CMD_STS.CMD_RDY` reads 1 before the first 0x901 (`csrng.adoc`: it resets to 0 and is polled before a write). After the write, within a bounded wait, `CMD_ACK` reads 1 and `INTR_STATE.cs_cmd_req_done` = 1; the second 0x901 sets `RECOV_ALERT_STS` bit 14 (`csrng.adoc`, `SW_CMD_STS`, `RECOV_ALERT_STS`). A write of 0 clears bit 14. The second 0x901 also completes: a command completes "successfully or unsuccessfully" (`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/csrng/doc/theory_of_operation.md:286`) and `cs_cmd_req_done` "will assert when a CSRNG command has been completed" (`:421`), so after it `cs_cmd_req_done` reads 1 and vector bit 23 rises again; before it both read 0 (the first command's bit is cleared by write-one). With `INTR_ENABLE.cs_cmd_req_done` = 1, vector bit 23 (source 24) rises to 1 within a bounded wait after the command write (`interrupts.adoc`, source N on bit N - 1). Before the command the leaf clears `cs_cmd_req_done` by write-one and requires the state bit and vector bit 23 at 0, so the rise is caused by the command. A DUT that does not become ready, does not acknowledge the first command, takes the second one without the alert, does not clear the alert on a write of 0, holds the state or the line at 1, or does not raise the line with the state and the enable at 1 after either command, fails. The `CMD_ACK` of the first read after the write is logged only, for the reason given in `CHK-EDN-SW-HS`. `CMD_RDY` in the read that shows `CMD_ACK` is logged only (`OBS-CSRNG-RDY-AFTER-ACK`), for the reason given in `CHK-EDN-SW-HS`. Anchor: CSRNG register readback. Control: bit 14 reads 0 after the first command (`CTRL-CSRNG-SW`). Evidence: `CHK-CSRNG-SW PASS seed=<n> cmd_rdy=1 ack=1 done=1 enable=1 vec23=1 done2_pre0=1 vec23_2_pre0=1 done2=1 vec23_2=1 done_pre0=1 vec23_pre0=1 vec_clk=<v> bound=<16 t_cmd> vec_clk2=<v> ctrl_first_invseq=0 invseq=1 cleared_by_write0=1`; `OBS-CSRNG-ACK0 seed=<n> ack_first_read=<a>`; `OBS-CSRNG-RDY-AFTER-ACK seed=<n> cmd_rdy=<r>`; the unstated effects go to `OBS-CSRNG-INVSEQ seed=<n> cmd_sts=<s> hw_exc=0x<h>`.
|CHK-EDN-BOOT-CMD
|Before any write, `BOOT_INS_CMD` reads 0x901 and `BOOT_GEN_CMD` reads 0xFFF003 (edn.adoc:128, :142). Decoded with the stated header (acmd [3:0], clen [7:4], glen [23:12]; `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/csrng/doc/theory_of_operation.md:156-188`), the words are an Instantiate (0x1) and a Generate (0x3) with clen 0 ("the hardware only supports a value of 0 for the `clen` field", edn.adoc:131, :145) and glen 0xFFF. A DUT with another reset value or a non-zero clen fails. Anchor: EDN register readback. Evidence: `CHK-EDN-BOOT-CMD PASS seed=<n> boot_ins=0x901 boot_gen=0xfff003 ins_acmd=1 ins_clen=0 ins_flag0=0x9 gen_acmd=3 gen_clen=0 gen_glen=0xfff`.
|CTRL-EDN-STS, OBS-EDN-STS
|Not a graded check. Control: a reserved acmd through `SW_CMD_REQ` acks with `CMD_STS` = 1 (INVALID_ACMD, `csrng.hjson:413-417`; EDN `CMD_STS` uses the CSRNG codes, edn.adoc:172-176) (`CTRL-EDN-STS`), so the non-zero acknowledge of `CHK-EDN-ACK-ERR` is the stated code; another code logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf. The `CMD_STS` at the `CMD_ACK` of the first EDN instantiate is logged only (`OBS-EDN-STS`): "require `CMD_STS` to report success" (`doc/programmer/src/sep-programming.adoc:457-462`) is stated for the sequence with `AUTO_REQ_MODE` = true, and this leg enables EDN with `AUTO_REQ_MODE` = false and the endpoints in reset. Evidence: `CTRL-EDN-STS seen: seed=<n> word=0x9<a> cmd_sts=1`; `OBS-EDN-STS seed=<n> cmd_sts=<s> word=0x901 auto_req_mode=false reason=success_stated_only_with_auto_req_mode_true`.
|CHK-EDN-ACK-ERR
|After the reserved acmd acknowledge with `CMD_STS` = 1, EDN `RECOV_ALERT_STS.CSRNG_ACK_ERR` reads 1: "This bit is set when the CSRNG returns an acknowledgement where the status signal is non-zero. Writing a zero resets this status bit." (`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/edn/data/edn.hjson:542-543`, the same text in `edn.rdl` `RECOV_ALERT_STS`); code 1 is "Request completed with an invalid application command error" (`csrng.hjson:415-416`). A DUT that does not latch the error status of the acknowledge fails. Anchor: EDN `RECOV_ALERT_STS` readback. Control: after a write of 0 and before the command the bit reads 0 (`CTRL-EDN-ACK-ERR`; a 1 logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf). A run with no acknowledge, or an acknowledge with `CMD_STS` = 0, fails the check. Evidence: `CTRL-EDN-ACK-ERR seen: seed=<n> ctrl_pre_ack_err=0`; `CHK-EDN-ACK-ERR PASS seed=<n> word=0x9<a> cmd_sts=1 ack_err=1 expected=1 ctrl_pre_ack_err=0`.
|CHK-EDN-ACMD-INV
|After the acknowledge of the same reserved acmd, sent through the EDN software port, CSRNG `RECOV_ALERT_STS` bit 13 reads 1 ("When sending an unsupported or illegal command, `CMD_STAGE_INVALID_ACMD_ALERT` will be triggered", `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/csrng/doc/theory_of_operation.md:50-51`; bit 13 "is set when an unsupported/illegal CSRNG command is received by the main state machine", `csrng.hjson:663-664`) and EDN `INTR_STATE.edn_cmd_req_done` reads 1 ("Asserted when a software CSRNG request has completed.", `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/edn/data/edn.hjson:30-31`; the request completes at its acknowledge, also with an error status). With `INTR_ENABLE.edn_cmd_req_done` read as 1, vector bit 27 (source 28) rises after the command write (`interrupts.adoc`, source N on bit N - 1). A CSRNG that does not flag a reserved acmd from the EDN interface, or an EDN that does not report the completed software request in the state bit or on the line, fails. Anchor: CSRNG `RECOV_ALERT_STS` and EDN `INTR_STATE` readback, the vector bit watch. Control: before the command, bit 13 reads 0 after a write of 0, and `edn_cmd_req_done` and vector bit 27 read 0 after a write of 1 (`CTRL-EDN-ACMD-INV`). Evidence: `CTRL-EDN-ACMD-INV seen: seed=<n> ctrl_pre_acmd13=0 ctrl_pre_edn_done=0 ctrl_pre_vec27=0`; `CHK-EDN-ACMD-INV PASS seed=<n> word=0x9<a> csrng_acmd_alert=1 edn_done=1 enable=1 vec27=1 vec_clk=<v> ctrl_pre_acmd13=0 ctrl_pre_edn_done=0 ctrl_pre_vec27=0`.
|CHK-CSRNG-CTRL-MUBI, CHK-EDN-CTRL-MUBI
|For each `CTRL` field, a write of a value other than 6 or 9 sets its `RECOV_ALERT_STS` bit (CSRNG bits 0 to 3: csrng.adoc:343-356; EDN bits 0 to 3: edn.adoc:280-291), and "Writing a zero resets this status bit": after the field is legal again, a write of 0 reads the bit 0. A DUT that ignores an invalid code, maps it to the wrong bit, or keeps the bit after the write of 0 fails. The other bits are not graded in this trial. Anchor: `RECOV_ALERT_STS` readback. Control: the bit reads 0 with every field False before the invalid write (`CTRL-CSRNG-CTRL-MUBI`, `CTRL-EDN-CTRL-MUBI`). Evidence, one line per field: `CHK-CSRNG-CTRL-MUBI PASS seed=<n> field=<f> value=0x<v> ctrl=0x<c> alert_set=1 recov=0x<r> cleared_by_write0=1`, the same for `CHK-EDN-CTRL-MUBI`.
|CHK-CSRNG-ACMD-INV
|A command word with a reserved acmd (the drawn 0x0, 0x6, 0x7, 0x8, 0xe or 0xf: the reserved values whose bits [2:0] are also reserved, see `OBS-CSRNG-ACMD-HI`; `theory_of_operation.md:277-279`) sets `RECOV_ALERT_STS` bit 13 ("an unsupported/illegal CSRNG command", csrng.adoc:335-338) and acks with `CMD_STS` = 1 ("Request completed with an invalid application command error", `csrng.hjson:413-417`); a write of 0 clears bit 13. A DUT that runs the word as a command, gives another code, or keeps the bit fails. Anchor: `SW_CMD_STS` and `RECOV_ALERT_STS` readback. Control: bit 13 reads 0 after the instantiate (`CTRL-CSRNG-ACMD-INV`). Evidence: `CHK-CSRNG-ACMD-INV PASS seed=<n> word=0x9<a> alert_set=1 cmd_sts=1 cleared_by_write0=1`.
|CHK-CSRNG-FLAG0
|An instantiate with a value other than 6 or 9 in [11:8] sets `RECOV_ALERT_STS` bit 4 ("the FLAG0 field in the Application Command is set to a value other than kMultiBitBool4True or kMultiBitBool4False", csrng.adoc:343-345); a write of 0 clears it. This grades the FLAG0 position (`theory_of_operation.md:172`) and its encoding (True 6, False 9, `sep-programming.adoc:390-391`). A DUT that decodes flag0 from other bits or with other codes fails a check or a control. Anchor: `RECOV_ALERT_STS` readback. Control: bit 4 reads 0 after an instantiate with flag0 0x9 and after one with flag0 0x6 (`CTRL-CSRNG-FLAG0`, two lines). Evidence: `CHK-CSRNG-FLAG0 PASS seed=<n> word=0x<w> alert_set=1 cleared_by_write0=1`; `OBS-CSRNG-FLAG0-CMD seed=<n> cmd_ack=<a> cmd_sts=<s> recov=0x<r>`.
|CHK-CSRNG-RESEED-LIMIT
|`RESEED_INTERVAL` = K sets "the number of generate requests that can be made to CSRNG before a reseed request needs to be made" (csrng.adoc:138-139), and the counter "counts the number of generate commands between reseed or instantiate commands. If the counter reaches the value of this register, the violating command will be acknowledged with a status error" (csrng.adoc:141-144). K Generates can be made, so the violating command is Generate K + 1: the first Generate after the instantiate that acks with `CMD_STS` = 4 (RESEED_CNT_EXCEEDED, `csrng.hjson:436-440`) is Generate K + 1, and `RECOV_ALERT_STS` bit 15 reads 1 at that ack (csrng.adoc:325-328); a write of 0 clears bit 15. A DUT that never refuses, refuses at Generate K or earlier, refuses with another code, or does not set or clear bit 15 fails. Anchor: `SW_CMD_STS` and `RECOV_ALERT_STS` readback. Control: the first Generate acks with `CMD_STS` other than 4 and bit 15 = 0 (`CTRL-CSRNG-RESEED-LIMIT`; K >= 3). Evidence: `CHK-CSRNG-RESEED-LIMIT PASS seed=<n> k=<K> max_generates=<K+1> expected_refused_index=<K+1> refused_index=<K+1> cmd_sts=[...] alert15=[...] words=[...] cleared_by_write0=1`.
|CHK-CSRNG-RESEED-HDR
|After the Reseed word 0x902 (acmd 0x2 "Reseed", clen 0, flag0 0x9: the General Command Format that EDN `RESEED_CMD` also takes, edn.adoc:225-230), the counter restarts (csrng.adoc:141-142), so the next Generate (glen 1) succeeds: it acks with `CMD_STS` = 0 ("If the command is successful, the `csrng_rsp_sts` signal will indicate the value 0 (`SUCCESS`)", `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/csrng/doc/theory_of_operation.md:287`; "Request completed successfully.", `csrng.hjson:406-410`), bit 15 = 0, and `GENBITS` gives four words (glen 1 "returns 1 * 128 bits of entropy", theory_of_operation.md:185; "This register must be read four times for each request made.", `csrng.hjson:474-476`). A DUT that does not decode the word as a Reseed keeps the count at the limit and refuses the Generate; a DUT that decodes it as another command (for example an Uninstantiate) acks the Generate with another code or no data. Anchor: `SW_CMD_STS` and `RECOV_ALERT_STS` readback. Control: the Generate before the Reseed was refused (`CTRL-CSRNG-RESEED-HDR`). Evidence: `CHK-CSRNG-RESEED-HDR PASS seed=<n> word=0x902 reseed_cmd_sts=<s> cmd_sts=0 alert15=0 words=4 expected_sts=0 expected_words=4`.
|OBS-CSRNG-ACMD-HI
|Not a graded check. The text reserves 0x0 and 0x6 to 0xf of the 4-bit acmd (`theory_of_operation.md:156-158`, :277-279), but the OpenTitan CSRNG decodes acmd bits [2:0] only, and its designers state that bit 3 is claimed for future use and that a reserved value gives an undefined result (lowRISC/opentitan issue 16203). No text states the result of a reserved value whose bits [2:0] are a defined command (0x9 to 0xd), so the leaf grades only the reserved values whose bits [2:0] are also reserved (`CHK-CSRNG-ACMD-INV`) and logs the result of 0x9. Evidence: `OBS-CSRNG-ACMD-HI seed=<n> word=0x909 cmd_sts=<s> recov=0x<v>`.
|CHK-EDN-AUTO-SEQ
|With `AUTO_REQ_MODE` true, `BOOT_REQ_MODE` false and `MAX_NUM_REQS_BETWEEN_RESEEDS` = N (N = 1, then the drawn N from 2 to 4), the command headers on the EDN to CSRNG port after the software instantiate are exactly N generates, one reseed and one generate, compared as whole 32-bit words: `GENERATE_CMD` = 0x1903 N times, then `RESEED_CMD` = 0x902, then 0x1903. A reseed after N + 1 generates, an early reseed, no reseed, or a forwarded word that differs from the staged word fails. Golden: `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/edn/regs/gen/adoc/edn.adoc:107` ("a `reseed` command is sent after every MAX_NUM_REQS_BETWEEN_RESEEDS number of `generate` commands") and `:258-262` (the down-counter triggers an automatic `reseed` request when it reaches zero). Anchor: the EDN to CSRNG command port tap. Control: `CTRL-EDN-AUTO-TAP`. Graded at `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/crypto/sep_drbg_cmd_sequence_rand_test.py:980-982`. Evidence: `CHK-EDN-AUTO-SEQ PASS n=<N> max_num_reqs=<N> gens=<N> acmds=[..] words=[..] expected=[..]`.
|CTRL-EDN-AUTO-TAP
|The first command on the EDN to CSRNG port in each auto-sequence trial is the software instantiate 0x901. Positive probe that the port watch sees the port, so the sequence compare is not graded on an empty record. A missing control fails the leaf. Logged as `n`, `first_word` and `expected=0x901`.
|CHK-CSRNG-KAT
|With `SW_APP_ENABLE` true, the Generate that follows an instantiate with flag0 True and twelve drawn seed words returns the four `GENBITS` words of the first block of the AES-256 CTR_DRBG of SP 800-90A without a derivation function, instantiated with that seed, least significant word first, and that Generate acknowledges with `CMD_STS` = 0. Golden: `env/sep_ctr_drbg_golden.py`; `hw/ip/drbg/doc/architecture.adoc:14` (AES-CTR-DRBG of 800-90A); `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/csrng/doc/theory_of_operation.md:227` (flag0 True: "Only provided additional data will be used as seed"). A DUT that deviates from the CTR_DRBG, mixes the seed differently or returns the words in another order fails; so does a Generate that does not acknowledge within the bound or acknowledges with another status (`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/csrng/doc/theory_of_operation.md:287`, `SUCCESS` is 0). Graded at `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/crypto/sep_drbg_cmd_sequence_rand_test.py:2010-2016`. Evidence: `CHK-CSRNG-KAT PASS ... drained=<d> ins_cmd_sts=<s> gen_cmd_sts=0 genbits=[..] expected=[..] expected_gen_sts=0`.
|CHK-EDN-INST-SEED
|With the ESRC generators off, an EDN instantiate written to `SW_CMD_REQ` reaches the EDN to CSRNG port, and for the drawn hold H there is no seed handshake, no `CMD_ACK` and no rise of vector bit 27. In the hold both seed handshake signals are read on every clock with no unknown bit allowed, so an X or Z on either fails rather than reading as no handshake. After the generators are on, the first seed handshake comes before the acknowledge; then `CMD_ACK` reads 1 with `CMD_STS` = 0, `INTR_STATE.edn_cmd_req_done` reads 1, the next command on the port is a generate, and `HW_CMD_STS` reads `AUTO_MODE` = 1 and `BOOT_MODE` = 0. Before the write, `CMD_REG_RDY`, `CMD_RDY` read 1, `INTR_ENABLE` reads the written enable, `edn_cmd_req_done` reads 0 and vector bit 27 is 0. An acknowledge with no seed, an acknowledge before the seed handshake, or an acknowledge with a non-zero status fails. Golden: `doc/programmer/src/sep-programming.adoc:466-468` ("CSRNG cannot acknowledge that command until it has accepted a complete ESRC seed"), `:457-462` (the sequence with `AUTO_REQ_MODE` true; `CMD_STS` reports success) and `vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/edn/regs/gen/adoc/edn.adoc:107` (the next command is a generate). Anchor: the seed-port taps `drbg_seed_valid_o` and `drbg_es_ack_o`, the EDN to CSRNG command port tap, and register readback. Control: the instantiate is seen on the port in the hold (positive probe that the instantiate was issued before the "no seed" claim), and the acknowledge rises once the generators are on. Graded at `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/crypto/sep_drbg_cmd_sequence_rand_test.py:1215`. Evidence: `CHK-EDN-INST-SEED PASS hold=<H> hold_clocks=<c> hold_cmd_ack=0 ins_req_clk=<c> hs_clk=<c> ack_clk=<c> cmd_ack=1 cmd_sts=0 edn_done=1 next_word=0x<w> auto_mode=1 boot_mode=0 ...`.
|CHK-CSRNG-GENBITS-GATE
|"for !!GENBITS to be able to deliver random numbers, also !!CTRL.SW_APP_ENABLE needs to be set to `kMultiBitBool4True`. ... Otherwise, the register reads as 0." (csrng.adoc:223-225): with a Generate pending and `SW_APP_ENABLE` at a value other than 6, four `GENBITS` reads return 0. That Generate is the second since the Reseed, inside the reseed interval, and after the gated reads it acknowledges with `CMD_STS` = 0 (`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/csrng/doc/theory_of_operation.md:287`). The `CTRL` write sets `RECOV_ALERT_STS` bit 1 when `SW_APP_ENABLE` is "a value other than kMultiBitBool4True or kMultiBitBool4False" (`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/overlay/regs/csrng/regs/gen/adoc/csrng.adoc:351-353`): bit 1 reads 0 before the write and, after it, 1 for a drawn value other than 9, 0 for 9. A DUT that does not gate the read, does not acknowledge the Generate with success within the bound, or does not flag the invalid field (or flags False), fails. Anchor: `GENBITS` readback. Control: after the drain `GENBITS_VLD` reads 0, and after the leg's own Generate it reads 1, so the pending data comes from that Generate; after the `CTRL` write that sets `SW_APP_ENABLE` not True it still reads 1 ("This bit is set when genbits are available on this application interface after a generate command has been issued", `csrng.hjson` `GENBITS_VLD`, with no `SW_APP_ENABLE` term), so the gated reads hit pending data; and with `SW_APP_ENABLE` True a known-answer Generate returns the golden words, so the data path is live and a zero read comes from the gate (`CTRL-CSRNG-GENBITS-GATE`, `kat=known_answer`). The golden: flag0 True with clen 12 uses "Only provided additional data ... as seed" (`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/csrng/doc/theory_of_operation.md:227`); "This block does not use a derivation function" (`.../csrng/README.md:47`); AES-256 at "256 bit security strength" (`README.md:35`). Evidence: `CHK-CSRNG-GENBITS-GATE PASS seed=<n> sw_app_enable=0x<v> drained=<d> vld_after_drain=0 sw_app_alert_pre=0 genbits=[0x0,0x0,0x0,0x0] sw_app_alert=<a> gen_cmd_sts=0 expected_gen_sts=0 expected_sw_app_alert=<a>`; `OBS-CSRNG-GENBITS-GATE seed=<n> genbits_vld_after=<v> sw_app_read_en=0x<v> lc_hw_debug_en=0x<v> lc_source=<skip_fuse_sense or fuse>` (not graded: `hw/ip/drbg/doc/architecture.adoc:47` says these signals gate the software application read by life-cycle state, and no text states their driver or their value per life-cycle state; the leaf reads the bench ports `csrng_sw_app_read_en_o` and `csrng_lc_hw_debug_en_o` and logs the run's life-cycle source, `+skip_fuse_sense` in this entry); `CTRL-CSRNG-GENBITS-GATE seen: seed=<n> pending_after_ctrl=1 sw_app_enable=0x<v>`; `CTRL-CSRNG-GENBITS-GATE seen: seed=<n> kat=known_answer drained=<d> ins_cmd_sts=<s> genbits=[...] expected=[...]`.
|OBS-DRBG-MAIN-SM
|Not a graded check. No text states the encoding of `MAIN_SM_STATE` of CSRNG or EDN. The leaf reads both registers at the steps named in step 2 and logs the value. Evidence: `OBS-DRBG-MAIN-SM seed=<n> unit=<csrng or edn> step=<idle, edn_invseq, csrng_inst_ack, csrng_invseq or edn_auto> state=0x<s>`.
|OBS-DRBG-ENABLE-ORDER
|Not a graded check. No text states the result of enabling EDN first, then CSRNG, then ESRC, and of an EDN software instantiate after it. The leaf logs whether the acknowledge arrived and the status, error code and recoverable alert registers of both units. Evidence: `OBS-DRBG-ENABLE-ORDER seed=<n> ack_seen=<0 or 1> edn_sw_cmd_sts=0x<s> csrng_sw_cmd_sts=0x<s> edn_err_code=0x<e> csrng_err_code=0x<e> edn_recov=0x<r> csrng_recov=0x<r>`; a timeout adds `reason=timeout:<what> bound=<b>`.
|OBS-DRBG-SEED-INGRESS
|Not a graded check. No text states the result of an entropy word that arrives at the DRBG seed adapter faster than the packer takes it, or the depth of the seed FIFO. The monitor counts, over the whole run, the words offered with the ready input high (accepted) and low (dropped), and the number of accepted words before the first dropped one (`na` when none is dropped). Evidence: `OBS-DRBG-SEED-INGRESS seed=<n> accepted=<a> dropped=<d> depth=<n or na>`.
|CTRL-CSRNG-BUS-CMP
|Reach control of OBS-CSRNG-BUS-CMP. The two known-answer Generates with the same seed return 4-word blocks that both equal the golden block of CHK-CSRNG-KAT (so they equal each other: two equal consecutive values on the software port), both acknowledge with `CMD_STS` = 0, and `RECOV_ALERT_STS` bit 12 (`CS_BUS_CMP_ALERT`) reads 0 after the write of 0 that precedes the second Generate. A failing control logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf. Evidence: `CTRL-CSRNG-BUS-CMP seen: seed=<n> blk1=[0x..x4] blk2=[0x..x4] golden=[0x..x4] blk1_eq_blk2=1 bit12_after_clear=0 gen_cmd_sts=[0, 0]`; failure: `CTRL-MISSING CSRNG-BUS-CMP: seed=<n> ...` (a timeout adds `reason=timeout:<what> bound=<b> step=<s>`).
|OBS-CSRNG-BUS-CMP
|Not a graded check. Bit 12 "is set when the software application port genbits bus value is equal to the prior valid value on the bus" (`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/csrng/data/csrng.hjson:655-656`). Between the two blocks the instance is uninstantiated, which zeroes "Values in the instance" (`theory_of_operation.md:270`); the instance table (`:60-90`) does not list the compare value, and no text states whether it survives. The leaf logs bit 12 and the register after the second Generate. Evidence: `OBS-CSRNG-BUS-CMP seed=<n> bit12=<0 or 1> recov=0x<r> blk1=[...] blk2=[...]`.
|CTRL-CSRNG-VCTR-WRAP
|Reach control of OBS-CSRNG-VCTR-WRAP. With flag0 True and clen 12 "Only provided additional data will be used as seed" (`theory_of_operation.md:227`), the block uses no derivation function (`README.md:47`), and SP 800-90A 10.2.1.3.1 gives V = AES-256_{K=0}(3) XOR seed[127:0], so the leg sets seed word 0 to put V[31:0] at 0xfffffffe. Block 1 of the glen 2 Generate (V[31:0] = 0xffffffff) equals the golden block of `env/sep_ctr_drbg_golden.py` and the Generate acknowledges with `CMD_STS` = 0, so the DUT took the seed and ran to the wrap point. A failing control logs `CTRL-MISSING` and fails the leaf. Evidence: `CTRL-CSRNG-VCTR-WRAP seen: seed=<n> seed_w0=0x<w> expect_w0=0x<w> gen_cmd_sts=0 block1=[0x..x4] golden1=[0x..x4]`; failure: `CTRL-MISSING CSRNG-VCTR-WRAP: seed=<n> seed_w0=0x<w> expect_w0=0x<w> ... block1=[...] golden1=[...]`.
|OBS-CSRNG-VCTR-WRAP
|Not a graded check. Block 2 follows the increment of V[31:0] = 0xffffffff, which depends on ctr_len (SP 800-90A 10.2.1.2), and no vendored or TT document states the ctr_len of the CSRNG (`env/sep_ctr_drbg_golden.py` raises at this point for the same reason). The leaf logs block 2 with the two candidates of a test-local golden subclass, ctr_len 32 and ctr_len 128, and the one it matches. Evidence: `OBS-CSRNG-VCTR-WRAP seed=<n> block2=[0x..x4] cand_ctr32=[0x..x4] cand_ctr128=[0x..x4] match=<32, 128 or none>`.
|CHK-CSRNG-VCTR-DISTINCT
|Block 2 of the glen 2 Generate differs from block 1. The CSRNG "Utilizes the CTR_DRBG construction specified in NIST SP 800-90A" (`vendor/lowRISC/opentitan/upstream/hw/ip/csrng/README.md:34`), and SP 800-90A Rev. 1 10.2.1.5.1 step 4.1 increments V as `(rightmost (V, ctr_len) + 1) mod 2^ctr_len` for every allowed ctr_len (Table 3: 4 <= ctr_len <= blocklen), so block 2 runs on a V other than the V of block 1 under the same key. AES-256 under one key is a permutation, so the two blocks differ whatever the ctr_len. Graded only after CTRL-CSRNG-VCTR-WRAP passed. Anchor: the eight `GENBITS` reads. Evidence: `CHK-CSRNG-VCTR-DISTINCT PASS: seed=<n> block1=[0x..x4] block2=[0x..x4] expect=block2!=block1`; failure: `CHK-CSRNG-VCTR-DISTINCT FAIL: seed=<n> block1=[0x..x4] block2=[0x..x4] expect=block2!=block1`.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every named checker PASSes. UVM_ERROR == 0.

*Known limitation*: CHK-CSRNG-VCTR-DISTINCT fails on this DUT on every seed: the CSRNG V counter holds V[31:0] at 0xffffffff instead of wrapping, so block 2 repeats block 1.

*Issue*: #3541

*Functional Coverage*: owner window 150. Windows per check: `cp_auto_hw_cmd[reseed_after_n1, reseed_after_n2_4]` take their sample at the generate handshake that follows the reseed; the leaf grades `CHK-EDN-AUTO-SEQ` first and, only after a PASS, opens the window for the one edge that takes the last handshake (N = 1 on every seed, the drawn N of 2 to 4 on every seed). `cp_kat_generate` is sampled on the fourth OKAY `GENBITS` read and equals the golden of the sampler; the window spans the known-answer step and closes before the grades (`CHK-CSRNG-KAT`). `cp_inst_ack_vs_seed` samples on the EDN `CMD_ACK` rise with `CMD_STS` 0 after at least 256 clocks without a seed handshake or acknowledge, with a seed handshake before the acknowledge; an unknown seed handshake bit before the 256 clocks complete disarms it; the window opens one clock before the `SW_CMD_REQ` write and closes at once on a seed handshake, an unknown seed handshake bit or a rise of bit 27 inside the hold (`CHK-EDN-INST-SEED`). `x_mode_fields` and `cp_reported_mode[boot_only]`: each graded boot runs first with the window closed and the same boot runs again with the window open only after the first passed (`CHK-EDN-BOOT`, `instance=first` and `instance=cov`). `x_inst_outcome[hw_port0_acknowledged]` samples only on the acknowledge of an EDN instantiate that a check grades: the second EDN instantiate of `CHK-EDN-INVSEQ` and its `RECOV_ALERT_STS` poll run with the window closed, and the window opens only after `CMD_ACK` reads 1, for the graded bit-14 read, the write of 0 and its readback. The boot words use `BOOT_INS_CMD` = 0x601, so the boot instantiate raises none of the cells that expect the 0x901 acknowledge. The header and reseed legs open the window after their first instantiate, so no ungraded instance of `x_inst_outcome[sw_first_done]` samples. In the reserved-acmd window of the EDN software port, `cp_recov_alert_set[invalid_acmd]` and the 3 bins of `cp_sw_port_hs` are graded by `CHK-EDN-ACMD-INV`; in the first-command auto trial, the 3 bins of `cp_sw_port_hs` are graded by `CHK-EDN-AUTO`. Each of the two checks polls `CMD_REG_RDY` to 1 before the write and `CMD_ACK` to 1 after it (a poll timeout fails the check) and reads `edn_cmd_req_done` = 1. In the reserved-acmd window the `x_line_gate` cell (1, 1) and `cp_hw_raise` of `edn_cmd_req_done` are graded by `CHK-EDN-ACMD-INV`, which reads the state bit, the enable and vector bit 27; in the CSRNG software leg both `cs_cmd_req_done` samples of each cell, from the first and the repeated instantiate, are graded by `CHK-CSRNG-SW`, which reads the state bit and vector bit 23 after each command. This owner samples no `x_line_gate` cell with the enable 0: no check of the leaf reads the vector bit with the enable 0. The reseed-leg window stays open across the `GENBITS` gate Generate and the known-answer Generate; `CHK-CSRNG-GENBITS-GATE` and `CHK-CSRNG-KAT` grade the acknowledge of each (`CMD_STS` = 0), so their `cp_sw_handshake` samples have a check; `cp_sw_handshake` samples a command header only, never an additional-data word of the known-answer instantiate; its `cmd_rdy_after_ack` cell is `ignore_bins` (`OBS-CSRNG-RDY-AFTER-ACK`). `cp_recov_alert_set[sw_app_enable_field]` from the gate `CTRL` write is graded by `CHK-CSRNG-GENBITS-GATE`, which reads bit 1. `cp_reported_mode` and `cp_auto_hw_cmd[generate_cmd]` sample once per window, at the command whose `HW_CMD_STS` the check of the window reads (the first write of `BOOT_MODE` in a boot window, the first hardware generate in the auto and instantiate-seed windows), so the later hardware commands of those windows sample nothing; the auto and instantiate-seed windows hold the software instantiate write to `SW_CMD_REQ`, and the one-edge windows after a `CHK-EDN-AUTO-SEQ` pass hold none, so they sample neither cell. The other bins of this owner sample the register value that their check compares. Each sample is held until the pass label `drbg_seq_pass`, which the leaf pulses after the checks of a window when every check and control since the last pulse passed; otherwise it pulses the drop value 2, which discards every held sample. No bin depends on the seed: N = 1 and a drawn N run on every seed, H is at least 256 on every draw, and the known-answer Generate runs on every seed. Open-value legs (`sep_open_value_leg_cg`): the leaf writes `csrng_bus_cmp_replay` after CTRL-CSRNG-BUS-CMP passed and `csrng_vctr_wrap` after CTRL-CSRNG-VCTR-WRAP passed, holds each two clocks and writes 0; a failed control writes nothing.

*Scope limit*: The Reseed header that `RESEED_CMD` takes is graded on the CSRNG software port (`CHK-CSRNG-RESEED-HDR`); `CHK-EDN-AUTO` grades the send of that word and of `GENERATE_CMD` from `HW_CMD_STS`, not the header bits. It does not grade the `CMD_STS` code of an EDN or a CSRNG instantiate, a repeated instantiate or an uninstantiate, the acknowledge of an ignored command, EDN `CSRNG_ACK_ERR`, `HW_EXC_STS` or `CS_HW_INST_EXC`: no sentence states these values (logged). It does not grade that the repeated instantiate is ignored (`csrng.adoc`, `RECOV_ALERT_STS` bit 14: "ignored and CSRNG continues to operate"): the text names no register value that shows it, and the internal state read needs `INT_STATE_NUM`, which is blocked. Of `RECOV_ALERT_STS` bits 0 to 4, 13, 14 and 15 are graded; bit 12 is not driven (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked-legs,Blocked legs>>). The index of the refused Generate, the `GENBITS` data and `GENBITS_FIPS` are logged or not read. The leaf drives no `clen` other than 0 (a non-zero `clen` in a boot word hangs EDN). It does not grade the boot time, the `CMD_ACK` value of the first read after a command write, or `CMD_RDY` after a software acknowledge (`OBS-EDN-RDY-AFTER-ACK`, `OBS-CSRNG-RDY-AFTER-ACK`). It does not grade the FIFO clear of `CMD_FIFO_RST` = True and does not drive `FIPS_FORCE` (see <<sep-vplan-phase3-blocked,Blocked features>>). The open entropy tests keep the data chain and the `ERR_CODE` and `RECOV_ALERT_STS` zero check after a boot instantiate (`CHK-ALERTS-ZERO`).

'''''

[[sep_trng_ext_stream_handshake_rand_test]]
===== 1.51 `sep_trng_ext_stream_handshake_rand_test`

*Phase*: 3

*Area*: Crypto and entropy (TRNG and entropy pool)

*Run mode*: `no_cpu` (`lsu_stub_all_live`), `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+esrc_noise_force`, `reseed = 4`; external stream driver (added hook, top-port drive), external stream response monitor and internal DRBG endpoint probe (observation taps); Verilator and VCS; `crypto.toml`, groups `cpu_stub` and `all`

*Source Module*: `sep_trng_ext_stream_handshake_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: ocah_axi_vip (CPU-LSU master). Probes and injects. Approved: CPU-LSU master readback (select, lock, pool `STATUS` and `DATA`); `axis1_*`, `axis2_*` and `km_entropy_*`; `crypto_edn_req_o[i]`, `crypto_edn_ack_o[i]` and `crypto_edn_fips_o[i]`; `pool_edn_ack_o` and `pool_edn_fips_o`; `sep_internal_interrupts_probe_o`; `trng_reset_active_probe_o`; the TRNG reset bit of `SW_RESET_N`; the `rst_ni` pulse. Added hook: the external stream driver, a top-port drive of `ext_trng_axis_req_i[2:0]` (with a source reset) and of `ext_trng_irq_i`, which the test adds to the port table of the HDL Top section of `docs/SEP_TB_ARCH.adoc`. The external stream response monitor (`ext_trng_axis_rsp_o[2:0]`) and the internal DRBG endpoint probe (`drbg_int_axis_req[i]`, `drbg_int_axis_rsp[i]`) are observation taps the test adds to the SEP_TB_ARCH observation-probe list. The checks `CHK-SEL-RW`, `CHK-SEL-LOCK`, `CHK-SEL-LOCKFIRST` and `CHK-SEL-GLOBAL` use approved items only. `CHK-SEL-HOLD` drives no external beat and uses the endpoint tap. Every other check uses the external stream driver (`CHK-EXT-IRQ` uses its `ext_trng_irq_i` driver).

*Randomization*: RAND-REP. `SepSeededRng` from the run seed. Dimensions and ranges: the order of streams 1 and 2 (both run on every seed); the beat count of the transfer leg (stream 1: 8 to 96; stream 2: an even number from 8 to 64, so that the empty 32-entry pool takes every beat); the gap pattern (back-to-back, random gaps of 0 to 20 clocks, or one long gap); the partial strobe (0x0 to 0xE); the partial-beat hold H (16 to 64 clocks); the hold window of the hold leg (64 to 256 clocks); the beat words (unique, non-zero, never equal to a neighbour); the select values d (any value other than the value held before the write: 0x7 on the first pass), s (the bit of the stream under test set, the other bits drawn, and not equal to d), a (not 0x7 and not the value held at the lock leg) and b (not a); the idle gap between the select write and the lock write (0 to 32 clocks); the initial pool fill (0 to 32 pairs); the random operation sequence (20 to 60 operations, in addition to the fixed corner run of the pop golden leg) and the feed positions. `reseed = 4` samples four sets of these draws. The leaf logs its plan first (`INFO-PLAN seed=<n> ...`).

*Objective*: The external stream has `tvalid`, `tready`, 32-bit `tdata`, 4-bit `tstrb` and no `tuser`; a source holds `tvalid` and `tdata` until `tready`. The mux gives each external beat a `tuser` of 1. The selected sink receives each accepted word unchanged and in order. The crypto and pool adapters accept only strobe 0xF, and a partial strobe stalls them. `ext_trng_irq_i` is routed to PIC source 17. `EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL.sel` resets to 0b111. Bit N selects the source of stream N (0 Key Manager, 1 crypto, 2 pool), 0 for the internal source and 1 for the external source. `EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL_LOCK.lock` (RW1S) freezes the select until the CPU-control bank resets, and a TRNG reset alone does not clear it. An unselected external input receives ready high and its beats are discarded. An unselected internal endpoint is back-pressured and keeps its queued data. A 64-bit read of the pool `DATA` pops one word. Spec: `hw/sys/sep/doc/trng.adoc` (Distribution and Source Selection); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_cpu_ctrl.adoc` (`EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL`, `EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL_LOCK`); `hw/sys/sep/doc/port_table.adoc`; `hw/sys/sep/doc/interrupts.adoc` (source 17); `hw/sys/sep/doc/entropy_pool.adoc` (Access Policy); `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/adoc/blocks/sep_entropy_pool.adoc` (`STATUS`, `DATA`).

*Test Coverage*: Streams 1 and 2, each with a drawn select value and then a select with the stream external. Full-strobe beats with drawn gap patterns; beats with `tstrb` 0x0 to 0xE as error injection (a partial strobe), each followed by a TRNG reset with a source reset of the driver and then a 0xF beat. The select bit of the stream cleared while beats arrive. All three streams with back-to-back beats under the drawn select bits. The pool filled to 32 entries with pairs {a, a}, then popped; a drawn fill, sequence of pops, `STATUS` reads and feeds. The internal endpoint held while the external source is selected, then reselected and drained, then held again with the external source selected, to count the words of the endpoint FIFO. `ext_trng_irq_i` high and low. The lock after a select write, before any select write, and after a `rst_ni` pulse. This entry extends `sep_trng_reset_recovery_test`, which grades that a non-reset select survives a TRNG reset; `sep_entropy_pool_aperture_test`, which grades the pool access shapes and `IRQ_CAUSE`; and `sep_address_map_test` and `sep_reg_bit_bash_rand_test`, which read the reset values of the select and the lock. This entry grades only the cells that those tests do not grade: the readback of drawn select values (the open test reads back one fixed non-reset value), the lock, the external stream and a pool pop with a driven golden. The open owners keep the rest. Out of scope: a source that drops `tvalid` of a live beat, an idle selected stream with a waiting consumer, the packing order inside a pair, and the `tready` fall of a selected stream 0 (logged only). Functional coverage: `sep_entropy_delivery_cg` (`x_sel_stream`, `cp_lock_order`, `x_ext_beat`, `cp_unselected_drop`, `cp_ext_stall`, `cp_hold_internal`, `cp_pool_pop`, `cp_ext_irq`, `cp_ext_tuser`, `cp_adapter_fifo_depth`), `sep_crypto_reset_cg` (`cp_reset_evidence`, bin `cpu_lock_survives`).

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Set the baseline. Read `EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL` and `EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL_LOCK` as the first accesses of the leaf, before any write, and require their RDL reset values (`CHK-SEL-RESET`). Write `SEP_RESET_CTRL.SW_RESET_N` = 0x24 (TRNG and AES released; Key Manager and the other engines held in reset) and log it. The baseline of every TRNG reset below is this value.
. Run steps 3 to 12 once for stream 1 and once for stream 2, in the drawn order. Then run steps 13 to 16 once. Every graded pool `STATUS` read of this entry is a settled read: wait until the pool path is quiet for 64 clocks (no transfer at external port 2, no handshake at `axis2_*` and no `pool_edn_ack_o`; a bound of 20000 clocks), then read `STATUS` until two consecutive reads agree (at most 64 reads). A settled read whose quiet wait or agreement expires fails the check that grades its level with `reason=timeout:settle`, and each graded check logs the read count of its settled reads as `settle_reads` (-1 for an expired read). A pool word takes about 4 clocks from the adapter to the pool, so a word that lands after the quiet window shows as a level mismatch. A beat counts as stalled when it stays pending for H clocks with `tready` low. The leaf samples the external port taps, the post-mux probes, the internal endpoint tap and the client acknowledge probes after each falling edge of `clk_i`.
. Reset the TRNG domain: write 0 then 1 to bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N` and wait for the fall of `trng_reset_active_probe_o` (a bounded wait; a timeout fails the leaf). The internal chain stays idle, because ESRC, CSRNG and EDN are not initialized, and the post-mux adapters and the pool are empty.
. Select leg. Write the select value d and read it back: expect d. Write the select value s (the bit of the stream under test set, the other bits drawn) and read it back: expect s. Steps 5 to 12 use s unless a step says otherwise.
. Transfer leg. For stream 1 trigger one AES PRNG reseed, so that a client request is pending on `crypto_edn_req_o[0]`. Offer the drawn number of beats with `tstrb` = 0xF and the drawn gap pattern, one beat at a time; the driver holds `tvalid` and `tdata` until `tready`. For stream 1 the leg also ends when a beat stays pending for H clocks (the adapter is full). Expect every beat that completes at the external port to appear at the post-mux probe (`axis1_*` or `axis2_*`) in the same order and with the same data, at least one beat to complete, and `edn_fips` = 1 for each word that the consuming adapter accepts (`crypto_edn_ack_o[0]` with `crypto_edn_fips_o[0]`, or `pool_edn_ack_o` with `pool_edn_fips_o`).
. Strobe leg: the control and the error injection. Reset the TRNG domain (step 3) so that the adapter is empty. Control: for stream 2 read `STATUS.fifo_level`, send one pair {a, a} with both beats at 0xF and expect `fifo_level` to rise by 1; for stream 1 send one 0xF beat, expect it to complete, and then reset the TRNG domain again (step 3), so that the adapter is empty before the partial beat. Error injection: a partial strobe. Point: `ext_trng_axis_req_i.tstrb` of the stream under test. Kind: a beat that the adapter does not accept (a legal source beat with a `tstrb` value from 0x0 to 0xE). For stream 2 send a pair whose first beat is 0xF and whose second beat is the partial beat; for stream 1 send the partial beat. Expected response: a stalled adapter. Wait until the partial beat is held at the port (stream 2: after the first beat completes; a bounded wait). Sample the post-mux probe for H clocks: expect `tvalid` = 1 and `tready` = 0 at every clock, and for stream 2 expect the settled `fifo_level` unchanged from its value after the control pair.
. Recover from the stall. Write 0 to bit 5 of `SW_RESET_N`. At the rise of `trng_reset_active_probe_o` apply the source's own reset to the driver; the sink reset and the source reset happen together, the driver forgets the held partial beat, and a running source does not drop `tvalid`. Log it. Write 1 and wait for the fall of the probe. Send one 0xF beat (stream 2: one 0xF pair) and expect it to complete. The select and the lock stay as written.
. Drop leg. For stream 2 read the settled `fifo_level`. Write s with the bit of the stream under test cleared and read it back. Send the drawn number of beats with `tstrb` = 0xF and wait until all of them complete at the port (a bounded wait). Expect `tready` = 1 at every clock of the leg, no word of these beats at the post-mux probe, and for stream 2 the settled `fifo_level` unchanged. Control: the transfer leg of step 5 completed beats in the same seed with the select bit set.
. Back-pressure leg (stream 2). Write s. Reset the TRNG domain (step 3). Offer pairs {a, a} with unique a, one beat at a time, until a beat stays pending for H clocks with `tready` low; a bound of 64 pairs (a test parameter) applies, and reaching it without a stall fails `CHK-EXT-BP`. Log N_cal, the number of beats accepted before the stall. At the stall read the settled `STATUS`: expect `fifo_level` = 32. Then pop `DATA` once for every pair sent, the pending pair included. Before each pop poll `STATUS` until `fifo_level` is at least 1 (at most 64 reads 8 clocks apart; a timeout fails `CHK-EXT-BP`). Expect each pop to answer OKAY and the pairs to appear once each and in the order sent. After the leg, set the select bit of stream 2 with no source driving it and offer no beat. Read the settled pool `STATUS` level, hold 4000 clocks, read it again, pop `DATA` once (a bounded wait) and restore the select. Grade the response of the pop against the second level (`CHK-EXT-NOSRC-POP`), and log both levels and the data (`OBS-EXT-NOSRC`).
. Ready leg. Write s. Reset the TRNG domain (step 3). Drive all three external streams with back-to-back valid beats (full strobe, unique words). For stream 1 and stream 2 with the select bit 1 and no sink draining, continue until every graded stream has a beat pending for H clocks with `tready` low, with a bound of 2000 accepted beats (a test parameter); reaching the bound without a fall fails `CHK-SEL-READY`. The fall of a stream is the start of the last stall run of the leg that lasts at least H clocks: the adapter staging FIFO takes one beat per clock and drains one word per sink handshake, so `tready` can drop for a few clocks earlier while the sink drains. Log the beats N_i accepted before the fall. For stream 2 expect at least 64 accepted beats before the fall (32 pool entries of two beats each). For stream 1, after the fall write `AES.TRIGGER.PRNG_RESEED` = 1 and expect the pending beat to complete (`tready` rises) within a bounded wait of 4000 clocks (a timeout fails `CHK-SEL-READY`). For stream 0, with the select bit 0 read back, expect `tready` 1 on every clock that stream 0 holds `tvalid` 1 (`CHK-SEL-READY0`; control: at least one such clock, `CTRL-SEL-READY0`); with the select bit 1, log `tready` in `OBS-SEL-READY0` and grade nothing.
. Hold leg. Bring up ESRC, CSRNG and EDN as the open entropy tests do, so that the internal endpoints fill. The bring-up writes `EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL` itself, so write 0x7 (every stream external) after it and read it back: expect 0x7. For stream 2 pop `DATA` until `STATUS.fifo_level` = 0 (at most 72 pops; the internal bring-up can fill the pool, and an empty pool can take words) and expect `fifo_level` = 0 before the hold. The external driver stays silent for the whole leg. Wait until the internal endpoint probe of the stream under test shows `tvalid` = 1 (a bounded wait; a timeout fails `CHK-SEL-HOLD`) and record the head word w. Sample the endpoint for the hold window: expect `tvalid` = 1, `tready` = 0 and `tdata` = w at every clock. Write 0x7 with the bit of the stream under test cleared (the internal source for that stream); for stream 1 write `AES.TRIGGER.PRNG_RESEED` = 1 so that a consumer is active (`aes.adoc`, `TRIGGER.PRNG_RESEED`: a reseed from the connected entropy source), and for stream 2 the pool is the consumer. Record the post-mux probe for a window of 4000 clocks from the reselect write; for stream 2 the pool is the consumer, and for stream 1 the leaf writes `AES.TRIGGER.PRNG_RESEED` at the start of each of 8 equal parts of the window (8 reseeds), so that the crypto clients take at least 8 words. Expect the first beat that completes at the post-mux probe to carry w. Expect at least one beat to drain for stream 1 and at least two beats (one pool entry of two beats) for stream 2 in the window, and log the count of drained beats (`CTRL-SEL-HOLD`: with the internal source selected and a consumer active the endpoint drains). Expect at least 8 beats to pass the post-mux probe in the window, the first of them the held word w (`CTRL-EDN-FIFO-DRAIN`). Then write 0x7 again (every stream external) and read it back (expect 0x7), so that EDN refills the endpoint FIFO while the output is held. Wait until the endpoint output has shown `tvalid` 1 and `tready` 0 for 64 clocks with no push (a bounded wait of 20000 clocks; a timeout fails `CHK-EDN-FIFO-DEPTH`), then 2 more clocks, and grade `CHK-EDN-FIFO-DEPTH`. After that write the select back to 0x7 with the bit of the stream under test cleared, outside the graded window.
. Pop golden leg (stream 2 only). Reset the TRNG domain (step 3), so that only the test fills the pool. Write the select with bit 2 set and bit 1 clear and read it back (`CTRL-POOL-DATA`). Draw the initial fill L (0 to 32 pairs {a, a}) and send it. Read the settled `STATUS` and expect `fifo_level` = L (`CHK-POOL-DATA op=fill`). Then run the corner run, then the drawn random operations. Each operation is a `DATA` pop (64-bit read at offset 0x10), a `STATUS` read (offset 0x0) or a feed of one pair. The corner run is a run of feeds to model level 32, one feed offered at level 32 (the pair can complete at the external port into the adapter or stay pending; the pool level stays 32 until a pop) and then a pop; it has 34 - L operations or more, and it does not count toward the drawn 20 to 60. A pop at model level 0 is replaced by a `STATUS` read. In the drawn operations a feed at model level 32 is also replaced by a `STATUS` read, so that no pair is staged above a full pool behind another staged pair (the specification states no staging depth); the corner run holds the only feed offered at level 32. After each operation wait for its settle event (the second beat of a feed completes at the external port, or, for a feed offered at level 32, either that or a beat that stays pending for H clocks with `tready` low; the response of a pop returns, and the completion of a pending feed if one exists; a pending feed whose beat stays pending for H clocks after the pop response fails `CHK-POOL-DATA` and ends the leg), then read the settled `STATUS`. A feed that neither completes nor stalls at level 32 within the bound fails `CHK-POOL-DATA` and ends the leg. The model level is the smaller of 32 and the pairs completed at the external port minus the pops; a pair above 32 is staged in the adapter and enters the pool at a later pop. Expect each pop to answer OKAY with the next pair in order, and the settled `fifo_level` to equal the model level.
. IRQ leg. Read the vector probe: expect bit 16 = 0 (control). Drive `ext_trng_irq_i` = 1 and expect vector bit 16 = 1 (a bounded wait of 64 clocks; a timeout fails `CHK-EXT-IRQ`). Hold the input for 8 clocks and log the clocks with bit 16 at the level (`OBS-EXT-IRQ-HOLD`; the text does not state that bit 16 is a level). Drive `ext_trng_irq_i` = 0 and expect vector bit 16 = 0 (a bounded wait of 64 clocks), then hold and log in the same way.
. Lock leg. Read the select as the held value. Write a select value a (not 0x7 and not the value that the plan holds at this leg) and read it back: expect a, and expect the held value to differ from a (control: the pre-lock write lands and changes the select). Wait the drawn idle gap, then write 1 to `EXT_TRNG_SRC_SEL_LOCK` and read it: expect 1. Write 0 to the lock and read it: expect 1. Write a select value b (not a) and read the select: expect a.
